kāppu
nīrvaḷanaṉ ṟōṅkun tiruvoṟṟi nīṇakariṟ
cūrvaḷantiṉ vēlōṟkut tūyanūl - cīrvaḷara
emmā liyaṟṟuvatoṉ ṟuṇṭā latuvākuṅ
kimmā mukattaṉ kaḻal.
nūl
1. neñcoṭukiḷattal
	nīrvaḷaṅ kaviṉṟa cīrvaḷar paḻaṉattu
	neṟkural kuviya naṟkuraṟ piṅkiḷi
	kāṉaṟ kāviṟ ṟāṉvirin teytip
	poṉvī ñāḻaṟ polintiṉi tiruppavuṅ,
5	karuṅkaḻi vāriya perumīṉ kuvii
	vaṉṟiṟaṟ paratavar muṉṟiṟoṟu muṇakkun
	tuṭikkaṭ ṭuṇiyal koṭikkulaṅ kavarntu
	marutaṅ kāviṟ peritu vikavuṅ,
	kuḷaṉuṟi vāḻkki vaḷanīrk kōḻi
10	maṉiyuṟi nīṅkit tuṇikūp peyarntu
	kālvaḻi yōṭik kaḻiyiṟ cēravum,
	pulavumaṇa muṉiiya nilavuṟaḻ kuruku
	kaṇṇakaṉ vicumpil naṇṇiya mukiṉmēl
	ivarnteḻu tiṅkaḷ pōlap parantukeḻu
15	tuṇinīrt taṭattiṉ maṇamalar ārum
	irumputirit taṉṉa aṟaṉmarup perumi
	karumpuṟa maruṅki ṉoruṅki niṟpavum,
	poṟikeḻu vāḷi veṟipaṭap pāyntu
	muṭikkaḻic cuṟavoṭu kaṟuvoṭu maṟiyavuṅ,
20	kāletirn tōṭuṅ kaḻikkayaṉ marutac
	cēloṭuṅ kūṭic cērntu tuḷḷavum,
	muḻuneṟi yaviḻkkuṅ koḻuṅkāl ōṭip
	poṉṉan tāmarip polantukaḷ vārik
	kōḻi muṭṭi pōḻpaṭut tāṅku
25	veḷḷayirk kāṉaṟ koḷḷiyiṉ vīcavum,
	veḷḷiveṇ ṭōṭṭut tāḻiyi ṉaḷiik
	kiyarik koṇṭa kaṭaṟkā lōṭi
	nīṟumey pūtta nēriyōṉ pōlac
	centā marikaḷ ciṟantiṉitu viḷaṅka
30.	veṇpoṭi vīcip paṇpoṭu mulāvavun,
	tēmoḻic cevvāyt tirumuka vuḻattiyar
	kāmaru koḻuñcuvi vāḻiyiṉ paḻaṉum
	puṉcey payanta paṉṉiṟap payaṟum
	naṉcey payanta poṉṉiṟa maṇiyum
35	vaṭṭikip peytu kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭiṉ
	eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṅ kaṭuttuyar pākkattuk
	kumiḻiṭi yukaḷum amiviḻip parattiyar
	pakuttuṇar viṉṟi mikuttuk koṭutta
	taraḷamum mīṉum niralpaṭa aḷantukoṇ
40	ṭuvappoṭu peyarntutam mirukki cēravum,
	marutamum neytalum mayaṅkiya marapiṉ
	ulaka mellām oruṅkuvan tuṟiṉun
	toliyā vaḷattoṭu nilavuṟu vippiṉ
	oṟṟi mānakark kuṟṟakaṇ ṇeṉavum,
45	akkaṇi ṉuḷvaḷa rarumaṇi yeṉavum,
	ammaṇi yuḷḷuṟi pāviyi yeṉavum
	pāviyi ṉakattup paṇpupala kāṭṭi
	mēvi yāṅkatu puṭipeyarn tiyaṅkat
	takka vāṟu mikkatu puriyum
50	ōriyal pillā āruyi reṉavum,
	iruvē ṟulakiṉum iraṇṭaṟa viravi
	aṟivun toḻilum neṟiyuṟa viḷakkum
	mātoru kūṟaṟ kōtiya mutalvaṉ,
	cellaluṟ ṟaḻuṅkiya mallaṟ ṟēvar
55	kuḻāmperi tuyya vaḻāvē lōccip
	pāṭil cūrukku vīṭu nalki
	vēntaṉ payanta kūntaṟ kumariyi
	maṇantula kaḷitta niṇantikaḻ vēlaṉ,
	aiyaṉ ceyyaṉ paṉṉiru kiyaṉ,
60	vaṟiyē miṭumpi poṟipaṭut tetirum
	periyōṉ periya raṟiviṉuk kariyaṉ
	ṟiruvuru vorumiyi ṉeḻuvit tarumiyoṭu
	vaḻipā ṭiyaṟṟi viḻinī ruṟippa
	aṉpuru vāki yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu
65	puṉporuḷ kavarumeṉ miṉpuri maṉaṉē!
	varampaṟu māṟṟal nirampiya murukaṉ
	ṟāṉeḻun taruḷum vāṉkuṭi lāka
	niṉṉit tantaṉa ṉākap, piṉṉi
	maṇṭiri vākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum
70	puṉaṟiri vākap pukuvaṉa cilavum
	aṉaṟiri vāka aṭivaṉa cilavum
	vaḷiyiṭa ṉākat teḷivaṉa cilavum
	vicumpiṭaṉāka ucumpuṉa cilavum
	ōvā tiruppa mēvuni, yataṉāṟ
75	ṟuṉpamuṅ kavaliyum niṉpuṟa ṉāka
	iṉpuṟa laṟiyā tiṭarppaṭu kuṉiyē, yatāaṉ
	ṟuṟutuṇi yākac ceṟiyu meṉṉiyum
	puṟcāyt tōṭum puṉalē pōla
	niṉvaḻip paṭīi yeṉṉuyirk kuyirām
80	aṟumukat tiyaṉi maruvaloṭ ṭātu
	vaṉmi ceytaniṉ puṉmiyō peritē
	yiṉinī yiṅṅaṉa moḻiyātu, paṉimalark
	kaṭampu cūṭiya taṭantōṇ murukaṉ
	ṟiruvurup potiyu morukala ṉāka
85	viḻumitiṉ nīṭu vāḻmati koḻuviya
	nalaṅkeḻu naṟumpāl peyta
	polaṅkalam poliyum peṟṟiyā leṉavē.	(1)
2. aruḷiyalpurittal
	peṟṟi yariya perumāṉ periyatiru
	voṟṟi nakarvanta voḷvēlāṉ - naṟṟavaruṅ
	kāṇā ariya kaḻalā ṉeṉatuḷattu
	nāṇā tamarntavā naṉṟu.	(2)
3. kāmamikka kaḻipaṭarkiḷavi
naṉṟaṉ ṟuṉakkut tiruvoṟṟi vēlari nāṭiyiṭar
iṉṟiṅku nīkoḷḷa leṉṟuri yīraka līrṅkaḻikāṇ!
miṉṟaṅku noyciṟi vaṇṭiṉaṅ kāḷiṉi mēlemakku
niṉṟiṅku nīrceyyu naṉmiyeṉ ṉōvoṉṟu nērvatiṉṟē.
4. tōḻi viravikkūṟal
	nēriṟi muṉki vārolik kūnta
	larikaṭi yoḻukiya perumatar maḻikkaṇ
	ilavuṟaḻ cevvāy nilavuṟaḻ tirunutaṟ
	koṭipuri maruṅkuṟ kaṭitikaḻ mēṉik
5	karumpiṉu miṉiyaveṉ ṉarumpeṟaṟ pāvāy!
	vaḻivaḻic ciṟakkumeṅ kaḻiperuṅ kulattiṟ
	pāṟkaṭaṟ ṟōṉṟiya cīrttiru vaṉiyāy!
	valampuri yaṉṉaveṅ kulampuri kōmakaḷ
	ariti ṉīṉṟa perumut taṉiyāy!
10	miṭiyaṟi yāveṅ kuṭiyeṉu muṟṟat
	taṉpunīr pāytti yiṉpuṟak kicey
	tāyamu miyāṉuñ cīritiṉ vaḷarkkum
	naṟpayaṉ kaṉinta poṟcuṭark koṭiyē!
	nakkum pukkum mikkamakiḻ ceyyuñ
15	ceyiraṟa veḻutiya vuyirō viyamē!
	arumiyiṉ mikuntavem perumutaṟ ṟanti
	yāṟṟā viruppoṭu mēṟṟip pārkkum
	viḷivaṟa viḷaṅkiya voḷimaṇi viḷakkē!
	niṉṉaru ṇāṭit tuṉṉi niṉṟiṅ
20	kaṭiyēṉ kū ṟu moṭiyāc ciṟumoḻi
	vaṟiteṉa voḻiyā taṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum,
	vayaluḻavar kuvaḷikaṭṭu
	viyalvarampiṟ ṟaṭintiṭṭa
	kayaliṉiṭip paṭaveṟiya
25	marutamakaḷ karuviḻipōṟ
	poruviṉṟit tiruviḷaṅkac,
	cennel vittiya poṉṉuṟaḻ vāṉmuḷi
	puriyaviḻat talivirintu
	piyatteṉṉap parantiruppat,
30	teḷḷoḷip paḷiṅki ṉuḷḷayir peytu
	pāciliyuñ cētāmpaluṅ
	kaṭkuvaḷiyum muṭṭāmariyum
	oruṅkupaṭa veḻuti maruṅkuvit tāṅku
	naṟpūṅ kayaṅkaḷ poṟpoṭu mimippat
35	taṇṇaṭi maruviya taṇiyā viḷiyuḷ
	oṟṟimā nakariṉ muṟpaṭa vamarnta
	uṭkuṭi murukaṉik kaṭkaṇ ṭāṅkup,
	pārttāṉāp payilvaṭiviṉ
	ōrttāṉā vuraṉuṇarviṟ
40	collāṉāp palpukaḻiṟ
	ṟāṭoṭa vīḻnta kiyaṉ, ṟōṭoṭu
	poṉñāṇ piṇitta villaṉ kaṇiyaṉ,
	miṉñāṇ piṇitta kuñciya ṉiḷiñaṉ,
	ariyaṉ periyaṉ ṟiruvaḷar celvaṉ,
45	kuṭiyiṉuṅ kulattiṉum vaṭuvoṉ ṟillāṉ,
	peṟuvatoṉ ṟuṭiyaṉ pōla maṟuvantu
	paṉṉā ḷeṉiṉumeṉ coṉṉili tavaṟāṉ,
	kuṟittatu kiḷavātu ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ,
	urumum uḷiyamum aravum piriyā
50	āratar nīntic cīritiṟ pōntu
	tiritaru nāḷkaḷum palavē, yorunāḷnam
	maliyakaṉ cāraṟ ṟalimiyoṭu poliyum
	ārā mattiṉī vārā yāka,
	orutaṉic ceṉṟēṉ parivoṭu pukuntu
55	pāṉīr vāviyiṉ mēvi niṟpa, avaṉumāṇ
	ṭeytiyeṉ ṉoṭuniṉ ṟaṉaṉē, noytiṟ
	ṟiriporu kariyiṟ puriyiṉ ṟōṅkip
	pacuṅkuḻi kalittu nacinaṉi yarumpit
	teṉṟaloṭu pukunta vaṇṭicip pāṇaṉ
60	iṉimiyiṉ miḻaṟṟun taṉiyicik kuvantu
	potiyaviḻ malariṉ naṟavuṇak koṭuttu,
	niṉitoṟuñ cuvikkum nīṉiṟak kaṉikaḷ
	ilitoṟuṅ kuḻuma nalamikap payakkuṅ
	kōḻari nāval kōṭṭi ṉukuppap,
65	pāliṭi viḻūum nīlamaṇi kaṭuppa
	āmiṭi viḻūu maruñcuvik kaṉikaḷ
	aḷiyiṭit tuñcu marumpeṭik kaḷippa
	vēppiṉ koḻunaṉi yēykkum neṭuṅkaṭ
	kavikkā lalavaṉ ṟāḷiṭi yiṭukki
70	yaṉpuṭaṉ ceṉṟāṅ karutta ṉōkki,
	eṉṉiyu nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ,
	uykuva taṟiyāṉ pōlac ceykuva
	toṉṟuṅ kāṇā taṉṟuniṉ ṟaṉaṉē,
	nīkaṇ ṭaṉiyēl neñcanek kuruki
75	yiṉṉuyir vāḻali maṉṉē, koṉṉuṟu
	pēykaṇ ṭaṉiya peṟṟiya ṉāki
	niṟiyum aṟivum muṟimuṟi cāyat
	tuṉṟunili nīṅkā marampōṟ
	kaṉṟiya nōkkamoṭu niṉṟava ṉiliyē.	(4)
5. tōḻimuṉṉuṟavuṇartal
niliyun tirintu niṟiyuṅ kaṭantu
taliyuṅ kaviḻnta takiyaṇ- malinivanta
teṉṉap poliyum eyiloṟṟic cevvēṇmēṉ
maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭiya mātu.	(5)
6. pirivāṟṟāta talivikkut tōḻikūṟal
mātē peritum varuntuva teṉṉi maṉantirital
ētē yaṟiyiṟ koṭumiyaṉ ṟōvaṉṉi yīrmpotumpiṟ
cūtēy tuṇimuli yākan taḻuvit tuyarmikutta
pōtēr poḻiloṟṟic cevvēḷ variyap pukuntaṉaṉē
7. iṉanalaṉurittal
	pukalaru nūḻiyiṟ pōkkarum poruḷpōl
	ikalaṟu civat tiṟañcelā neñciṉar;
	neṟippaṭu makkaṭ kaṟivu pētuṟukkuṅ
	kavaliyiṟ piṟari mayakkunar uvaliṭu
5	patukkiyiṟ puṉporuḷ akattiṭu karaviṉar;
	puṉimāṇ kōti poruntac cūṭṭiyum
	viṉimāṇ viḷaṅkiḻi vīṟupaṭat tiruttiyuṅ
	koḻuṅkayaṉ maḻikkaṇ piṟaḻtoṟu maṉantirintu
	ceḻuntuvarc cevvāy muttaṅ koṇṭum
10	nacipiṟak kiṭātu mulitaḻīic cellum
	neñcamar kātalar piṉcelu maṭavār
	pañciṉ mellaṭi pitaluṟ ṟuḻappa
	uṟuttuk kiḻikkum paralkeḻi murampiṟ,
	puliyata ḷuṭukki tii valikeḻu
15	mūvili vaṭivē loruvayiṉ viḷaṅkak
	koṉṟinaṉi piṇitta maṉṟalan toṭali
	pavaḷamali yuṟaḻum mārpiṟ ṟuvaḷa
	malitaru maṭanti kuṟaṅkiṉmici yiruntu
	valampaṭu kiyāṟ perumpuṟaṅ kavii
20	ulampaṭu tiraṭō ḷorumuli yoṟṟa
	nariyē ṟūrnta puricā ṟōṟṟam
	niṉitoṟuṅ kuḻiyu muravōrkku vikaluṅ
	kēṭupuri neñciṟ pīṭilā vamaṇar,
	pīṭuyar centamiḻk kūṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟip
25	poypaṭu puṉporuḷ kāṉṟiruḷ parappi
	meytaru civa naṉṉeṟi piḻippa
	māṇā vaḻippaṭūuṅ kūṉpāṇ ṭiyaṉmutal
	āṟuceṉ mākkaḷic cūṟikoṇ ṭeṟintu
	peruntuya ruṟukkuṅ kāli, yaruntavam
30	niṟpeṟu poruḷiṟ paṟpaka lāṟṟi
	vēṇṭiya periyōrkku vēṇṭiyāṅ kaḷippak
	karukkuḻik kiṭakkun taṉmiyi yaṉmiyiṉ
	uruppeṟa varukuni pōla vavarakat
	toruperu māyam perukuṟac ceytu
35	puṟampeyarn teyti yaṟantaru perumic
	caṇpiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉi mātō, piṉpu
	kiṇkiṇi yolippat tantipiṟ ceṉṟu
	taṇkaḻu nīruñ centā mariyum
	oruṅkutali mayaṅkiya peruntaṭa maruṅkiṉ
40	arumpeṟaṟ ṟantiyit talikkūṭiṉiyē, ataṟpuṟam
	muruntuṟaḻ veṇṇakip perumpeya raṉṉi
	karumpuṅ kaṉiyum peruñcuvip pālun
	tiruntiya tēṉum oruṅkuṟak kūṭṭik
	kuḻitteṭut taṉṉa viḻivaṟā marapiṉ
45	mulipoḻi yamiḻtam vaḷḷat tūṭṭap
	pavaḷavāy maṭuttut tivaḷoḷi ciṟantu
	paḻakuṟu tantikku maḻaviṭi yamarnta
	tantiyit tāyoṭuṅ kāṭṭip, piṉṟik
	kāṇṭaku tiruviḷi yāṭaṉ māṇṭakac
50	ceytiṟam aṟiyār uytiṟam nōkkip
	paṟpaka liyaṟṟiya piṉṉar muṟpaṭarn
	taruntami ḻāynta peruntamiḻk kūṭaṟ
	kulacciṟi muṉiva ṉetirkoḷac ceṉṟu
	pāṟkaṭaṟ piṟanta cīrttiru vākaliṉ
55	irumulik kuṭattiṉ oruvaḻi yaṭakki,
	malimaka ḷātaliṉ mulitaral curuṅka
	vaḷḷat tēntiya cillamu tarunti
	āṉā vēṭkiyiṉ varuvō ṉivaṉeṉa
	mulimukan tiṟanta vaḻivaḻip pōkkip
60	pāṟkaṭa lūṭṭiya pavaḷac cevvāyt
	teṉṉavaṉ ṟēvikkum poṉpōṟ putalvaṉ
	āyiṉi yeṉpa taṟiyat teruṭṭi,
	vaḻuti koṇṭa muḻunōy tīra
	aṉṉi yūṭṭiya vamiḻti yiṉṉuric
65	centami ḻākkit tantatu keṭuttup,
	pāṟpaṭu pūtamum mēṟpaṭu poruḷun
	taṉvaḻipa paṭūun taṉmiya vākaliṟ
	ṟāṉpiṟi tākal vēṇṭiṟ ṟalimayaṅkit
	tantoḻil tirintu nantu meṉpatum,
70	iḻikkum poruḷuñ ceḻikku meṉpatum
	nīriṉum neruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉi yorutiṟam
	paṉiyiṉum eṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉi maṟutiṟam, iṉiyē
	aṟivoḷi koḷīip purineṟip poyccamaṇ
	muṟiya nūṟi meyttiṟaṅ kiḷakkum
75	meypporuṭ civam meyvaki viḷakkip
	paraṅkuṉ ṟamarntaṉi mātē, atupeyarn
	tiraṅkuvā laruvi nirampat tōṉṟum
	āviṉaṉ kuṭiyiṉ  aciyiṉi mātō, ataṟpuṟam
	ērakat teḻuntaṉi mātō, nēritiṉ
80	orumoḻi vitta vuṭporuḷ virippat
	tiruvaḷar taṇikiyi ṉamarntaṉi, ataṟpuṟam
	niṉkaḻaṟ kiṭanta arukā aṉpiṟ
	civam vaḷarkkum meyvaki marapiṉ
	uṭalporu ḷāvi yuṉakkeṉa niṟuvi
85	niṟkuṟit teḻūum aṟkum iṉpattu
	niṉpeya rallatu piṟitoṉṟu navilā
	maṉperum pulavōṉ, ṟantiyik kāṭṭun
	tāyeṉap pōntu nāyiṉuṅ kaṭiya
	eṉṉiyu moruporuṭ paṭuttu niṉṉiyal
90	porutuṟip pukutti yurikku muravōṉ
	cōma cuntara tēcikap peyariṉ
	ēmuṟa vantōṟ karuḷak kāmuṟṟup
	poruḷuṭa lāvi poruḷpeṟa vaḻaṅki
	vēṇṭuḻi yellām virumpiniṉ ṟētta
95	avaṟkeḷi vanta tavapperun taṉmiyi
	yoṟṟiyūrp pukuntu vikiṉi, muṟṟavum
	niṉperun taṉmiyi yaṟintu niṉṉaṭi
	yiravoṭu pakalum oruvātu vaṇaṅki
	nāttaḻu taḻuppa ētturi kiḷantu
100	neñcanek kuṭintu meyvitir vitirppap
	peṟuvataṟ keḷiyēṉ ariye ṉāyiṉum
	avaṉoṭu keḻūuñ cārpiṉ ivaṇatu
	civaṇavum peṟukuve ṉaṉṟē, kuvaṭukeḻu
	peruniṟap poṉmali yaṭinta
105	karuniṟak kākkik kuṟuvatā leṉavē.	(7)
8. vaḷḷaṉmi kiḷattal
uṟuporuḷ kāṇā vuṇarvilār māṭṭup
peṟuporuḷ vēṇṭal piḻiyāl - naṟuneñcē
yēntumuli vaḷḷi koḻunaṉ eḻiloṟṟip
pōntu kuṭiyirukkum pōtu.	(8)
9. talimakaḷ  tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal
pōtāyntu veṇṇilavokkum poṭimaṇaṟ pūñciṟṟiloṉ
ṟōtāyañ cūḻa iḻittāṭuṅ kāli yoruvaṭivēṟ
cūtāṉa kāḷi tiruvoṟṟi yīcar tuṇipputalvaṉ
kātāruṅ kaṇṇi citittunak kōṭik karantaṉaṉē	(9)
10. oppumikūṟi aruṇili vēṇṭal
	karavaṟu māntarkku viravuṟu paḻiyiṉ
	maṟuvoṭu viḷaṅku niṟikatir matiyam
	veṇkatir virikkun taṇṇeṉ kālip
	pulavumaṇaṅ kamaḻum pākkattu nalaṅkeḻu
5	teriviṉ mākkaḷ puritimil pukuntu
	niriniri vakutta vakiyami viḷakkam
	vāṉa mīṉiṉ vayiṉvayi ṉimippavuñ,
	ciṟutuṭi maruṅkiṟ karuṅkaṇ nuḷicciyar
	eḻuvarum eṇmarum uḻiyuḻik kipiṇin
10	toruvā marapiṟ kuravi yayaravum,
	avartaru ciṟāar ciṟumīṉ mukantu
	maṇalakaḻ kēṇiyiṉ muṟimuṟi viṭuttum
	porikeḻu puṉṉip pūntuṇar vīḻppap
	purivaḷi yeṟiyak kōṭu tākkip
15	palavē ṟuṭinta nalaṅkiḷar nittilaṅ
	koḻuviya naṉiyeṉak kuñcip peytu
	vaḻīi vīḻak kaṇkaḷuḻpu peyaravuṅ,
	koḻumaṭa laviḻnta kuḻūukkoḷ kiti
	vāṉcitar aḷāya tēṅkamaḻ āmpal
20	nīṟuṟu taḻalē pōlavum pālkeḻu
	kuṟunaṭip putalvar tuvaritaḻ pōlavum,
	aṟiviṉ mākkaṭkum aṟivu pētuṟukkum
	puṉiyāp poṟpiṉ muṟimuṟi ciṟappavum,
	neytal cāṉṟa kitiyaṅ kāṉal
25	oṟṟiyū ramarnta veṟṟivēṟ kuricil!
	koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuveṉ kēṇmati peruma!
	nīyē, āruyirt tokutikkup pēṟutaral vēṇṭi
	aintoḻil iyaṟṟuvi mātō, yāṉē
	oruneṟi yiṉṟip poṟivaḻi yōṭi
30	aimpulaṉ nukarntāṅ kamikuve ṉaṉṟē,
	nīyē, makkaṇmēṟ koṇṭa poccami laṉpiṟ
	ṟāyē yaṉiya vaḷiyiṉi, yāṉē
	maṉiviyum makkaḷum poruḷeṉak koṇṭu
	niṉivatoṉ ṟillāp pēraṉ piṉaṉē,
35	nīyē, yāṉiveṇ maruppun tēṉiṉ iṟālum
	mayiṟṟaḻip pīliyum virikamaḻ cāntamum
	oruṅkutali mayaṅkiya irumperuṅ kuṉṟat
	tāṉā tuṟiyum iyalpiṉi, yāṉē
	pulamil kalviyum nalamil pukaḻum
40	vaḻuviya voḻukkamuṅ kaḻuvāk kuṟṟamum
	oruvaḻi yamintu perukiya cerukkeṉuṅ
	kōṭukeḻu kuṉṟiṟ pīṭuṟu kuveṉē,
	nīyē, aṟalneṟit taṉṉa ceṟikuḻaṟ kaṟṟiyum
	piṟiceṟit taṉṉa naṟikamaḻ nutaluṅ
45	kayaliṇit taṉṉa akaliru viḻiyuṅ
	kumikimukiḻt taṉṉa amivaru mūkkum
	paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tuḷaṅkoḷik katuppum
	vaḷḷi yaṉṉa teḷḷoḷic ceviyuṅ
	kulikan tōytta ilavuṟaḻ itaḻum
50	muṟuval pūppa miṉṉeṉa miḷiruṅ
	kuṟumut taṉṉa ciṟumuḷ ḷeyiṟuṅ
	koḻunīrp paṇilattu viḻumiya kaḻuttum
	emakkaruṇ marapiṟ ṟamakkami kiyuñ
	civiṟivirit taṉṉa ciṟutiru vaṭiyum
55	emmuḷam nīṅkāc cemmiyiṟ ṟōṉṟa
	vīṟukeḻu pakutiyiṉ vēṟiru maṭavār
	kūṟukoṇ ṭirupuṭi viḷaṅkac cīṟiya
	mayiṉmici varukuvi maṉṉō, yāṉē
	karuneṟi maṟamum arukiya vaṟivum
60	virivuṟu mayakkamun tirukiya ciṉamun
	toṇṭarp paṇiyā nāṇamum eṇṭōṇ
	mukkaṭ perumāṉ perumpukaḻ nakkuc
	camiyak kaṇakkar amivila kūṟum
	pulluri koḷḷum pollāk kiḻamiyum
65	vekuḷiyoṭu paṭūun takiyiṉ māṟṟamum
	aṟivōr nakippa maṭavōr tikippap
	poruḷoṭu poruntāp pukuntucoṉ moḻiyum
	uṟuvarip pēṇā teḻumiṟu māppum
	eḷivan tōrum iravaṉ mākkaḷum
70	aḻuviḷik kampali koḷḷak kuḻiviṉṟip
	paḻutuṟac ceṉṟāṅ keṟintunaku muruṭum
	aṟivoṭu paṭāac ciṟutoḻiṉ muṟimiyum
	nīṅkā marapiṉ ōṅkip poliya
	yāṉeṉa teṉṉuṅ kūṉaṟ kiḻattiyar
75	perumpuṟaṅ kavavi vayiṉvayiṉ amittu
	maṟantēr vāḻkki mayiṉmicik koṇṭu
	ciṟantatōr tiṟattiṟ civaṇuve ṉaṉṟē,
	nīyē, kāntaḷaṅ kaṇṇi yēntiya mārpattuc
	ceñcāntu nīviya ciṟanta kōlamoṭu
80	kuṟavar makaḷir viḻavoṭu kuḻīi
	veṟiyayar kaḷattil viḷaṅkuvi, yāṉē
	mulliyan toṭali murukeḻa aṇintu
	veṇcāntu pūciya kēḻkiḷar mārpamoṭum
	iḻiciṉa makaḷir iyinta iṉpa
85	veṟiyāṭu kaḷattu veḷippaṭu kuveṉē, eṉavāṅku
	niṉṉoṭu meṉṉiṭip paṭṭaviv viyipāl
	eṉakkunī aruṭaral iyiyun, taṉakku
	nikarā kunaroṭu nakitaru kēṇmi	
	ceyalā kuvateṉa moḻikuvar, ataṉāl
90	nuntiyum muṉikuvaṉ allaṉ aṉṉiyum
	nunti vaḻippaṭūun, takiyaṇ, mintukeḻu
	poruppip pōḻntu viruppoṭum etirnta
	arimā mukattaṉip poripaṭa nūṟik
	kāḻuṟu marakkari nūḻi lāṭṭip
95	poriyari māvāy virikaṭaṉ mūṭiya
	cūrmutaṉ mutalaṟa murukkip pēraruḷ
	aḻivupaṭum uḷḷattup paḻiyoṭu piṟaṅkum
	uliyā vāḻkkit tēvarkkut
	talimiyoṭu nalkun tamiḻkiḻa vōyē.	(10)
11. pirivāṟṟāta talivi matiyoṭu varuntal
kiḻami niṉiyāta kēḷmatiyē! oṟṟik
kuḻakaṉ pirinta kuṟippāṟ - poḻutaṟintu
cellumuyirp pētikkut tīṅkiḻippāy niṉṉiyaṭum
vallaravik kollā mayil.	(11)
12 . tōḻi paruvaṅkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttal
mayiṉmī tivarntu tiruvoṟṟi yūrcceṉṟa maṉṉavartām
ayiṉmēl amarttakaṇ ṇāypirin tārallar aṉpumikkup
payiṉōṭu cērttiya kaṟpōṟ ṟuṇiyip payirntakuraṟ
kuyiṉmā voṭuṅkum poḻutumvan taṉṟiṉik kūṭuvarē.	(12)
13. aruḷnili viyattal
	kūṭiya iruḷiṉ vāṭuva toṉṟō
	pīṭuyar niṉpukaḻ nāṭā toṉṟō
	peṟuva taṟiyāc ciṟumi yoṉṟō
	maruvarak kiṭanta terivil kālattup
5	poruvil iṉpan taruvatu kuṟittup
	puṉmuta lākiya meyyiṟ pukutti
	ōraṟi vuṟuttiṉi cilanāṭ, cilanāḷ
	nantumuta lākiya vuṭampiṟ pukuttut
	tantaṉi īruṇar vaṉṟē, cilanāṭ
10	citaṉmuta lākiya kurampiyiṟ celīi
	mūvuṇar vuṇarttiṉi maṉṉō, cilanāḷ
	naṇṭumuta lākiya poṉṟuṭaṟ kaṭavi
	nāṉkaṟi vōṅkuṟac ceytaṉi, īṅku
	māvum mākkaḷum mēviya piṉṟi
15	aiyuṇar veṅkum meypeṟak koḷuvi
	oḻuṅkuṟa uṇarttiṉi yorucila pakalē,
	āṅṅaṉam oḻinta aḷavil kālattu
	niṟivuṟu kuṟṟam muṟimuṟi tēyap
	pakuttuṇar kāṭci mikuttut tōṉṟa
20	makkaḷ yākkiyiṟ ṟakkavā niṟuvip
	palapiṟap puṟīiya nāḷkaḷum palavē,
	avvā ṟoḻintaṉa pōkac, cevviya
	innāḷ eṉakku naṉṉeṟi kāṭṭac
	cevvantip pōnta poyyā nāviṟ
25	centamiḻ varampu muntukaṇ ṭuṇarntu
	meypporu ḷaṟivuñ coṟporuḷ vaṉmiyum
	poṟiyuñ ceṟivum niṟiyak koṇṭu
	tamiḻmā ṇākka ramiḻteṉa nukara
	viritturi kiḷakkun teripparum perumi
30	nārā yaṇaṉeṉun tōlāp pulavōṉ
	nērpaṭu muḷattuc cīrpaṭa vamarntu
	karuvinū luṇarttiṉi palavē, yataṟpiṉ
	oruvā yāṇar maruvuṟu ceṉṉit
	tavampeṟat tōṉṟi naviyaṟu tamiḻum
35	vaṭanūṟ parappum nilikaṇ ṭuṇarntāṅ
	kuḷḷuṟi karuvāyt teḷḷoḷi virikkuñ
	civa cittānta muṭiporu ḷeṭuttīṅ
	kaṟaṅkari nāviṟ ṟiṟampaṭa viḷakkip
	puṟampaṭu camayap poypporuḷ nūṟi
40	nalaṅkiḷar taṉmiyum pulaṅkoḷāk kāṭciyum
	iyintoruṅ kīṇṭiya amiyāp perumic
	cōma cuntarat tōmaṟu kuruvaṉ
	uṭalporu ḷāvi yiṭameṉak koṇṭu
	poruḷār civan teruḷuṟak koḷīi
45	yataṟkami poruḷum nīyeṉak kāṭṭit
	tiṟappaṭa vuṇarttiṉi mikavē, yataṟpiṉ
	toḻumpupūṇ ṭoḻukum aḻuṅkā vuḷḷattut
	toṇṭarum narakarum aṇṭarum piṟarum
	vaḻivaḻi yaṭimi perukuṟa veḻutiya
50	ōli koṇṭa vorumara pāṉum
	ulakiṟ ciṟantutā ṉoṉṟē yākiyum
	alaki lāṟṟal talivaru meṉpatu
	valloṟ ṟākkiya kuṟippi ṉāṉum
	oṟṟi yiyakkum uyirē pōla
55	neṟṟiyaṅ kaṇṇaṉ nilavuta lāṉum
	iṟivaṉ aintoḻil niṟiyuyir māṭṭup
	poruntac ceyyum aruntiṟa lāṉum
	aintoḻiṟ paṭṭa āruyirt tokikaṭ
	kantaḻip poruttum minti ṉāṉum
60	oṟṟiyū reṉumpeyar muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	perunaṉ mānakar tiruviṟ polintu,
	kuvaḷi pūtta pavaḷat tāmari
	ceviramum paṇilamum puṭipaṭa iṭiyiṟ
	ṟōṉṟi yaṉṉa kāṇṭaku tirumukaṅ
65	karuneṟik kūntalum perukiya kaḻuttum
	mēluṅ kīḻun tōṉṟa vāliya
	naṟumut taṉṉa muṟuval ceṟitoṟum
	miṉṉeṉa miḷirum poṉmā mēṉi
	makaḷiroṭu viḷaṅkun takicāl tōṟṟattu
70	vaṭivē lēntik kaṭimayi lūrnteṉ
	uḷḷat tāmari viḷḷaṟvīṟ ṟiruntaṉi,
	pakaloḷi viḷakkum palkatir ñāyiṟu
	malinivan taṉṉa niliyuyar māṭattuṅ
	kuṭikaḷ taṅkāp paḻiyoṭum iṭintu
75	veruppēy cēra erukku muḷittup
	pāḻpaṭu pollā villiṉum
	pōḻkatir virittup polivatā leṉavē.	(13)
14. talimakaḷ pirivāṟṟātu pulampal
poliyum matiyum pulipōlat teṉṟal
naliyum namaṉāṉāṉ nalvēḷ - olitamiḻ
ōvā ticikkun tiruvoṟṟi yoḷvēlāṉ
mēvāṉ ceyalaṟiyēṉ mēl	(14)
15. arumiceytu ayarppat talivi kūṟal
mēlāṉa muttē tiruvoṟṟi mēviya vēlavaṉē
kōlā kalaṅceytu kūṭāma lēkal kuṟivukaṇṭāy
nūlā vuṭikaṭṭi yēlāta kuṉṟiṉum nōṉmimikuṅ
kōlā vuyarkuṇak kuṉṟiṉuñ ceṉṟa kuṟippaṟintē.	(15)
16. aruṇiliyurittal
	aṟivum avāvum oruvaḻic ceṉṟāṅ
	korutiṟa ṉiṉṟip palatiṟap paṭumē;
	kuykamaḻ kaṟiyum neykamaḻ tuviyum
	poṉṉiṟap puḻukkaluṅ kaṉṉalpey pālun
5	taṉṉikar kuḻampum paṇṇiya vakiyum
	mikkeḻu cuviyiṉ mukkaṉi vakiyuṅ
	kōḻari vāḻik kuruttakam virittup
	pālkeḻu taṉmiyiṟ palavē ṟamittu,
	muravi pōkiya muḻuveḷ ḷarici
10	kivaṉ maṭiyaṉ aṭutaliṉ neykaṉintu
	viraleṉa nimirnta aviyaṉ meṉpatam
	mulli mukiyeṉa mellitiṟ kuviip,
	puḻukkaluma piṟavum aḷāay viḻuttaka
	neyyuṭaṉ peytu meymmaṟan tuṇṭāṅ
15	kāṉā tuṟivōr aḷavila raṉṟē;
	mukiṟoṭu kuṭumiyum muḻikeḻu vāyum
	aruvi yeṉṉum aṟuviyum uṭīi,
	yantik kālattu ñāyiṟoṭu porutu
	cevvā ṉeṉṉum meykāl kurutiyuṅ
20	kaṇitoṭu puṇṇiṉ mukuḷit taṉṉa
	pavaḷamuñ citarnta tivaḷoḷi keḻūum
	arakkaṉ aṉṉa poruppiṉ kāṭciyum,
	maṭamāṉ piṇikaḷ kaṉṟoṭu kuḻīik
	kāṉyāṟ ṟoṇpuṉal niriniri yaruntak
25	kāṉaṅ kōḻi tuṇikūp peyarntu
	ñāyiṟu tōṉṟuṅ kāli yaṟivippa
	niṟaṅkeḻu tārāk kuṟuṅkayam maruṅkiṟ
	kuṟukuṟu naṭantu nīnta veṟipaṭu
	taḷavam arumpi eyiṟeṉa viḷaṅkak
30	koṉṟi poṉvī yukuppa maṉṟal
	kāvataṅ kamaḻuṅ kāṭukeḻu kāṭciyuṅ,
	koḻuvaḷi yīṉṟa ceḻunīr muttañ
	centā mariyiṉ miḷirvaṉa maṅkiyar
	tirumukaṅ kuṟuviyar poṭittaṉ māṉak
35	kuṭampuri ceruttaṟ ṟaṭaṅkaṇ mēti
	illuṟi kuḻakkaṉ ṟuḷḷutō ṟoḻukun
	tīmpāṉ māntip paruvarāl ukaḷa
	malipūm poyki marutak kāṭciyun;
	tēṟṟāp pulavar nūṟṟiṟaṅ kaṭuppa
40	eḻuvāy ōṅki muṟimuṟi tēyntu
	karicārn taviyun tiriyē, karitoṭuttu
	maṟukari kāṇā takaṉṟunaṉi yāḻntu
	nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷ pōlap
	palporuḷ ceṟitta kaṭalē, pollā
45	oḻukkan talivanta paḻippuṭi yāḷarum
	viḻukkuṇa mēvuva reṉpatu nalattaka
	muḷḷuṭik kuḻiyiṭik koḷḷimaṇam aviḻttu
	vālitiṉ virinta kitiyaṅ kaḻiyē,
	miṉcitarn taṉṉa poṉcitar parappi
50	erikiḷarn taṉṉa viripū ñāḻaṟ
	poḻilē, yeṉumivi yeḻilpeṟat tōṉṟum
	neytaṟ kāṭciyuṅ kaṇṭu meyyeṉa
	aṟivu māḻāntu vāḻvōrum palarē;
	kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṟ pāṇṭiṉ mēlāl
55	aivaki yamaḷiyuṅ kūṟupaṭa vamittuk
	kālkaḷin tāynta vāṉmuki mulli
	kaṭimaṇaṅ kamaḻat tūuy maṭipaṭu
	nurimukan taṉṉa tūveḷ ḷaṟuvi
	ceṟivuṟa virittu naṟuṅkuṟa ṭuritta
60	kēḻkiḷar tēyviyiṟ kōḻakil neyyum
	puḻukum māṉmatamuñ ceḻuṅkarup puramuṅ
	koḻumpaṉi nīrum vaḻuvaṟak kūṭṭi
	vēriyiṉ amitta civiṟiyiṟ ṟōyttu
	niriniri maṅkiyar muṟimuṟi yiraṭṭa
65	uṟaṅkum vāḻkkiyiṟ piṟaṅkuvōr palarē;
	tannalam pakarum poṉvili mātar
	tivaviyāḻ eḻīi navināṉ kakaṟṟi
	miṭaṟṟeḻu mōciyum narampimi roliyum
	oruneṟic ceṉṟāṅ korunili nikaḻap
70	puḷḷiṉan tuṇimaṟan totuṅka vaḷḷiya
	kuḻimukan tōṉṟi vaṟumaram uyirppa
	viḻumitiṉ eḻūum miṭaṟṟiṉ pāṭaluṅ
	koṉṟiyaṅ kuḻaluṅ kuṉṟamar vēyun
	turuvi yūtum poruvi lōciyuñ
75	cevivāy māntic ciṟantōrum palarē;
	pittikik koḻunaṉi muttār mulli
	naṟuvirit tāmari tuvarkkāl vakuḷañ
	caṇpakaṅ kuvaḷi poṉmaṭaṟ ṟāḻi
	koḻuntum maruvuñ ceḻuntaṇ vēriyun
80	toṭali yākkiyum viṭupūc citaṟiyuñ
	ceṇṭum pantuṅ koṇṭu viḷiyāṭiyum
	naṟumporuḷ virāay meyyiṟ ṟimirntum
	vaṟitiṉ vāḻum makkaḷum palarē;
	aimpoṟi vaḻicceṉ ṟimpariṉ nukarum
85	aivakic cuviyum orukāṟ kāṭṭum
	miviḻi maṭavār poypaṭu miṉpattu
	meypaṭat tuḷaṅkum viḻalarum palarē,
	tāmun tuvvātu piṟarkkum īyātu
	veruvaru pēyeṉa uravōr ikaḻa
90	āṉā tarumporuḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi
	māṇā vāḻkki makkaḷuṅ kaḷvaruṅ
	kavarntu cella nimirntuvāṉ nōkkic
	ceyva taṟiyāk kiyarum palarē;
	ivvā ṟoḻintaṉar pōkac cevviya
95	meypporuḷ uṇartumeṉ ṟoppuṭaṉ pukuntu
	kaṭavuṭ ṭaṉmiyum uyiriṉ ṟaṉmiyum
	puṭipaṭa voṟṟi varampaḷan tuṇarātu
	mayaṅkak koṇṭu makkaḷit talivareṉ
	ṟokkak kūṟi yuḻitaru vōrum,
100	aṟivāy aruḷāyc ceṟiyum oruporuḷ
	ciṟumiyum maṟumiyum eyti veṟuvitiṉ
	uyirum ulakamum ākum eṉpōruṅ,
	kaṭpulaṉ ākuva karuttiṉ ṟōṟṟamāy
	uṭpukun tuṇarvōrkkup poypporu ḷākaliṟ
105	kōyi leṉṉē! mēvi yāṅkuṟiyun
	tūya pēroḷip piḻampeṉa leṉṉē!
	vañca māntarpiṟar neñcam piṇikkak
	kalliyum maṇṇiyum vallitiṟ poruttic
	cūḻcciyiṉ amitta kīḻttiṟa maṉṟō
110	eṉṟuri kūṟuvōrum ākap
	poṉṟiya mākkaṇ maṇaliṉum palarē;
	āṅṅaṉam oḻinta aḷaviṉ māntaruḷ
	yāṉum oruva ṉāka nōṉātu
	vēṟu piritteṭuttuk kūṟupaṭu matiyiṉ
115	maṟipukaḻ civam niṟivuṟa virittut
	teḷivār aḷaviyiṉ viḷakki malivutarum
	māṟuporu tōṭṭi vīṟuṟat tikaḻuñ
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉoṭu kūṭṭi,
	iṉpam eṉpa timpoṟi yāṉun
120	tuṉpamoṭu muraṇit tuyppatō vaṉṟē,
	mukiḻnaki mātar kuḻuvoṭu keḻīi
	itaḻcuvit tirukkun tiṟattatō vaṉṟē,
	oṉṟiṉum paṟṟātu taṉṟiṟat teḻūuṅ
	kuṉṟalil poruḷō vaṉṟē, toṇṭareṉum
125	vaṇṭiṉaṅ kuḻumik koṇṭi koḷḷumniṉ
	tiruvaṭit tāmari yatuveṉa moḻivit
	teṉṉiyum uyyak koṇṭu, piṉṉum
	maṟinavil antaṇar vikaṟi yuṇarntu
	vāvi kuṭintārkkun tīviya vōtiyun,
130	tāmarip paḷḷit tuyiluṇar puḷḷiṉam
	pārppoṭu keḻīic cilampum ōciyuñ,
	ciṟunutaṟ karuṅkaṭ kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
	kuṅkumac cāntuṅ koṅkulāṅ kūntalum
	pālkeḻu taṉmiyiṟ palavē ṟaḻīi
135	nīṉiṟa mukiliṉ maṟiyum miṉpōl
	nīrmūḻ kāṭum pērvilārp poliyun,
	tiruviṉ celvi yarumiyiṟ peṟṟa
	kuṟunaṭic ciṟuvar ciṟutē ruruṭṭit
	tiṟalkeḻu maṟavar maṟpayil kaḻakattuk 
140	kipuṭit tārkkum iciyiṉuñ ciṟantu
	viṭṭuviṭ ṭicikkum maṭṭil kampaliyum
	ōvātu kaṟaṅkum oṟṟi mānakar
	tāvā iḷami talittali ciṟappap
	paṉṉiru viḻiyeṉuṅ kaṇṇakaṉ cuṉiyil
145	aruḷeṉum aruvi virivuṟap peruki
	muḻavut tōḷeṉuṅ koḻuviya kuṉṟiṟ
	paṉmukam parantu taṇṇeṉac cella,
	nittilam peyta viḻuttuvarc ceppiṉ
	mūytiṟan taṉṉa vāynaki tōṉṟa,
150	varivilaṅki yōṭum miṉṉē pōla
	aramporu vivēl uraṅkiṭan toḷirat,
	tirumāṉ mukattiṉ iruviḻi yeṉṉa
	maññiyiṟ kiṭanta virutāḷ malara,
	aññiyar iruvarum irumaruṅ kiruppa,
155	amitta kiyum aruṭaru kiyum
	imippa tillāt tēvarum piṟaruṅ
	kaṇṭukaṇ ṭuvappak kaviṉ ṟunaṉi viḷaṅkuṅ
	kāṇṭaku kōlan taḻīi māṇṭaka, eṉ
	kaṇṇetir polintaniṉ iṉṉaruḷ niṉippiṉ
160	vayāvuṟu nōyum pārā tīṉṟa
	putalvōṟ kaṇṭu kaḷikkuñ
	citivil tāyiṉ aruḷiṉum peritē.	(16)
17. neñcaṟivuṟuttal
tēvā tiruvoṟṟic cēvār putalveṉa
nāvāra vāḻtti naṭampurivāy! - āvā
viyantu piḻiceyyum naṉṉeñcē vēlōṉ
nayantuṉ etirtōṉṟu nāḷ.	(17)
18. taṉṉuṭ kiyā ṟeytiṭu kiḷavi
nāḷāṉ malarntu maṉakkiṉi yāṉceṉṟa nāḷaṟintu
vāḷār maṭaliṉ aḷiyēm uyirpōḻ vakiyumaṉṟik
kōḷāṉa collip puṇarntār kariyuṅ kuṟippaṟiyāy
kēḷāṉa meyyum muṭaṅkumoṟ ṟikkaḻik kētakiyē 
19. aruṇili peṟuta lariteṉa moḻital
	kētaki paḻitta mātoru kūṟaṟku
	nutalkiḻit timikkuṅ kataṅkeḻu viḻiyiṟ
	poṟiyeṉat tōṉṟi veṟikamaḻ cuṉiyil
	aṟuvar ūṭṭiya naṟumpāṉ mānti
5	aṟuvē ṟuruviṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭayarntāṅ
	kammiyum appaṉum aṇiyac cemmiyiṉ
	ulaku purantaruḷum umitiru maṭiyil
	aṟuvēr uruvum oruvaṭi vāki
	viḷiyāṭ ṭamarnta iḷiyōy! uḻippirintu
10	naṟṟavar ōṅkum oṟṟi mānakar
	veṟṟi vivēl orutiṟam poliyap
	paḷikkaṟip pukunta oḷipparu matiyiṉ
	muṟimuṟi miḷirnatu niṟikaviṉ poḻiyūn
	tirumukac celviyar irupuṟam viḷaṅka
15	ulakelām viḻuṅkum alakilā vaṉṟiṟaṟ
	kaḷikkaru maññiyiṉ nalappaṭa amarnteṉ
	viḻiyetir tōṉṟiya aḻiyāp periyōy!
	tīyiṭi niṉṟum, nīriṭi mūḻkiyum,
	maramē livarntu talikīḻt toṅkiyum,
20	aṟuvaki yirukkiyil neṟipaṭa iruntum
	puṟampaṭut tiḻuttāṅ kakampaṭa niṟittut
	teḷinili mukiḻkkum vaḷinili yuḻaṉṟun, 
	taṉṟoḻil taputtut taṉimutaṟ poruḷiṉ
	aruḷeṉum veḷḷam varampiṟa mikuntu
25	niliyum iṭaṉeṉa ulakelām nōkkiyum,
	aimperum pūtamum aimpuṟam niṟīi
	aivakit tēvum āṅkāṅ kamittup
	poymmuṟi yiṉṟi meymmuṟi paraviyuṅ,
	karuviyum pulaṉum puriviṉṟi nikaḻa
30	orumutaṟ poruḷoṭum aṟiviṉik kaṭaviyum,
	iruvaki niliyum orumuṟi yiṟantu
	mēṉili niṉṟu vāṉporu ḷuṇarntum
	ūḻi yūḻiyum ōviṉṟi nōṟpap,
	pōḻmatic caṭiyōṉ pōtā ṉākak,
35	koḻuṅkuṟi taṉmeyiṟ palamuṟi taṭintum
	uṟuppiṉi yīrntu neṟippaṭa vīciyum,
	pacciḷaṅ kurutiyi nacci yukuttum,
	uyirppali vēḷviyiṟ kaṭaṉpala kaḻippi
	yaritiṉ muyaṉṟa piṉṟiyum puricaṭip
40	periyōṉ pōntila ṉāka, erikiḷar
	vēḷviyāṟṟum āḻkuḻiyiṉ
	vinnuṉiya eḻuniṟīi
	muḻuvuṭampum paḻutupaṭap
	pōḻntu paṭuttup poṉṟat, tampiyar
45	pāyvataṟ kūkkiya kāli, vēypuri
	paṇittōṭ pāviyun tāṉumāṅ keḻuntu
	vāṉavar talivaṉ cīritiṉ aruḷiya
	varampeṟu perumiyiṉ uramperi teytiniṉ
	cīṟaṭit tāmari vēṟiṉṟi vikum
50	aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ vaḻivaḻic ciṟanta
	cūrmutal pōlap pēraṉ pillēṉ;
	ālavāy amarnta aḻaṉiṟak kaṭavuḷ
	centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuniṉ ṟicippak
	kaṭāviṭi nikaḻttun taṭāpperum pēṟum,
55	iṟivaṉ kaṇṭa poruḷvaram paṟintu
	coṉṉeṟi māṭciyum poruṇeṟi māṭciyum
	aḷaviyiṉ viḷivun teḷivuṟa virittuc
	cuvipeṟa vuritta naviyil pulamiyum,
	maṟipporuṭ kuṟippu neṟippaṭa āyntu
60	civaṉiyē mutaleṉac civaṇiya kāṭciyuñ,
	cīritiṉ iyinta kīraṉ pōlac,
	coṟṟoṟuñ curakkum, aṟṟamil iṉpamum
	paṭṭāṅku kiḷakkum muṭṭaṟu ciṟappuṅ
	kaṉṉeñcu nekiḻttum aṉpuṟu moḻiyum
65	oruvaḻic ciṟappa varumuṟi koḷīi
	yāṟṟup paṭicolum āṟṟalu millēṉ;
	āṅkataṉ karuvāy uḷḷuṟi nuṇporuḷ
	viḻumitiṉ eṭuttu vaḻuvaṟa amittut
	toṇṭa rārat taṇṭātu koṭuttu, niṉ
70	ṟiruvaṭik kiṭanta perukiya aṉpiṉun
	tamiḻvaram puṇarnta kamaḻuṟum aṟiviṉum
	urittiṟam niliyiṭum varippaṭā viṟaliṉun
	tāṉē taṉakku nikareṉa viḷaṅkiya
	nacciṉārk kiṉiyaṉum allēṉ; niccalum
75	varukkic cuḷiyum porukkari māvuṅ
	koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻiyuñ ceḻuñcuvik kaṉṉalum
	oruṅkutali mayaṅkiya arumperuṅ kalaviyiṉ
	aruñcol vaḻakkamun tiruntiya naṭiyum
	vaṇṇa vēṟṟumiyun taṇṇeṉum oḻukkamum
80	oṉṟu nirampiya kuṉṟāt tiruppukaḻ
	vēṟuvē ṟiyampiya vīṟuṟu tavattiṉ
	aruṇa kiriyeṉum periyaṉum allēṉ;
	nūliṭi vitta vāliya poruḷiṉum
	uriyiṭi virinta puriyami teḷiviṉuṅ
85	kēṭpōr uṇarap pāṟpaṭa vakuttu
	niṉpukaḻ aṉittun taṉperu matiyiṉ
	uḷaṅkoḷa viḷakkum vaḷaṅkeḻu pulami
	nārāyaṇaṉ eṉuṅ kuruvaṉu mallēṉ;
	kaḷaviyal taṉakkup palapaṭa viyampip
90	paḷakaṟu naṉporuḷ teḷiyā tuḻaṉṟa
	pulavōr kaḷippa nalamuṟat tōṉṟi
	meypporuḷ kāṭṭit tamiḻvaḻip paṭuttum,
	pullamaṇ mikuntu nalluṇar vaḻintu
	perumpeyar vaḻutiyum aruntamiḻk kūṭalum
95	uyvaḻi kāṇā tuḻitaru kāli
	oyyeṉap pōntu meynneṟi kāṭṭiyun,
	taṇaruḷ purinta niṉṉē pōlac,
	centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virittu
	muṭinilic civam viṭiñāyiṟeṉap
100	piṅkatir virittuk kaṅkuliṟ kūmpiya
	viriyaviḻ tāmari puriyura vōruḷam
	puriñekiḻn talarat tarumiyal ciṟappa
	uriyiṉuṅ karuttiṉum variyami nōkkiṉum
	poruntak kāṭṭi varuntiṟaṉ mikuttu
105	malivupaṭu muḷḷat taṟivoṭu kūṭāc
	ciṟupuṉ māntar kuṟumoḻi kaḷintu
	niṉpukaḻ virikkum aṉpiṟ ciṟanta
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉu mallēṉ;
	poruḷperi tīṭṭap perukuṟum avāviṉum
110	īṭṭiya oṇporuḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi
	makkaḷum maṉiviyun tuykka nalki
	niṉkaḻal vaṇaṅkā iṉṉā maṭiyiṉum
	ariya nāṭkaḷi vaṟitē pōkki
	aṟantiṟam puḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉṉē;
115	iṉiya tīyeṉniṉ niṉivarun tiruvaṭi
	viḻinīr uṟippa muḻumeyum paṉippa
	niriniri vārā turinaṉi kuḻaṟap
	poṟivaḻi aṟivuñ ceṟiyum uṭkaruviyum
	palavaḻik kavarā toruvaḻi nikaḻa
120	aṉpuru vākuniṉ toṇṭar pōla
	nīṅkā aṉpiṉ niliniṉ
	ṟiyāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō pāṅkupaṭa moḻimō!	(19)
20. tōḻi kiḷḷiyit tūtuvēṇṭal
moḻiyuṅ kuḻaṟa muḻuvuṭampum pittu
viḻiyun tuyilkūṭā veyyōḷ - paḻiyit
tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟ kuriyāy! kiḷiyē
yuruvuṉṉi yoppa tuṇarntu.	(20)
21. aruṇiliyuṇarttal
uṇarntār aṟiviṉun tōṉṟā toḷikkum orumurukaṉ
iṇarntār curikuḻaṉ maṅkiya rōṭeṉ iruviḻiyiṟ
puṇarntā reyiloṟṟi mēviya vāṟu puṉiṟucellā
vaṇantāḻ kuḻaviyit tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmiyaṉṟē
22. eḻiṉalaṅ kūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttal
	vaṇpuṉal niliyil taṇṇaṟal pōlavun
	tāmari moytta vaṇṭiṉam pōlavum
	paḷiṅkiṟ ṟōynta nīṉiṟam pōlavun
	tirumuka maruṅkiṉ varumuṟi tuṇarntāṅ
5	kiṭiyiṭi curintu nīṇṭu kaṭikuḻaṉṟu
	kaṇṇoḷi katuvuṅ kuñciyi yaṉṉi
	irupāṟ kūṟiṭ ṭorupāṟ cūḻiyum, maṟ
	ṟorupāṟ paṉicciyun tiruvatiṉ amippa
	naṟuṅkatup poḷirum peruñciṟap pāṉuñ,
10	cemmaṇip palakiyiṉ ammaṇi kuyiṟṟi
	varampuṟa viṭutta vaṇṇam pōlap
	puruvakkoṭi paṭarnta virinuta lāṉum,
	annutal kiḷarnta poṉpōṟ cevviḻi
	utti eṉṉun tuttip piyara
15	muṭimēṉ maṇiyeṉak kuṭikoḷa lāṉum,
	veṇpaṉit tiraḷik kaṇṇuṟap pōḻntu
	tuvarniṟam ūṭṭic civaṇuṟa amittāṅ
	kirupuṟaṅ katuppu maruvuta lāṉuṅ,
	kuḻiyoṭu keḻumun taḻicevi yiraṇṭum
20	ōveṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvuta lāṉum
	naṭuviṟ kuḻintu vaṭṭit taminta
	paṭupoṟ kiṇṇam mukaṅkaviḻt taṉṉa
	mōvāy nivanta muṟimi yāṉuṅ,
	kiḷiyari nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaṟkīṉṟa
25	muḷiyum muruntum viḷinīr muttamuṅ
	kūrntu nirintu valleṉa oḷivirin
	teḻuntoḷi reyiṟṟoṭu poruntuta lāṉuṅ,
	kovvik kaṉiyuṅ koḻuviya pavaḻamuñ
	cevviya alleṉa ilavitaḻ puriyuñ
30	cennīr itaḻoṭu maṉṉuta lāṉum,
	muttak kōvi muṟimuṟi vitta
	nattuvarc ceppu naṉkaṉan tiṟanteṉa
	arakkuru vūṭṭiya āmpal naṟumuki
	taḷinekiḻn talarnta taṉmit teṉṉa
35	muḷḷeyi ṟilaṅka nakutoṟum nakutoṟuñ
	cevvāy tōṉṟum avvāṟ ṟāṉum,
	eḻumiṭaṅ kuḻintu viḻumitiṉ oḻukit
	taḷavarum peṉṉa vaḷaṅkaṉin tilaṅki
	muṉṉeḻu mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmi yāṉum,
40	ōraruṭ kaṭaliṅ kīruru vākik
	karipuṇarntu kiṭanta muṟimi pōlap
	puruvakkoṭi kaṭavāp peruviḻi yāṉum,
	viriyaviḻ tāmari naṟiyukut teṉṉak
	kēṭṭoṟum iṉikkum pāṭṭoṭu taḻīic
45	ceḻuvāy malarnta moḻiyi ṉāṉum,
	valampuri yaṉṉa vāṉaṟuṅ kaḻuttoṭum
	ēṟṟiṉat teruttiṉ murippeṭut taṉṉa
	kuvavip puṭitta piṭari ṉāṉum,
	muḻaveṉac carintuval ulameṉat tiraṇṭu
50	variyeṉa nimirntupiṉ eḻuveṉat tiṇintu
	maliyoṭu poruta mālkaḷi ṟeṉṟu
	pulavōr kūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭa
	yāṉi yeṉṉum pāṉaku tirumoḻi
	koṅki maruppiṉ nuṅkak kutti
55	orupāṟ perumpōr viḷippa, orupāl
	vaḷḷi yeṉṉuṅ kaḷḷaviḻ kuḻalāḷ
	taṭamuli vētiṟ paṭupuṇ oṟṟa
	uḷaṅkoṇ ṭeḻīi yuṟumiṭaṉ paṭāa
	torupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟi nōkkik
60	koḻunaṉi piṇitta marānaṟun toṭali
	muṟuvalit teṉṉa muṟukkuṭin taciya
	vāḷvali niliyun tōḷi ṉāṉuṅ
	kival vēkaṭi aituṟak kaṭintu
	vaḻuvaḻup pākkiya koḻuntukirp palaki
65	puṇarntoruṅ kiraṇṭu kiṭantatu pōlak
	kaṇṇakaṉ ṟōṅkiya kaviṉkeḻu mārpat
	tiyāṉimarup puḻuta cuvaṭu pōlavum
	variyoṭu paṭarnta valli pōlavum
	varimūṉ ṟoḻukiya tiruvi ṉāṉum,
70	maḻiyum maraṉuṅ kuḻintoruṅ kōṭa
	viḻumiya poruḷpala vēṇṭinark kutavun
	tāṭoṭu taṭakkiyiṟ ṟāyuvan tītta
	vālitiṉ viḷaṅkum vēli ṉāṉum,
	pāṉuri mukanta paricoṭu porūun
75	tūveḷ ḷaṟuvi yacii mūvaki
	maṭiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅki vaṭivuṭaṉ carintu
	naṭattoṟuṅ kuluṅkum vayiṟṟi ṉāṉum,
	eḻumiṭan tacintupiṉ ṉoḻukutoṟum melintu
	maḻikkaṭak kaḷiṟṟiṉ puḻikki yottuṅ
80	koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻiyiṉ kōḻari yēyppa
	ūṟum oḷiyum vīṟuṟat tikaḻum
	niṟaṅkeḻu marapiṟ kuṟaṅki ṉāṉum,
	eppāṟ poruḷun tuppuṟa vikum
	nilikkaḷa meṉpatu peyariṟ kāṭṭi
85	muḷiyiḷa ñāyiṟṟiṟ ṟaḷiñekiḻntu virinta
	eriyaki tāmari pōla viripukaḻt
	toṇṭarka ḷetiriṟ kaṇṭiṭa malarntu
	nikaril iṉpa naṟimuṟi yoḻukkip
	perumiyoṭu vikun tiruvaṭi yāṉum,
90	perumaṇal ekkarc ciṟukuṭi vāḻkkik
	kuṟumoḻi mākkaḷ uṟumīṉ paṭuttum
	āḻtiri mūḻki vīḻmaṇi yeṭuttuṅ
	karuṅkaṭa lūrnta veḷvaḷi vāriyum
	niriniri kariyiṟ kuviyiṉar cella
95	maṟikaya lukaḷun tirumuka nuḷicciyar,
	koṭicci tanta kaṭuppuṭi naṟavuṅ
	kuṭatti koṇarnta palappaṭu mutiriyum
	uḻatti yeṭutta viḻuttaku paḻaṉum
	peṟuvili yākac ciṟumī ṉoṭuttum,
100	iṭiyiṭi muttam puṭipuṭi yaḷantuṅ,
	kōṭu koṇṭu pīṭuṟa māṟiyum,
	orucār pēroli nikaḻtta, orucār
	paṉimali nivanta paṇpu pōlac
	cēṇṭoṭa vōṅkiya vuppuk kuṉṟan
105	taliyaḻit tumaṇar potiyuṟac cērttiya
	oḻuki yuruḷi muḻumaṇaṟ putitaliṟ
	piṭarkoṭut teḻuppik kaṭumpaka ṭurappun
	tāḷāṇ mākkaḷ talippaṭum uñaṟṟum,
	pulavukkaḻi muḷitta tuvaritaḻt tāmari
110	puṉṉi nuṇṭātu poṟippat tuṉṉiya
	uruvoṭu viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam eriyiṭi
	yurukiya pacumpoṉ kaviḻppa ōṭiya
	tiruvoṭum poliyuñ cevviyum maruviya
	neytalaṅ kāṉal aituṟak kiṭanta
115	oṟṟimā nakariṟ kaṟṟit tōki
	mayiṉmē liruntu payiluta lāṉuṅ,
	kāṇṭoṟum iṉikkuṅ kāṭciyu muṭiyaṉ
	urippat tīrāc ciṟappu muṭiyaṉ
	tammuyirk kiṉiya cemmoḻip putalvari
120	nōkka nōkka mīkkiḷar kātal
	tantiyark keḻutal pōlak kaṇṭiṭum
	viḻiyaḷa vamiyāk kaḻiperuṅ kātal
	eytiṉark kuṟukkuñ ceytiyu muṭiyaṉ,
	urukeḻu tōṟṟam maruvuva ṉeṉṟu
125	veruvara loḻitiyā liṉṟē, murukaṉ
	kuḻavik kōlattum viḻumiti ṉamarvaṉ
	maṭavaraṉ makaḷir taṭamuli kuḻikkum
	iḷamik kōlattum vaḷamiyoṭu polivaṉ, ataṉāṉ
	miṉṉeṉat tōṉṟik koṉṉuṟa maṟintum
130	vātuvaṉ naṭavāk kalimā pōlat
	tītuṟu neṟiyiṟ ṟiṟampaṭac ceṉṟum
	pākaṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉ paṇpu pōla
	uṟuti koḷḷāc ciṟumiyiṉ mikuntum
	oruvaḻi niliyā tōṭip
135	palavaḻik kavarkkumeṉ ṉilimiyiṉ maṉaṉē.	(22)
23. aruṭara vēṇṭal
maṉṉu moruyāṉi yūṭa maṟuyāṉi
taṉṉutavi vaḷḷi taṭamulikaḷ - tuṉṉuvippa
oṟṟi nakarvikum vēlāy! oruperitō
paṟṟi yeṉiyāḷ paricu.	(23)
24. tōḻi talivi kuṟippaṟital
	paricu piṟitoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parumuttap pimpaṇicūḻ
	viripuka ḻoṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum viṭikkaḷiṟoṉ
	ṟaritiṟ peyarntiṅku vantatuṇ ṭōveṉpa raṅkucelvar
	teriviṟ periyar ceyalō ciṟiyareñ cēyiḻiyē.	(24)
25. kaḻaṟṟetirmaṟi
	iḻinekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ
	maḻittaṭan tōḷka ḷiḷittut tōṉṟap,
	pāṟkaṭal pōlat tūttakap paranta
	nūṟpaṭu kēḷviyuñ cīrppaṭā toḻiyac,
5	ciṟuneṟic cellum aṟiyā māntarum
	nalvaḻic cellat teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum
	iḻukkā naṭiyum vaḻukkip pōka,
	innili nīyun tiriyiṟ piṉṉoru
	puvanīr vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunī ruṇṭō?
10	kuṉṟunili tavaṟip pantupō luruḷiṟ
	ceṉṟuvaḻi yaṭikkuṅ kuṉṟiyu muṇṭō?
	kaṭuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal toṭumaṭut tuṇṇiṉ
	muruṅkā tōmpum peruṅkala muṇṭō?
	eṉṟupala kūṟi niṉṟeṉi neruṅki
15	naṉmi nāṭiya eṉṉuyir naṇpā!
	maṟiyiḷaṅ kaṉṟu muṟiyuṇun tāyoṭu
	kuḻipputal tōṟuṅ kuḻīi yukaḷa,
	variyāṭu varuṭi yiṭivāy tāṇṭi
	yuḷḷañ cerukkit tuḷḷit tiritara,
20	nilantoṭṭu vīḻnta kuluṅkumayirk kavari
	meṉmela acii mētakac cella,
	muḷavumā tolicciya vaṉkaṭ kāṉavar
	ñelikōṟ pottiya neruppiṟ kāycca,
	ōri paranta tēṉiṟāl eṭumār
25	kaḻikkaṇ kuṟittu nalattaka iyaṟṟiya
	mālpuvit tiṭantoṟuṅ kuṟava rēṟat,
	tiṉikkuṟu makaḷir cuvippaṭa miḻaṟṟuṅ
	kiḷḷi yuṟaṅkum vaḷḷi yolippa,
	maruppiṭic cuṟṟiya poruppuyar vēḻattup
30	puḻikki yēyppa viḻuttaka mutirnta
	tāḷoṭu vaḷinta kuraṟṟiṉip puṉattup
	paṭukiḷi yōppum viṭukavaṇ cuḻaṟṟik
	kōlvaḷik koṭicci mēlitaṇ amara,
	iruṅkal viyalaṟi varippat tāaya
35	nalamuṟu vēṅki polamvī yukuppat,
	tēkkili puytta puṉṟalic ciṟāar
	mīkkiḷar kōṭṭiṟ paritiyiṟ ṟoṭutta
	tēttaṭi piṟikkō ṭuḻuteṉak kīṇṭu
	māyōṉ mārpiṟ ceñcēṟu kaṭuppap
40	pāay iḻiyum paṭunaṟavu mukantu
	pacuntiṉip piṇṭiyo ṭacumpuṟa aḷāy
	niriniri vittu virivoṭum ārak,
	kaṇkaḷi koḷḷum paṉmali yaṭukkattu
	maravamuṅ kuravamum porikeḻu puṉkum
45	erikiḷar ceyaliyum viriyiṇar māvum
	parāric cāntum pāvi ñāḻalum
	viritali vāḻiyum viyaṉciṉip palavuṅ
	koṉṟiyum puṉṉi yun tuṉṟiya naṟavamuṅ
	kuḻimukan tōṟṟik koḻunaṉi yarumpip
50	puriyaviḻ malariṟ ciṟuvaṭuk kiḷarntu
	tīvaḷi yākit tīṅkaṉi tūṅkit
	taṇṇiḻal payantu kaṇkavar vaṉappiṉ
	mukilkaṇ ṇuṟaṅkat talimiyoṭu polimē; orupāṟ
	cuḷiniri yaminta koḻumpaḻan tūkkik
55	kuṟumala raviḻnta naṟuviri teḷittu
	vīcukā laciyum pācili narantamum,
	nalaṉuṟu makaḷi rilavitaḻ katuviya
	muṟuva laṉṉa ciṟuviti peyta
	polaṉkuṭa meṭutta ulavi mātuḷiyuṅ,
60	karumaṇi puriyum orukaṉi nāvalum,
	uvarkkaḻi maṇakkun tuvarkkāl vakuḷamum,
	poṟṟiraḷ kaṭuppat teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum
	eḻiṟkaṉi paḻutta elumiccam putaluṅ,
	kāvataṅ kamaḻa mēvuva vaṉṟē; orupāṟ
65	kulikam ūṭṭiya talimiyiṟ ṟikaḻntu
	navvi nōkkiyar cevvāy pōlak
	koḻuṅkaṉi yuṭiya kovvik koṭiyuṅ
	kaṟpuṭi makaḷir muṟpaṭac cūṭum
	variciyiṉ mikunta paricuṭi mulliyun,
70	tiṉpatu kallāp puṉṟali manti
	kaṟittut tuḷḷuṅ kaṟivaḷar koṭiyum,
	aṇiṟpuṟaṅ kaṭukkum variyuṭik koṭuṅkāy
	koḷḷiyiṟ kāytta veḷḷarik koṭiyuñ,
	colluyar marapiṉ mellilik koṭiyuñ,
75	ciṟupaḷik kuruṇṭi ceṟivuṟut taṉiya
	tīṅkaṉik kulikaḷ tūṅka vayiṉvayiṉ
	nantātu vaḷarum muntirik koṭiyum
	iṭiyiṭi tāay miṭiyumāl; orupāl
	naṭuviṟ, pāyoḷi matiyam pariti vemmiyiṉ
80	iḷakip puṉalā yiḻintatu kaṭuppat
	teḷinīr vāviyoṉ ṟuḷatāl; maṟṟatu
	ciṟukāl tōṟum uṟunī roḻukki
	vālukam paranta kōliya pāttiyiṟ
	pacciḷam pulli niccalum vaḷarkkum, atā aṉ
85	ṟaṉṉac cēva laṇimayirp peṭiyoṭum
	poṉṉan tāmarip polintuvīṟ ṟiruppavum,
	nittilam payanta nattu vāṉmaṭuviṉ,
	mīṉiṉam vaḷiiya nāṇmati pōlap
	pālkeḻu marapiṟ parantu cellavuṅ,
90	koḻuṅkayaṉ mātar viḻiyeṉa miḷiravuṅ,
	karuverin uṭiya paruvarā lukaḷavum,
	muṟṟā mañcaṭ ciṟupuṟaṅ kaṭukkum
	iṟavu karimaruṅkiṟ curintu tuḷḷavuñ,
	ciṟuvaḷi eṭuppac ciṟutiri yeḻīi
95	muṟimuṟi yurāayk kariyiṉic cāravuñ,
	ceḻunīrk kuvaḷiyuṅ kaḻunīrp pōtum
	muḻuneṟi āmpaluṅ kaḻiyavuṅ kuḻumik
	kāṇpōrp piṇikkum māṇpiṉa taṉṟē; āṅkup
	potiyac cāntan tatiya acii
100	arumpaṟa virinta naṟumpū aḷii
	viriyun tātum niṟiya mukantu
	talittali vīci nalattaka ulāan
	teṉṟalum eṉṟum ōvātu, ata aṉṟu
	kuṉṟik kaṇṇa kuyilkaḷ eṅkun
105	tuṉṟiya makiḻviṉ naṉṟukū vummē,
	āṭuvāṟ ciralkaḷ nāṭoṟuṅ kūṭi
	yōṭumī ṉarunti vīṭuṟā vaṉṟē,
	tukirkōt taṉṉa ukiruṭic ciṟukāṟ
	puṟamarap potumpiṉ iṟikū rummē,
110	tūṅkaṇaṅ kurīip pāṅkupaṭat teṟṟiya
	aruntoḻiṟ kuṭampiyiṟ poruntu maṉṟē,
	piñciṟik kiḷḷi orukāl tūkkip
	puṉciṟu kiḷiyiṟ kaṇṭuyil koḷumē,
	aṟuvimaṭit taṉṉa paṟikeḻu nāri
115	ciṟukāl tōṟum varumīṉ nōkkit
	tokuti yāka mikutiyoṭu vatimē,
	vaṇṭun tēṉum varik kaṭip piracamum
	immeṉa muraṉṟunaṉmalar tōṟum,
	aḷinaṟa māntik kaḷikū rummē,
120	pūvāk kaṇṇa tōki virittu
	muḷiyiḷa ñāyiṟ ṟiḷaveyi leṟippap
	pīṭuṟu maññi yāṭuṟu maṉṟē,
	iṉṉuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭiṟ piṉṉumeṉ
	collaḷa vamiyā mallalam potumparp
125	palperuñ ciṟappuṅ kaṇṭili maṉṉō! aṉṟiyāṅ
	kaṉiccamēṟ paṭiṉun taṉittuyar uḻakkun
	taḻimalarc cīṟaṭi kuḻipaṭa naṭantu
	poḻiliṭam pukuntu pūntaṭa maruṅkiṉ
	viḻitaka niṉṟa paḻutil pāviyi,
130	eṉṉuḷam eṉṉun taṉṉamar kiḻiyil
	vaḻuviṉ ṟeḻutiya eḻilō viyatti,
	yamiḻtupoti tuvarvāyk kiḷavicila miḻaṟṟum
	māniṟaṅ koṇṭa tūniṟak kiḷiyik,
	kāṭuṟi vāḻkkit taṉṉiṉam pirintu
135	nāṭuṟi vāḻkki naṉkaṉam maruviya
	kuṭamāṇ koṅki maṭamāṉ piṇiyik,
	kiḷicciṟi yeṉṉum paḻippaṟu pacumpoṉ
	karittīṇ ṭeḻutiya tarikkiḷar valliyik,
	kāmak kaṭumpaci nāmuṟak kaḷiyum
140	peṟutaṟ kariya uṟutuṇi maruntik,
	kāṇṭal cellā aruvap poruḷeṉap
	pūṇṭōr pukaḻuri puripaṭṭu oḻiya
	miṉṉeṉa miḷirnteṉ kaṇṇetir polinta
	karumpiṉum iṉikkumeṉ ṉarumpeṟa luyiri,
145	eṉṉuḷam maṉṉiya tuṉṉiruḷ nīṅka
	muṟpaṭak koḷuviya poṟcuṭark koḻuntip,
	pulaṅkoḷak kāṇum poṟiyum iliyāl!
	āvaṇa oliyum aruṅkaṭa loliyum
	oṉṟutali mayaṅki eṉṟum ōvā
150	oṟṟimā nakariṟ ṟaṟṟakap polinta
	paciyatōr maññi miciyamar cevvēḷ,
	tēvarum mayaṅku mūvā māyam
	palamuṟi iyaṟṟiya talimitīr cūriṉ
	pēruram vacinta kūrvaṭi vēlaṉ
155	nōṉā vuḷḷattu nāṉmukaṉ ṟeḷiyat
	taliyiṟ kuṭṭiya nalaṉuṟu kumaraṉ,
	viṇṇavark kariyaṉ, emmaṉōrk keḷiyaṉ,
	piṟimuṭi putalvaṉ, maṟimuṭi mutalvaṉ,
	umitaru ciṟuvaṉ, emiyaḷi yuṟuvaṉ,
160	paṉṉiru kiyaṉ, eṉṉuyirk kiyaṉ,
	tiruvaṭi niṉiyā aṟivilar pōla
	eṉṉetir tōṉṟip paṉmuṟi kaḻaṟiṉi!
	īṅkitu viṭuttumaṟ ṟāṅkuc ceṉṟu
	tuṭiyiṭi oruki ūṉṟi vaṭiviḻi
165	ticimukam parappi nacipaṭap pārkkum
	iruṅkaṇ māṉpiṇi kāṇiṉ
	neruṅki niṉṟiṅ kuriyali yiṉiyē.	(25)
26. pirivāṟṟāta talivi tōḻiyoṭu kūṟal
iṉiyā reṉakkiṅ kiṉiyā riyintār
muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - paṉimoḻiyāy
puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu pukaḻoṟṟit tannakarkku
naḷḷiruṭkaṭ ceṉṟār namar.	(26)
27. neñcaṟi vuṟuttal
namarā yiruntupiṉ ētila rāvar nayamilarun
tamarāva reṉṉiṟ ṟamarpiṟa reṉṟu takavucolli
amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitukaṇ ṭāyali yāḻiyoṟṟik
kumarāveṉ ṟōtik kuṭantaṅkoṇ ṭēttak kuṟikkoṇeñcē
28. pulavarāṟṟuppaṭi
	neñcunek kuṭintu pañcukaṇ aṭiyā
	uṭumpurit taṉṉa kaṭumpaci maruṅkuṟ
	paḻuppuṭi tōṉṟic ceḻippiṉṟi vika
	vaṟumi yuḻanta uṟumaṉik kiḻattiyoṭu
5	nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu
	nōṉāp paciyiṉ āṉā taḻūum
	puṉṟalik kuḻavi taṉṟiṟam nōkkik
	kaṉavukāṇ poḻutum naṉaveṉat tōṉṟum
	aruntuya reṉṟum peruntuya ruṟuppa
10	miṭikeḻu vāḻkkiyiṟ kuṭiyā yiruntu
	maḻuṅkiya vuḷḷat toṭuṅkiya pulavōy!
	iyaṟṟamiḻ varampu tiṟappaṭa vāyntu
	pulaṅkoḷa nirampiya nalaṅkiḷar aṟivōy!
	vaḻuvoṉ ṟillā viḻumiya oḻukkam
15	uyiriṉum ōmpuñ ceyiraṟu periyōy!
	koḻuntamiḻ pōlak kuḷirmāṇ kuṇattōy!
	uḻunturuḷ aḷaviyiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pākkaḷ
	nūṟunū ṟiyaṟṟuṅ kūṟupaṭu matiyōy!
	oḷiccela vataṉiṉum vaḷiccela vataṉiṉum
20	neṟippaṭak kiḷakkum miṟikkavi vallōy!
	aḷavinūṉ marapiṟ piṟaḻā tiyāṇṭuṅ
	koḷavuri nikaḻttum vaḷamuṟum uṇarvōy!
	poruḷiyal aṟintu maruḷaṟap puṉintu
	kēṭpōrp piṇikkuṅ kāṭcicāl uravōy!
25	paṇṭip piṟaviyiṟ ṟaṇṭā tāṟṟum
	peruntava muṭiyi maṉṉē! poruntiyiṉ
	ṟīṅkeṉit talippaṭ ṭaṉiyē; ōṅki
	ūḻi celliṉum nīṭu vāḻmati!
	kīḻttici eḻīi mēṟṟicip paṭarun
30	tītteṟu ñāyiṟu vaṭaticip puṇarntu
	teṉṟici nōkkit ticitaṭu māṟiṉum,
	vāṉak kaṭaliṉ mīṉeṉa vayaṅkum
	uṭuniri utirntu koṭumi yāyiṉum,
	ulavā vāḻkkiyiṉ nilimati ciṟantē!
35	vaṟumi kaḷintu ciṟumi nīṅki
	naṉṉili palavum iṉṉē peṟuti!
	varampaṟum iṉpam nirampak kōṭi!
	niṉṉuyirk kuṟuti nāṭit tuṉṉiniṉṟu
	niriniri kūṟumeṉ uripiḻi yaliyē!
40	iṉṉuṅ kēṭṭal vēṇṭiṉ muṉṉumeṉ
	collaḷa vamintu nalampala eytiṉar
	pallōr pallōr paṇṭum uṇṭāl;
	iṉiyoṉṟu kūṟuveṉ kēṇmati! paṉikeḻu
	mulliyaṅ koṭikku mallañ ceḻuntēr
45	aruḷvara ītta periyaṉō ilaṉē!
	viḻiyāk kaṇṇa ceḻumayirt tōkikku
	naṟumpaṭām vīciya uṟuvaṉum ilaṉē!
	pavaṉamā ulakiṉ naviyaṟap peṟṟa
	amiḻtupoti nelli auvik kītta
50	citiyāp pēṟṟiṉ atikaṉum ilaṉē!
	kuṟunaṭip puṟaviṉ ṟaputikaṇ ṭañcik
	kurutikeḻu pintaṭi muṟimuṟi taṭintu
	kaṉṉampuk kēṟiya maṉṉaṉum ilaṉē!
	aruntamiḻp pulavōr peruntiṟam uṇarntu
55	poṉṉum maṇiyun taṇṇaṭi nilaṉum
	variyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarum ilarē!
	meyyoṭu miṭiyāp poyporuḷ īṭṭit
	tāmaṟi aḷaviyiṟ piṟarkkoṉ ṟīyātu
	tāmē nukarun tīyarō uḷarē!
60	tamiḻvaḻak kaṟiyā tamivila kūṟuṅ
	kaṟṟaṟi villār maṟṟiṉip palarē! ataṉāṉ
	maṟṟavar kaṭitoṟum nittalum paṭarntu
	vāyvā ḷātu vallāṅkup pāṭik
	koṉṉē tirital iṉṉātu maṉṉō!
65	koḻunaṉi arumpiya kāñciyaṅ kōṭṭiṟ
	puṉṟalic ciṟuvar ēṟit tuṉṟukaṭaṟ
	curitirip pāyntu viḷiyāṭ ṭayaravum,
	veṇṭōṭu virinta kitiyan tātu
	meypaṭat timirnta noyciṟi vaṇṭiṉam
70	viliyaṟu muttamoṭu talimayak kuṟṟuk
	kaṇmayak kēṟi eṇṇila kiṭappa
	nuḷicciṟu makaḷir vaḷikkiyiṉ vārik
	kuṭampuri paṇilam nirampap peytu
	āmi yaṭuppil āmpal neruppiṟ
75	ṟāmaṟi tiṟattāl aṭuvuḻi mayakkaṟṟut
	tuṇṇeṉap paṟantu viṇṇiṭic cella
	viyappiṭi eḻunta mayakkoṭu niṟkavum,
	vēṟucila ciṟumiyar cāṟukeḻu karumpiṉ
	mīṉciṉi aṉṉa veṇmaṇal kuviit
80	tēmpoti maḻaliyoṭu ciṟupāṭṭicittu
	vayiṉvayiṉ niṉṟu makiḻvoṭu kuṟuvavum
	aḻakucāl neytal nalamuṟa maruviya
	oṟṟimā nakariṟ poṟṟoḻiṟ polintu
	viṇṭoṭa nivanta iñci vaḷiiya
85	urukeḻu tirunakar kāṇṭoṟum paraci
	malikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa niliyuyar talikkaṭi
	añcuvaru nōkkamoṭu niṉṟaṉeṉ ākac,
	curuḷiruṅ kuñci poṉñāṇ piṇittu
	nilaṉṟoṭa vīḻnta polantukil aciic
90	cāntupular mārpiṟ pūntoṭi puraḷa
	ēṟucel celaviṉ vīṟupaṭap pōntu
	yāriyō pulavōy! aṟumukat tiyaṉ
	pukuttuka niṉṉiyeṉṟu aruḷiṉaṉ, pōti! yeṉṟu
	iṉṉuri oruvaṉ pakara, ataṉetir
95	colluvatu aṟiyēṉ melleṉap pukkup
	poṉpuṉi viḷakkan taṅkiyil ēnti
	miṉpuṉi makaḷir niralpaṭa niṟpak,
	kival pāṭiṉi kaṟiyaṟap peṟṟa
	payaṉuṭi eḻāal nalaṉuṟa marīi
100	viralnuti teṟitta narappici nōkki
	miṭaṟṟoli porutti neṟippaṭa miḻaṟṟa,
	urumatirn taṉṉa maṇkaṉi muḻavamum
	varivēy turuviya urikeḻu kuḻalum
	pēṇuṟu marapiṟ pāṇiyum vallōr
105	pālkeḻu marapiṟ cīritiṉ iyakkac,
	cennerup pārnta poṉṉurit taṭavil
	akiṉey peytu palarniṉṟu pukippa,
	vaṭamalip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut
	teṉmalip piṟanta naṟuṅkuṟa ṭurittu
110	viḻumiya tiraṭṭiya ceḻunaṟuṅ kuḻampil
	aivaki viriyum virāay oyyeṉat
	turuttip peytu ceṟippap palarniṉṟu
	talippeyaṉ māriyiṉ maliccān tuṟippa,
	vāḻiyuṅ kamukum vayiṉvayiṉ nāṭṭi
115	ōviya nuṭpamum oṇpaṇi nuṭpamum
	mēviya paṭāam mēluṟak kaṭṭik
	kaḻaṅkupuri nittilam ilaṅkat tūkkik
	kēḻkiḷar paṉmaṇi kāḻpaṭa nāṟṟip
	`peruntaṇ kaṇavīra naṟuntaṇ māli
120	pālvēṟu marapiṟ palapaṭa iyaṟṟi
	elimayirk kampalam nilaṉuṟa virittu
	viriyiṭi eḻuppiya ariyaṇi mītu,
	veṅkatir ñāyiṟu maṇineṭuṅ kōṭṭil
	taṅki irunta takimi pōlavum
125	vāṉkatir matiyam mīṉkuḻāñ cūḻat
	tāṉpolin teḻunta talimi pōlavum
	aṟumukat toruvaṉ kuṟunaki ilaṅka
	nacikeḻu viḻikaḷ ticimukam parappik
	kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅkak, kaṇṭunaṉi
130	meynnilit toṇṭar tannili yaṟiyār
	viḻinīr cinta moḻiyiṭi kuḻaṟa
	uccik kūppiya kiyiṉar paḻicci
	aruṭkuṟip puṇarntāṅ kamivoṭu niṟpa,
	veruvaru nōkkamoṭu marampō lāki
135	aruṇili uṇarēṉ ṟeruḷiṉṟi yōṭik
	kuṭantaṅ koṇṭetir niṉṟaṉe ṉāka,
	maṭattapu ciṉmoḻi makiḻak kūṟi
	aṉpuṟu pulavōy! añcal ōmpumati!
	kālaṅ kaṭanta kōla vippil
140	nikarā iṉpam mikipaṭi nukarntu
	kaṭivaḻi illā niliyoṭu polikuvi!
	eṉṟupala nalluri piṉṟiyuṅ kūṟit,
	taṉmaruṅ kiruntōṟ kuṟippa, avaṉumeṉ
	aṅki paṟṟic ceṉṟu koṅkukamaḻ
145	nīrāṭu tuṟiyil niṟuviya aḷavi,
	kārmiṉ aṉiya ērkeḻu makaḷir
	kuṟunaki miḷireṉ arukiṟ pōntu,
	mācu potintu pāci āki
	īrum pēṉun tīrā tuṟiyumeṉ
150	puṉṟalik kuñci puṇarppaṟa uḷarip
	puḻukuney urittup paṉinī rāṭṭi
	īram pulara iṉpuki yūṭṭit
	tāmeṉi viṭuttup pōka, ēṉōṉ
	piṟitōr nalliṭam pukutta āṇṭik
155	kaviṉkeḻu cilatar viriyiṉar pukuntu
	viṇṇōr vēntaṉ kalṇeṉap polinteṉ
	ṟuṉṉaṟ citāar nīkki muṉṉiya
	aravuri aṉṉa āṭi uṭīik,
	kalavic cāntam pulara aṭṭi,
160	mukinekiḻntu virinta takicāl naṉmalar
	piṇiya lākki maṇamuṟa miliccit,
	tuḷaṅkoḷik kalaṉkaḷ viḷaṅka aṇintu,
	maṇittavi ciṭṭataṉ miciyeṉi irīik,
	kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttakam virīi
165	aṟucuvik kaṟiyum aṭicilun tuviyum
	veṟikamaḻ kaṉiyum naṟuneyum pālun
	tīñcumit tayirun tēṅkaṉi paḻaṉuṅ
	kaṉṉalum piṟavun tuṉṉa iyaṟṟik,
	kaṭumpaci tīrayāṉ micikoṟum micitoṟum
170	iṭumpi iṉṟi iṭiyiṭi iṭupu
	pāḻvayi ṟeṉṟum nirampap peytaliṟ
	perumporuḷ eytiya vaṟiñaṉiṉ allān
	tiruntapiṉ eṉṉip poruntak kūuy
	ēṉiyōr iṭattiṟ ṟāṉeṉic celuva, āṅkup
175	poḻinī ṟiṭṭu viḻimaṇi tāṅkik
	kāntaḷ pōlac cēntoḷir aṅkiyiṟ
	civañāṉa pōtam naliyaṟac civaṇip
	paḻutaṟu māṇavar kuḻuvoṭu cūḻa,
	vāṉkatir maṇṭilam īṅku vantatupōl
180	tiruvoṭu viḷaṅkum urukupoṟ ṟaviciṟ
	kaṉmaṉam urukkuṅ kāṇṭaku kōlamoṭum
	eṉmaṉam urukki eḻuntiṉi taruḷiya
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉik kāṇūuc,
	ceykuva taṟiyēṉ meytaṭu māṟi
185	nilampaṭa vīḻntu kalaṅki niṟpa,
	yāriyō? eṅkaḷ aṟumukat tiyaṉ
	tantatōr aruḷiṉ vantaṉi pōlum!
	nalli! nalli! elliyil kālam
	aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ niliyiṉi! viḻumiyi!
190	cevviyi! periyi! auviyam iliyeṉ
	ṟiṉiyaṉa palavum paṉivarak kiḷantutaṉ
	malarkkiyeṉ puṉṟalic cērttip pulappaṭa
	nīyē, uṭampuṅ poṟiyuṅ kaṭampaṭu karuviyum
	aṟivum vaḷiyum alli; aṟivāyc
195	ceṟiyum peṟṟiyi; maṟṟavi aṟiyāp
	paṇpiṉa; aṉiyi āyiṉum, muṉpē
	paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇṇiṉ miṭipaṭu
	mācu potinta nīrmiyi; vīciya
	oḷippiḻam pāki veḷippaṭum murukaṉ
200	āṉātu curanta aruḷiṉaṉ ākaliṉ,
	niṉṉi maṟitta tuṉṉiru ṭurantu
	taṉṉaruṭ polivāṉ niṉṉaṟivu koḷīi
	arukā iṉpan tarutaṟ  poruṭṭu
	meynnili irutti mācu pōkkit
205	taṉṉili niṉakkut tantaṉaṉ kōṭiyeṉ
	ṟuṟuti kūṟi viṭuppat, tēṟip
	paṉṉiru kiyaṉ muṉṉar eyti
	imayam pūtta paṉikeḻu cuṉiyil
	amiyā tāṭiya umitaru putalvōy!
210	eriviḻit timikkun tirunutaṟ ṟantik
	korumoḻi viritta teruḷuṟum uṇarvōy!
	vāṉōr uṟukaṇ tīrppa mēlōr
	aivaṭi vēlik kiyiṉil eṭuttōy!
	vēntaṉ tanta ēnteḻiṟ celvikku
215	varipuri mārpam variyātu aḷittōy!
	kuṉṟavar vaḷartta poṉṟikaḻ vaḷḷikkuk
	koḻukom pākiya viḻumiya tōḷōy!
	aruntamiḻk kīraṉip poruntik kāttōy!
	emmaṉōrk keḷivanta cemmi yāḷa!
220	oruva! ciṟuva! karumayiṉ muruka!
	maṟimuṭi taṉṉil niṟitiru vuruviṉi!
	kaṇṇum uṇarvuṅ katuvāp peṟṟiyi!
	aimperum pūtamum āki niṉṟaṉi!
	ulakuniṉ ṉuruvē, oḷikaṇiṉ vaḻiyē,
225	alakilāc camayat tavvavar tamakkup
	palavē ṟuruviṉ nilavuni nīyē!
	āṟuniṉ mukamē, aintuniṉ vaṭivē,
	nāṉkuniṉ moḻiyē, mūṉṟuniṉ kaṇṇē,
	iraṇṭuniṉ ṟuṇiyē,oṉṟu niṉ vēlē,
230	maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimi eytā
	muruṭa ṉēṟkun tiruvaruḷ taruniyeṉ
	ṟiyāṉaṟi yaḷaviyiṉ ēttat tāṉuvantu
	kuṟipparu miṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉiyē, atumutal
	iṉpam ārntaṅ kiruttal allatu
235	tuṉpa meṉpa taṟiyēṉ, aṉpoṭu
	nīyum avaṉvayiṟ celiṉē pāyaruḷ
	oruvantan tarukuvaṉ maṉṟa; maruvantu
	talippeyaṉ māriyiṉ malippaṭu nīttam
	pinniṟap pīliyuñ cenniṟat tukirum
240	vēḻa maruppuṅ kāḻakiṟ ṟuṇiyum
	oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viriik
	kuṇṭukaṭ paṭukariṉ maṇṭi niṟintāṅku,
	avalak kavaliyum uvalic cuviyum
	muraṇuṟu aṟivun tirukiya cerukkum
245	mutalaṟap peyarttuc citiyā tōṭi
	aṉpiṉ niṟinta iṉpa veḷḷattup
	paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṟ ṟuvaṉṟi
	ēḻula kaḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu
	ūḻi yūḻiyum vāḻmati ciṟantē!	(28)
29. kāppuk kimmikkuk kāmam perukiyurittal
ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cēvvēḷ ivari
maṟantiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
tiṇṭōḷ taḻuvuvam iṉṟēṟ ceḻuvariyik
kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.	(29)
30. aṭiyuṟiyiṭutal
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyi yēṉuṅ kacinta aṉpāṟ
pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅku moṟṟi
vāṇap peruntaki yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōviyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.	(30)
tiruvoṟṟimurukar
mummaṇikkōvi
muṟṟum

ōm civam
tiruvoṟṟi murukar 
mummaṇikkōvi uri


kāppuc ceyyuḷ
nīr vaḷam naṉṟu ōṅkum tiruvoṟṟi nīḷ nakaril - nīrvaḷam peritum ōṅkukiṉṟa tiruvoṟṟiyū  reṉṉum pukaḻālnīṇṭa paṭṭiṉattil tirukkōyil koṇṭa, cūrvaḷam tiṉ vēlōṟku - cūrmāliṉatu ceḻumiyit tiṉṟa vēṟpaṭiyi yuṭiya murukakkaṭavuḷukku, tūyanūl - `muṉṉamaṇik kōvi yeṉṉum ikkuṟṟamaṟṟanūli, cīr vaḷara - ciṟappōṅkumpaṭi, emmāl iyaṟṟuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - emmikkoṇṭu ceyvatu oṉṟu uḷḷatu, atu kimmā mukattaṉ kaḻal ākum - aṅṅaṉañ ceyyuṅ karuvi yāṉimukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭi yākumeṉka.
`naṉṟu perumipporuḷatu; naṉṟu peritākum eṉṉun tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇka.
`cūrvaḷantiṉ vēlōṉ eṉpatu, cūraṉuṭiya valimi celvam mutaliyavikaḷi aḻitta vēlō ṉeṉṉum poruṭṭu; uṭiyōṉ ṟoḻil uṭimimē lēṟṟappaṭṭatu.
`tūyanūl tūyaṉākiya murukakkaṭavuḷip pāṭutalpaṟṟi innūl tūya eṉa aṭikoṭukkappaṭṭatu.
innūlippāṭum ākkiyōṟkum itaṉi ōtuvārkkuñ ciṟappuṇṭāka eṉṉumporuṭṭuc `cīr vaḷara veṉṟār.
`emmāl āl karuvipporuḷil vantatu.
āciriyar innūlit tam muṉippiṉṟi iṟivaṉ vaḻiyāy niṉṟu avaṉiyaṟṟa iyaṟṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, iyaṟṟuvatu eṉattaṉviṉiyāṟ kūṟiṉār; āl, aci.
`kimmā kiyiyuṭiya vilaṅku; aḵtu yāṉi.
`atu kaḻal ākum eṉṟu viṉi muṭikka.
eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūl iṉitu muṭiyumāṟu mutaliṟ kāppuc ceyyuḷ kūṟutal nūlāciriyarkku marapātaliṉ, aṭikaḷum icceyyuḷāṟ kāppukkūṟiṉar. piṟkāla vaḻakku yāṉimukak kaṭavuḷ mēl niṟṟaliṉ icceyyuḷum avariyē paraviṟṟu.
ippāṭṭu nērici veṇpā. itu `nīr eṉpatu mutaṟ `kaḻal eṉpatu iṟuvāyāka muppatu coṟkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭu talāl, innūlum muppatu pāṭṭukkaḷā lākkap paṭṭateṉpatu kuṟippu. itaṉkaṇ il ku āl eṉṉum vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷum ēṉic coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. eṉṉi? avvurupukaḷ tāmuñ coṟkaḷōṭoppap poruḷuṇarttuta luṭimiyi ṉeṉpatu. aṟṟāyiṉ, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉi vikutikaḷum poruḷuṇarttuta luṭimiyiṉ, avi tāmum avvurupukaḷip pōlac coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭal vēṇṭumā leṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu, oru coṟ ṟoṭariṉiṭiyē il ku āl mutaliya vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ coṟkaḷiṉuḷ niṉṟum poruḷuṇarttutaṟ kaṭ pirinticittal pōl, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉi vikutikaḷ aṅṅaṉam pirinticiyāmiyiṉ, av vikutikaḷ coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭutaṟku uriyaṉavallaveṉ ṟuṇarntukoḷka.
iṉi, ikkāppucceyyuḷ `nīr eṉṉuñ colli mutalākavum, `kaḻal eṉṉuñ colli iṟutiyākavuṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅkalāl, nūlum avvāṟē `nīr eṉpati mutaliluṅ `kaḻal eṉpati iṟutiyiluṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅku meṉpatūuṅ kuṟippā ṉuṇarappaṭum.
iṉṉum, ippāṭṭiṉ mutalaṭiyil tiruvoṟṟinakarc ciṟappum, iraṇṭāmaṭiyiṟ cūraṉaḻivum, mūṉṟāmaṭiyil tāmum piṟarum payaṉ peṟumāṟum, nāṉkāmaṭiyil avarip payaṉ peṟuvikkum iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭiniliyuṅ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ, avitām muṟiyē poruḷaṟiviṉ viriviyum, aṟiyāmi yiṉ tēyviyum, avviraṇṭiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya uyiriṉ takutiyiyum, attakutik kēṟpa atu peṟum pēriṉpap pēṟṟiyuṅ kuṟittu, avvāṟṟāl ittiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇikkōviyeṉṉum nūlun tiruvoṟṟi nakaric ciṟappikkumukattāl uyiriṉaṟivip puṟapporuḷkaḷiṟ celutti akaṉṟuviriyumāṟu ceytu, piṉṉar ammukattāl aṟiyāmitēyttu ataṉ uṇarviṉi nuṭpamākki, iṟivaṉatu pēriṉpattiṉi nukarntu kiṭakkac ceyyumeṉṉum muṟi teḷivittavāṟu.
mēlum, icceyyuḷ `ākum eṉṉum viṉiyiyē muṭiviṟ peṟutaṟkurittāyiruppavum, āciriyar ataṉic `cīrvaḷar eṉpataṟkuṅ `kaḻal eṉpataṟkum iṭiyē yiṭṭu muṭittiruttalāl, innūlun taṉṉip payilvārkku immippayaṉkaḷi muṟṟun tantu iṟutiyil iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭippēṟṟip payakkumeṉap payaṉ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟellāṅ kāppuc ceyyuḷeṉpatu nūliṉ kaṭkāṇappaṭum nūlamitikaḷiyum poruṭkūṟupāṭukaḷiyum payaṉ mutaliya piṟavaṟṟiyu mellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉkaṭkoṇṭu viḷaṅkutal atutaṉakku ilakkaṇamāmeṉka. āciriyar civañāṉamuṉivarun tamatu civañāṉapōtamāpāṭiyattu maṅkalavāḻttuc ceyyuḷiṉ uriyiṟutiyilum iṅṅaṉam nūṉutal poruḷellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉṉakat taṭakkiniṟṟal maṅkalavāḻttukku ilakkaṇameṉṟuṇarka eṉṟu uri kūṟiṉar. kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkameṉpatum maṅkalavāḻt teṉpatum orupuṭiyoppumiyuṭiya.
1. ceñcoṭu kiḷattal
neñcoṭu kiḷattal eṉpatu taṉ talivaṉ perumiyum avaṉṟaṉ aruḷuruvi niṉitalāl taṉakku viḷiyum naṉmiyun talivi taṉ neñciṟku eṭuttukkūṟal; immuṟiyiṉi `neñcaṟivuṟūu veṉavum vaḻaṅkupa.
itu mutal nāṟpattōraṭikāṟum maruta nilattiṟkum neytaṉilattiṟkun tiṇimayakkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(1 mutal 4 aṭi) nīr vaḷam kaviṉṟa cīr vaḷar paḻaṉattu - nīr vaḷattāl aḻakupeṟṟa ciṟappōṅkiya kaḻaṉiyil, nel kural kavviya nal kural pim kiḷi - neṟpayiriṉ katirik kuviya aḻakiya kuraloliyiṉi yuṭiya paccikkiḷi, kāṉal kāvil tāṉ virintu eyti - kaṭaṟkariccōliyil virivāyp paṟantuceṉṟu, poṉvīñāḻal polintu iṉitu iruppavum - poṉniṟamāṉa malarkaḷiyuṭiya nāka marattiṉmēl maṉeḻucciyōṭu aḻakāy uṭkārntirukkavum.
`vaḷaṅ kaviṉṟa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit toki, cīr - viḷiviṉperumi. kural - neṟkatir; itu neṟkatirum... kuraleṉamoḻipa eṉṉum piṅkalantiyā laṟiyappaṭum.
itu, maruta nilattukkiḷi neytal nākamarattiṟ ceṉṟiruttal kūṟiṟṟu.
(5-8) karum kaḻi vāriya peru mīṉ kuvii - kariyaniṟat tiṉiyuṭiya kaḻiyiliruntu vāriyeṭutta periya mīṉkaḷik kuvittu, val tiṟal paratavar muṉṟiltoṟum uṇakkum - valiya uṭaluram vāynta cempaṭavar tammuṭiya vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅka ṭōṟum ularttukiṉṟa, tuṭikkaṇ tuṇiyal koṭikkulam kavarntu - uṭukkiyiṉ kaṇkaḷippōl vaṭṭattuṇṭu kaḷāyirukkuṅ karuvāṭukaḷik kākkik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ tiruṭiyeṭuttukkoṇṭu, marutaṅ kāvil peritu vikavum - maruta nilattu iḷamaraccōliyil ēkip perumitattuṭaṉ amarntirukkavum,
tiṟal - uṭampiṉ uram.
ularttutaṟkāka vakuntu veyilil virittuvikkap paṭṭukkāyum mīṉ kāṇpārkku uṭukkiyiṉ kaṇkaḷippōl iraṇṭu vaṭṭattuṇṭukaḷāyt tōṉṟutalāl, atu `tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiya leṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtivvāṟu tōṉṟutal koḻumīṉ kuṟiiya tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiyal eṉṉum maturik kāñciyālum (320) āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tuṭiyiṉkaṇ pōluruṇṭa tuṇikaḷ eṉṟu ataṟkuk kūṟiya uriyālun teḷiyappaṭum.
itu, neytalnilattuk kākkiyum mīṉum marutanilattuc ceṉṟami kūṟiṟṟu.
(9-11) kuḷaṉ uṟi vāḻkki vaḷa nīrkkōḻi - maruta nilattuk kuḷattiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya uṭaṟceḻumi vāynta nīrkkōḻi, maṉiyuṟi nīṅkit tuṇi kūp peyarntu - avviṭattu uṟitalit tavirntu taṉatu peṭippaṟaviyik kūviyaḻittuk koṇṭu, kālvaḻi ōṭik kaḻiyiṟ cēravum - vāykkāl vaḻiyāka nīnticceṉṟuneytaṉilattuk kaḻinīriṟ cēravum.
`vaḷam eṉpati nīrkkuk kūṟutalumām. `uṟi iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu viṉittoki, piṉṉatu mutaṉilittoḻiṟpeyar. `tuṇi iccol irupāṟkum urittēyāyiṉum, perum pālum peṇpāl mēṟṟākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
`kūppeyartal orucol; aḻittaleṉṉum poruṭṭām.
itu, marutanilattu nīrkkōḻi neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyiṟ cērntami kūṟiṟṟu.
(12-17) pulavu maṇam muṉiiya nilavuuṟaḻ kuruku - neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyil mikiruntamiyāṟ pulāl nāṟṟatti veṟuttuviṭṭa nilaviyotta veḷva kaṇ akal vicumpil naṇṇiya mukilmēl ivarntuḷiya nārikaḷ eḻutiṅkaḷ - iṭamakaṉṟa vāṉiṟ poruntiya kariya mukiliṉ mēl ēṟit tōṉṟun tiṅkaḷip pōla, parantu keḻu tuṇinīrt taṭattiṉ maṇam malar ārum - iṭamakaṉṟu uḷḷa teḷinta nīriṉi yuṭiya marutanilattuk kuḷattiluḷḷa maṇaṅkamaḻum pūkkaḷi mēyntukoṇṭirukkum, irumputirittu aṉṉa aṟal maruppu erumi karumpuṟam maruṅkil - irumpi muṟukkiṉāṟpōl aṟuppu aminta maruppi yuṭiya erumikaḷiṉ kariya mutukākiya iṭattil, oruṅki niṟpavum - kūmpi niṟkavum,
`muṉiiya veṟutta; iḵtipporuṭṭātal cēṟṟu nili muṉiiya ceṅkaṭ kārāṉ eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṟ (4) kāṇka. `nilavu niṟattiṟ kuvami. `naṇṇiya poruntiya. `ivarnteḻu orucol nīra.
`tuṇinīr eṉpataṟkut `teḷintanīreṉṟē āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tuṇinīr mellaval (maturikkāñci, 283) eṉpuḻi uri kūṟiṉār.
`irumputirittaṉṉa veṉpatu, maruppiṉ valimi yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvuvamiyi irumputirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppu eṉṟu kuṟuṅkuṭimarutaṉārum akanāṉūṟṟil (46) eṭuttāṇṭār.
aṟal, toḻilaṭiyākap piṟantapeyar.
`puṟamaruṅku puṟamākiya iṭattil; iṅku maruṅku eṉpatu iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟatu; `maruṅkiṉkaṇ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu urupu keṭaccāriyiyuṭaṉ niṉṟatu; melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi eṉṉuñcūttirattil meypeṟa eṉṟamiyāl iṉṉōraṉṉa muṭikkappaṭumeṉpar urikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tolkāppiyam, eḻuttu, 157).
vicumpu nīrkkuḷattiṟkum, mukil erumikkum, tiṅkaḷ kurukukkum uvami; erumikkuk karumpuṟaṅ kūṟiṉamiyāl, ataṉ uvamiyākiya mukilukkuṅ karumiyurikkappaṭṭatu.
itu neytal nilattu nāri marutanilattu vayalerumi mēṟceṉṟu niṉṟami kūṟiṟṟu.
(18-19) poṟikeḻuvāḷi - marutanilattu nīr nili kaḷiluḷḷa viḷakkam aminta vāḷimīṉ, veṟipaṭap pāyntu muṭikkaḻiccuṟavoṭu kaṟuvoṭu maṟiyavum - matarppiṉāṟ carēleṉap pāyntu muṭi nāṟṟattiyuṭiya kaḻikkaṇuḷḷa cuṟāmīṉoṭu ciṉattuṭaṉ puraḷavum,
`poṟi keḻu vāḷi `oḷi poruntiya vāḷi yeṉap         poruṭarutal poṟi vari varāl eṉpuḻi (cīvakacintāmaṇi, 44) nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruritta uriyālaṟiyappaṭum.
veṟi - matarppu.                                                                                                                               
`kāletirn tōṭuṅ kaḻikkaya leṉṟu piṉvarutaliṉ, ivvāḷi mīṉuṅ kālvaḻicceṉṟu pāyumeṉak koḷka. `kālvaḻiyōṭik kaḻiyiṟcēravu meṉṟu mēlē vantamiyum niṉivu kūraṟpāṟṟu.
`oṭu urupukaḷ iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum muṉṉatu kalappuṟu poruḷilum, piṉṉatu aṭimoḻip poruḷilum vantaṉa.
`muṭikkaḻi pulālnāṟṟa muṭiya kaḻi; ēṉiyellā nīr nilikaḷiṉum uppaḷattukkaḻiyē muṭimikutiyum iyalpiluṭimiyāl, ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām am muṭināṟṟattik kaḻikkē ēṟṟikkūṟutal marapu. pulavunāṟiruṅkaḻi yeṉṟu akappāṭṭilum (180) vantatu. pulavukkaḻi yeṉṟu aṭikaḷē mēlum (22 - 109) urippar.
itu marutanilattu vāḷi neytalnilattuc cuṟavoṭu pōr purital kūṟiṟṟu.
(20-21) kāl etirntu ōṭum kaḻikkayal - nīrkkālkaḷil etirmukamāy ōṭum neytaṟkaḻiyiṉ kayal mīṉkaḷ, marutaccēloṭu kūṭiccērntu tuḷḷavum - marutanilattuc cēlmīṉkaḷoṭu kalantu oṉṟāyk kutikkavum,
marutanilattu vāykkālkaḷ kaṭaṟpakkam nōkkic cellumātaliṉ, ak kaṭaṟpakkattiliruntu marutanilatti nōkki ēku kayal mīṉkaḷ akkālvāykaḷi etirntu ōṭiṉaveṉṟār. etirntu, etirttu; ivvaṉmi meṉmikaḷiṟ poruḷvēṟupāṭu uṇṭu; muṉṉiyatu etirmukamāyc cellutaliyum, piṉṉiyatu pōrkku muṉitaliyum uṇarttum. kayaloṭu cēluntuḷḷiṟṟeṉpār, `cērntu tuḷḷavum eṉṟār.
itu neytaṟkaḻiyiṉ kayal mīṉkaḷ marutanilattuc cēṟkeṇṭikaḷoṭu cērntu tuḷḷutal kūṟiṟṟu.
(22-25) muḻuneṟi aviḻkkuṅ koḻuṅkāl ōṭi - maruta nilattu arumpukaḷi itaḻaviḻkkum avviṭattuk koḻuviya kāṟṟu ōṭicceṉṟu, poṉ am tāmarip polam tukaḷ vāri - poṉṉiṟamāṉa aḻakiya centāmarippūkkaḷiṉ poṉṉiṟamāṉa poṭiyi aḷḷi, kōḻimuṭṭi pōḻpaṭuttāṅku - kōḻimuṭṭiyi uṭittāṟpōla, veḷ ayirk kāṉal koḷḷiyiṉ vīcavum - veḷḷiya nuṇmaṇaliyuṭiya kaṭaṟkariccōliyil mikutiyāyt tūvavum,
`muḻuneṟi muṟukkuviṭāmuḻukkaṭṭuṭiya pūmoṭṭu.
nīrvaḷa nilavaḷamuṭiya marutak kāṟṟākaliṉ `koḻuṅkāl eṉappaṭṭatu.
tukaḷ, iṅku makarantappoṭi; atu poṉṉiṟamā yiruttaliṉ, `polantukaḷeṉa aṭi koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
kōḻimuṭṭiyi uṭittakkāl, mañcaṭkaru viraviya veṇkuḻampē nilattiṉkaṭparavutaliṉ, atu tāmarippūviṉ mañcaṭ poṭiyiṉi veṇmaṇaliṟ citaṟutaṟku uvami yāyiṟṟu.
`āṅku uvamaccol leṉṟār parimēlaḻakar (tirukkuṟaḷuri, 2: 5).
`ayir nuṇmaṇalukkup peyarātali iṭikkalap paṉṉa īr ayir (cilap, 6 : 146) eṉṟu āciriyar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ōruvamiyiṉ vittu urittār.
itu marutanilattuk kāṟṟu annilattut tāmarimalariṉ pūntātukaḷi aḷḷikkoṇṭu neytal nilattuk kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṟceṉṟu tūvutal kūṟiṟṟu.
(26-30) veḷḷi veṇ tōṭṭut tāḻiyiṉ aḷii veḷḷittakaṭṭiyotta veḷḷiya maṭalkaḷi yuṭiya tāḻampū vittuḻavi, kiyarikkoṇṭa kaṭal kāl ōṭi - ataṉ makarantap poṭiyi vārikkoṇṭa kaṭaṟkāṟṟu maruta nilattukku ōṭi, nīṟu meypūtta nimalaṉpōla - tirunīṟu uṭampeṅkum polivupeṟat tōṉṟiya malattiṉ nīṅkiṉa civaperumāṉippōla, cemtāmarikaḷ ciṟantu iṉitu viḷaṅka - civanta tāmarip pūkkaḷ niṟañciṟantu aḻakāyt tōṉṟumāṟu, veṇpoṭi vīcip paṇpoṭum ulāvavum - av veṇpoṭiyic citaṟi iṉitāy vīcavum,
`tāḻiyiṉ iṉ cāriyi, kaṇ urupu tokkatu.
`kiyarikkoḷḷal kinirampa arittukkoḷḷal; iṅku vāḷāvārik koḷḷal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu; ivi oru coṉṉīrmiya. vaṭamoḻi `upacarkkaṅ kaḷippōlat tamiḻ moḻiyiluṅ `ki yeṉpatu, uriya peyar viṉic coṟkaḷōṭu iyintuvarum iṭiccollāy niṟkum. kaḷittaṟiyē ṉeṉpatu kiviṭuka veṉpatiṟpōla, (tirukkuṟaḷ 93, 8).
`pūttal polivupeṟṟuttōṉṟal: `mīṉpūttaṉṉa tōṉṟalar (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 169).
kaṭaṟkāṟṟu kuḷircciyu miṉimiyu muṭimiyāl, avviṉimi yataṉ `paṇpoṭu meṉac cuṭṭappaṭṭatu.
`nimalaṉ malamillātavaṉ; avaṉ civaperumāṉ; iccolliṉ mutalil iṉmipporuḷi yuṇarttum `nir eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṭiccol (upacarkkam) īṟu keṭṭup puṇarntatu.
aṭikaḷ civapirāṉ māṭṭu iṭiyaṟā aṉpuceluttuñ civap periyārākaliṉ, tamatu aṉpiṟkuriya poruḷik kitiyantātu paṭinta centāmari malarukku uvami kūṟiṉār; ītu aṉpumikkārkku iyalpēyām. toṇṭar cīr paravuvārāṉa āciriyar cēkkiḻār kitiyantātu paṭinta kamalavaṇṭiṟku nīṟupuṉinta toṇṭari uvami kāṭṭik kamala vaṇṭalar kitit, tuṉṟu nīṟupuṉi mēṉiyavākit tūyanīṟupuṉi toṇṭarkaḷeṉṉac, ceṉṟu ceṉṟu muralkiṉṟaṉa veṉ ṟaruḷicceyyum aruṇmoḻiyilum ivviyalpiṉik kāṇka.
(31-40) tēm moḻic cevvāyt tirumuka uḻattiyar - iṉiya moḻikaḷ nirampiya, civanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya mukam vāynta uḻavar makaḷir, kāmaru koḻum cuvi vāḻiyiṉ paḻaṉum - viruppam mikuttaṟkuriya mikka cuviyiṉiyuṭiya vāḻiyiṉ paḻaṅkaḷum, puṉcey payanta pal niṟap payaṟum - kolliyil viḷinta palaniṟaṅkaḷ vāynta palavakip payaṟukaḷum naṉcey payanta poṉniṟa maṇiyum - vayalil viḷinta poṉṉiṟam vāynta neṉmaṇikaḷum, vaṭṭikip peytu - kūṭiyiṟ koṭṭi kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭiṉ eṭuttucceṉṟu - vaḷittuk kaṭṭiya cummāṭṭiṉmēl eṭuttuk koṇṭupōy, aṅku aṭuttu uyar pākkattu - avviṭattiyaṭuttu maṇaṉmēṭāy uyarntuḷḷa cempaṭavar cēriyil, kumiḻ iṭi ukaḷum amiviḻip parattiyar - kumiḻam pūvi yotta mūkkiṉ aṭiyiṟ puraḷum aminta viḻikaḷiyuṭiya neytal makaḷir, pakuttu uṇarvu iṉṟi mikuttuk koṭutta - ivvaḷavukku ivvaḷavu eṉṟu pirittu uṇarum uṇarcciyillāmal mikutiyāyk koṭuttuviṭṭa, taraḷamum mīṉum niralpaṭa aḷantu koṇṭu - muttukkaḷiyum mīṉkaḷiyum tām viṟṟa paṇṭaṅ kaḷukku oḻuṅkāka aḷanteṭuttuk koṇṭu, uvappoṭu peyarntu tam irukki cēravum - tām mikutiyāyppeṟṟa makiḻcciyoṭu tirumpit tam iruppiṭam aṭiyavum,
moḻi cevvāy mukam eṉpavaṟṟiṟku ummi virittalu mām. `tēm moḻi eṉpataṟkut `tēṉaṉiya moḻi yeṉṟum poruḷurippar.
`kāmaru eṉpatik kāmam maru eṉap pirittu viruppam poruntiya allatu viruppam mikuttaṟkuriya eṉap poruḷurikka; iṉik kāmaru kuvaḷi yeṉpataṟkuk `kāmam varumeṉpatu vikārattāṟ kāmaruveṉaniṉṟu viruppamvaru meṉpatāyiṟṟu eṉṟu āciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār urittār (cilappatikāram, anti. 40).

vayalmēl viṉiceyvār vikkōṟ ṟāḷic curuṭṭi vaḷituppaḷinārāṟ kaṭṭic cummāṭeṭupparātaliṉ, kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭi ṉeṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ iṉ eṉṉum cāriyi niṟka ēḻaṉ urupu tokkatu.

(41-44) marutamum neytalum mayaṅkiya marapiṉ - ivvāṟu marutanilap poruḷkaḷum neytaṉilapporuḷkaḷum oruṅku kalakkumiyalpiṉāl, ulakam ellām oruṅku vantu uṟiṉum - ulakattārellārum oṉṟāy vantu cērntālum, toliyā vaḷattoṭu - avarkaḷ nukarvaṉavellām vaḻaṅkiyum aḻiyāta vaḷattoṭu, nilavuṟum vippiṉ oṟṟi mānakarkku - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ūriṉiyuṭiya tiruvoṟṟiyū reṉṉum periya paṭṭiṉattiṟku,
marutamum neytalum ākupeyarāy avaṟṟiṉ karup poruḷkaḷi uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. karupporuḷ iviyeṉpatu,
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷ paṟi

eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum. iccūttirattāṉ nuvalap paṭāvāyiṉum, iṅku aṭika ḷeṭuttāṇṭa nīrvāḻvaṉavum vaḷi niliyum pūvum nilamakkaḷum, `avvakipiṟavu meṉpataṉāl aṭakkik koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉi? piṟavu meṉṟataṉāl nilamakkaḷum nīr niliyum vārkoṭiyum pūvuṅ koḷḷappaṭu meṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikārappatika uriyiṟ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ.
