(44 - 46) uṟṟa kaṇ eṉavum - poruntiya kaṇṇeṉavum, akkaṇiṉ uḷ vaḷar aru maṇi eṉavum - akkaṇṇiṉ uḷḷē oḷivaḷarum ariya karumaṇiyeṉavum, ammaṇi uḷ uṟi pāviyi eṉavum - akkaruviḻiyiṉuḷḷē uṟiyum pāvi yeṉavum,
pāviyi, ai cāriyi; iṟavariyumpar eṉṉun tiruk kōviyāriṟpōnta `kuṟavari yārkkum eṉpataṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟiya vuriyikkāṇka.
iṟivaṉi mutaṉmiyum arumiyum nōkki iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṇṇeṉavum maṇiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉamē kaṇṇē kaṇṇiṟ karumaṇiyē maṇiyāṭu pāvāy (tiruvāvaṭutuṟi) eṉṟu appar perumāṉum aruḷicceytaṉar. `arumaṇi yeṉṟatu karumaṇiyēyā meṉpatum itaṉkaṭ peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
oṟṟiyamānakarkkuk kaṇṇeṉavumeṉṟu toṭarka.
(47-50) pāviyiṉ akattup paṇpu palakāṭṭi mēvi - appāviyiluḷḷē iyalpukaḷ pala viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiyuṟintu, āṅku atu puṭi peyarntu iyaṅka - ak karuviḻiyiṉuḷ atu pakkam peyarntu aciya, takkavāṟu mikkatu puriyum - nērattiṟkut takuntapaṭi ciṟantatu ceyyum, ōr iyalpu illā ār uyir eṉavum - ōriyalpiṉṟip palvēṟu iyalpukaḷ uṭiya ariya uyireṉavum, eṉṟurittu mēl `viḷaṅkum eṉpataṉoṭu kūṭṭuka.
paṇpu, uyirppaṇpik kāṭṭum nuṇṇiya ceyalkaḷ, mēvi. uyiriṉviṉi; atu, pāvi, `pāviyiṉakattu mēvi atu iyaṅka mikkatu puriyum āruyi reṉka.
`takkavāṟu mikkatupuriyum eṉṟatu nērattiṟku icinta vāṟu ceyyum uyiriṉtiṟatti; eṉavē `paṇpu uyiriṉ palvēṟiyaṟ  kiyiṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu. `mikkatu aṟiviṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭiṟṟu. `mikkatu oru tokutippaṭṭa ciṟanta ceyalkaḷi yuṇarttutaliṉ orumip paṉmi mayakkam.
uyir, viḻivu aṟivu ceyalkaḷ (iccā ñāṉakkiriyi) pala uṭiyavātalpaṟṟi ōriyalpillā āruyir eṉṟār.
(51-53) iruvēṟu ulakiṉum - uyirpporuḷ ulakam uyiril poruḷ ulakam eṉṉum iraṇṭu vēṟupaṭṭa ulakaṅkaḷilum, iraṇṭu aṟa viravi - avi vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉa iraṇṭāyp pirintu niṟṟal illiyāmpaṭi avaṟṟōṭu oruṅku kalantu, aṟivum toḻilum neṟi uṟaviḷakkum mātu oru kūṟaṟku - uyirkaḷiṉ aṟiviyum uyirillaṉavaṟṟiṉ ceyaliyum muṟiporunta viḷaṅka vikkum umiyammiyi yorupākamā vuṭiya civapirāṉukku, ōtiya mutalvaṉ - ōmeṉum orumoḻi maṟipporuḷi ōtiyaruḷiya mutaṟkaṭavuḷ,
iruvēṟulaku; aṟivuḷḷa uyirttokiyum aḵtillā maṇ nīr tī mutalāṉa uyiril poruṭṭokiyum,
`iraṇṭaṟaviravi yeṉṟār, kalapput taṉmiyāl oṉṟāyum poruṭṭaṉmiyāl iraṇṭāyum viravi oṉṟeṉṟum iraṇṭeṉṟuṅ kūṟa muṭiyāmik kalantu niṟṟaliṉ; ivviravutaliyē `iraṇṭaṉmi (attuvitam) eṉṟu civacittānta nūl kūṟum.
iṟivaṉ ammiyiṉ vāyilākap pirippiṉṟi niṉṟu ivaṟṟi viḷakkutaliṉ, avaṉi `mātoru kūṟaṉeṉṟu vitantār. iṅku ammiyoṉṟatu aruḷ.
viḷakkuṅ kūṟa ṉeṉṟu koḷka.
ivvaṭikaḷ,
  aviyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉiyīṟ
  pōkku varavu puriya āṇiyiṉ
  nīkkam iṉṟi niṟku maṉṟē
eṉṉuñ civañāṉapōta nūṟpāvip peritottut teḷinta tamiḻil viḷaṅkiniṟṟal peritum viyantu makiḻaṟpālatu.
`pāviyiṉakattu... āruyireṉavum eṉṟu mēlvanta āruyircceyali iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu `iruvēṟulakiṉum... mātoru kūṟa ṉeṉṉum iv iṟivaṉ ceyalukku uvamit taluñ ceyaṟpālatu. uṭampiyiyakkum uyiri ulakuyirkaḷi yiyakkum iṟivaṉukku uvamikāṭṭum muṟi.
kaṭṭu muṟuppuṅ karaṇamuṅ koṇṭuḷḷa
  miṭṭatoru pēraḻikka veṉṉeṉṟāṅ koṭṭi
  yavaṉuḷamā killā ṉuḷamavaṉā māṭṭā
  tavaṉuḷamā yallaṉumā maṅku
eṉṟu āciriyar meykaṇṭa tēva nāyaṉāraruḷicceyta civañāṉapōta veṇpāviṟ kāṇappaṭum.
iṉik `kaṇṇeṉavum `maṇiyeṉavum `pāviyi yeṉavum eṉpavaṟṟiyum iṅṅaṉamē uvamitturikkalāmō veṉiṉ, tiruvoṟṟimānakar mātoru kūṟaṉit toṭarntu, `mutalvaṉ eṉṟa murukavēḷit toṭarātoḻiyumākaliṉ, avvāṟurittalā kātu.
iṉi `viḷakku meṉpati mutalvaṉukkuk kūṭṭi muṭittu mālōveṉiṉ, ulakuyirkaḷukkellām aṟivun toḻilum viḷakkum mātoru kūṟaṉukkē ōmeṉu morumoḻi ōtiṉāṉ mutalvaṉeṉṉum peruñciṟappu āṇṭup peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ, atuvum uriyaṉṟeṉka. mēlum, `mutalvaṉ eṉpataṉ coṟporuḷāṟṟal ataṉkaṭ ciṟavāmiyum, ataṉāl ataṉip peytu niṟuttiya aṭikaḷiṉ karuttu āṇṭu nirampāmiyuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
(54-58) cellal uṟṟu aḻuṅkiya mallal tēvar kuḻām peritu uyya - tuṉpamaṭintu iraṅki muṟiyiṭṭa viṇvaḷam poruntiya tēvar kūṭṭam nīṇṭu uyirvāḻa, vaḻā vēl ōccippāṭu il cūrukku vīṭu nalki - kuṟi tavaṟāta vēṟpaṭiyi yeṟintu aḻitalillāta cūrapatumaṉukku vīṭupēṟaḷittu, vēntaṉ payanta kūntaṟkumariyi maṇantu ulaku aḷitta - tēvarkku ariyaṉ āṉa intiraṉ peṟṟeṭutta teyva yāṉiyit tirumaṇam porunti ulakavarkku aruḷ purinta, niṇam tikaḻ vēlaṉ-  koḻuppuk kaṟi viḷaṅkum vēli yuṭiyavaṉ,.
cellal, tuṉpam; aḻuṅkal, iraṅkal; ivaṟṟic, `cellaluṟ ṟiraṅki yeṉṉum maṇimēkaliyiṟ kāṇka (2, 9).
`mallaṟ ṟēva reṉṟatu, `celvappēṟṟil vikuntēva reṉṉum poruṭṭu; tām tēṭāmalē ellā nukarporuḷum peṟṟa tēvareṉka; mallal - vaḷam, celvum; mallalvaḷaṉē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār (uri - 7).
`peritu kālap perumi yuṇarttiṟṟu; `peritu vikavum eṉpatiṟpōla (ivvakaliṉ 8ām aṭi)
`pāṭil cūr eṉṟār, cūraṉ taṉatu tava valimiyāl aḻiyāta niliyip peṟṟiruntāṉātaliṉ; ivaṉṟaṉ ṟavavalimiyi aṭikaḷ mēṟ pattoṉpatām pāṭṭiṟ patiṉeṭṭām aṭimutal aimpattōrām aṭivariyil mikavum viritturippar; āṅku avaṉ tavac ciṟappiṉ vakimikaḷi viḷaṅka urippām. pāṭu, mutal nīṇṭa mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar. cūr, īṟukeṭṭatu.
`vīṭunalki yeṉṟamiyiṉ, cūraṉatu ūṉuṭampi vēlōccik koṉṟaṉa reṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. `ōcci yeṉṉum viṉiyeccattukkuk `koṉ ṟeṉpatu avāy nili.
cūraṉ pakivaṉāy etirttup porutāṉāyiṉum, avaṉuyir viṉivi ṉīṅki iṟivaṉ ṟiruvaṭiniliyi yuṟutaṟkēṟṟa muṟṟiya tavappēṟum nalviṉiyum uṭimiyiṉ, iṟivaṉ avaṉit taṉṟiruvaṭikkaṭ cērttami kuṟittu `vīṭunalki yeṉṟār.
`vēntaṉ maruta nilak kiḻavaṉ, aḵtu īṇṭu vāṉōr talivaṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu. payattal īṉṟeṭuttal.
`kūntaṟ kumari kūntaliyuṭiya kumari yākiya teyvayāṉi.
`ulakaḷitta veṉpatic cūraṉik koṉṟamiyāl tēvariyum, ammuṟiyē makkaḷiyum pātukātta veṉak koḷka.
iṟivaṉ aruḷoṭu puṇarntu ulakatti yaḷikkum uṇmi paṟṟik, `kūntaṟ kumariyi maṇantu ulakaḷitta vēla ṉeṉṟu ōtiṉār; iṉi atu varalāṟṟuṇmiyumām.
(59-65) aiyaṉ - evvuyirkkun tanti, ceyyaṉ - cenniṟa muṭiyavaṉ, paṉṉiru kiyaṉ - paṉṉiraṇṭu kikaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ, vaṟiyēm iṭumpi  poṟipaṭuttu etirum periyōṉ - ēḻiyēmākiya em tuṉpattit tukaḷākki etirtōṉṟum pērāṟṟaluṭiyōṉ, periyar aṟiviṉukku ariyaṉ - tavattāṟ periyarāyiṉā raṟivukkum eṭṭātavaṉ (ākiya murukapperumāṉatu), tiru uru orumiyiṉ eḻuvittu - tiruvuruvatti neñcorumiyuṭaṉ eḻuntaruḷacceytu, arumiyoṭu vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟi - ciṟappuṭaṉ vaṇakkavaḻipāṭu ceytu, viḻi nīr uṟippa aṉpu uruvāki iṉpu uṟal aṟiyātu -kaṇṇīrtuḷippa aṉpu vaṭivāki iṉpam aṭital  aṟiyāmal, puṉporuḷa kavarum eṉ miṉpuri maṉaṉē - ciṟumiyuṭiya poruḷkaḷip piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ koḷḷum vēṭki vāynta eṉ miṉṉali yokka neñcē.
civapirāṉ piḷḷiyār murukappirāṉ eṉpārellām uṇmiyāṉ nōkkuṅkāl orē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāy muṭitaliṟ civapirāṉic cemmēṉi yemmāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla murukakkaṭavuḷiyum īṇṭuc `ceyyaṉ eṉṟār.
`paṉṉirukiyaṉ eṉṟatu, avaṉatu āṟṟaluṇarttiyapaṭi yeṉka.
`vaṟiyēmeṉpatu iṅku iṉpattiṉ ēḻimiyik kuṟittatu; tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ mutaliyōr aṭiyār perumakkaḷi ēḻiyaṭiyārkaḷ (tiruvikāvūrt tēvāram, 1) eṉṟu aruḷicceytatum atu. vaṟiyēmeṉṉun taṉmip       paṉmi, taṉṉiṉamāṉa ēṉiyōriyun taḻuvi niṉṟatu. itu pōlvaṉavaṟṟi uḷappāṭṭuttaṉmi (tolkāppiyam, viṉiyiyal 5) yeṉpar cēṉāvariyar; taṉakku orumiyalla tiṉmiyiṟṟaṉmip paṉmiyāvatu taṉṉoṭu piṟari uḷappaṭuttatēyām eṉṟu avarataṟku ētu urittalum āṇṭukkāṇka.
etirtal, etirvaral; āvatu etirtōṉṟal.
`tavattāṉ muṟṟiya periyarkkum ariyaṉāya murukaṉ vaṟiyē miṭumpiyip poṟipaṭuttetirum periyaṉāyiruttaliṉ, avaṉi vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi iṉpuṟalaṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavarkiṉṟaṉiyē neñcē yeṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟiṉāreṉṟu koḷka.
`uṟippa tuḷippa; `uṟippaveṉṟataṉit tuḷippa veṉṟu āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi (379) yuriyil urippar.
`puṉporuḷ ciṟumiyiyuṭiya ciṟṟiṉpap poruḷkaḷ. avi `maṇṭirivākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavu meṉpatu mutalāka mēlē kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷeṉka.
`kavaru meṉṟār, piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ koḷḷutaliṉ; iṉi `virumpu meṉṟurittalumām.
ennēramum etaṉiyēṉum neñcam caṭutiyiṟ caṭutiyil māṟi māṟi niṉittukkoṇṭiruttaliṉ, maṉaṉi `miṉpurimaṉaṉ eṉṟār. ītu toḻiluvamam. maṉaṉ, īṟṟuppōli; ē, viḷiyurupu.
`oṟṟimānakar mutalvaṉ tiruvuru eḻuvittu vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavaru maṉaṉē yeṉka.
(66-76) varampu aṟum āṟṟal nirampiya murukaṉ - aḷavu illāta aṟivāṟṟal nirampap poruntiya murukap perumāṉ, tāṉ eḻuntaruḷum vāṉkuṭil āka niṉṉit tantaṉaṉ āka - tāṉ vīṟṟiruntaruḷun tūya ciṟu vīṭāka niṉṉi eṉakkuk koṭuttāṉāka, piṉṉi - ataṉpiṉpu, maṇtirivu ākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum - maṇṇiṉ tiripukaḷākak kāṇappaṭum uṭampu mutalākiya cila poruḷkaḷum, puṉaltirivākap pukuvaṉa cilavum - nīriṉ tiripukaḷāka māṟit tōṉṟum pāl mutalākiya cila uṇavukaḷum, aṉaltirivāka aṭivaṉa cilavum - tīyiṉ tiripukaḷākap poruntum maṇiyiṉ oḷi mutalākiya cilavum, vaḷi iṭaṉākat teḷivaṉa cilavum - kāṟṟē tōṟṟuvāyākat teḷiyappaṭum maṇam mutalākiya cilavum, vicumpu iṭaṉāka ucumpuṉa cilavum - vāṉam piṟappiṭamāka iyaṅkum ici mutalākiya cilavum, ōvātu iruppa mēvuni - oḻivillāmal vantirukka virumpukiṉṟaṉi, ataṉāl tuṉpamum kavaliyum niṉpuṟaṉ āka- akkāraṇattiṉālē tuṉpamuṅ kavaliyum niṉpakkattaṉavāka, iṉpu uṟal aṟiyātu iṭarppaṭukuniyē - iṉpam nukartalaṟiyāmal nī iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉiyē, atu aṉṟu - atuvallāmal,
`kuṭil ciṟuvīṭu; atāvatu kuṭici; iṟivaṉatu elliyaṟṟa perumikku uḷḷam oru kuṭilāvatēyāyiṉum avaṉ eḻuntaruḷutaṟkēṟṟa aṉpurimi ataṟkuṇmiyiṉ, atu `vāṉkuṭilāyiṟṟu.
neñcam mey mutalāṉa aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vāyilāy maṇ mutalāṉa aimporuḷkaḷilum porunti ūṟu mutalāṉa aimpulaṉkaḷiyum nukarumeṉṟu iṅkuk kūṟiyavāṟām; ivvuṇmiyi,
  ōcinaṟ parica rūpa viratakan taṅka ḷeṉṟu
  pēcumāt tirika ḷintum piṟakkumpū tātikatti
  ṉēcavin tiriyaṅ kaṭku nikaḻaṟi vitaṉāṟ kāṇṭu
  mācicēr maṉāti taṉmāt tiripuri yaṭṭakantāṉ
(civañāṉacitti, 2-āñ cūttiram, 64) eṉṉum aruṇanti civaṉār tirumoḻiyiluṅ kāṇka.
`ucumputal iyaṅkutal; ōciyaṇukkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu unti iyaṅkic cevippulaṉāmeṉṟu iyaṟkipporuṇūlār kūṟuvar.
`kaṇṭaṉa mutaliya aintum viṉippeyarkaḷāyc `cilavum eṉpataṉoṭu paṇput tokiyāy niṉṟaṉa.
`mēvuni yeṉpatil aikāram muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu vikutiyeṉavum, nakaramey aḵtūrntu varutaṟkēṟṟa eḻuttup pēṟēṉavuṅ koḷka. `iṭarppaṭukuni yeṉpatil ivaṟṟoṭu `ku cāriyiyeṉṟaṟika. ēkāram, irakkapporuḷatu.
`nī yeṉṉuñ col mēl `iṉi nī yiṅṅaṉa moḻiyātu eṉṟa viṭattuḷḷatu. puṟam, pakkam.
`atāaṉṟu, atu aṉṟu; varumoḻi mutal nīṇṭu aḷapeṭuttup puṇarntatu;
(77-81) uṟutuṇi ākac ceṟiyum eṉṉiyum - uṉakku uṟṟa tuṇiyākap poruntum eṉṉiyum, pul cāyttu ōṭum puṉalpōla - tāṉ tuṇiyātaṟkuriya pullit taṉvaḻic cāyt tōṭum veḷḷam pōla,niṉ vaḻippaṭīi  - niṉ vaḻippaṭutti, eṉ uyirkku uyirām aṟumukattu aiyaṉi maruvaloṭṭātu - eṉ uyirkku uyirākum āṟu tirumukaṅkaḷiyuṭiya talivaṉi aṇiyavoṭṭāmal, vaṉmi ceyta niṉ puṉmiyō peritu - koṭumi ceyta niṉ ciṟumiyō mikapperitu,
puṉmi, iṅku vañcittoḻukuñ ciṟumi yuṇarttiṟṟu. ēkāramiraṇṭum aci.
`maṉaṉē! murukaṉ niṉṉip paṭitta aruṭkuṟippiṉi yaṟiyāmal iṭarppaṭukuniyē; atāaṉṟu, eṉṉiyum niṉvaḻip paṭīi aiyaṉi maruvaloṭṭātu vaṉmi ceyta niṉ puṉmiyō peritu eṉṟu iṇittukkoḷka.
(82-87) iṉi nī iṅṅaṉam oḻiyātu - iṉi nī ivvāṟu aruṭkuṟippiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal, paṉimalarkkaṭampu cūṭiya taṭam tōḷ murukaṉ - kuḷirnta malarkaḷiyuṭiya kaṭampamāliyic cūṭiya periya tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya murukapperumāṉatu, tiru urup potiyum oru kalaṉāka viḻumitiṉ nīṭu vāḻmati - tiruvuruviṉip pātukāttu vikkum iṇiyaṟṟa kalaṉākac cīruṭaṉ nīṭu vāḻvāyāka; koḻuviya nalam keḻu naṟum pāl peyta polam kalam poliyum peṟṟi eṉa - iṉiya cuviporuntiya nallamaṇamuṭiya pāli iṭṭu nirappiya poṉṉālāṉa kalamviḷaṅkun taṉmiyi yoppaveṉka.
`kaṭampu māliyi yuṇarttutaliṉ ākupeyar.
`taṭam perumip poruṭṭātalit taṭavuṅkayavum naḷiyum perumi eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāl (uri 22) aṟika.
viḻumañ cīrmiyuñ ciṟappu meṉṟu tolkāppiyaṉār (uri. 55) kūṟutaliṉ, `viḻumiti ṉeṉpataṟkuc cīruṭaṉeṉṟu uriyurikkappaṭṭatu.
`vāḻmati, mati, muṉṉiliyaci.
polam, poṉ; `poṉ ṉeṉṉuñcol īṟukeṭṭu lakaramum makaramum muṟiyē poruntip `polam eṉattirintu muṭintateṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, 61) yeṉṉum nūṟpāviṟ kaṭṭaḷi yiṭuvar.
`eṉa uvamaccol; itaṉip piṉṉuḷḷōr uvamavurupeṉṟē koṇṭaṉar. eṉṉa vikala viḻiya vetira veṉpatu taṇṭiyalaṅkāram (poruḷaṇi 33.)
`peṟṟiyeṉa orukalaṉāka vāḻmati yeṉṟu muṭikka.
eṉavē `oṟṟimānakar mutalvaṉ tiruvuru eḻuvittu vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavaru maṉaṉē yeṉṟu neñci viḷittu, `murukaṉ tāṉeḻuntaruḷum vāṉkuṭilāka niṉṉit tantaṉaṉākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum pukuvaṉa cilavum aṭivaṉa cilavum teḷivaṉa cilavum ucumpuṉa cilavum mēvuniyataṉāl tuṉpamuṅ kavaliyum niṉpuṟaṉāka iṉpuṟal aṟiyātu irppaṭukuniyē, atāaṉṟu eṉṉiyum niṉvaḻippaṭīi aiyaṉi maruvaloṭṭātu vaṉmiceyta niṉ puṉmiyō peritu eṉṟu ataṉ piḻipāṭṭucceyali yeṭuttukkūṟi, `iṉi nī iṅṅaṉam oḻiyātu naṟumpāl peyta polaṅkalam eṉa murukaṉ tiruvuruppotiyum oru kalaṉāka nīṭuvāḻmati yeṉṟu aṟivuri kiḷantārātaliṉ, itu neñcoṭu kiḷatta lāṉami kāṇka.
2. aruḷiyalurittal
`peṟṟi ariya perumāṉ eṉpati ariyapeṟṟip perumāṉ eṉa māṟi, uyarnta iyalpukaḷiyuṭiya eṉ talivaṉ eṉṟurikūṟuka; periya tiruvoṟṟi nakar vanta oḷvēlāṉ - periya tiruvoṟṟi nakarattil eḻuntaruḷiya oḷimikka vēṟpaṭiyi yuṭiyavaṉ; nal tavarum kāṇā ariya kaḻalāṉ - mikka tavamuṭiyāruṅ kāṇamāṭṭāta ariya tiruvaṭiyi yuṭiyavaṉ ākiya murukaṉ, eṉatu uḷattu nāṇātu amarntavā naṉṟu - ēḻiyēṉuḷḷattiṟ kūcātu eḻuntaruḷiya vāṟu mika naṉṟu! `ariya peṟṟipperumā ṉeṉka. ariyapeṟṟi yāvaṉa, `taṉṉurimiyaṉātal mutaliya eṭṭiyalpukaḷumām. taṉṉurimi yaṉātal, tūya vuṭampiṉaṉātal, iyaṟki yuṇarviṉaṉātal, muṟṟu muṇartal, iyalpākavē viṉikaḷi ṉīṅkutal, pēraru ḷuṭimi, muṭivilāṟṟaluṭimi, varampiliṉpa muṭimi eṉavivi.
`ariya iyalpukaḷ, vāynta tiruvoṟṟiyaṅkaṭavuḷ tavamuṭiyārāṉuṅ kāṇṭaṟkariya kaḻaluṭiyaṉāyiruntum, ēḻiyēṉuḷḷattum eḻuntaruḷiyavāṟu ā, mikanaṉṟu eṉṟu viyanturittapaṭiyām; eṉavē avaṉ aruḷiyalpurittā rāyiṟṟu.
`kāṇā, `amarntavā, eṉpavaṟṟil īṟu kuṟintaṉa.
`eṉatuḷattu meṉṟu iḻivuciṟappummi viritturittalu moṉṟu;  `naṟṟavarum eṉpataṉ ummi uyarvu ciṟappu.
`nāṇātu, `naṉṟu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ uyiriṉ tāḻviyum iṟivaṉ uyarviyum viḷakkāniṉṟaṉa.
`perumāṉ vēlāṉ kaḻalāṉ eṉṟavum, `mutalvaṉ vēlaṉ ceyyaṉ kiyaṉ periyōṉ ariyaṉ eṉṟu muṉ vantaṉavum oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟupeyark kiḷavi (tolkāppiyam kiḷaviyākkam, 42)
3. kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷavi
akal īrm kaḻikāḷ - akaṉṟa kuḷirnta kaḻikaḷē, miṉ taṅku noy ciṟivaṇṭu iṉaṅkāḷ - miṉoḷi poruntiya melliya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya vaṇṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷē, naṉṟu aṉṟu uṉakkut tiruvoṟṟi vēlari nāṭi iṭar iṉṟu iṅku nī koḷḷal eṉṟu uriyīr  -  `tiruvoṟṟi nakariluḷḷa vēluṭi murukari virumpi iṅkippoḻutu nī tuṉpaṅkoḷḷutal uṉakku nallataṉṟu’ eṉṟu colkiṉṟilīr!, iṉimēl emakku niṉṟu iṅku nīr ceyyum naṉmi eṉṉō - iṉimēl eṉakku nīṅkaḷ arukiruntu ceyyum naṉmi eṉṉavō, oṉṟum nērvatu iṉṟu - ētum uṅkaḷāl āvatu illi.`naṉṟu, peyar. `nī emakku, orumippaṉmi mayakkam. noymi, meṉmi.
ō oḻiyaci; ē īṟṟaci.
`kaḻikāḷ vaṇṭiṉaṅkāḷ uriyīreṉṟatu ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ aṟivē nāṇē meṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum (tolkāppiyam, ceyyuḷ 202). kāmam mikka uṟutuyarc collātaliṟ, kaḻikaḷiyum vaṇṭukaḷiyum viḷittu ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.
4. tōḻi viravik kūṟal
`tōḻi viravikkūṟal eṉṟatu, talivaṉatu kuṟiyirappukku matiyuṭampaṭṭa tōḻi, taṉ taliviyiyum ataṟku icivittal karuti, meṉmiyum vaṉmiyum viravikkūṟa leṉpatu, mutalil meṉmiyāṟ kūṟiyatōḻi, talimakaḷ taṉatu nāṇattāṟ kuṟi nērāmi kaṇṭu, iṅṅaṉam viravik kūṟukiṉṟāḷeṉka. tirucciṟṟampalakkōviyāruḷ (84) `viravikkūṟa leṉṟu varuntuṟikku āciriyar pērāciriyar meṉmoḻiyoṭu ciṟitu vaṉmoḻipaṭak kūṟāniṟṟal eṉṟu iṅṅaṉamē viḷakkamurittār.
(1 mutal 5 aṭi) nēr iṟi muṉki - otta varikaḷiyuṭiya muṉkikaḷiyum, vār olik kūntal - nīṇṭa taḻitta kūntaliyum, arikaṭi oḻukiya perumatar maḻikkaṇ - cevvarikaḷ muṉivariyil ōṭiya periya kaḷippupporuntiya kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum, ilavu uṟaḻceyvāy - ilaviṉ itaḻiyotta civanta vāyitaḻkaḷiyum, nilavu uṟaḻ tirunutal - eṭṭāmpiṟit tiṅkaḷippōṉṟa aḻakiya neṟṟiyiyum, koṭipurimaruṅkul - vañcikkoṭiyi nikarkkum iṭiyiṉi yum, kaṭitikaḻmēṉi - oḷiviḷaṅkum niṟamaminta uṭampiṉiyum uṭiya, karumpiṉum iṉiya - karuppañcāṟṟiṉum iṉiya iyalpiṉiyāṉa, eṉ arumpeṟal pāvāy - eṉ peṟalariya pāviyiyaṉiyāy! eṉṟatu, tōḻi taliviyiyeṉka.
`nēriṟi muṉki eṉṟatu ottavarikaḷi (irēkikaḷi) yuṭiya muṉkiyi; iṟi eṉpatu variyeṉap poruṭarutal piṟaṅkuvari poṟivari iṟiyākum eṉṉum piṅkalantiyiṟ kāṇka (āṭavarvaki). `olikūntaleṉṟatu, kūntaliṉ aṭartti kūṟiyavāṟu. olimeṉ kūntal eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcippāṭṭut (2) toṭarkku āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarurikkum uriyil ivvuṇmi kāṇka. olittal, taḻittaleṉṉum poruṭṭu. viḷaṅkumnutal ōr arivaṭṭampōṟ ṟōṉṟalāl, `nilaveṉṟaṟku īṇṭu eṭṭāmpiṟit tiṅkaḷeṉṟu urikkappaṭṭatu.
mākkaṭal naṭuvaṇ eṇṇāṭ pakkattup
  pacuveṇ ṭiṅka ṭōṉṟi yāṅkuk
  katuppayal viḷaṅkuñ ciṟunutal.
eṉṟu kuṟuntokip pāṭṭilum (129) ivviyalpu āciriyar kōpperuñcōḻarāl viḷakkamāy eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭṭiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatu.
mātariṭikku vañcikkoṭiyuvamiyātal, vañcim    maruṅkul, vañciyañcum iṭi eṉṉun tirukkōviyāriṟ (22, 32) kāṇka.
mēṉiyi niṟameṉavum uṭampeṉavum iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu koḷka. eṉṉi? niṟamamiyātu uṭampiṉkaṇ viḷakkan tikaḻātākaliṉeṉpatu.
`kiyuṅ kūntaluṅ kaṇṇum vāyum nutalum maruṅkulum mēṉiyum uṭiya pāvāyeṉa eṇṇummi virittut toṭariyittuk koḷka.
pāvāy, uruvakam; avaṟṟuḷ i īyākum aiāy ākum (tolkāppiyam, col, 4) eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāṉ itaṉkaṇ aiyīṟu āyeṉattirintu viḷiyēṟṟatu.
(6-7) vaḻivaḻic ciṟakkum em kaḻiperum kulattiṉ - talimuṟi talimuṟiyākap perumiyiṟ ciṟantuvarum ematu mikuyarnta kulattiṟpiṟanta, pālkaṭal tōṉṟiya cīrttiru aṉiyāy - tiruppāṟkaṭaliṉiṉṟum eḻunta aḻakiya tirumakaḷiyottavaḷē;
vaḻi, kālvaḻi; aṭukkup paṉmipporuḷatu.
em, taṉmippaṉmi; uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmiyaṉṟu.
`aṉiyi yeṉṉum muṉṉilimuṟṟuccol īṟutirintu viḷiyēṟṟatu.
(8-9) valampuri aṉṉa - valampuric caṅkiṉiyotta, emkulampuri kōmakaḷ - eṅkaḷ kulattōr virumpukiṉṟa kōpperuntāy, aritiṉ īṉṟa perumuttu aṉiyāy - arumiyāyp peṟṟeṭutta muttiyoppavaḷē;
valampuri, valamākac cuḻintaminta caṅku; itaṉiyē caṅkiṉaṅkaḷil mikavum uyarntateṉpa.
ilaṅkunīrp parappiṉ vaḷimīkkūṟum
  valampuri yaṉṉa vacinīṅku ciṟappiṉ.
eṉṉum perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi yaṭikaḷilum, (34-35) viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīriyuṭiya kaṭaliṭattup piṟanta caṅkil, mēlāka ulakaṅkūṟum valampuriccaṅkiyotta kuṟṟanīṅkun talimiyiṉiyum eṉṟu avvaṭikaṭku āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyarurikkum uriyilum iḵtu aṟiyaṟpālatu, iṅkum `valampuriyaṉṉa kōmaka ḷeṉpataṟku ittalimipporuḷē poruḷām. eṉavē itu paṇpuvamiyeṉka.
kulattukkēṟṟa oḻukkamuṭimiyiṟ,kōmakaḷik `kulampuri kōmaka ḷeṉṟār. kulam, ākupeyar.
kōmakaḷeṉṟatu naṟṟāyi. naṟṟāyi valampuri yeṉṟamiyiṉ, avaḷ aritiṉ īṉṟamakaḷ perumut teṉappaṭṭāḷ. ivvāṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟutal ilakkiyamarapu; itu,
tīmpāl cumantu mulivīṅkit tirumuttīṉṟa valampuripōṟ
  kāmpēr tōḷār kaḷiṟīṉṟār
eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷāṉum (muttiyilampakam, 104) aṟiyappaṭum.
muttukkaḷuḷḷum parumuttaṅkaḷē ciṟantaṉavātal parūukkāḻ āram (cilappatikāram, 4, 41) eṉṉum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tirumoḻiyilum, kimpurippakuvāyk kiḷarmuttu (cilap. 5, 150) eṉṉumiṭattuk `kiḷarmut teṉpataṟkup `parumuttu eṉa urikkum aṭiyārkku nallār uriyilum aṟiyappaṭum.
(10 - 13) miṭi aṟiyā em kuṭi eṉum muṟṟattu - vaṟumiyeṉpataṟiyāta ematu kuṭumpameṉṉum muṉṟilil, aṉpunīr pāycci - aṉpu eṉṉum nīri nāṭōṟum pāycci, iṉpuuṟakkiceytu - nīyum niṉṉik kaṇṭārum makiḻcci poruntumpaṭi oppaṉiceytu, āyamum yāṉum cīritiṉ vaḷarkkum - tōḻiyar kūṭṭamum yāṉuñ celvamāy vaḷarttuvarum, nalpayaṉkaṉinta poṉ cuṭarkkoṭiyē - nallavākiya payaṉkaḷ mutirnta poṉṉiṟamāṉa oḷimikka pūṅkoṭiyaṉiyāy;
muṟṟam, vīṭṭukku muṉṉiṭam; itaṉi `muṉṟileṉavum urippa.
nekiḻcci, iṭaṅkaṇṭavaḻi virintōṭal, aṭittum aṭipaṭāmi, uyirkaḷi vaḷarttal, īramuṭimi mutaliya otta viyalpukaḷāṉ, aṉpi nīreṉavē ellāt tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kaḷum ottu uruvakappaṭutturippavāyiṉa.  uriyāciriyar parimēlaḻakar mūṉṟām paripāṭaluriyil (65) aṉpiṉi meṉmiyeṉṟurittamiyum iṅku niṉivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
koṭikaḷukkuk kiceytalāvaṉa: koḻukoḻumpukaḷi arukaṇittu avaṟṟiṉ mēl avikaḷi eṭuttuviṭal, muṭkaḷāl nerukkuṇṭu maṭintukiṭakkum ilikaḷiyum malarkaḷiyum koḻuntu varuṅ koṭikaḷiyum nimirttiviṭal mutalāyiṉaveṉka.
`āyamum yāṉum eṉṟu taṉṉi āyattiṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟiṉāḷ, mutaṉmi karuti.
maṅkipparuvattiṟ peṇṭirkku nāṇam maṭam accam payirppu mutalāṉa iṉiya peṇmiyiyalpukaḷ viḷitaliṉ, avitāmē īṇṭut talivikku `naṟpayaṉ eṉappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
muṟṟam, nīr, payaṉ, koṭimutalāṉavellām uruvakaccoṟkaḷ.
(14-15) nakkum - cirittum, pukkum - em āyattiṭip pukuntum, mikka makiḻceyyum - emakku mikka makiḻcciyi viḷikkiṉṟa, ceyir aṟa eḻutiya uyir ōviyamē - kuṟṟam illiyāka eḻutiya uyiruṭiya ōviyamaṉiyāy.
kāṭcikku iṉitākiya ōr ōviyam nakutalum acitalu māṉa uyircceyalum uṭiyatāyiṉ, atu kaṇṭārkku mikka makiḻcciyi viḷittiṭumākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu. makiḻ, mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar.
(16-18) arumiyiṉ mikunta emperumutal tanti - arumiyiṉāṟciṟanta eṅkaḷ perumimikka mutalvaṉāṉa kōmakaṉ, āṟṟā viruppoṭum ēṟṟippārkkum - tāḻāta viruppattōṭum ēṟṟip pārkkiṉṟa, viḷivuaṟa viḷaṅkiya oḷimaṇiviḷakkē - avitaliṉṟi eṉṟum oru peṟṟittāy viḷaṅkuñ cemmaṇiyiṉ oḷivāynta viḷakkaṉiyāḷē;
kalvi, kēḷvi, celvam, celvākku, nalloḻukkam, īki, talimi, mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ piṟarellāriṉum ariyaṉāyc ciṟantavaṉeṉṟaṟku `arumiyiṉ mikunta veṉappaṭṭatu.
viḷakkileriyun tīppiḻampu cemmaṇiyiṉ vaṭivatti ottiruttaliṉ, maṇiviḷak keṉṟu uvamikkappaṭṭatu. aṉṟi aḻakiya viḷakkeṉalum ām.
viḷakkēṟṟip pārttalit talivikku urikkumpōtu, `tanti avaḷi īṉṟeṭuttukkāṇṭaleṉṟu karuttuk koḷka. ovvoru kuṭiyilum naṉmakkaḷip peṟṟeṭutta leṉpatu akkuṭikku viḷakkēṟṟi vittal pōlām eṉak koḷvatu tamiḻnāṭṭār vaḻakku. ivvuṇmi,
 varunāḷeṉ ṟumpiḻiyāt teyvap poṉṉi
	vaḷamperukka vaḷavarkulam perukkun taṅka
  ṭirunāṭu pōṟceḻiyar teṉṉāṭu viḷakkuñ
	cīrviḷakkiṉ ceyyacī ṟaṭikaḷ pōṟṟi
eṉṟu cēkkiḻār perumāṉ maṅkiyarkkaraciyāric `cīrviḷakku eṉakkūṟi makiḻum aruḷuriyiṉum naṉku peṟappaṭum.
(19-21) niṉ aruḷ nāṭi - niṉatu aruḷicceykiyi etirvirumpi, iṅkut tuṉṉi niṉṟu -  niṉṉi aṇuki niṉṟu, aṭiyēṉ kūṟum oṭiyāyc ciṟumoḻi - aṭiyēṉ colkiṉṟa kēṭillāc ciṟumoḻiyi, vaṟitueṉa oḻiyātu aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum - ciṟiyateṉṟu nīkkāmal tiruvuḷampaṟṟal vēṇṭum;
iṅku `aruḷ eṉṟatu, kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭavaḻip poṟukkum aruḷi. `tuṉṉi niṉṟiṅ keṉpatu, uḷḷattiṉ aṇukkatti uṇarttutaṟkeḻuntatu; uṭalaṇukkamum ataṉvaḻit tākaliṉ.
`oṭiyāyciṟumoḻi payaṉ viḷittaliṟ keṭāta ciṟucol; eṉṟatu poruḷuriyākumeṉṟaṟku; oṭiyā ipporuṭṭātal oṭiyāvuḷḷam eṉpataṉuriyiṟ kāṇka (paripāṭal, 2, 36). vaṟituciṟitākum (tol. uriyiyal, 40). paṇivukāṭṭal karutic ciṟumoḻiyeṉṟāḷ. `aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭu meṉṟatu, ārāytal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku; ciṟu moḻiyi ēṟṟoḻukal vēṇṭumeṉpatu kuṟippu.
aṭikaṇ muṉṉar yāṉaṭi vīḻntēṉ
  vaṭiyāk kiḷavi maṉakkoḷal vēṇṭum
eṉṉuñ cilappatikāravaṭikaḷ (13) ivvaṭikaḷōṭu ottunōkki yuṇaraṟpālaṉa.
`pāvāy, tiruvaṉiyāy, muttaṉiyāy, cuṭarkkoṭiyē, uyirōviyamē, maṇiviḷakkē, aṭiyēṉ kūṟum oṭiyāyc ciṟumoḻi aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭu meṉat toṭarpupaṭuttuka.
iṉi, mēl `vayaluḻavar mutal `viḷiyuḷ īṟākap patiṉāṉkaṭikāṟum oṟṟimānakaric ciṟappikku mukattāl, marutanilavaḷaṉ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(22- 26) vayaluḻavar kuvaḷikaṭṭu - kaḻaṉiyiluḷḷa uḻavarkaḷ kuvaḷippūṭu kaḷintu, viyalvarampil - akaṉṟa varampiṉmēl, taṭintiṭṭa kayaliṉ iṭippaṭa eṟiya - koṉṟupōṭṭa kayalmīṉkaḷiṉ naṭuvil viḻumpaṭi eṟiya, marutamakaḷ karuviḻipōl poruvu iṉṟit tiruviḷaṅka - akkuvaḷiyiṉ malarkaḷ marutanila makaḷiṉ karuviḻiyippōl oppiṉṟi aḻakuviḷaṅkavum,
 `veḷḷi veḷē leṉṟu miṉṉikkiṭakkuṅ kayal mīṉkaḷ marutamakaḷiṉ veḷviḻiyāka, avaṟṟiṭiyiṟ poruntiya kuvaḷi malarkaḷ avaṭkuk karuviḻiyāy viḷaṅkiṉaveṉka.
vayaluḻavar, - vayaṟkaṇ uḻavar.
kaṭṭal, kaḷital; eṉṟatu `kaḷikaṭṭa leṉka; kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉili iṭiniliyum viṉiyecca vikutiyumāṉa takaravukarañ cērntu, ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum (tol. eḻuttu, 150) eṉṉum puṇarcci vitippaṭi ḷakaratakaraṅkaḷ ṭakaramāy iṭitirintu `kaṭṭu eṉa muṭintami kāṇka.
viyal, uriccol; viyaleṉ kiḷavi yakalap poruṭṭē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār (uri. 68).
marutanilam, vayalum vayalcārnta iṭamumām. innilam iṅku `makaḷ eṉṟu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
(27-29) nennel vittiya poṉ uṟaḻ vāṉmuḷi - ciṉṉāṭkumuṉ vittiya cennel vitiyiṉ poṉṉokkun tūya muḷikaḷ, puri aviḻat tali virintu - muṟukkuṭiyat tāḷ virintu, pittueṉṉap parantu iruppa - paccip pacēleṉap parantu tikaḻavum; 
`vittiya cenneṉmuḷiyeṉa māṟuka.
`cennel, neṟkaḷuḷ uyarntatu; itaṉāl itaṟku `naṉṉel eṉavum oru peyaruṇṭu; itu, cenne ṉaṉṉel ceñcālip peyar eṉṉum piṅkalanti (marappeyar) yiṟ kāṇka.
`poṉ pacumpoṉ; iṅku niṟattiṟku uvami.
`pittu, pacumiyeṉṉum paṇpuppeyariṉ ṟiripu; īṟukeṭṭu muṉṉiṉṟa meytirintu taṉṉoṟṟiraṭṭi muṭintateṉak koḷka; itu pacuminiṟatti yuṇarttutal tivākarattiṟ (8) kāṇka.
(30-34) teṉoḷip paḷiṅkiṉuḷ - teḷinta oḷiyiyuṭiya oru paḷiṅkuppēḻiyuḷ, ayirpeytu - nuṇmaṇaliyiṭṭu, pāciliyum cētāmpalum kaḷ kuvaḷiyum muḷ tāmariyum - paciya ilikaḷiyuñ civanta āmpaṉ malarkaḷiyun tēṉ poruntiya kuvaḷippūkkaḷiyum muḷḷuḷḷa tāmari malarkaḷiyum, oruṅkupaṭa eḻuti maruñku vittāṅku - oṉṟāy eḻutip pakkalil vittāṟpōla, nalpūmkayaṅkaḷ poṟ poṭum imippa - aḻakiya pūkkaḷ niṟinta nīrkkuḷaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅku aḻakāy viḷaṅkavum.
paḷiṅku kuḷattiṉ teḷinīrukku uvami. `ayir nuṇmaṇaṟ poruṭṭātal `veḷḷayirkkāṉal (innūl, 1, 25) eṉpuḻik kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
pacumi ili `pāciliyeṉavuñ, cemmi āmpal `cētāmpa leṉavun tirintu puṇarntaṉa. itaṉi `īṟupōtal (naṉṉūl, pata. 9) eṉpataṉāl muṭikka.
kuḷattiṉ nīrip paḷiṅkeṉakkoṇṭu, ataṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa maṇaṟṟari `ayirpeytu eṉṉuntoṭarāṟ kaṇṇukkuk kāṇappaṭuvatāka urikkumukattāṟ kuḷattiṉ aṭimutal mēlvariyil eṭuttukkāṭṭutalē īṇṭu āciriyar karuttu. āmpal, kuvaḷi, tāmari, mutalākupeyar.

taṇṇaṭi, marutanilam; iḵtipporuṭṭātal, taliyaḷittāṉ taṇṇaṭiyun tantu eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyiṟ (2, 12) kāṇka.
`viḷiyuḷ uḷ vikutipeṟṟa toḻilaṭiyākap piṟanta peyar; peyalum viḷiyuḷum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (545).

(36-44) oṟṟimānakariṉ - tiruvoṟṟimāk kōyiliṉkaṇ, muṟpaṭa amarnta - civapirāṉeḻuntaruḷiya karuvaṟikkunērē muṉṉiṭattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭeḻuntaruḷiya, uṭku uṭi murukaṉ - teyvattaṉmiyāṟ kaṇṭār añcutaṟku uriya murukap pirāṉi, kaṇkaṇṭāṅku - kaṇṇetirē kaṇṭāleṉṉa, pārttu āṉāp payilvaṭiviṉ - kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu amiyāta akakkaṇṇetirē palakālun tōṉṟum vaṭiviṉiyum, ōrttu āṉā uraṉ uṇarviṉ - ārāyn tamiyāta ciṟanta aṟiviṉiyum uṇarcciyiṉiyum, col āṉāp palpukaḻiṉ - colliyamiyāta mikka pukaḻiṉiyum uṭiya, tāḷtoṭa vīḻnta kiyaṉ - muḻantāḷiṟ poruntumpaṭi vaḷarnta kiyiṉaṉum; tōḷtoṭu poṉñāṇ piṇitta villaṉ-  tōḷiṟporuntum poṟkayiṟukaṭṭiya villiṉaṉum; kaṇiyaṉ - avvilliṟ ṟoṭukkum ampukaḷiyuṭiyavaṉum; miṉñāṇ piṇitta kuñciyaṉ - miṉṉukiṉṟa poṉñāṇāl vaḷittukkaṭṭiya mayirmuṭiyiṉaṉum; iḷiñaṉ - kaṭṭiḷam paruvattiṉaṉum; ariyaṉ - yāvarālum aṇitaṟkariyavaṉum; periyaṉ - ataṟkēṟpap peruntakimiyuṭiyavaṉum; tiruvaḷar celvaṉ - celvaṅkaḷ ōṅkukiṉṟa niṟivuṭiyavaṉum āṉa avaṉ,
vaṭiviṉ uṇarviṟ pukaḻiṟkiyaṉ; villaṉ; kaṇiyaṉ; kuñciyaṉ; ariyaṉ; periyaṉ celvaṉ eṉa iyikka.
innūlkoṇṭa murukapperumāṉ tiruvoṟṟiyūrc civapirāṉ tirukkōyiṉ mukappil eḻuntaruḷiyiruppava ṉeṉpār, muṟpaṭavamarnta veṉṟu kiḷantār.
āṉā, īṟukeṭṭa etirmaṟip peyareccam; amiyāta veṉpatu poruḷ; amitippaṭāta veṉṟukoḷka; koliyāṉāk kūṟṟam eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli (kāñci, 13).

vaṭivu, uṇarvu, pukaḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatu virikka. iṉ, cāriyi.
`tāṭoṭa vīḻntaki eṉpatu tāṭōy taṭakki (puṟanāṉūṟu, 90) eṉṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ payiṉṟuvaralaṟika.
kiyaṉ, villaṉ mutalāyiṉa kuṟippuviṉip peyarkaḷ. `celvam niṟivu; celvameṉpatu cintiyiniṟivē yeṉṉum maṇimēkaliyaṭikaḷ iṅku niṉivukūraṟpālatu.
(45) iṉṉuṅ, kuṭiyiṉāluṅ kulattiṉāluṅ kuṟṟamoṉṟu millātavaṉ.

(46-47) peṟuvatu oṉṟu uṭiyaṉpōla - eṉ māṭṭup peṟuvatoru poruḷuṭi yāṉpōla, maṟuvantu - cuḻaṉṟu, palnāḷ eṉiṉum eṉcol nilitavaṟāṉ - ataṉip peṟutaṟkup palanāṭkaḷ kaḻintaṉavēṉum yāṉ eṉatu collāl niṟuttiya niliyiṉiṉṟun tavaṟāṉ.
maṟuvantu, maṟuvā pakuti; cuḻaṉṟu, iḵtipporuṭṭātal maṟuvantumayaṅki eṉṉum peruṅkatiyilum (33, 127), maṟuvaraṟpoḻutil eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṉuṅ (22)  kāṇka.
(48) kuṟittatu - tāṉ colluvataṟku uḷḷattiṟ kuṟittati, kiḷavātu ceṟittalum ceṟippaṉ - veḷippaṭac collāmal taṉṉuḷḷattē aṭaṅkutaluñ ceyvaṉ.
kuṟittatu, ceyappāṭṭu viṉipporuḷiṉ vanta viṉippeyar kiḷattal -vāyviṭṭuc collal; ceṟittal, uḷḷaṭakkutal; ipporuṭṭātal ceṟivaṟintu cīrmi payakkum eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (123) kāṇka. ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ eṉṟatiṟ piṉṉiṉṟatu ceyvaṉ eṉak kāriyavācaka māy niṟṟal nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruriyiṟ (tolkāppiyam, 112) kāṇka.
(49-51) urumum uḷiyum aravum piriyā - iṭiyōciyum karaṭikaḷum pāmpukaḷum viṭṭunīṅkāta, āratar nīntic cīritiṉ pōntu - koṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyik kaṭantu nalamāy vantu, tiritarum nāḷkaḷum pala - nām irukkum puṉaṅkaḷiṟṟirikiṉṟa nāḷkaḷum palavākum; uḷiyam - karaṭi; kōḷval uḷiyam eṉpatu cilappatikāram (13, 5). atar - vaḻi, āṉiṉaṅ kalitta atar eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu (138); ārkoṭumi mēṟṟu; aṉṟik kaṭattaṟkariya vaḻiyeṉalumām, nīnti, kaṭanteṉṟaṟku; āvatu kaṭantuvarutaliṉ arumiyik kuṟittatu, ummi muṟṟu.

(65-73) pāl iṭi viḻūum nīlamaṇi kaṭuppa - pāliṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟa nīlaniṟamāṉa maṇiyi oppa, ām iṭi viḻūum arum cuvikkaṉikaḷ - kuḷattu nīriṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟa mikka cuviyiṉi yuṭiya appaḻaṅkaḷi, aḷiiṭit tuñcum arumpeṭikku aḷippa - vaḷiyiṉuḷ tuyilkoṇṭirukkum arumiyuṭiya taṉ peṭṭi naṇṭukkuk koṭukkum poruṭṭu, vēppiṉ koḻunaṉi ēykkum neṭum kaṇ kavikkāl alavaṉ tāḷ iṭi iṭukki - vēmpiṉ ceḻumiyāṉa arumpiyotta nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum irupiḷavāṉa kālkaḷiyum uṭiya āṇñeṇṭu taṉ kālkaḷiṉiṭiyil iṭukkiyapaṭiyāy, aṉpuṭaṉ ceṉṟu āṅku aruntutal nōkki - aṉporu pōy avvaḷiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa taṉpeṭi naṇṭukku ūṭṭutalip pārttu, eṉṉiyum nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ-  ataṉ kātali viyantu eṉṉiyum pārttāṉ ataṉiyum pārttāṉ, uykuvatu aṟiyāṉpōlac ceykuvatu oṉṟum kāṇātu aṉṟu niṉṟaṉaṉē - uyirpiḻippataṟku vaḻi yoṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉ pōlac ceyvatoṉṟum aṟiya māṭṭātavaṉāy aṉṟu alamantu niṉṟāṉ.

(74-79) koṉ uṟu pēykaṇṭa aṉiya peṟṟiyaṉ āki - añcutal poruntiya pēyiṉip pārttāṟpōlvatoru taṉmiyaṉāy, niṟiyum aṟivum muṟimuṟi cāya - uḷatti oruvaḻi niṟuttutalum aṟivum oṉṟaṉ piṉoṉṟāyk keṭa, tuṉṟu nili nīṅkā marampōl - poruntum niliyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāta marattip pōl, kaṉṟiya nōkkamoṭu niṉṟavaṉ nili - ninta nōkkattoṭu niṉṟavaṉatu niliyi, nī kaṇṭaṉiyēl nī - pārttaṉiyāṉāl, neñcam nekkuuruki - uḷḷam uṭintu uruki, iṉ uyir vāḻali - niṉatu iṉiya uyiroṭu vāḻtal ceyyāy. avaṉ eṅṅaṉam vāḻtal ceyvāṉ eṉa oḻintaporuḷi yuṇarttaliṉ, maṉ, oḻiyicip poruḷatu; ēkāraṅkaḷiraṇṭum acikaḷ. `vāḻali etirmaṟik kaṇ vanta muṉṉili muṟṟu.

niṟi, toḻilaṭiyākap piṟanta peyar; karutiya vaḻiyellām uḷḷattic cellaviṭāmal niṟuttuta leṉpatu; kaṇṭuḻiyellām uḷḷatticcelīi uḷḷattiṉ vaḻi ōṭumākalāṉ niṟiyilaṉām eṉavaruṅ kaḷaviyaluri yilum (35) ivvuṇmi kāṇka. `tuṉṟu nili, poruntiya nili; āvatu niṉṟanili yeṉka.
`niṉṟavaṉ nili nī kaṇṭaṉiyēl uyirvāḻali maṉṉēyeṉka. 65 ām aṭimutal iv īṟṟaṭikāṟum urikkappaṭṭa ipporuḷ,

eṉṟu tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāruḷ (84) `tōḻi viravik kūṟal eṉṉum ittuṟimēlatākavē collum poruḷum oppa uṇarttappaṭṭiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatākum.
icceyyuḷi, `arumpeṟaṟpāvāy! (5) tiruvaṉiyāy (7) muttaṉiyāy (9) cuṭarkkoṭiyē (13) uyirōviyamē (15) maṇiviḷakkē (18) aṭiyēṉ kūṟuñciṟumoḻi aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum (18-21), kuvaḷi karuviḻipōl (25) tiruviḷaṅka (26), vāṉmuḷi (27) pitteṉṉap parantiruppa (29), naṟpūṅkayaṅkaḷ poṟpoṭum imippat (34) taṇṇaṭi maruviya taṇiyāviḷiyuḷ (35) oṟṟimānakariṉ (36) murukaṉik kaṇṭāṅku (37); kiyaṉ (41), villaṉ, kaṇiyaṉ (42), kuñciyaṉ, iḷiñaṉ, (43) ariyaṉ periyaṉ, tiruvaḷarcelvaṉ (44), vaṭuvoṉṟillāṉ; peṟuvatoṉṟuṭiyaṉ pōla maṟuvantu (46) eṉcoṉṉili tavaṟāṉ (47); kuṟittatu kiḷavātu ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ (48); cīritiṟ pōntu (50) tiritarunāḷkaḷum pala; orunāḷ nam (51) maliyakaṉcāral (52) ārāmattil nī vārāyākat (53)taṉicceṉṟēṉ pukuntu (54) vāviyiṉ mēviniṟpa, avaṉum āṇṭu (55) eyti eṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ (56); aṅkuk kariyiṉ ōṅki (57) nalamikap payakkum (63) nāval ukuppa (64); āmiṭiviḻūum aruñcuvik kaṉikaḷip (66) peṭikkaḷippa (67) alavaṉ iṭukkic (69) ceṉṟu aruttal nōkki (70), eṉṉiyum nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ (71), ceykuvatoṉṟuṅ kāṇātu niṉṟaṉaṉ (73); niṉṟavaṉnili (79) nī kaṇṭaṉiyēl (74), iṉṉuyirvāḻali (75); yeṉṟu viṉimuṭivu ceytukoḷka.
5. tōḻi muṉṉuṟavuṇartal
eṉṟatu, tōḻi, talivikku muṉiyaṟkippuṇarcci yuṇmiyik kuṟippāluṇartal; eṉṉi?
muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal
  iruvaru muḷvaḻi yavaṉvara vuṇartaleṉ
  ṟammūṉ ṟeṉpa tōḻik kuṇarcci
eṉpatu kaḷaviya(7)lātaliṉ, `muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal eṉpataṉi `muṉ `uṟavu `uṇartal eṉa mūṉṟu collākap pirittu, `muṉṉē talivikkun talivaṟkum uṇṭāṉa uṟavākiya iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉi yuṇartal eṉṟu poruḷurittuk koḷka; muṉṉuṟṟataṉi yuṇartalāṉē itaṟku muṉṉuṟa vuṇartaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉṟār pērāciyarum, (tirukkōviyār, 62).
niliyun tirintu niṟiyuṅ kaṭantu
  taliyuṅ kaviḻnta takiyaṇ - malinivanta
  teṉṉap poliyu meyiloṟṟic cevvēṇmēṉ
  maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭiya mātu.
(i-ḷ) malinivantatu eṉṉap poliyum - mali uyarnta taṉmittu eṉṉumpaṭi viḷaṅkukiṉṟa, eyil oṟṟic cevvēḷmēl - matilkaḷiyuṭiya tiruvoṟṟiyūril tirukkōyil koṇṭaruḷiya murukapperumāṉmēl, maṉṉum karuttuuṭiyayi mātu - nilipeṟṟa uḷḷam uṭiya talivi, niliyum tirintu - peṇmi niliyum māṟi, niṟiyum kaṭantu - maṉattuḷḷa maṟiporuḷiyun taṉvayamiṉṟiyē veḷiviṭṭuc colli, taliyum kaviḻnta takiyaḷ - piṉ atu colliṉami niṉintu nāṇattāl taliyuṅ kuṉinta taṉmiyaḷāvaḷ.
cevvēṇmēṉ maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭiyamātu taliyuṅ kaviḻnta takiyaḷeṉka.
nilitiritalāvatu, peṇmiyeṉappaṭum nāṇam maṭa maccam payirppukaḷ taṉvaya miṉṟiyē māṟutal.
niṟi, uḷḷattuḷḷa maṟiviṉip pulappaṭātu niṟuttutaleṉpatu.
talivi, maṟiviloḻukiya kaḷavoḻukkattāl niliyum niṟiyuṅkaṭantu nāṇmikkut taliyuṅ kaviḻntatakiya ḷāyiṉāḷeṉka.
ummikaḷ, eccam.
mali, uvami; nivanta, uyarnta; ōṅkiya vorukuṭi yurukeḻu matiyiṉivantu eṉpar āciriyar kōvūrkiḻārum (puṟanāṉūṟu, 31).
mēl: ēḻāmvēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷatu.
`maṉṉum nilipēṟuṇarttum maṉṉeṉṉum iṭic  collaṭiyākap piṟanta ceyyu meṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccaviṉi (5).
6. pirivāṟṟāta talivikkut tōḻi kūṟal
mātē peritum varuntuva teṉṉi maṉantirita
lētē yaṟiyiṟ koṭumiyaṉ ṟōvaṉṉi yīrmpotumpiṟ
cūtēy tuṇimuli yākan taḻuvit tuyarmikutta
pōtēr poḻiloṟṟic cevvēḷ variyap pukuntaṉaṉē
(i-ḷ) mātē - eṉ arumit talimakaḷē, peritum varuntuvatu eṉṉi - nī mikavun tuṉpuṟuvatu eṉ, maṉam tirital ētu aṉṉi aṟiyiṉ koṭumiyaṉṟō - niṉatu neñcan tiritaṟkāṉa ētuvittāy aṟiyumāṉāl tīṅku nērumaṉṟō, īrm potumpil - kuḷirnta poḻilil, cūtu ēy tuṇimuli ākam taḻuvit tuyarmikutta - cūtukkāykaḷi māṟupaṭukkuṅ koṅkikaḷ neruṅkiya niṉmārpi aṇintu ittuṉpatti viḷitta, pōtu ērpoḻil oṟṟiccevvēḷ - malarkaḷiṉ aḻaku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa cōlikaḷ cūḻnta tiruvoṟṟip perumāṉ, variyap pukuntaṉaṉ - varitaṟku vantuviṭṭaṉaṉ; eṉavē nī varuntaṟka veṉpatu.
maṉan tiritalētu, ētup poruḷiṉ varum nāṉkaṉurupum payaṉum virittukkoḷka.
ōkāram, viṉā; ītu aṟiporuḷ viṉāviṉ kaṇaṭaṅkum; iḷam pūraṇar iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟi `aṟivoppukkāṇṭal viṉā eṉpar. ivviyalpukaḷellām ceppum viṉāvum vaḻāa lōmpal eṉṉun tolkāppiyac collatikāra nūṟpā (13) vukkuppōnta iḷampūraṇar cēṉāvariyaruri kaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷḷappaṭum.
aṉṉi, īṇṭu naṟṟāy; aṉṟic cevilittāy eṉiṉum avariruvareṉiṉum amiyum.
potumpeṉappaṭṭatu, maracceṟivu mikka cōliyātaliṉ; īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum ākantaḻuviya ceyti kuṟiyiṭattu nikaḻcciyi yuṇarttukiṉṟamiyāṉ, annikaḻccik kēṟpap potumpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. ip potumpiṉiyalpi nallicippulami vāynta āciriyar nakkīraṉār.
cantaṉamuñ caṇpakamuntēmāvun tīmpalavum āciṉiyum acōkuṅ kōṅkum vēṅkiyuṅ kuravamum virintu, nākamun tilakamum naṟavum nantiyum mātaviyum mallikiyum muvaloṭu maṇaṅkamaḻntu, pātiriyum pāvi ñāḻalum piṅ koṉṟiyoṭu piṇiyaviḻnta porippuṉkum puṉṉākamum murukkoṭu mukiciṟantu, vaṇṭaṟintu, tēṉārntu, varikkuyilka ḷicipāṭat taṇṭeṉṟal iṭivirāyt taṉiyavari muṉivuceyyum poḻil eṉṟum,
vaṇṭun tumpiyum varikkaṭip piracamuṅ koṇṭupuṇar narampeṉṟu muraṉṟu, aintu vāyilāṉuṅ koḷḷappaṭun tuppuraviṉit taṉṉakattuṭittāyp palvakippaṭṭa maraṅkaḷāṉum polivuṭittāyp, puṟattār akattārik kāṇpataritāy akattārpuṟattārik kāṇpateḷitāy viḻivu viṭutta viḻumiyōriyum viḻivu tōṟṟuvikkum paṇpiṟṟu (iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ, 2, 18). eṉṟum mika aḻakāka urittu viḷakkukiṉṟami iṅkup peritum niṉivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum.
ēy, uvamavurupu; kañcukamēykkuṅ kaṉaṅkuḻiyē (tirukkōviyār, 15) eṉpatiṟpōla.
`pōtu kālavākupeyarāy malari yuṇarttiṟṟu.
`pukuntaṉa ṉeṉṟatu, iṉippukuvāṉi; iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāḷ, pukutaliṉ virivu uṇarttit talimakaḷit tēṟṟutaṟporuṭṭu; viriviṉkuṟipput tōṉṟu iṟanta kālattiṉ vittu ivvāṟu kiḷattalellām vārākkālattu (tolkāppiyam, viṉiyiyal, 46) meṉpataṉāṉ muṭikkappaṭum.
ēkāraṅkaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ muṉṉatu viḷiyeṉavum ēṉiya acikaḷeṉavuṅ koḷka



7. iṉanalaṉurittal
aṭikaḷ, tamakkuc camayanūluṇmikaḷ aṟivuṟutta camayāciriyariyē `iṉanalaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, avaratu cārpāl tamakkut tiruvaruḷuṇeṟicārum vāyil vāyttami itaṉkaṇ urit taruḷukiṉṟār.                                            
(1-2) pukal aru nūḻiyil - pukutal arumiyāṉa ciṟiya nuḻivāyilil, pōkku arum poruḷpōl - nuḻittal arumi yāṉa periya poruḷippōl, ikal aṟu civat tiṟam celā neñciṉar - māṟupāṭaṟṟa civakkōṭpāṭu kaḷ ēṟāta neñciṉiyuṭiyavarum;
`pukalum, pōkkum toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ; ikal, toḻilāku peyar; nuḻiyum vāyil `nūḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu; ipporuṭṭātal piṅkalanti (4) yiṟkāṇka.
civacamayam ēṉic camayaṅkaḷoṭu māṟupaṭāmal, aviyaṉittiyun taṉakku uṟuppukkaḷākakkoṇṭu avaṟṟoṭu poruntiyamitaliṉ, `ikalaṟucivam eṉap paṭṭatu; ivvariya uṇmi,
 ōtucama yaṅkaḷporu ḷuṇaru nūlkaḷ
	oṉṟōṭoṉ ṟovvāma luḷapalavu mivaṟṟuḷ
  yātucama yamporuṇūl yātiṅ keṉṉi
	ṉituvāku matuvalla teṉumpiṇakka tiṉṟi
  nītiyiṉā ṉiviyellā mōriṭattē kāṇa
	niṉṟatiyā torucamaya matucamayam poruṇū
  lātaliṉā liviyellā marumaṟiyā kamattē
	yaṭaṅkiyiṭu maviyiraṇṭu maraṉaṭikkī ḻaṭaṅkum
eṉac civañāṉacittiyā (8,13) reṉṉuñ civacamaya nūlil teḷivāy eṭuttōtappaṭutal kāṇka. ītuṇaramāṭṭāp piṟacamayattār civacamayan tattañ camayaṅkaṭkumāṟāvateṉṟu piḻipaṭakkaruti, ataṉi ēlātoḻivarākaliṉ, avar karutumāṟupōṟ civacamayam attaṉmiya taṉṟeṉṟu iṅkut teḷivāy uṇarttiyavāṟu.
`tiṟa meṉṉuñ col īṇṭuc civacamayattiṉ poruṭ kūṟupāṭu kuṟittu niṉṟatu; tiṟam - kūṟupāṭu; niṟṟiṟañciṟakka eṉṟār puṟattiṉum (6).
`celā neñciṉa reṉṟatu, nuṇporuḷ cellun takutiyaṟṟa parupporuṇiṉiviṉi yuṭiyā reṉṟaṟku.
(3-4) neṟippaṭu makkaṭku - vaḻiccellum makkaṭku, aṟivu pētuṟukkum - avaraṟivi mayaṅkac ceykiṉṟa, kavaliyiṉ - kavartta vaḻiyippōl, piṟari mayakkunar - piṟari mayaṅkac ceykiṉṟavarum; 
`neṟippaṭumakkaḷ eṉṉuṅ kuṟippuc civaneṟiyiloḻukā niṟkuñ civa naṉ makkaḷik kuṟittatu; piṟari eṉṟatum avariyē; ītu atikārattāṟ peṟappaṭum. `kavali yeṉṟatu īṇṭuc camaṇ camayattiṟkuvami. mēlvaruvaṉavum aṉṉa.
`pētuṟuttal aṟivi mayaṅkacceytal.
`mayakkunar eṉpatil nakaravoṟṟup peyariṭi nili.
(4-5) uval iṭu patukkiyiṉ - taḻikaḷ peytu mūṭappaṭṭa kaṟkuviyalpōl, puṉporuḷ akattu iṭu karaviṉar - iḻintakaruttukkaḷi neñciṉuḷ iṭṭuvitta kaḷḷa muḷḷavarumāṉa,
`uval taḻiyeṉṉup poruṭṭātal puṟanāṉūṟiṟ kāṇka (262).
`patukki, maṟippiṭam; patukkivittaliṉ vanta toḻilāku peyar; vaḻippaṟikārar tammāṟ kollap paṭṭārmēṟ kaṟkaḷik kuvittu akkuviyalmēl taḻi peytu mūṭivikkum uṇmi,
centoṭi piḻiyā vaṉkaṇ āṭavar
  ampupaṭa vīḻntōr vampap patukki
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟ (3) ṟiṉālum peṟappaṭum; patukki, akattukku uvami.
poruḷeṉṟatu, karuttu; niṟpeṟu poruḷil eṉṟu mēl (39) varutaluṅkāṇka.
(6-13) puṉimāṇkōti poruntac cūṭṭiyum - puṉital māṭcimippaṭṭa pūmāliyik kūntalukkup poruntumāṟu cūṭṭiyum, viṉimāṇ viḷaṅku iḻi vīṟupaṭat tiruttiyum - toḻil nalañ ciṟanta oḷiviḷaṅkum maṇikaḷiḻitta aṇikaḷit taṉicciṟappuṇṭākat tiruttamāy aṇintum, koḻumkayal maḻikkaṇ piṟaḻtoṟum maṉamtirintu - koḻuviya kayal mīṉkaḷiyotta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ puraḷuntōṟum aṟivu mayaṅki, ceḻumtuvarccevvāy muttam koṇṭu - ceḻuviya pavaḷampōṉṟa civanta vāyiṉ muttam peṟṟu, nacipiṟakkiṭātu - vēṭki piṟpaṭāmal, mulitaḻīic cellum neñcu amarkātalar piṉ celum maṭavār - muliyaṇittup pirintu piṟaku muṉṉē cellum neñcuvirumpiya tam kātalariṉ piṉṉē uṭaṉ cellum makaḷiruṭiya, pañciṉmēl aṭi - pañcip pōṉṟa melliya aṭikaḷ, pital uṟṟu uḻappa - tuṉpam aṭintu varuntumpaṭi, uṟuttuk kiḻikkum paral keḻu murampiṉ - kuttik kīṟum parukkik kaṟkaḷ nirampiya mēṭṭu nilattippōl;
puṉi, mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar.
naci, iṅku vēṭki; nannaci vāyppa eṉṉum akanāṉūṟ (22) ṟuriyil iḵtip poruṭṭātal kāṇka.
`neñcamar kātalar eṉpatu uḷḷamvirumpiya kātalari; mukattāṉ amarntiṉitunōkki eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (93) pōla.
`pañciṉ, murampiṉ eṉṉum aintaṉurupukaḷ oppup poruḷaṉa.
pital, tuṉpam; patimaruṇṭu pitaluḻakkum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (12-29).
talivaṉ taṉ taliviyip piriyuṅkāl, avaṉ avaṭkuk kōticūṭṭi, iḻitirutti, mulitaḻīic cellum ivviyalpukaḷellām. valimuṉpiṉ valleṉṟa veṉṉum pālikkaliyiṉ naṉkeṭutturikkap paṭutal kāṇka.
paralkeḻu murampu maṭavāraṭikaḷi varuttu miyalpiṉatātal,
oḷicceñ cīṟaṭi yurukkarak kēyppa
  uḷittali vempara lūṉṟupu naliya
veṉavarum peruṅkati (53: 163-4) yaṭikaḷāl naṉkuṇarappaṭum.
(14-23) puli ataṉ uṭukki tii-puliyiṉ tōlākiya uṭiyiṉi uṭuttu, valikeḻu mūili vaṭivēl oruvayiṉ viḷaṅka - valimiporuntiya mūṉṟu ilikaḷip pōṉṟa vaṭivaminta kūrvēl orupakkattē tōṉṟa, koṉṟi naṉi piṇitta maṉṟal amtoṭali - koṉṟi mukikaḷit toṭuttukkaṭṭiya maṇam poruntiya aḻakiyamāli, pavaḷamali uṟaḻum mārpil tuvaḷa - pavaḷaṅ kaḷālāṉa maliyi okkum civantamārpiṟ puraṇṭaciya, malitaru maṭanti kuṟaṅkiṉ mici iruntu - maliyaracaṉ īṉṟeṭutta umippirāṭṭiyār tamatu toṭiyiṉmēl amarntu, valampaṭukiyāl perumpuṟam kavii -valimi poruntiya valakkiyāl akaṉṟa mutukittaḻuvi, ulampaṭu tiraḷtōḷ orumuli oṟṟa - uruṇṭa kallukku nikarāṉa tiraṇṭatōḷi orupakkattuk koṅkiyāl ñemuṅka, nari ēṟu ūrnta puricāltōṟṟam niṉitoṟum - veṇṇiṟamāṉa viṭiyiṉ mēl ēṟiya uyarvumikka civapirāṉatu tōṟṟatti eṇṇu toṟum, kuḻiyum uravōrkku neñcurukum aṟiñarāṉa civarkku, vikalum kēṭupuri neñciṉ pīṭuilā amaṇar - nāḷun tīmiceyyum neñciyuṭiya perumiyillāta camaṇarkaḷ;
ataḷ, tōl; uṭukki toḻilākupeyar.
iti, aḷapeṭi iciniṟittaṟporuṭṭāy vantatu; īkikkaṇṇiyilaṅkat tii eṉpuḻip (puṟanāṉūṟu, 353) pōla.
`valampaṭukiyāṟ perumpuṟaṅ kavii yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippāl umiyammiyār civapirāṉatu iṭappakkattut toṭimici amarntiruttal uṇarappaṭum.
`ulampaṭutiraṭōḷ eṉṉum vaḻakku ulantarutōḷiṉāy  eṉac cīvakacintāmaṇiyilum (nāmaka, 175) varutal kāṇka.
`puri īṇṭu uyarviṉmēlatu; aracuvīṟṟiruntu puriyōrp pēṇi (25: 81) eṉpatu maṇimēkali.
`neñciṉar, mayakkunar, karaviṉar, camaṇar eṉat toṭarntu koḷka.
`paralkeḻu murampiṉ (13) kēṭupurineñciṟ pīṭilāvamaṇar eṉa īṇṭuk kūṭṭiyurittukkoḷka.
(24-29) pīṭu uyar centamiḻk kūṭalil tōṉṟi - perumimikka centamiḻmoḻippayiṟci viḷaṅkiya maturimānakariṟ putitu vantu, poypaṭu puṉporuḷ kāṉṟu iruḷparappi - poymi nirampiya tam pulliya camaṇ kōṭpāṭukaḷik kakki eṅkum iruḷipparappi, meytaru civa nalneṟi piḻippa - meymiporuntiya civam eṉappaṭuma nalvaḻitavaṟi, māṇāvaḻippaṭūum kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ mutal - ciṟavāta tīyavaḻiyil akappaṭuṅkūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ mutalāṉa, āṟucel mākkaḷic cūṟikoṇṭu eṟintu - vaḻiccelluma māntari vaḻipaṟittu ūṟuceytu, perumtuyar uṟukkuṅkāli - mikka tuṉpatti viḷikkumpoḻutu;
kūṭalil tamiḻccaṅkaṅkaḷ niṟiip pulavarperumakkaḷ tamiḻārāyntu vantaṉarākaliṟ `pīṭuyar centamiḻkkūṭal eṉṟu maturi ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu. maturikku uriya ēṉic ciṟappukaḷiṉun tamiḻārāyntaciṟappē ataṟkup peruñ ciṟappākum. kūṭaṉmānakaril tamiḻārāynta perumiyi māṇikkavācakapperumāṉum uyarmatiṟ kūṭaliṉāynta oṇṭīntamiḻ (tirukkōviyār, 20) eṉṟaruḷic ceytal kāṇka.
camaṇkōṭpāṭukaḷ `poypaṭu puṇporu ḷeṉappaṭṭaṉa, avi kaṭavuṭkōṭpāṭṭiṉ vaḻippaṭāmi yiṉ; aṅṅaṉamē civa camayamum, `meytaruciva meṉap paṭṭatu; eṉṉi, atu kaṭavuṭ kōṭpāṭṭiyē taṉakku uyireṉakkoṇṭu niṟṟaliṉeṉpatu.
camaṇari muṉ `murampu eṉṟurittamiyiṟ, civacamayaṅkaḷ `āṟuceṉmākka ḷeṉavum, avarkaḷ civamoḻukutalit taṭiceytu neṟiyallā neṟipukutti ūṟuceytal `iruḷparappi neṟipiḻippac cūṟinoṇ ṭeṟita leṉavum ēṟṟapeṟṟi iṅku uruvakañceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi `murampiṉ (13) tuyaruṟukkuṅkāli (29) yeṉa viṉimuṭivuceytal okkumeṉiṉ, aḵtamiyātu; ēṉeṉṟāl, `piṉcelu maṭavār (11) kuḻiyumuravōrkku (22) uvami yātaliṉeṉpatu.
(29-36) aruntavam niṉpeṟuporuḷiṉ palpakal āṟṟi - niṉṉittamakku makavākappeṟṟeṭukkum nōkkattāl ariyatavattic `civapātavirutayar palanāḷum iyaṟṟi, vēṇṭiya periyōrkku vēṇṭiyāṅku aḷippa - virumpiya cāṉṟōrkku avar virumpiyavāṟē vēṇṭuvaṉa īya, karukkuḻikkiṭakkum taṉmiyi aṉmiyiṉ - karuppi eṉṉuṅ kuḻiyiṟkiṭantu varuntum iyalpuṭiyi alliyākaliṉ, uruppeṟa varukuni pōla - uruvuṇṭāka immaṇṇulakil eḻuntaruḷuvōypōla avar akattu oru perumāyam perukuṟacceytu - avar māṭṭuk karuvilamarntāṟpōl oruperiya māyacceykiyi mikavañ ceytukāṭṭi, puṟam peyarntu eyti - avar maṭiyakattiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu vantu, aṟam taru perumic caṇpiyil tōṉṟiṉi - aṟattip piṟarkku aruḷum perumiyiṉi yuṭiya caṇpiyeṉṉun tiruppatiyil tōṉṟiyaruḷiṉi;
pakal, nāḷ; āṟṟalōṭu iyaṟṟutaliṉ; `āṟṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu. `ava reṉṟatu, iṅkuc `civapātavirutayarum avartaṅ kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta kātaṉ maṉiviyār pakavatiyārumeṉka.
viṉiyiṉīṅkiya muḻumutaliṟivaṉāṉa murukapperumāṉ piṟaviyuṭpaṭum iyalpiṉaṉ allaṉākaliṟ, pakavatiyār maṇivayiṟṟiṟ karuttaṅki uruppeṟṟatu īṇṭu orumāyamākak kiḷanturikkappaṭṭatu. ellām valla murukapperumāṉē tiruñāṉacampantarāyp piṟantaruḷiṉāreṉpatu purāṇavaḻakku.
 pūtala mataṉiṟ kāḻimā nakariṟ
	puṇṭarī kaṉṟaṭat torucār
  mātavam purivōṉ muṉṉiḷaṅ katirpōṉ
	mataliyāy vantaṉaṉ kantaṉ
eṉṟu cīkāḻit talapurāṇam `tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār tiruvavatāra vattiyāyattil (6) iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟutal kāṇka.
`puṟampeyarn teṉpatu, īṇṭuppiṟattal.
`caṇpi yeṉṟatu `cīkāḻiyi, cīkāḻikkuriya paṉṉiraṇṭu tiruppeyarkaḷiṟ `caṇpi yeṉpatum oṉṟātal caṇpinakar vaḷarkāḻi (tiruñāṉacampantar purāṇam, 14) tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattālaṟiyappaṭum.
`mātu, ō acikaḷ.
(36 - 40) piṉpu - aṅṅaṉantōṉṟiya piṉṉar mūṉṟāmāṇṭi lorunāḷ, kiṇkiṇi olippat tantipiṉ ceṉṟu - cataṅki oli ceyyat tam tantiyiṉpiṉṉē pōy, taṇ kaḻunīrum cen tāmariyum oruṅku talimayaṅkiya perum taṭamaruṅkiṉ - kuḷirnta kaḻunīr malarum civanta tāmarimalarum oṉṟāykkalantu viḷaṅkiya akaṉṟa tirukkuḷattiṉ karippakkattil, arumpeṟal tantiyit talikkūṭiṉi - peṟalariya tantiyiṉi etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyaruḷiṉi.
`kiṇkiṇi īraṭukkolikkuṟippiṉāl vanta kāraṇap peyar.
`oruṅku talimayaṅkuta lāvatu, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu pirivu tōṉṟāmaṟ kalattal.
taṭamaruṅku, kari; maruṅkiṉkaṇ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu maruṅki ṉeṉaccāriyiniṟka urupukeṭṭatu.
tantiyeṉṟatu, īṇṭu iṟivaṉi; tiruñāṉacampantari murukappirāṉeṉ ṟurittaliṉ, avarkkuc civapirāṉ tanti yeṉappaṭṭār; mēlum iṅṅaṉamē koḷka.
`talikkūṭutal, eṉpatu ōriṭattu oruṅkukūṭutal.
(40-48) ataṟpuṟam ataṉpiṟaku, muruntuuṟaḻ veḷnakip perumpeyar aṉṉi- mayil iṟakiṉaṭimuḷḷippōlum veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya periya pukaḻmikka tiruvaruṭṭāyāṉa umip pirāṭṭiyār, karumpumkaṉiyum perumcuvippālum tiruntiyatēṉum oruṅkuṟakkūṭṭi - karuppañcāṟum paḻaccāṟum mikkacuviporuntiya āviṉpālun teḷintatēṉum oṉṟāy viravaccērttu, kuḻittu eṭuttu aṉṉa - iḷakupatamāyk kāycciyeṭuttāṟpōla, viḻivuaṟā marapiṉ mulipoḻi amiḻtam vaḷḷattu ūṭṭa - vēṭkitīrāta cuvit taṉmiyiṉiyuṭiya tamatu tirumuliyiṟ curantoḻukiya pālip pirāṭṭiyār poṟkiṇṇattiṉāl ūṭṭa, pavaḷavāy  maṭuttut tivaḷ oḷi ciṟantu - tamatu pavaḷampōṉṟa cevvāyiṉāl uṭkoṇṭu aciyum oḷiyāl mikuntu, paḻakuṟu tantikku maḻaviṭi amarnta tantiyit tāyoṭumkāṭṭi - paḻakutal poruntiya tantiyārāṉa civapātavirutayarukku iḷiya ēṟṟiṉmēl eḻuntaruḷivanta uriyatantiyārāṉa civaperumāṉi ammiyoṭuñ cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyaruḷi;
muruntu, paṟkaḷiṉ veṇmikkuṅ kūrmikkuñ ciṟumikkum uvami; iḵtivvāṟu uvamikkappaṭṭuvarutal, muruntēriḷanaki (tuyileḻup, 88) eṉṉum maṇimēkali yāṉum, muruntēykku muṭpōleyiṟṟiṉāy eṉṉum ēlāti (8) yāṉum aṟiyappaṭum.
`peyar, iṅkup pukaḻ; perumpeyart talittāṇ maṉperuñciṟappiṉ mānitikkiḻavaṉ eṉṉuñ cilappati kārattiṟ (16: 84) pōlaveṉpatu.
kuḻitteṭuttalāvatu, kuḻiyakkāycci yeṭuttaleṉka.
`vāymaṭuttal, uṇṇutal; matiyuṇaramakaḷeṉa vāmpal vāymaṭuppa veṉṉum paripāṭa laṭiyiṉum (78) ipporuṇmi teḷiyappaṭum.
`tivaḷoḷi, aciyum oḷi; nilantivaḷa eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇi (3022) yāṟpeṟappaṭum.
civapātavirutayar paḻakuṟu tantiyeṉappaṭṭār, avar ciṟitu kālam maṭṭumē tantiyeṉa uṟavu payiṟaliṉ; aṭikaḷ civaperumāṉi vāḷāt tantiyeṉak kuṟittal koṇṭu, avarē tiruñāṉa campantarkku urimit tantiyārāvareṉpatu iṉituṇarappaṭum.
(48-51) piṉṟi - piṉpu, kāṇtaku - tiruviḷiyāṭal - kāṇṭaṟkuttakka ceyaṟ karuñceyalkaḷi, māṇtaka - māṭcimiyuṇṭāka, ceytiṟam aṟiyār uytiṟamnōkki - tavañceytaṟkuriya vakikaḷit teriyātavarkaḷ uyyum vakikaruti, palpakal iyaṟṟiyapiṉṉar - paṟpalanāṭkaḷ ceytaruḷiyapiṉ;
kāṇ, mutaṉilittoḻiṟpeyar; tiruñāṉacampantar ceytaruḷiya tiruviḷiyāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum aṟivāṉ aḷantukāṇṭaṟkuriya arumiperumi vāyntaṉavā meṉṟaṟkuk `kāṇṭaku veṉa aṭikoṭuttōtappaṭṭatu. kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṟ kaṇṇaki yeṉṉuñ cilappatika rattukku (2, 90) urikārar aṭiyārkku nallār `kāṭcitakuñ ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇaki yeṉṟurittatūum iṅku uṟṟunōkkaṟ pālatākum.
piḷḷiyar ceyta tiruvaruṭceyalkaḷ piṟarkkellāñ ceyaṟkaruñ ceyalkaḷēyāyiṉum, avarkkavi eḷiyavātal paṟṟi, aṭikaḷ avaṟṟit `tiruviḷiyāṭa leṉac cuṭṭi makiḻntār. aviyāvaṉa; `poṟṟāḷam peṟṟami, muttuccivikiyum muttukkuṭiyum muttucciṉṉaṅkaḷum peṟṟami, kollimaḻavaṉ putalvikku muyalakanōy tīrttami, kuḷirkāyccal nīkkiyaruḷiṉami, muttuppantar niḻaṟṟap peṟṟami, āyirampoṉ aṭintami, vaṇikaṉukkup pāmpiṉnañcu tīrttami, vācitīrak kācu peṟṟami, tirumaṟikkatavu aṭittaruḷiṉami mutaliyaṉa. ivaṟṟi yellān tiruñāṉacampantarvaralāṟu kūṟun tiruntoṇṭar purāṇattuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
`uytiṟam eṉṟaviṭattut tiṟam vaki eṉṉumporuṭṭu.
(51-53) muṉpaṭarntu - muṉvirintuceṉṟu, aruntamiḻ āynta perumtamiḻkkūṭal - ariya centamiḻc caṅka miruntu ārāynta periya tamiḻvaḷam vāynta maturiyiṉkaṇ ṇirunta, kulacciṟi muṉivaṉ etirkoḷacceṉṟu - kulacciṟiyeṉṉun tavamuṭiyōṉ tammi etirkoṇṭaḻikka aṅku eḻuntaruḷi;
muṟpaṭarntu, muṟceṉṟu; eṉṟatu, nāḷuṅkōḷum nalla vallaveṉa apparperumāṉ taṭuttun, tiruñāṉacampantar, tiruvaruṭṭuṇi iruppiṉ aṭiyārkku ellām nallaṉavē yāmeṉak kūṟip pāṇṭināṭu ceṉṟami kuṟittateṉka.
kūṭaṉ mānakarkkuc centamiḻaṭikoṭuttuc ciṟappikkumuṇmi `pīṭuyar centamiḻkkūṭa leṉpuḻi (24) yurikkappaṭṭatu.
`tamiḻkkūṭa leṉṟu mīṇṭun tamiḻaṭikoṭuttatu, tamiḻvaḷarccikkuk kūṭaṉmānakarē urittāyiruntami valiyuṟuttaṟku. aṉṟiyun, tamiḻnāṭṭunakarkaḷuḷ ellāc ciṟappukaḷālun talimi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉamiyāṉum cāṉṟōrkaḷāṉiyaṉṟa centamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ nirampa uṭimiyāṉun, tiruñāṉacampantar tēvāram mutaliya tamiḻvētaṅkaḷ taṉmāṭṭē arunikaḻccikaḷ vāyilāy meyppikkappaṭṭamiyāṉum `peruntamiḻkkūṭal eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.
kulacciṟi muṉivaṉ, pāṇṭimaṉṉaṟku amiccar.
(54-62) pālkaṭalpiṟanta cīrttiru ākaliṉ - tiruppāṟ kaṭaliltōṉṟiya pukaḻporuntiya tirumakaḷātaliṉ, irumulikkuṭattiṉ oruvaḻi aṭakki - appāṟkaṭalirunta pāl muḻumiyum taṉ iraṇṭu koṅkikaḷeṉṉuṅ kuṭaṅkaḷil orumikka aṭakkivittu, malimakaḷ ātaliṉ mulitaral curuṅka vaḷḷattu ēntiya cilamutu arunti āṉāvēṭkiyiṉ varuvōṉ ivaṉeṉa mulimukamtiṟantavaḻi - cīkāḻiyiṉkaṇ tirumulippālūṭṭiya umiyammiyār maliyaracaṉ putalviyākaliṉ avartam tirumulikaḷiṟpāṟ curappukkuṟiya avvāṟṟāl avaṟṟiṉkaṇ iruntavariyil oru poṟ kiṇṇattiṟ kaṟanteṭutta ciṟitāṉapālip paruki ataṉāl aṭaṅkāta peruvēṭkiyuṭaṉ varukiṉṟavaṉ ivaṉeṉṟu taṉmulikaḷiṉ kāmpunuṉikaḷ vāytiṟanta viṭattu, vaḻippōkkiya pālkaṭal ūṭṭiya pavaḷac cevvāyt teṉṉavaṉ tēvikkum - attiṟantavaḻiyē yoḻukac ceytu tāṉ aṭakkivittirunta pāṟkaṭalmuḻumiyum ūṭṭiya pavaḷatti yotta civanta vāyitaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉikkiḻattiyārāṉa maṅkiyarkkaraciyārukkum, poṉpōl putalvaṉ āyiṉi eṉpatu aṟiyatteruṭṭi - poṉṉip pōṉṟa ariya putalvaṉāyiṉi yeṉpati ellārum aṟiyumpaṭi teḷivittaruḷi.
tirumāliṉ kūṟāṉa aracaṉ ṟēviyākaliṉ, maṅkiyark karaciyār `tiru veṉa uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭār.
pāṟkaṭaliṟ piṟantamiyiṉ araciyārkkup pāl mikuntiruntateṉavum, maliyakattup piṟantamiyāṉ umiammiyārkku atu kuṟintirunta teṉavum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟintiruntamiyāl ammiyār tammakattirunta acciṟitaḷavu pāliṉiyē vaḷḷattēnti yūṭṭiṉāreṉavum, avvāṟṟāl tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār pāluṇṇum vēṭki taṇiyārāy avāmikkup pōntāreṉavum, atukaṇṭu tiru mulippālniṟinta pāṇṭimātēviyār mulimukantiṟantu tāṉē perukkeṭuttu vaḻintapāli avarkkuvēṇṭumaḷavum ūṭṭi vēṭki taṇivittā reṉavum, ataṉāṟpiḷḷiyār avvaraciyārkkum poṉpōlum putalvarāyiṉāreṉavum āciriyar tiṟampaṭak kaṟpittuk kūṟiṉami nuṇmāṇuḻipula mikka pulavaraṉivarāṉum peritumpārāṭṭi makiḻaṟpālatākum. maṅkiyarkkaraciyārkkut tirumulicuranta uṇmi,

`cillamutu ciṟitaḷavāṉa amuteṉaka; tēṉeṉap pāleṉac cillamir tūṟṟeṉa eṉṉuñ cīvakacintāmaṇi yiṉum (nāmaka, 163) iḵtipporuṭṭāy varutal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
`vēṭkiyiṉ eṉṟaviṭattu, iṉ oṭupporuḷatu;
`poṉpōṟ putalvaṉ; eṉṟatu, poṉṉippōṟ pōṟṟappaṭum putalvaṉeṉṟaṟku.
`ceṉṟu, teruṭṭi yeṉattoṭuttukkoḷka.
(63-65) vaḻutikoṇṭa muḻunōy tīra - pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭumāṟaṉ aṭinta nōy muḻumiyun tīrumporuṭṭu, aṉṉi ūṭṭiya amiḻti - umiyammiyār cīkāḻiyil uṇpitta tiruvaruṭpāli, iṉ uric centamiḻ ākkit tantu - iṉiya coṟkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa centamiḻppāṭṭukaḷāka iyaṟṟikkoṭuttu, atukeṭuttu - annōy muḻumiyum nīkkiyaruḷi,
`nōy eṉṟatu īṇṭu `veppunōy mutaliyavaṟṟiyeṉka. camaṇkurumār tīrttaṟkeṉṟu pakuttuviṭappaṭṭa uṭampiṉ iṭappākattu nōyiyum avar tīrkkamāṭṭāmiyiṟ piḷḷiyār tāmē tīrttaruḷiṉamiyoṭu pāṇṭiyaṟkup piṟavitoṭṭē iruntakūṉiyum nimirntiṭṭamitōṉṟa, `muḻunōy tīra veṉṟār.
aruṭ karuttukaḷit tamiḻālicittāreṉṟaṟku, `amiḻtit tamiḻākkittantu eṉṟār. atu, nōy.
(66-69) pāl paṭu pūtamum mēlpaṭuporuḷum  aintu kūṟṟiṟpaṭṭa mutaṟporuḷkaḷum avaṟṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇporuḷkaḷum, taṉ vaḻippaṭūum taṉmiya ākaliṉ - taṉniṉiviṉvaḻiyē naṭipeṟum iyalpuṭiyaṉa vākiyāl, tāṉ piṟitu ākal vēṇṭiṉ - orukāl oruporuḷ piṟitoru poruḷāka māṟutalit tāṉ tiruvuḷat teṇṇiṉāl, talimayaṅki - avimuṉṉilimi māṟi, tamtoḻil tirintu nantum eṉpatum - tamakku iyalpāka uḷḷa toḻilkaḷum māṟupaṭṭuk keṭum eṉpatum;
`pāṟpaṭu pūtameṉṟatu maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl vāṉ eṉṉum mutaṟporuḷkaḷ aintum aintu vakiyākappirintu niṟṟaliyuṇarttiṟṟu.
`mēṟpaṭu poru ḷeṉṟu vēṟu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, īṇṭup pūtameṉap paṭṭavi tūvā māyiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi maṇmutalāka eṇṇappaṭṭa ipparupporuḷkaḷ aintumēyām eṉpatum, iṉimēṟpaṭuporuḷka ḷeṉappaṭuvaṉa ivvimperum poruḷkaḷukkum vēṟākat tūyamāyikkaṭṭōṉṟum vintuvum nātamum mutalāṉa nuṇporuḷkaḷāmeṉpatun tuṇiyappaṭum. tāṉ, iṟivaṉ.

iḻikkumeṉpatu ceyappāṭṭu viṉi.
(71) muṟiyē nīrilum neruppilum vittu orupāl         viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉi;
nīr, pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ vikiyāṟṟu nīr.
neruppu, tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār kaṭṭaḷippaṭi      pāṇṭiyaṉ aracavikkumuṉ oru tīkkuḻiyil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa tīyākum.
nikaḻnta muṟiyē ivaṟṟi neruppiṉu nīriṉumeṉa māṟṟip poruḷurippiṉumām.
tiṟam, īṇṭup pāl: pakkam.
nīriṉum neruppiṉu meṉpuḻic cāriyi niṟka urupu tokkatu. mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.
(72) kāyāta paṉimarattilum elumpilum vittu maṟṟorupāl viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉi;
`paṉi yeṉpatu tiruvōttūriṟ civaṉaṭiyāroruvar vaḷartta paṉikaḷ; avi  kāyāta āṇpaṉikaḷāy niṉṟaṉa.
`eṉpiṉu meṉappaṭuvatu pūmpāviyiṉelumpu.
nīriṉuneruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉi yorutiṟameṉpatu, `tantoḻiṟirintu nantutali yeṉavum, paṉiyiṉumeṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉi maṟutiṟameṉṟatu `iḻikkumporuḷ ceḻittali yeṉavum niraṉiṟi yākavittup poruḷurittukkoḷka.
pāṇṭimānāṭṭu maturimānakarkkuc ceṉṟa piḷḷi yār, tamakku māṟāyniṉṟa camaṇkurumār virumpiyapaṭi coṟpōrilaṉṟi nikaḻccippōrilum velvāṉkaruti oru tēvārappāṭṭeḻutiya tiruvēṭṭi eriyiliṭa, atu ventaḻiyāmaṟ pacceṉṟiruntatu. aṅṅaṉamē piṉṉum avar, mikavumvirivāy ōṭukiṉṟa vikiyāṟṟu nīril maṟṟoru tēvārac centamiḻeḻutiya ēṭuiṭa, atu nīrōṭum vaḻiyē cellāmal etirēṟi nīrikkiḻittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟatu. `neruppiṉu nīriṉuṅ kāṭṭiya orutiṟam iviyām; ivaṟṟāl nīruntīyumeṉṉum mutaṟporuḷkaḷ talimayaṅkit tantoḻil tirintu nantiṉami kāṇka.
iṉip, pāṇṭināṭṭi viṭṭu nīṅkip piṟa tiruppati kaḷiyum vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭuvanta tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷi yār, toṇṭi nāṭu pōntu tiruvōttūriṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi yirunta ñāṉṟu, āṇṭirunta civaṉaṭiyāroruvar civa perumāṉukkāka vaḷarttuvanta paṉimaraṅkaḷellām āṇpaṉikaḷāy vaḷarntu kāyāvāy niṟpa, atukaṇṭu avvaṭiyāroṭu māṟupaṭṭa avvūrc camaṇmāntaraṉivarum avari atuvē ētuvākak koṇṭu iḻitturittār; piḷḷiyār aḵtaṟintu uṭaṉē oru tīntamiḻttiruppatikam ōti avvāṇpaṉikaḷellāṅ kurumpik kulikaḷuṭaṉ peṇpaṉikaḷāka māṟit tōṉṟumāṟu ceytār. piṉpu tirumayilāppūriṉ kaṇṇuñ civanēcareṉṉum meyyaṉpa roruvar pāmpukaṭittamiyāl uyir nīṅkiya taṉ arumip putalviyiṉ uṭampelumpukaḷi eṭuttuvittuk koṇṭu tiruñāṉa campantariṉ nalvaravu nōkkiyiruppa, avarum aṅkeḻun taruḷiyakkāl annikaḻcciyaṟintu avvelumpu kaḷit tirukkōyiliṉ muṉṉark koṇarvittu, ariyatoru tiruppatikam pāṭip `pūmpāvāy eṉa aḻittu attirumakaḷi eḻuppiyaruḷiṉār. `paṉiyiṉum eṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiya maṟutiṟam iviyām. ivaṟṟāl, iḻikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa paṉimaraṅkaḷum elumpukaḷuma piḷḷiyāratu tiruvaruṇōkkattāṟ peṇ paṉikaḷākavum pūmpāviyākavuñ ceḻittami teḷiyappaṭum.
(72-76) iṉiyē - ataṉmēl, aṟivu oḷi koḷīi - aṟivu viḷakkatti ellāruṅ koḷḷavittu, purineṟip poyc camaṇmuṟiya nūṟi - kuṟṟamporuntiya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya poyyāṉa camaṇmatatti iṭiyaṟṟu viḻat tukaḷākki, meyttiṟam kiḷakkum meypporuḷ civam meyvakiviḷakki - meypporuḷ vakikaḷik kiḷantukūṟum meymmiyiyē poruḷākkoṇṭa civa camayattiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷi meymmuṟikaḷāl viḷakkikkāṭṭi, paraṅkuṉṟu amarntaṉi - tirupparaṅkuṉṟeṉṉun tiruppatiyiṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi yiruntaṉi;
koḷīi, koḷḷacceytu; āvatu viḷaṅkacceytal;
`puri yeṉpatu, parupporuḷaṟiviṉārkku uṇmi pōl mēltōṉṟic, ciṟitu nuṇṇaṟivuṭiyarāy uḷnōkku vārkkum aṅṅaṉam ētōruṇmiyum uṭiya taṉṟāyp puri paṭutalākum. eṉavē, atu `poyccamaṇ eṉappaṭṭateṉka.
`nūṟutal poṭipaṭuttal, iṭittal; tivākarattilum eḻuteḻiṉ māṭat tiṭaṉelā nūṟi yeṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli (uḻiñi, 26) yilum itaṟku ipporuḷkaḷ uṇmikāṇka.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟu; tirumurukaṉatu āṟu paṭivīṭukaḷu ḷoṉṟu; ēṉiya tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi (paḻaṉi), tiruvērakam (cuvāmi mali), tiruccīralivāy (tiruccentūr), kuṉṟu tōṟāṭal, paḻamutircōli yeṉpaṉavām.
innāṉku aruntiṟalnikaḻccikaḷun tiruvaruṭkōṭ pāṭṭāl iyaṟkikku vēṟākavum nikaḻntamiyāl, attiruvaruṭkōṭpāṭu vāyāta camaṇmatam ivvāṟṟāl tāṉē aḻintoḻiyā niṉṟateṉ ṟaṟintukoḷḷappaṭum.
uyiruṭipporuḷkaḷ allāta nīriṉuneruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiya tiṟatti `orutiṟa meṉṟu muṉṉōti uyiruṭip poruḷkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiya ēṉiyiraṇṭaṉiyum maṟutiṟameṉap piṉṉōtiṉār āciriyar.
(76-81) atu peyarntu - piṉṉart tirupparaṅkuṉṟiṉiṉṟu nīṅki, iraṅkuvāl aruvi nirampattōṉṟum āviṉaṉ kuṭiyiṉ aciyiṉi - olikkiṉṟa veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiṉ vīḻcci kuṉṟeṅkuṅkāṇappaṭun tiruvāviṉaṉ kuṭiyeṉṉun tiruppatikkaṇ iruntaṉi; ataṟpuṟam ērakattu eḻuntaṉi - ataṉpiṟaku tiruvērakameṉṉun tiruppatiyiṉkaṇ eḻun taruḷiṉi; nēritiṉ - nuṭpamāka, orumoḻivitta uṭporuḷ virippat tiruvaḷar taṇikiyiṉ amarntaṉi - ōmeṉṉum orumoḻi taṉkaṭpotintuvitta uṭporuḷkaḷi virittu viḷakkumporuṭṭuc ciṟappu ōṅkukiṉṟa tiruttaṇikimaliyi lamarntaṉi.
`iraṅkutal, olittal, `iraṅkuvālaruvi yeṉa itaṉkaṇ varumāṟupōlavē, ilaṅkuveḷḷaruvi (maturikkāñci, 299) yeṉavum, vālveḷḷaruvi (akanāṉūṟu, 308) yeṉavum paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil varutal kāṇka.
`acital iruttaleṉṉumporuṭṭu; āviṉaṉkuṭi yacitalu muriyaṉ (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 176) eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyarurikkum uriyāṉum ītaṟiyap paṭum.
`nēritiṉ virippa veṉakkūṭṭuka.
murukakkaṭavuḷ tam tantiyārāṉa civaperumāṉukku `ōm eṉṉum orumoḻiyi āciriyar muṟiliyiruntu ceviyaṟi vuṟuttu taṟporuṭṭut tiruttaṇiki mēviṉāreṉpatu.
(81-96) ataṟpuṟam ataṉpiṉ, niṉkaḻal kiṭanta arukā aṉpiṉ - niṉatu tiruvaṭikkaṭ patintukiṭanta keṭāta aṉpiṉāl, civam vaḷarkkum meyvaki marapiṉ - civacamayatti nāṭeṅkum parappukiṉṟa meykkūṟṟiṉkaṭpaṭṭa muṟimi yiṉālē, uṭal poruḷ āvi uṉakku eṉa niṟuvi - tam uṭalum poruḷum uyirum uṉakkē uriyaveṉa niṟutti, niṉkuṟittu eḻūum ataṟkum iṉpattu - avvāṟṟāl niṉṉikkuṟik koṇṭu eḻukiṉṟa niliyāṉa iṉpattiṉāl, niṉpeyar allatu piṟituoṉṟu navilā - niṉatu pukaḻiyallāmaṟ piṟaroruvar pukaḻi nāvāṟkūṟāta, maṉperum pulavōṉ - mikapperiya pulamiyiṉiyuṭiyavaṉum, tantiyik kāṭṭum tāy eṉappōntu nāyiṉumkaṭiya eṉṉiyum oruporuḷ paṭuttu niṉ iyalpu orutuṟippukutti urikkum uravōṉ - ētum aṟiyāta orumakavukkut tantiyikkāṭṭi yuṇarttun tāyippōl eḻuntaruḷivantu nāyiṉuṅkīḻppaṭṭa ēḻi yēṉiyum oruporuḷākat tiruvuḷattaṭittu niṉ eṉ peruṅkuṇaṅkaḷiyum yām aṟiyattakkatoru tuṟiyilē yamittu aṟivuṟuttum pēraṟiviṉiyuṭiyavaṉum, cōmacuntara tēcikappeyariṉ ēmuṟavantōṟku aruḷa - `cōmacuntara vāciriyaṉeṉṉun tiruppeyarōṭu ellārum pēriṉpa neṟiyi aṭiyumāṟu tōṉṟiṉavaṉumāṉa eṉ arumi āciriyaṉukku aruḷceyya, kāmuṟṟu - virumpi, poruḷ uṭal  āvi poruḷpeṟa vaḻaṅki - niṉporuḷum uṭalum uyirum uṇminiliyiṟ poruntakkoṭuttaruḷi, vēṇṭuḻi ellām virumpiniṉṟu ētta avaṟku eḷivanta tavapperuntaṉmiyi - virumpiyapōtellām avaṉ aṉpukoṇṭeḻuntu vaḻutta avaṉukku eḷitāy veḷippōnta mikapperiya aruḷiyal piṉiyuṭiyiyāy, oṟṟiyūrppukuntu vikiṉi - tiruvoṟṟiyūriṟ pōntu amarntaruḷiṉi;
`arukā keṭāta, ipporuṭṭātal, parukuvaṉṉa arukā nōkkamōṭu eṉṉum porunarāṟṟuppaṭi (77) yuriyiṟ kāṇka.
marapu - muṟimi; puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli (1, 1) tolkuṭimarapiṉ eṉpataṉ uriyik kāṇka.
`aṟkal, nilipeṟal; aṟkā iyalpiṟṟuccelvam eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷik kāṇka.
`eṉa, uvamurupu.
tiruvaruṇeṟiyiṉ kaṇṇum vīṭupēṟu naṇṇum uyirkku ammiyē appaṉik kāṭṭiyaruḷu muṇmi,

  vīyaviṭ ṭōṭṭi veḷiyē

  ēyuma tēniṭṭi yeṉṟāṉ 
		eḻiṟkacci yēkampaṉē
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ 	(tiruvēkampamāli, 10) tirumoḻiyiṉāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum.
`cōmacuntaravāciriyaṉ taṉṉuṭiya uṭalporuḷuyir kaḷi niṉakkē uriyaveṉavaḻaṅki aṉpuceytamiyiṉ, nīyum avaṉaṉpukku uvantu niṉṉuṭiya uṭalporuḷuyirkaḷi avaṉukku vaḻaṅki aruḷkāṭṭiṉi yeṉṉum ivvaṉpumuṟi eṉ uṭaluyirātiya vellām nī yeṭuttukkoṇṭu uṉ uṭaluyirātiyavellām uvanteṉakkē aḷippāy. eṉṉum irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷiṉ (6, piriyēṉeṉṟal, 3) tiruvaruṭ pāviṉum eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭutal iṅku niṉivukūraṟpālatu.
`cōmacuntarakurava ṉeṉṟatu, tamakkuc camayanuṇporuḷ ceviyaṟivuṟuttiya āciriyari.
taṉmiyi, muṟṟeccam.
(96 - 105) muṟṟavum niṉperuntaṉmiyi aṟintu - muḻutum niṉatu mēlāṉa aruṭṭakimiyit terintu, niṉ aṭi iravoṭu pakalum oruvātu vaṇaṅki - niṉatu tiruvaṭiyi iravoṭu pakalumāy oḻiyāmal vaṇakkañceytu, nā taḻutaḻuppa ēttuuri kiḷantu - nākkut taḻutaḻuppaṭiyumāṟu vaṇakkavurikaḷ colli, neñcam nekkuṭintu uḷḷam uruki, meyvitirvitirppap peṟuvataṟku - uṭampu naṭunaṭuṅka aruḷpeṟutaṟku, eḷiyēṉ - ēḻiyēṉ, ariyeṉ āyiṉum - arumiyuṭiyēṉāṉālum, kuvaṭukeḻu peru niṟap poṉmali aṭinta karuniṟakkākkikku uṟuvatueṉa - kuṭumi poruntiya mikka oḷivāynta poṉniṟamāṉa maliyi naṇṇiya karuniṟamaminta kākkikku appoṉṉiṟamē poruntalpōla, avaṉoṭu keḻūum cārpiṉ ivaṇ atu civaṇavum peṟukuveṉ - accōmacuntara kuravaṉoṭu cārkiṉṟa cārpiṉāl iṅku avvaruṇiliyi yaṭiyavum peṟuvēṉeṉpatu.
nāttaḻutaḻuttal, nākkuḻaṟal nekkuṭintu, uruki, vitir vitirttal, naṭunaṭuṅkal, civaṇal, poruntal.
`kākkik kuṟuvatāleṉac civaṇavum peṟukuva ṉeṉṟu toṭari māṟṟiyiyittup poruḷurittukkoḷka.
`meyvitirvitirppap peṟuvataṟku eṉpataṉ iṭiyil avāyniliyāl aruḷ eṉṉum orucol varuvitturikka.
peṟukuveṉ eṉpatiṟku cāriyi, aṉṟu, āl, ē acikaḷ. 
maliyiṉ poṉmi iṟivaṉaruḷukkum, kākkiyiṉ karumi uyiriṉ malattukkum uvamiyām; `poṉ maliyaṭinta karuniṟak kākkikkup poṉṉiṟamuṟutalpō leṉṟurikka.
8. vaḷḷaṉmi kiḷattal
  uṟuporuḷ kāṇā vuṇarvilār māṭṭup
  peṟuporuḷ vēṇṭal piḻiyā - ṉaṟuneñcē
  yēntumuli vaḷḷi koḻuna ṉeḻiloṟṟip
  pōntu kuṭiyirukkum  pōtu.
(i-ḷ) naṟu neñcē - nalla neñcamē! ēntumuli vaḷḷikoḻunaṉ - uyarntuniṟkuṅ koṅkikaḷiyuṭiya vaḷḷiyiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa āṟumukaṉ, eḻil oṟṟippōntu kuṭiyirukkumpōtu - aḻakiya tiruvoṟṟit tiruppatiyiṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷivantu kuṭiyāy amarntirukkumpōtu, uṟuporuḷ kāṇā uṇarvu ilārmāṭṭu - tammuyiroṭu varuvaṉa nalviṉi tīviṉiyēyallāmal vēṟu allaveṉṟu aṟiya māṭṭāta pakuttuṇarvu kūṭātavarkaḷiṭattil, peṟuporuḷ vēṇṭal piḻi - nī aṭitaṟkuriya poruḷi virumpik kēṭṭal kuṟṟamākum.
ākavē, ellām valla āṟumukapperumāṉē namakkuvēṇṭu vaṉavellām uvantaḷippāṉeṉpatu karuttu; eṉavē avaṉatu ciṟanta vaḷḷaṉmi kiḷantavāṟāmeṉka;
  poymmiyāḷarip pāṭātēyenti
	pukalūrpāṭumiṉ pulavīrkāḷ
  immiyētaruñ cōṟuṅkūṟiyu
	mēttalāmiṭar keṭalumām
  ammiyēciva lōkamāḷvataṟ
	kiyātumiyuṟa villiyē
eṉṉuñ cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉāratu tēvārattirumoḻiyum iṅku niṉivukūraṟ pālatu.
`uṟuporuḷ, īṇṭu nalviṉi tīviṉi.
`peṟuporu ḷeṉpataṟkuc ciṟitu muyaṟciyeṭuttāl evarāṉum eḷitilaṭitaṟkuriya celvapporuḷeṉ ṟurippiṉumām.
`naṟuneñcē yeṉappaṭṭatu, aṟivuṟuppati ēṟkun takutiyuṭiya neñcameṉṟaṟku.
`ēntuta lāvatu, tāḻātu uyarntu allatu nimirntu niṟṟal.
ilvāḻvārē evar eti virumpiṉālum illi yeṉṉāmal aḷikku niliyarākalāṉ, iṅku murukap perumāṉatu vaḷḷaṉmi kiḷantōtum aṭikaḷ avaṉi ilvāḻvāṉākak kuṟippāṉ karutik `kuṭiyirukkum pōtu eṉakkūṟi makiḻvārāyiṉar. āl aci.
9. talimakaḷ tōḻik kaṟattoṭu niṟṟal
  pōtāyntu veṇṇila vokkum poṭimaṇaṟ pūñciṟṟiloṉ
  ṟōtāyañ cūḻa viḻittāṭuṅ kāli yoruvaṭivēṟ
  cūtāṉa kāḷi tiruvoṟṟi yīcar tuṇipputalvaṉ
  kātāruṅ kaṇṇi citittunak kōṭik karantaṉaṉē.

pōtu āyntum ciṟṟil iḻittum viḷiyāṭutal makaḷir ceyalām; pōtu āyntapiṉṉar īṇṭut talivi ciṟṟil iḻittāḷeṉṟu koḷka.
poṭimaṇal veḷēleṉṟiruttaliṉ, ataṉi nilavō ṭoppiṭṭuk kūṟutal marapu; nilavumaṇal viyaṉkāṉal (puṟanāṉūṟu, 17) eṉavum, `nilāmaṇal (maṇimēkali, 8, 11) eṉavum paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ varutalkāṇka.

  kōti parintu varippantu koṇṭōṭi
  nōtakka ceyta ciṟupaṭṭi
eṉṉuṅ kalittokik kaṇṇuṅ (kuṟiñcikkali, 15) kūṟappaṭutal iṅku niṉivukūraṟpālatu.
aṟattoṭu niṟṟalāvatu: aṟam eṉpatu takkatu; takkataṉic colliniṟṟal.... allatūum, peṇṭirkku aṟameṉpatu kaṟpu, kaṟpiṉṟali niṟṟaleṉpatūumām. iṉit `tōḻikkum urittu eṉṟa ummiyāl talimakaṭkum aṟattoṭunili uritteṉpatu, aḵtāmāṟu iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarntāṉum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭiyāṉun teruṇṭuvaritaluṟṟut tamari viṭum; viṭṭa viṭattu avarmaṟuppa; aḵtu ilakkaṇamākalāṉ, aṅṅaṉam maṟutta viṭattut talimakaḷ vēṟupaṭam. emperumāṉ maṟukkap paṭṭamiyāṉ maṟṟoruvāṟāṅ kollōveṉak kalaṅki vēṟupāṭu eytiṉa poḻutē tōḻikkuppulaṉām; pulaṉāyiṉa viṭattu emperumāṭṭi, niṉakku ivvēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu? eṉṉum, eṉṟaviṭattu, iḵteṉakkup paṭṭatu; iṉṉaviṭattu nīyum āyaṅkaḷuntaḻiyuṅ kaṇṇiyuṅ kōṭaṟku eṉṉiṟ ciṟitu nīṅkiṉāy; yāṉ niṉṟu oru maṇiccuṉi kaṇṭēṉ; kaṇṭa ammaṇiccuṉitāṉ āmpalē kuvaḷiyē neytalē tāmariyē eṉṟippūkkaḷāl mayaṅki mētakkatu kaṇṭu vēṭkiyāṉ āṭuvāṉ iḻintēṉ; iḻukkik kuṭṭam pukkēṉ; pukkuttōḻiyōveṉa,    nī aṅṅaṉaṅkēḷāyā yiṉāyāka, orutōṉṟal tōṉṟivantu eṉatutuyar nīkkutaṟkākat taṉ ki nīṭṭiṉāṉ; nīṭṭiṉaviṭattu malakkattāṉ niṉkiyeṉap paṟṟiṉēṉ; paṟṟa vāṅkik karimēl niṟīi nīṅkiṉāṉ; nī aṉṟu kavalutiyeṉaccollēṉāyiṉēṉ; nī evvellikkaṇṇuṅ kiviṭātāy aññāṉṟu kiviṭaliṉi ākkiṟṟu vitiyākātē? iṉip, piṟitoṉṟāṅ kollō veṉakkalaṅki vēṟupaṭṭē ṉeṉṟu tōḻikkut talimakaḷ aṟattoṭuniṟkum eṉṟu āciriyar nakkīraṉār uritta uriyāl (iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷ, 29 - āñcūttiram) aṟattoṭu niṟṟal iṉṉateṉṟuṇarntu koḷka.
10. oppumikūṟi aruṇili vēṇṭal
itaṉkaṇ aṭikaḷ tamakkum iṟivaṉukkuñ cila kaṟpaṉi ētukkaḷ koṇṭu oppumivaṟi, avvoppumi yuṇmiyiṉ avaṉ tampāṟ kēṇmiporunti aruḷtaral iyiyumeṉa vaḻakkāṭi aruṇilivēṇṭal kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(1-3) karavu aṟu māntarkku viravuṟupaḻiyiṉ - kaḷḷam nīṅkiya cāṉṟērpāṟ kalantu tōṉṟum paḻiyippōl, maṟuvoṭu viḷaṅkum niṟikatir matiyam - kaḷaṅkattoṭu viḷaṅkum niṟinta katirkaḷiyuṭiya muḻunilā, veṇkatir virikkum taṇ eṉ kāli -veḷḷiya oḷiyi eṅkum virikkiṉṟa kuḷirum muṉṉirāppoḻutil;
karaveṉṟatu, iṅkuk kaḷḷattaṉamāṉa tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷi; māntarkku, vēṟṟumiyurupu mayakkam. cāṉṟōrmāṭṭuk kāṇappaṭuṅ kuṟṟamē yārkkum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutalpaṟṟik karavaṟumāntartam paḻi uvamikūṟappaṭṭatu. iṅku uvamiyum poruḷumāka vanta māntarum matiyamum muṟiyē poruḷum uvamiyumākavē varal vaḻakkām; ēṉeṉiṟ, karavaṟu māntariṉum maṟuvoṭuviḷaṅku matiyamē evarkkum viḷakkamāyt terintatoṉ ṟātaliṉeṉpatu; maṟṟu avi iṅkup poruḷ uvamamākavum uvamam poruḷākavum vaḻakkiṟku māṟāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; iṅṅaṉam māṟivaḻaṅkuta lellām ilakkiyaccuvi mikuttaṟkām; iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟip piṟkālattār `viparītavuvami (taṇṭiyalaṅkāram, poruḷaṇi yiyal, 2, 14) yeṉakkūṟā niṉṟaṉar. viravuṟal, orucol.
(4-7) pulavumaṇam kamaḻum pākkattu - pulāl nāṟṟam nāṟuñ cempaṭavar cēriyil, nalam keḻu terivu il mākkaḷ puritimil pukuntu - naṉmimikka pakuttaṟivu illāta cempaṭavar kaṭṭukaḷ aminta tōṇiyil ēṟi, nirinirivakutta vaki ami viḷakkam - varicivariciyākap pakuttuvitta muṟi yaminta viḷakkukaḷ, vāṉamīṉiṉ vayiṉ vayiṉ imippavum - viṇmīṉkaḷippōl iṭantōṟum oḷiviṭavum;
kaṭaliṉiṉṟuṅ koṇarappaṭum mīṉkaḷāṟ cempaṭavarcēriyil eññāṉṟum pulāṉāṟṟam nāṟutaliṉ, accēri `pulavumaṇaṅkamaḻumpākkam eṉappaṭṭatu; kamaḻtal - nāṟutal, kamaḻkaṭāattu (puṟam, 3) eṉpuḻippōla, timilpukuntu mīṉ piṭikkuñ cempaṭavar tāñceyyun toḻil tīteṉat teḷintukoḷḷāmiyiṉ, avar `teriviṉmākkaḷ eṉappaṭṭāreṉka.
viḷakkam, īṇṭuccuṭar; itu toḻilākupeyar.
nīla niṟamāṉa vāṉattil miṉniṟamāṉa viṇmīṉkaḷ oḷiviṭutalpōlavē, nīṉiṟamāṉa kaṭaṉaṭuvil miṉṉiṟamāṉa viḷakkoḷikaḷ oḷiviṭṭuppolitaliṉ, kaṭaṟpaṭakukaḷiṉ viḷakkukaḷukku vāṉmīṉkaḷ uvamiyāyiṉa.
(8-10) ciṟutuṭi maruṅkul karum kaṇ nuḷicciyar - uṭukkiyippōṉṟa nuṇṇiya iṭuppiṉiyum kariyakaṇ kaḷiyum uṭiya neytal nilamakaḷir, eḻuvarum eṇmarum uḻiuḻik ki piṇintu - eḻuvarum eṇmarumāy iṭantōṟuṅ kikōttu, oruvāmarapiṉ kuravi ayaravum - oruvari viṭṭoruvar nīṅkāta taṉmiyoṭu kuravikkūttu āṭāniṟpavum;
kuraviyeṉpatu kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravic ceyyuḷpāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉuṅ kipiṇintāṭuvatu eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyavuriyāṉum (cilappatikāram, araṅkēṟṟukāti, 12), avareṭuttukkāṭṭiya
kuravi yeṉpa teḻuvar maṅkiyar
  cennili maṇṭalak kaṭakak kikōt
  tannilik koṭpaniṉ ṟāṭa lākum
eṉṉum mikappaḻiya tamiḻppāṭṭāṉum (cilappatikāram, patikam, 77) iṉituṇarappaṭum.
ayartal - āṭutalicceytal.
(11-17) avartaru ciṟāar cimīṉ mukantu maṇal akaḻkēṇiyil muṟimuṟi viṭuttum - anneytalnila makaḷir peṟṟeṭutta ciṟuvar kaḻikaḷiṟ kiṭakkuñ ciṟumīṉkaḷik kuṭuviyiṟ paṟṟi mukantukoṇṭuvantu arukē maṇalnilattil avarkaḷāṟ ṟōṇṭiyeṭukkappaṭṭa ūṟṟukkiṇaṟṟilē oruvarpiṉ oruvarāy viṭṭu, (ataṉpiṉ) porikeḻu puṉṉip pūntuṇar vīḻappap purivaḷi eṟiya - neṟporipōlum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉimarattiṉ pūṅkottukkaḷi vīḻttum poruṭṭu muṟukkuṇṭa caṅkiṉi mēl vīca, kōṭu tākkip palavēṟu uṭinta nalam kiḷar nittilam - avvāṟṟāl ammarattiṉ kiḷikaḷ muṭṭutalālē palavāy vēṟu vēṟāy uṭintu citaṟiya naṉmimikka accaṅkiṉ muttukkaḷi, koḻuviya naṉi eṉak kuñcippeytu - ceḻumiyāṉa appuṉṉi marattiṉ moṭṭeṉakkarutit tamatu talimayiriṟ ceruki, vaḻīi vīḻakkaṇ kaluḻpu peyaravum - avi maḻamaḻa veṉṟiruttalāl vaḻuviviḻa ataṉāṟ kaṇkalaṅki yaḻutu aṅku niṉṟu nīṅkavum;
 mukanteṉṟamiyiṉ, kuṭaviyil mukanteṉṟu koḷka.
kēṇi - neytalnilattuc ciṟukiṇaṟu.
puṉkamarattiṉ  pū neṟporiyi yottirukku meṉpatu, `porippūmpuṉkiṉ eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟuc ceyyuḷilum (368) kāṇka.
caṅkiṉvaṭivu, muṟukkuṇṭāṟpō liruttaliṟ `purivaḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu. caṅku uṭintāl avi muttukkaḷāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ `nittilam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. valampuriyiṉiṉṟum muttup piṟakkumuṇmi muṉ (4: 8-9) kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. vīḻppaveṉpatu piṟaviṉi; kuñci, āṇpāṉ mayir.
kaluḻpu, ceypuveṉ vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam. `ciṟāar ici niṟi aḷapeṭi, `vaḻīi collici yaḷapeṭi.
puṉṉip pūṅkottiṉi vīḻttumporuṭṭu ataṉmē leṟinta caṅkukaḷ kuṟitappik kiḷi kaḷiṟṟākkuṇṭamiyiṉ, uṭintu citaṟiṉa; avaṟṟi anneytalañ ciṟār puṉṉi naṉikaḷē utirntaṉaveṉa mayaṅkit tantalimayiriṟ cerukiṉārāka, avi maḻamaḻa veṉṟiruttalāl ammayiṟ poruntāmal vaḻuvivīḻntaṉa; avaṟṟi avar mīṇṭumīṇṭuñ cerukutalum avitāmum mīṇṭu mīṇṭu naḻuvivīḻaliṉ, atukaṇṭu acciṟār aḻutu kaṇkalaṅki aṅkuniṉṟum peyarntāreṉpatu.
(18-23) koḻumaṭal avinta kuḻūukkoḷ kiti vāṉ citar aḷāya tēmkamaḻ āmpal - ceḻumiyāṉa itaḻvirinta kūṭṭaṅ koṇṭatāḻiyiṉuṭiya veḷḷiya pūntātukaḷ kalanta tēṉmaṇakkuñ cevvāmpaṉmalarkaḷ, nīṟu uṟu taḻalē pōlavum - nīṟu pūtta neruppippōlavum, pālkeḻu kuṟunaṭipputalvar tuvar itaḻ pōlavum - pāl oḻukuṅ kuṟukuṟuveṉṉuñ ciṟunaṭiyiṉiyuṭiya putalvarkaḷiṉ civanta vāyitaḻkaḷip pōlavum, aṟivu il mākkaṭkum aṟivupētuṟukkum puṉiyāp poṟpiṉ – meyyaṟivu peṟāta makkaṭkum avaratu ulakuṇarviṉi mayakkuṅ kiyāṟ puṉitalceyyāta iyaṟkiyiṉāl, muṟimuṟi ciṟappavum - oṉṟiṉmēloṉṟāyc ciṟantuviḷaṅkavum;
tāḻampūviṉ itaḻkaḷ ēṉimalarkaḷiṉ itaḻkaḷiviṭap periya vāyuñ ceḻiyavāyumiruttaliṉ, `maṭa leṉavuṅ `koḻu veṉavum āciriyar aṭikoṭuttōtiṉār. tāḻañceṭikaḷ putarputarāyiruttalāṟ, `kuḻūukkoḷ kiti yeṉappaṭṭatu.
`vāl vāṉ eṉattirintatu; `vāṉkatirtirumaṇi (puṟam, 150) eṉpuḻippōla.
`tēṅkamaḻ eṉpuḻit `tēṉ eṉṉuñcol īṟukeṭṭu varumoḻi vaṉmikkēṟpa meṉmi mikkatu. itu
tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉamilli. (tolkāppiyam, puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, 45, 46) eṉpavaṟṟāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
`nīṟuṟutaḻal eṉpatil, nīṟu kitiyiṉ pūntātu eṉavum, taḻal āmpaleṉavuṅkoḷka; iṅṅaṉamē pāl kitittāteṉavum, tuvaritaḻ āmpaleṉavum ataṟkaṭutta uvamiyilum uṇaraṟpāṟṟu.
`pālkeḻu tuvaritaḻ eṉṟu kūṭṭuka.
kuḻantikaḷ kuṟunaṭiyiṉa reṉpatip pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampiyuṅ `kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟukinīṭṭi eṉap puṟattiṟ (188) kūṟiṉār. tuvar, civappu.
`mākkaḷ eṉpār makkaḷuṭ pakuttaṟivu illātavar; marapiyalurikārar māvumākkaḷum aiyaṟiviṉavē. (tolkāppiyam, marapiyal, 32) eṉṉum nūṟpāvukku uri kūṟukiṉṟuḻi mākkaḷeṉappaṭuvār maṉavuṇarcci yillātār eṉṟu urippar. iṉi `aṟivupētuṟukku meṉpatu attakiya mākkaḷukku ulakiyaṟporuḷkaḷiyuṇarum poṟiyuṇarvē uḷatākaliṉ, avarkku avvulakiyaluṇarviṉiyum mayakkumeṉpatām. evvāṟeṉiṉ, kitiyan tātu paṭinta cevvāmpaṉ malar civapirāṉatu nīṟupūtta taḻaluruvatti avarkku niṉippūṭṭaṉamiyiṉ `mākkaṭkum eṉpataṉ ummi iḻivu ciṟappu.
iyaṟkit tōṟṟattiṟ kāṇappaṭum aḻaku makkaḷ kiyāṟ puṉiyappaṭāta takāliṟ puṉiyāp poṟpu eṉṟār; mēṟ kūṟiya neytal nila aḻakukaḷ puṉiyāp poṟpiṉa eṉṟapaṭi.
(24-26) neytal cāṉṟa kiti am kāṉal oṟṟiyūr amarnta veṟṟivēl kuricil - ivvāṟāka neytaṉilattiṉ aḻaku niṟinta tāḻikaḷ vāynta kaṭaṟkariyiṉi yuṭiya tiruvoṟṟiyū reṉṉun tiruppatiyil tirukkōyil koṇ ṭeḻuntaruḷiyaveṟṟi mikka vēṟpaṭiyiṉit tāṅkiya cemmalē! koṉ oṉṟu kiḷakkuveṉ kēṇmati peruma! - perumiyāṉatōr oppumic ceyti colluvēṉ kēṭṭaruḷka, perumāṉē;
`kitiyaṅ kāṉal, am cāriyi; kuricil, āṇṭaki; iḵtu iyalpuviḷi; koṉ, periyatu; iḵtipporuṭṭātal `koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuval eṉṉum maturikkāñciyaṭikku (207) ataṉ urikārar kūṟum uriyāṟ peṟappaṭum;
`kēṇmati itaṉkaṇ mati muṉṉiliyaciccol; `uṟimati peruma (maturikkāñci, 781) eṉpuḻip pōla.
(27-30) nīyē ār uyirt tokutikkup pēṟutaral vēṇṭi aintoḻil iyaṟṟuvi - tēvarīr ariya uyirkkūṭṭaṅkaṭku numatu tiruvaṭip pēṟṟi aḷittal karutip paṭittal mutalāṉa aintoḻilkaḷic ceyvīr, yāṉē oruneṟi iṉṟip poṟivaḻi ōṭi aimpulaṉ nukarntāṅku amikuveṉ - ēḻiyēṉ ōr aruḷ neṟiyil niṟṟalillāmal meyvāy mutalāṉa aintu poṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē pāyntu avaṟṟiṉ ūṟu cuvi mutalāṉa aintu pulaṉkaḷi nukarntapaṭiyē amintiruppēṉ;
`aintoḻil eṉpaṉa, paṭittal kāttal aḻittal maṟittal aruḷalākum. `paṭittal eṉpatu, aṟiyāmiyāl mūṭuṇṭu kiṭanta uyirkaḷukku uṭampik koṭuttu, avvāṟṟāl avaṟṟiṟku aṟivit tōṟṟuvittal; `kātta leṉpatu, uṭampeṭuttu uyirkaḷukku varavara aṟiyāmi tēyntu kaḻiya, avaṟṟiṉ aṟivi vaḷaracceytal; `aḻittal eṉpatu uyirkaḷ tammuṭiya iruviṉip payaṉkaḷit toṭarntu nukarutalāl avaṟṟiṉ uṭampu valikuṟiya avitāmum iḷippuṟutalāl, avaṟṟiṉ paḻavuṭampu kaḻittu, mīṇṭum avvuyirkaḷip puttuṭampiṟ piṟappikkumuṉ cila kālam iḷippāṟac ceytal. `maṟittal eṉpatu ovvoru piṟaviyilum uyirkaḷ mēlummēlum iruviṉip payaṉkaḷi avāvuṭaṉ nukarum poruṭṭu avitamakku muṟpiṟappukkaḷil nikaḻnta iruviṉi nukarccikaḷi maṟittu maṟakkumpaṭi ceytal. `aruḷal eṉpatu immuṟikaḷāl aṟiyāmi muṟṟun tēyntu pēraṟivu kiḷarappeṟṟa uyirkaḷukku vīṭṭiṉpatti vaḻaṅkutalākum. ellām valla iṟivaṉ āruyirttokutikaḷ poruṭṭuc ceyyum ivvintoḻi liyalpukaḷ,
aḻippiḷip pāṟṟa lākka mavvavar kaṉma mellāṅ
  kaḻittiṭa ṉukarac ceytal kāppatu kaṉma voppiṟ
  ṟeḻittiṭaṉ malaṅka ḷellā maṟipparuḷ ceyti tāṉum
  paḻippoḻi pantam vīṭu pārttiṭi ṉaruḷē yellām
eṉac civañāṉacittiyāril (1: 37) viḷakkamāyk kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
`poṟikaḷ eṉap potuvākak kūṟiṉamiyāl aimpoṟikaḷumeṉa urikkappaṭṭatu; aviyāvaṉa, mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi; aimpula ṉeṉpaṉa av vimpoṟikaḷiṉ uṇarvukaḷ; avi ūṟu cuvi oḷi nāṟṟam ōci yeṉpaṉa; poṟivaḻiyōṭi avvuṇarvukaḷi nukarta leṉka. āṇṭavaṉ paṭittal mutalāṉa aintoḻilkaḷiyum eṉṟum iyaṟṟutal pōl, āciriyar tāmum uṟutal mutalāṉa aintoḻilkaḷiyum ōyātu iyaṟṟuvatākak kūṟi oppumi kāṭṭiṉār.
`ōṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu, poṟivaḻic cellutalil uḷḷattukku uḷḷa virinta viruppatti veḷippaṭuttutaṟku.
(31-34) nīyē makkaḷmēl koṇṭa poccam il aṉpil tāyē aṉiya aḷiyiṉi - tēvarīr tam makkaḷpāl vikkum poymiyillāta aṉpil tāyiyē otta aṉpiṉi yuṭiyīr; yāṉē maṉiviyum makkaḷum poruḷ eṉakkoṇṭu - ēḻiyēṉ maṉiviyiyum makkaḷiyumē uṟutip poruḷkaḷeṉak karuti, niṉivatu oṉṟu illāp pēr aṉpiṉaṉē - ēṉiyoru perumporuḷiruppatākavum niṉital ciṟitu millāta pēraṉpiṉi uṭiyēṉ;
`poccam, poy; pokkam eṉpataṉ tiripu (piṅkalanti); poccamilaṉpu maṉṉar (tirunāṭṭuppaṭalam, 31) eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam.
`tāyē eṉpataṉ ēkāram, piriniliyoṭu tēṟṟam.
`aḷiyiṉi, kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu, `aṉpiṉeṉ eṉpatum atu;
`oṉṟu īṇṭuc ciṟiteṉṉum poruḷil vantatu; iḵtiṅṅaṉam varumāṟu, `māyik kuṇarvoṉṟu milli yeṉṟē vittiṭumataṉāl (civañāṉacittiyār, 1, 17) eṉpatiṟ kāṇappaṭum.
makkaḷu meṉpataṉ īṟṟilum pirinilit tēṟṟēkāram viritturittuk koḷka.
itaṉkaṇ, nī niṉmakkaḷmēl aṉpuṭiyiyātalpōl, yāṉum eṉ maṉivimakkaḷmēl aṉpuṭiyē ṉeṉṟu oṟṟumi kāṭṭiyavāṟām; iṟivaṉukku makkaḷāvaṉa uyirkaḷeṉka.
(35-42) nīyē - tēvarīr, yāṉiveṇ maruppum tēṉiṉ iṟālum mayil taḻippīliyum virikamaḻ cāntamum oruṅku talimayaṅkiya irumperuṅkuṉṟattu - yāṉiyiṉ veḷḷiya kompukaḷum tēṉiṉ kūṭukaḷum mayiliṉ taḻitaliyuṭiya tōkikaḷum maṇaṅkamaḻuñ cantaṉakkaṭṭikaḷum oruṅku kalantukiṭakkum mikapperiya maliyil, āṉātu uṟiyum iyalpiṉi - viruppam nīṅkātu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum iyal piṉiyuṭiyīr! yāṉē - ēḻiyēṉ, pulam ilkalviyum nalam ilpukaḻum vaḻuviya oḻukkamum kaḻuvākkuṟṟamum oruvaḻi amintu perukiya cerukku eṉum kōṭukeḻukuṉṟil - pakuttaṟivillāta paṭippum naṉmiyillāta pukaḻum tavaṟiya oḻukkamuṅ kaḻuviyakaṟṟāta kuṟṟamum oṉṟāypporuntip peritāṉa āṇavam eṉṉum uccikeḻumiya malimēl, pīṭu uṟukuveṉ - amarntu perumi peṟuvēṉ;
iṟāl, `tēṉkūṭu; `iṟāṟēṉ kūṭē eṉpatu tivākaram (vilaṅkiṉ peyarttokuti). `taḻippīli, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumittoki; `taḻi mutaṉilittoḻiṟpeyar. `talimayaṅkutal oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalantukiṭattal; `vaki terivaṟiyāvaḷantali mayaṅkiya (cilappatikāram, 14, 178) veṉpuḻippōla.
`āṉātu, īṇṭu nīṅkātu eṉṉum poruṭṭu; koḷḷi māntariṉāṉātu eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum (3) iccol ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka. `pulam, aṟivu; iṅkuppakuttaṟiviṉi yuṇarttum.
`perukiyakuṉṟu eṉattoṭuttukkoḷka. tīya iyalpukaḷellām īṇṭu maliyāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa; tirukkuṟaḷil (3:9) nalliyalpukaḷellāṅ `kuṇameṉṉuṅ kuṉṟu eṉa uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭāṟpōla veṉpatu.
itaṉkaṇ, murukapperumāṉ kuṉṟukaṇmēl eḻuntaruḷi yiruttalpōla, ākkiyōrtāmun tīmiyeṉṉuṅ kuṉṟukaṇ mēl ēṟiyuṟivatāka oṟṟumi tēṟṟiyavāṟu kāṇka; murukap perumāṉ kuṉṟukaṇmēl amarntaruḷum uṇmi, avaṉeḻun taruḷutaṟkuriya āṟupaṭi vīṭukaḷuṭ `kuṉṟutoṟāṭal eṉpatum oṉṟātal koṇṭum, `kuṉṟuto ṟāṭalu niṉṟataṉ paṇpē eṉṉun tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyiṉ (217) ṟirumoḻi koṇṭum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. pīṭu, perumi.
(43-58) nīyē - tēvarīr, aṟal neṟittaṉṉa - ceṟikuḻal kaṟṟiyum- karumaṇal muṭaṅkiṉāṟpōl neḷital uḷḷa mayirttokutiyum, piṟi ceṟittaṉṉa naṟikamaḻ nutalum - eṭṭām piṟit tiṅkaḷip poruttiṉāṟ pōṉṟa maṇamvīcum neṟṟiyum, kayal iṇittaṉṉa akal iruviḻiyum - iraṇṭu keṇṭi mīṉkaḷi mukañcērttu vittāṟpōl akaṉṟa iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷum, kumiki mukiḻttaṉṉa amivaru nāciyum - iḷiya eḷḷiṉ moṭṭu arumpiṉāṟpōl aminta mūkkum, paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tuḷaṅku oḷikkatuppum - paḷiṅkiṟpōla acintu niḻalāṭum viḷakkattiṉiyuṭiya kaṉṉaṅkaḷum, vaḷḷi aṉṉa teḷ oḷic ceviyum - vaḷḷittaṇṭi otta teḷinta oḷiyiyuṭiya cevikaḷum, kulikam tōytta ilavu uṟaḻ itaḻum - iṅku likattiṟ ṟōytteṭutta ilavam pūvi okkuma civanta vāyitaḻum, muṟuval pūppa miṉ eṉ miḷirum kuṟumuttu aṉṉaciṟumuḷ eyiṟum - puṉṉaki malaruṅkāl miṉpōl oḷiviṭukiṉṟa kuṟiya muttuppōṉṟa ciṟiya muḷ okkum paṟkaḷum, koḻunīrp paṇilattu viḻumiya kaḻuttum - ceḻumiyāṉa nīrvāḻ caṅki okkuñ ciṟanta kaḻuttum, emakku aruḷ marapiṉ tamakku ami kiyum - ēḻiyēṅkaṭku aruḷ ceyyum iyalpiṉāl tamataruṭṭaṉmikku icinta aruṭkiyum, civiṟi virittu aṉṉa ciṟu tiruvaṭiyum - civiṟiyi virittu vittāṟ pōṉṟa ciṟiya tiruvaṭikaḷum, em uḷam nīṅkāc cemmiyiṉ tōṉṟa vīṟu keḻu pakutiyiṉ vēṟu irumaṭavar kūṟukoṇṭu irupuṭi viḷaṅka - eḷiyēṅkaḷ uḷḷattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāta cevviyil iṭiyaṟātu tōṉṟumāṟu mēṉmi keḻumiya iṭavalap pakkaṅkaḷil vēṟuvēṟāka maṅkiyar iruvar paṅkukoṇṭu irupakkaṅkaḷilum viḷaṅka, cīṟiya mayilmici varukuvi - cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭuvanta mayiliṉ mēl eḻuntaruḷi varuvīr!
neṟittal, muṭaṅkutal, neḷital, neṟikoḷ varikkuṭar (puṟam, 160) eṉpataṉ uriyik kāṇka. makkaḷ uṭampupōl vālāmiyum ataṉāl tīya muṭināṟṟamum illāmal, ellām valla iṟiviyiṉ aruḷuṭampu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻtalē uṭimiyiṉ nutal, `naṟikamaḻ nutal eṉappaṭṭatu; cāntum nīṟum aṇitaluṭimiyiṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟappaṭṭa teṉṟalumoṉṟu.
makaḷir neṟṟikkup piṟi uvamiyātaliṉ vaki muṉṉarē viḷakkappaṭṭatu.
kumiki - iḷiyeṉ, (piṅkalanti)
kaṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ tuḷaṅkoḷi avaṟṟiṉ pakkattē yaminta ceviyiṉ mēlumpaṭṭu oḷiceytaliṉ, acceviyun teḷḷoḷiyuṭiya eṉṟār. maṟṟu viḷakkam mikka maṇikaḷāl iḻikkappaṭṭatōṭu aṇinta cevikaḷākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉan teḷḷoḷiyuṭiya vāyiṉa veṉṟalum oṉṟu, aṉṟi; ivviruvaki yoḷikaḷālum avi viḷaṅkiṉa veṉalumām.
iyaṟkiyē cenniṟa muṭiyatākiya ilavamalar iṅkulikattiṟ ṟōytteṭuttavaḻi mēluñ civanta niṟattiṉa tāy viḷaṅkaliṉ aḵtu ammiyariṉ vāy itaḻukku umiyāyiṟṟu.
attuṇic civanta itaḻkaḷ tiṟantavaḻit tōṉṟum paṟkaḷ muttupōl veṇṇiṟattiṉa vāyiṉum, accivanta itaḻ oḷiyuṭaṉum viravutaliṉ, avi miṉṉoḷiyoṭu miḷirum eṉṟār, muttu veṇmikkum oḷikkum vaṉmikkum uvami.
paṇilam, maḻamaḻappukku uvami; nīriṉkaṇ iruntu uṇṭātaliṉ, `nīrppaṇilam eṉappaṭṭatu; `nalla nīrmiyuḷḷa paṇila meṉalumām.
`civiṟi virittaṉṉa veṉṉuṅ kuṟipput tiruvaṭi viralkaḷ taṉittaṉi neruṅki niṟṟalik kuṟittavāṟām.
vīṟu - `vēṟoṉṟaṟ killā aḻaku eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (cīvaka cintāmaṇi, 489)
maṭavār, iṅku vaḷḷi teyvayāṉiyammiyār.
cīṟiyamayil - cīṟṟattuṭaṉ vanta cūraṉākiya mayil; kaṭippaṭiyāka murukappirāṉatu vēlāl irukūṟākap pōḻappaṭṭa cūraṉatu uṭal cēvalum mayilumākip pirāṉoṭu porutaṟkup peruñcīṟṟattuṭaṉvara, iṟivaṉ cēvalik koṭiyāka niṟutti, mayili ūrtiyākak koṇṭaṉeṉak kantapurāṇaṅ kūṟum. atu,
tāvaṭi neṭuvēṉmīḷat taṟparaṉ varattāl vīṭā
  mēvalaṉ eḻuntu mīṭṭu meypakiriraṇṭu kūṟuñ
  cēvalumayilumākic ciṉaṅkoṭu tēvar cēṉi
  kāvalaṉ ṟaṉṉināṭi yamarttoḻil karuti vantāṉ.
eṉṉuñ cūrapaṉmaṉ vatippaṭalac ceyyuḷāl (492) aṟiyappaṭum. maṉ, ō acinili.

`tirukiya ciṉam muṟukiya ciṉam; āvatu mikunta ciṉameṉka; uravuc ciṉantirukiya eṉṟār puṟanāṉūṟṟilum (25).

civaperumāṉ ulakameṅkaṇum niṟinta muḻumutalākaliṉ ulakattiṉ eṭṭut ticikaḷiyum avaṉukku `eṇṭōḷka ḷeṉavum, ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷun tīyum avaṉukku `mukkaṇka ḷeṉavum aṟintōr urippa.
`camayak kaṇakkar, camayanūl vallunar; camayak kaṇakkartam mativaḻi kūṟātu (kallāṭam, 15) eṉpatu kāṇka. amivila, kuṟippu muṟṟeccam.
`kiḻami, iṅku iyalpu, iḵtu ipporuṭṭātal vāramuṅ kuṇamuṅ kāṇiyuṅ kiḻami yeṉṉum piṅkalantiyāl (3370) aṟiyappaṭum.
piṟar virumpāta niliyil tāṉē muṉvirintu  pataṟimoḻiyuñ col `pukuntu coṉ moḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu. `poruḷoṭu poruntā eṉpatiṟ poruḷ meymmiyiṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu; poyyuriyē yaṉṟu poruḷuriyē eṉpatiṟ pōla (cilappatikāra, 9, 18)
uṟuvar, mēlōr; uṟuvarp pēṇal eṉṉuñ cīvaka cintāmaṇit toṭarkku (2816) `mikkōri virumputal eṉṟu urikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal iṅku uṟṟu nōkkaṟpālatu.

eṉṉun tolkāppiya uriyiyal (53) nūṟpāvāl uṇarappaṭum. aḻu eṉṉum mutaṉili viṉiyeccap poruḷil vantatu; varip puṉi pantu eṉpatiṟpōla (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 68).
`eṟital āvatu `aṭittu varuttutal mutaliyaṉa veṉka.
