iṟivaṉ kaṇṭaporuḷ eṉpatu iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum akapporu ḷilakkaṇanūl; ataṟkup pēruri vakuttavar nakkīrar.
civaṇal - aḷavaḷāval, poruntal (piṅkalanti).
`kīraṉ eṉpatu peyar; atu centamiḻ vaḻakkiṟ ciṟappu nōkki varum nakaramākiya iṭiccolloṭu cērntu `nappūtaṉ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉapōl `nakkīraṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu; kīr - col (tivākaram); nakkīraṉ - nallacollaṉ allatu colvaṉmiyuṭiyaṉ.
`aṟṟam melivu, cōrvu (tirukkuṟaḷ parimēlaḻakaruri, 1186).
`paṭṭāṅku, paṭṭapaṭi, nikaḻntapaṭi, atāvatu uṇmi.
`āṟṟuppaṭi īṇṭut tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi.
(67-74) āṅku ataṉ karuvāy uḷ uṟi nuṇporuḷ - at tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyiṉkaṇ ataṉ karupporuḷāy akattē kiṭakkum nuṭpak karuttukaḷi, viḻumitiṉ eṭuttu vaḻuvu aṟa amittu - ciṟappākat turuvi yeṭuttuk kuṟṟam iṉṟāka amittu, toṇṭar ārat taṇṭātu koṭuttu - centamiḻt teyvat toṇṭarkaḷ nukarntu niṟiyat taṇiyāmaṟ koṭuttu, niṉ tiruvaṭik kiṭanta perukiya aṉpiṉum -niṉatu tiruvaṭiyiṉkaṭ kiṭanta mikunta aṉpiṉālum, tamiḻ varampu uṇarnta kamaḻuṟum aṟiviṉum - centamiḻ elli teḷinta maṇam viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉālum, urittiṟam nili yiṭum varippaṭā viṟaliṉum - urivaṉmi nilikāṇum aḷavupaṭāta āṟṟaliṉālum, tāṉē taṉakku nikar eṉa viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉum allēṉ - tāṉē taṉakku nikar eṉṉumpaṭi pukaḻ viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉ eṉṉum uriyāciriyaṉum allēṉ;
`karu, karukkoṇṭa poruḷ; eṉṉuḷak karuvi yāṉ kaṇṭilēṉ (tiruvācakam, tiruccatakam, 41) eṉṉum aruṇmoḻiyik kāṇka.
taṇṭātu - taṇiyātu; taṇṭā īkit takimāṇ kuṭumi puṟam, 6.
`ārtal nukartal, uṇṇutal; iccol ipporuṭ ṭākavē maṇamalar ārum irumpu tirittaṉṉa aṟal maruppu erumi eṉa muṉṉum (1, 15-6) vantami niṉivukūrka.
urittiṟam niliyiṭalāvatu, tollāciriyar pāṭṭu kaṭku  ituvō atuvōveṉa ētum nilippaṭāmal mayaṅkak kiṭakkum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa urip poruḷkaḷil vaṉmiyuḷḷatu ituvē yeṉat tuṇintu nūlāciriyar karutti niliyiṭutal eṉka. āciriyar nakkīraṉār iyaṟṟiya tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭikku uriyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uriyē iññāṉṟuñ ciṟantu viḷaṅkutalkoṇṭum itu teḷiyappaṭum.
(74-82) niccalum -  nāṭōṟum, varukkiccuḷiyum porukkari māvum koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻiyuñ ceḻuñcuvik kaṉṉalum oruṅku talimayaṅkiya arumperum kalaviyiṉ - palāccuḷiyum porukkari māmpaḻamuṅ koḻumiyāṉa vāḻikkaṉiyum vaḷamāṉa iṉiya cuviyuḷḷa carukkariyum oṉṟucērntu  kalanta arumi perumiyuṭiya iṉcuvik kūṭṭi oppa, aruñcol vaḻakkamum - ariya ceñcoṟ kōppum, tiruntiya naṭiyum - tiruttamāṉa ceyyuḷnaṭiyum, vaṇṇavēṟṟumiyum - palappala vaṇṇappāṭṭukaḷiṉ vakikaḷum, taṇ eṉum oḻukkamum - avaṟṟiṉ kuḷirnta ōcinaṭiyum, oṉṟu nirampiya kuṉṟāt tiruppukaḻ vēṟuvēṟu iyampiya vīṟu uṟutavattiṉ aruṇakiri eṉum periyaṉum allēṉ - oruṅku niṟinta nalaṅ kuṟiyāta tiruppukaḻ eṉṉun tēvarīratu pukaḻmāliyip palavēṟu vakippaṭat tiruvāy malarntaruḷiya mēṉmimikka tavapperumiyiṉi yuṭiya aruṇakirinātar eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōṉum allēṉ;
`niccalum iyampiya veṉka; `nicca eṉuñ col nāṭōṟum eṉṉum poruṭṭātal niccamum peṇpāṟkuriya veṉpa eṉṉun tolkāppiyakkaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattiṟ kāṇka (8); `nicca niccal eṉat tirintatu;  `niccal ēttu nelvāyilār eṉat tēvārattum pōntatu; iccol vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu `nitya eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
porukku - takaṭṭuvaṭivāy veṭikkum vaṇṭal, māmarattiṉ aṭi atupōl veṭittiruttaliṟ porukkarimā eṉṟār; itaṉip poriyari yeṉṟuṅ kūṟupa.
palāccuḷipōl ovvoru collum aṭivē patintu niṟṟalum, māṅkaṉipōl ellāccollun tiraṇṭu oru  poruṇ mēlavāy iṉimi payantu niṟṟalum, vāḻiyaṅkaṉi cīppuccīppāyp pirintu kāṇappaṭutalpōṟ ceyyuṭōṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ vēṟu vēṟāyppirintu iṉitu tikaḻtaluṅ, karampiṉ pākupōl avvaṇṇaṅkaḷ kuḷirnta ōciyiṉavāy oḻukutalum nālvēṟuvamikaḷāṟ kuṟittār.
vīṟu ciṟappu (tirukkuṟaḷ, 665): vīṟu uṟutavam - mēṉmēṟ ciṟakkun tavam.
(83-88) nūl iṭi vitta vāliya poruḷiṉum - nūlkaḷiṉiṭiyil nūlāciriyarāṟ peytu vikkappaṭṭa tūya poruḷkaḷiyum, uriyiṭi virinta puri ami teḷiviṉum - annūlkaṭku uriyāciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa urikaḷiṉuḷ virintuḷḷa uyarvu poruntiya teḷivuṇar viṉiyum, kēṭpōr uṇarap pāṟpaṭa vakuttu - pāṭaṅkēṭkum māṇavarkaḷ teḷivāy uṇarumpaṭi avaṟṟip pala pakutikaḷāka vakuttukkoṇṭu, niṉpukaḻ aṉittumtaṉperu matiyiṉ tēvarīruṭiya pukaḻtakka iyalpukaḷi yellāntamatu akaṉṟa aṟiviṉāl uḷamkoḷa viḷakkum vaḷam keḻu pulami nārāyaṇaṉ eṉum kuruvaṉum  allēṉ - ammāṇavariṉ uḷḷaṅ koḷḷum muṟiyil viḷakkavalla vaḷamicāṉṟa pulamiyiṉiyuṭiya nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷi yeṉṉun tamiḻāciriyaṉum   allēṉ;
`vāliya,iṅkut tūyaveṉṉum poruṭṭu; (piṅkalanti, 4058)
poruḷiṉum teḷiviṉum eṉpaṉa iraṇṭaṉ ṟoki;
`puri uyarviṉ mēlatu. puri yuyarvākum (tolkāp piyam, col, 302).
pulamikku vaḷamiyāvatu, aiyamiṉṟit teḷitalun teḷivuṇṭāka viḷakkutalumākum.
`nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷi yeṉpār aṭikaṭkuc centamiḻ aṟivuṟuttiya tamiḻāciriyarāvar. kuruvaṉ. āciriyaṉ; vāṉōr kuruvaṉē pōṟṟi  eṉat tiruvācakattil (5, 68) iccol ipporuṭṭāy vantatu.
`kurucil eṉṉuñ colluṅ kuruveṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṟ ṉōṉṟiyatākum. kuru = oḷi, aṟivoḷiyi yuṭiyavaṉ, ataṉit tarupavaṉ kuruveṉap paṭṭāṉ. vaṭamoḻikkaṭ ceṉṟa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟu.
(89-97) kaḷaviyal taṉakkup palapaṭa iyampi-  `kaḷaviyal eṉa vaḻaṅkum iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷukkup pala vakiyākap poruḷkūṟi, paḷakuaṟu naṉporuḷ teḷiyātu uḻaṉṟa pulavōr kaḷippa nalamuṟat tōṉṟi - avaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟam nīṅkiya nalluri īteṉat teḷiyamāṭṭāmal uḷḷam alivuṟṟa kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarkaḷ tamatu varuttam nīṅkik kaḷikkumāṟu avarmuṉ naṉmiyuṇṭāka eḻuntaruḷivantu, meypporuḷ kāṭṭit tamiḻ vaḻippaṭuttum - uṇmiyuri ituvēyeṉa āciriyar nakkīratēvar eḻutiya  uriyik kāṭṭuvittu akattamiḻp poruḷi oru vaḻippaṭuttiyum, pul amaṇ mikuntu naluṇarvu aḻintu- muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiruppi uṇarāmiyiṉ iḻipuṟṟa camaṇ camayam paravi iṟimutaṟ poruḷaṟivu vāynta civacamaya nalluṇarvu keṭṭu, perum peyar vaḻutiyum aruntamiḻk kūṭalum uyvaḻi kāṇātu uḻitarukāli - periya pukaḻvāynta pāṇṭimaṉṉaṉum ariya centamiḻppayiṟci mikka maturiyampatiyum naṉṉeṟippaṭṭu uyyum vakikaḷ kāṇāmal alivuṟṟuk kiṭantapoḻutu, oy eṉap pōntu mey neṟi kāṭṭiyum - tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉāka virintu vantu tōṉṟic civameṉappaṭum meyvaḻi tēṟṟi viḷakkiyaruḷiyum, taṇ aruḷ purinta niṉṉē - pōla iṉiya tiruvaruḷ ceyta tēvarīrē pōla;
iṟiva ṉaruḷāṟ peṟṟa iṟiyaṉārākap poruḷ eṉṉum akattami ḻilakkaṇattiṟku aññāṉṟip pulavar palaruṅ kaṇṭa urikaḷuḷ nakkīraṉāruriyē ciṟantateṉap pulappaṭuttiya uruttiracaṉmar murukappirāṉ piṟaviyē yeṉṉum akkaḷaviyal urippāyiram.
taṉ, cāriyi; paḷaku, kuṟṟam; paḷakaṟuttuṭiyāṉ kaḻal paṇintili (tiruvācakam, tiruccatakam, 35) eṉpatiṟpōla.
`oy, virivupporuḷatu; iḵtipporuṭṭātal nuṅkiyatu kēḷā vaḷavi yoy yeṉa eṉṉum porunarāṟṟup paṭi (152) yaṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum.
`niṉṉē, ēkāram tēṟṟam; iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ neṭumāṟaṉ eṉṉuṅ kūṉpāṇṭiya ṉāvaṉ.
murukapperumāṉē tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉāka eḻuntaruḷivantu pāṇṭināṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu amaṇ aḻittuc centamiḻt tēvārattāṟ civam viḷakkiyaruḷiṉāreṉṟu nampiyāṇṭār nampikaḷ, mayilēntiya vaḷḷal taṉṉi yaḷippa matipuṇarnta eyilēntiya caṇpi nātaṉ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc ceyyuṭkaḷil nuvalutal kāṇka (tiruñāṉa campantar tiruvantāti, 64.)
(98-108) centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virittu - centamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya vaḻakkumuṟikaḷi mutalil virittu viḷaṅkacceytu, viṭi ñāyiṟu eṉa - viṭiyaṟkālattil eḻukiṉṟa pakalavaṉippōla,  muṭinilic civam ēṉi ellāccamayaṅkaṭkum muṭinta niliyil vīṟṟiruppatāṉa uyarnta civacamayattiṉ, pim katir virittu - putiyavāṉa aṟivoḷi kaḷi eṅkum virittu, kaṅkulil kūmpiya viri aviḻtāmari puri uravōr uḷam puriñekiḻntu alarat tarum iyal ciṟappa - iraviṉkaṭ kuvinta maṇam parappun takiyavāṉa tāmari moṭṭukaḷi okkum aṟiñar uḷḷaṅkaḷ kaṭṭaviḻntu malarumpaṭi ceyyum āṟṟal ciṟantutōṉṟa, uriyiṉum karuttiṉum vari ami nōkkiṉum poruntak kāṭṭi varuntiṟaṉ mikuttu - collālum accolliṉ karuttālum aḷavaminta nōkkālum poruttamāka viḷakki varum valimi yiṉi mikuttukkoṇṭu malivupaṭum uḷḷattu aṟivoṭu kūṭāc ciṟupuṉ māntar kuṟumoḻi kaḷintu - muṉpiṉ aṟivu māṟupaṭum uḷḷaṅkaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ meyyaṟivoṭu poruntāta ciṟiyarāṉa iḻinta makkaḷiṉ ciṟu coṟkaḷi nīkki, niṉ pukaḻ virikkum aṉpil ciṟanta cōmacuntara kuruvaṉum allēṉ - tēvarīruṭiya aruṭpukaḻkaḷiyē viritturikkum pēraṉ piṟ ciṟanta cōmacuntara āciriyaṉum allēṉ;
centamiḻ nūl aṟiviṉiṉṟi annūliṉkaṭ ceṟit tuṇarttappaṭṭirukkum muṭinilic civavuṇmikaḷi viḷakkutal ēlātākaliṉ, `centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virippā rāyiṉareṉpatu.
takuti vāynta makkaṭkaṉṟi ēṉiyōrkkuc camaya vaṟivunūṟ poruḷ aṟivuṟukkap paṭāvākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉan takuti vāyntu kēṭpāri ēṉit takutiyillā makkaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟu pirittuc ciṟappittal vēṇṭumākaliṉ avar `uravōr eṉṟum, avaratu nalluḷḷam `viriyaviḻ tāmari yeṉṟuñ ciṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum avaraccamayavaṟivunūṟ poruḷ kaḷi ārāycci muṟiyiṟ kēṭṭu aruḷmaṇam viriyap peṟāmiyiṉ, avaruṭiya takuti vāynta viḻumiya uḷḷaṅkaḷ malarun takutiyi luḷḷa mukikaḷi oppavē uḷḷaṉaveṉṟaṟkuk kaṅkuliṟ kūmpiya viriyaviḻ tāmari puri uravōr uḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. ivvariya uvamikaḷiṉ nuṭpa aḻaku iṅkup peritum uḷḷuṇarntu makiḻaṟ pālatākumeṉka.
uriyiṉum karuttiṉum nōkkiṉu meṉpavaṟṟiṟku mūṉṟaṉurupu virittukkoḷka; uri-col; karuttu - coṟporuḷ; nōkku - poruḷi nuṉittuk kāṇṭal.
`ciṟupuṉ māntar ēṉic camayattavar; kuṟumoḻi, avaratu poruḷ nirampāc ciṟumiyuṭiya col, muṭinta meyccamaya mākiya civattiṉ mēṉmiyi yuṇarum pēraṟiviṉṟi vēṟu camayap poyp poruḷkaḷi yellām vīṇ paṟṟuḷḷattāl vaṟitē virittu vaḻakkiṭṭut tiritaliṉ avarellāñ `ciṟupuṉ mānta reṉavum, avaratu poruḷil moḻi `kuṟumoḻi yeṉavum iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
ip pakutiyiṟ cōmacuntara kuravar tiruñāṉa campantarōṭu uvamikkappaṭutal kaṇṭukoḷḷappaṭuma, cōmacuntara kuravar tiruñāṉa campantarēpōl, itteṉṉāṭeṅkum māyāvāta vētāntam, pāñcarāttiram mutalāṉa ayal mataṅkaḷ taliyeṭukka voṭṭāmal avaṟṟiṉ ciṟumiyi eṭuttuka kāṭṭic ciṟanta cittānta civattiṉ perumiyi naṉku viḷakki aḵtu iṉitu taḻittōṅkumāṟu ceyta periyārāvar. ivaratu camayat toṇṭiṉ viḻuppam aṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya cōmacuntarak kāñcic ceyyuṭkaḷāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
(109-114) poruḷ peritu īṭṭap perukuṟum avāviṉum - celvatti nirampavuñ cērppataṟku mikukiṉṟa avāviṉ kaṇṇum, īṭṭiya oḷ poruḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi makkaḷum maṉiviyum tuykka nalki niṉkaḻal vaṇaṅkā iṉṉā maṭiyiṉum - aṅṅaṉan tokutta mēṉmiyāṉa poruḷi mēlum avā mikuntuperukkit tam putalvarkaḷum maṉiviyum nukarakkoṭuttut tēvarīruṭiya tiruvaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkāta tīya cōmpaliṉ kaṇṇum, ariya nāṭkaḷi vaṟitē pōkki - ēḻiyēṉuṭiya ariya vāḻ nāṭkaḷi vīṇāyk kaḻittu, aṟam tiṟampuḷiyil ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉ - aṟanili piṟaḻum iṭattu mikutiyuñ ciṟanta viḷaṅkiṉēṉ;
immi maṟumip payaṉkaḷip payattaliṟ, poruḷ `oṇporuḷ eṉappaṭṭatu; oṇporuḷ kāḻppa iyaṟṟi yārkku eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷukkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟiya uriyik kāṇka (760).
`ivaṟal, avā mikal; īṭṭamivaṟi icivēṇṭā āṭavar (1003) eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ.
`makkaḷum maṉiviyun tuykka nalki yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippu apporuḷik kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkam mutaliya nalvaḻikaḷiṟ celavaḻiyāmiyik kuṟittatu.
`tiṟampu  uḷi tiṟampum iṭattu; uḷi iṭattu eṉa ēḻaṉurupi ṇaṉ poruḷil vantatu; iyalpuḷik kōlōccum eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟpōla (545) uḷi mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷ paṭuvatōr iṭiccol eṉṟa parimēlaḻakiyāruriyi maṟuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mantira vitiyiṉ marapuḷivaḻā eṉpuḻi muṟimiyiṉ vaḻuvāta antaṇareṉa aintāvatu viritalāṉum, `iyalpuḷik kōlōccum maṉṉavaṉ eṉpuḻi muṟimiyilē ceṅkōl naṭāttum eṉa ēḻaṉurupu viritalāṉum uḷi yeṉpatu mūṉṟaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantataṉṟu. eṉak kūṟumuri niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
`ciṟantaṉeṉ eṉpatil eṉ taṉmi yorumi viṉimuṟṟu vikuti (tolkāppiyam, viṉiyiyal, 6).
aṟantiṟampā neṟiyiṟ ciṟantu niṟkavēṇṭuvatāyirukka atu ceytilēṉ eṉa oḻinta poruḷi yuṇarttutaliṉ `maṉ oḻiyicikkaṇ vantatu.
(115-122) iṉiya tīyeṉ niṉ niṉivarum tiruvaṭi - ittakiya tīmiyuṭiyēṉ tēvarīruṭiya niṉittaṟ keṭṭāt tiruvaṭikaḷi, viḻinīr uṟippa - kaṇṇīr tuḷippa, muḻumeyum paṉippa - muḻu uṭampum naṭuṅka, niri niri vārātu uri naṉi kuḻaṟa - muṟi muṟiyēvarāmaṟ pēccukaḷ mikavun talitaṭumāṟi vara, poṟivaḻi aṟivum  ceṟiyum uṭkaruviyum palavaḻik kavarātu oruvaḻinikaḻa - meyvāy mutaliya aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aṟivum av vimpoṟikaḷoṭu nerukkamuṟum maṉam mutaliya akak karuvikaḷum palavaḻiyākap pirintu pōkāmal orēvaḻiyāy nikaḻa, aṉpu uruvākum niṉ toṇṭar pōla - aṉpiṉ  uruvamāy amiyun tēvariṉ tiruttoṇṭarkaḷi oppa, nīṅkā aṉpil niliniṉṟu - aṟāta pēraṉpil uṟutiyāy nilittu, yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō pāṅkupaṭamoḻimō - eṅṅaṉam aṭivēṉō urimiyuṇṭākak kūṟuvīrāka veṉpatu.
icceyyuḷ āṇṭavaṉiyē viḷittut tamatu tāḻnili kūṟumukattāl, avaratu aruḷnili peṟutal ariteṉak kūṟiya vāṟām. `moḻimō veṉpatil `mō muṉṉili yaciccol (tolkāppiyam, iṭiccolliyal, 26); kāmañ ceppātu kaṇṭatu moḻimō eṉṉum kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷiṟ pōla (2).
niṉ niṉivarun tiruvaṭi (115) aṉpuruvāku niṉ toṇṭar pōla aṉpil niliniṉṟu (120-1) yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō (122) eṉak kūṭṭuka.
`uṟittal, tuḷittal; iccol ipporuṭṭātal teṇpaṉi yuṟikkuṅ kāl eṉṉuṅ kalittokiyāl (15) teḷiyappaṭum.
paṉittal naṭuṅkutal; narimuka vūkam pārppoṭu paṉippa eṉak kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷil (249) iccol ipporuṭṭāy varutal kāṇka.
`kavartal, palavēṟākap pirivupaṭal; antaṇ kāviri vantukavarpu ūṭṭa (puṟanāṉūṟu, 35) eṉpuḻip pōla.
viḻinīruṟittalum meypaṉittalum uri kuḻaṟalum aṟivum ēṉi uṭkaruvikaḷum oru vari nikaḻtalumellām aṉpuruvākun toṇṭarkaṭku aṭiyāḷamāku meṉpatu.
meytā ṉarumpi vitirvitirt tuṉviri yārkaḻaṟkeṉ
  kitāṉ ṟalivittuk kaṇṇīr tatumpi vetumpiyuḷḷam
  poytāṉ ṟavirntuṉṉip pōṟṟi cayacaya pōṟṟiyeṉṉuṅ
  kitā ṉekiḻa viṭēṉuṭi yāyeṉṉik kaṇṭukoḷḷē
eṉṉum maṇivācakap perumāṉatu tiruvācakat tēṉuriyiṉ kaṇ (tiruccatakam, 1) iṉitu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. pāṅku - urimi (tivākaram); niṉṟiruvaṭikaḷip peṟutaṟ kurimi yillāta eḷiyaṉēṉ ataṉip peṟutaṟ kurimiyuṭiyaṉāmāṟu kūṟuvīrāka eṉṟavāṟu. (19)
eṉavē, `iḷiyōy (9) periyōy (17) eṉa ellām valla iṟivaṉi viḷittuc `cūr mutalpōlap pēraṉpillēṉ (51) kīraṉpōla (61) āṟṟuppaṭi colum āṟṟalu millēṉ (66) tāṉē taṉakku nikareṉa viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉu mallēṉ (73-4) aruṇakiriyeṉum periyaṉu mallēṉ (82) nārāyaṇa ṉeṉuṅ kuruvaṉu mallēṉ (88) cōmacuntara kuruvaṉu mallēṉ (108)aṟan tiṟam puḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉ (114) iṉiya tīyeṉ niṉniṉivarun tiruvaṭi (115) yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō (122) eṉṟu avaṉatu aruḷnili peṟutal ariteṉak kūṟiyavāṟu.
20. tōḻi kiḷḷiyit tūtuvēṇṭal
  moḻiyuṅ kuḻaṟa muḻuvuṭampum pittu
  viḻiyun tuyilkūṭā veyyōṉ - paḻiyit
  tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟ kuriyāy kiḷiyē
  yuruvuṉṉi yoppa tuṇarntu.
(i-ḷ) moḻiyum kuḻaṟa- collum taṭumāṟa, muḻu uṭampum pittu - muḻuuṭampum pacantu, viḻiyum tuyil kūṭā veyyōḷ - taṉṉi virumpiya eṉ arumit taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷun tūkkam poruntā; paḻiyi - ūravar alarukku ētuvāṉa aṭakkutaṟkariya ittuyara nilikaḷi, tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟku uriyāy - tiruvoṟṟi murukaṟku eṭuttuc colvāyāka, kiḷiyē uru uṉṉi oppatu uṇarntu - arumik kiḷiyē! teḷiviṉṟic collāṭalālum muḻu vuṭampum pacantu niṟṟalāluṅ kaṇṇimikaḷ tuyil kūṭāmiyālum eṉ taliviyiṉ uruvum uṉṉiyē ottu uṉakku iṉamātal uṇarntu eṉpatu;
`uṇarntu uriyāy eṉak koḷka.
`pittu pacumi eṉpataṉaṭiyākap piṟanta viṉiyeccam; `pi eṉpatē pacciniṟatti yuṇarttum eṉṉum piṅkalanti.
murukappirāṉi virumpi niṉṟāḷākaliṉ, talivi veyyōḷeṉap paṭṭāḷ; vemmi, viruppam; aṉṟik kātal veppam uṭiyāḷ eṉiṉum poruntum; aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ ac collukku `veppameṉap poruḷ kūṟikkoḷka.
iṉi ivviraṇṭu poruḷkaḷumē iṅkup poruntu mākaliṉ ataṉi iraṭṭuṟa moḻintu koḷḷalumām.
kiḷḷi tūtupōtaṟku iyiyumeṉak kāraṇaṅ kaṟpitta tiṟam itaṉkaṇ mika aḻakitātal kaṇṭu makiḻtalvēṇṭum (20)
21. aruṇili yurittal
  uṇarntā raṟiviṉun tōṉṟā toḷikku morumuruka
  ṉiṇarntār curikuḻaṉ maṅkiya rōṭeṉ ṉiruviḻiyiṟ
  puṇarntā reyiloṟṟi mēviya vāṟu puṉiṟucellā
  vaṇantāḻ kuḻaviyit tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmiyaṉṟē
(i-ḷ) uṇarntār aṟiviṉum tōṉṟātu oḷikkum oru murukaṉ - tāṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷān taṉmiyi uṇarntava raṟiviṉukkum veḷippaṭāmal oḷikkum oppaṟṟa murukappirāṉ, iṇarntuār curikuḻal maṅkiyarōṭu eṉ iruviḻiyil puṇarntu - neruṅki niṟinta curuṇṭa kūntalkaḷiyuṭiya vaḷḷi teyvayāṉiyeṉṉum ammiyar pākarāy eḻuntaruḷivantu eṉ iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷiluṅ kalantavarāy, eyil oṟṟi mēviyavāṟu - matilkaḷ uyarnta tiruvoṟṟinakaric cērntavaki, puṉiṟu cellā vaṇam tāḻ kuḻaviyit tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmi yaṉṟē - īṉṟaṇimi kaḻiyāta pacciḷantaṉmi poruntiya kuḻantiyit tāyē virumpic ceṉṟu taḻuviya aruṭ ṭaṉmiyēyāmaṉṟō; eṉpatu.
kaṭavuḷiṉ uṇmiyuṇarntālum avaṉi meyyaṉpāṟ karinturuki vaḻipaṭātārkku avaṉ nērē tōṉṟā toḷintiruntē aruḷpurivaṉākaliṉ, uṇarntā raṟiviṉun tōṉṟātoḷikku moru murukaṉ eṉṟār; uṇarntārk kuṇarvariyōṉ tillic ciṟṟampalattoruttaṉ eṉṉun tirukkōviyāruriyum ikkaruttē paṟṟi vantatu.
iṇarntu - neruṅki; iṇariya ñāṭpiṉuḷ eṉṟār kaḷavaḻiyilum (34).
uṇarntāraṟiviṉun tōṉṟātoḷikkum orumurukaṉ ētumaṟiyā ēḻiyēṉ iruviḻiyiṟ puṇarntāreṉṟu tammit tāḻtturittal karutip, `puṉiṟucellā vaṇam tāḻ kuḻavi yeṉṟu kuḻaviyi ētumaṟiyāp puṉiṟṟaṇimip paruvattil vittu vitantaruḷiṉār. kuḻantiyit tāy ceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmiyi iṅku uvamiyākak koḷka. vaṇmiyi oppataṉṟē yeṉṟu urittukkoḷka. (21)
22. eḻil nalaṅkūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttal
(1-9) vaṇpuṉal niliyil taṇaṟal pōlavum - vaḷamāṉa nīr nilikaḷiṉ aṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭuṅ kuḷirnta karumaṇali oppavum, tāmari moytta vaṇṭiṉam pōlavum - tāmari malarkaḷi mūciya kariya vaṇṭukkūṭṭaṅkaḷi oppavum, paḷiṅkil tōynta nīlniṟam pōlavum - oru paḷikkuk kaṇṇāṭiyiṉkaṇ ūṭuruvit tōṉṟuṅ karunīlaniṟatti oppavum, tirumuka maruṅkiṉ varumuṟi tuṇarntu - tirumukattiṉ pakkattē oḻuki varum vakiyil aṭara vaḷarntu, āṅku iṭiiṭi cuḻaṉṟu nīṇṭu kaṭi kuḻaṉṟu - aṅṅaṉamē iṭiyiṭiyē cuḻintu nīḷamāy vaḷarntu nuṉicuruṇṭu, kaṇ oḷi katuvum kuñciyi - kaṇṇoḷiyik kavarun talimayiriṉi; aṉṉi irupāl kūṟiṭṭu orupāl cūḻiyum maṟṟorupāl paṉicciyum tiruvatiṉ amippa - umippirāṭṭiyārāṉa tāyār irupirivāka vakuttu orupirivil uccikkoṇṭiyum maṟṟorupirivil piṉṉal muṭippuñ cevvitiṉ amippa, naṟumkatuppu oḷirum perum ciṟappāṉum - avvāṟṟāl iṉiya at talimayir mēṉmēlum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa periya mēṉmiyiṉālum;
puṉal nili, nīrnilikaḷ; avi `vaṇpuṉalnili yeṉappaṭṭa miyiṉ, eṉṟum aṟāta vaḷamāṉa nīroḻukkum nīrpperukkum uṭiya āṟukaḷiṉ maṭukkaḷ vāvikaḷ mutalāṉa nīrnilikaḷē īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum; avaṟṟiṉkīḻ iṭiyiṭiyē karumaṇaṟ parappukaḷ kāṇappaṭutalum iṅku niṉivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
puṉalum paḷiṅkum mukattiṉ teḷivukkun, tāmari mukattiṉ cevviya vaṭivukkum, aṟalum vaṇṭiṉamum nīṉiṟamum at tirumukattiṉ pakkaliṟ carintukiṭakkun talimayirukkum uvamikaḷāy niṉṟaṉa.
`kuñci, āṇpāṉ mayir; mēṟ katuppeṉa varutalum atu. iṭiyiṭi curintu nīṇṭu kaṭi kuḻalaluṅ kaṇṇoḷiyik kavarumāṟu karumiyiṟ paḷapaḷappuṭimiyum aḻakiya talimayiriṉ iyalpākum.
`tuṇarntāṅku eṉpatil, āṅku, aṅṅaṉamē eṉṉum poruṭṭu; tuṇarntu - kottāka neruṅkivaḷarntu; `tūmaruḷiruḷ tuṇarntaṉiya kuñciyaṉ eṉṟār cūḷā maṇiyilum (kumārakālac carukkam, 6).
katuval - kavartal; paḻaṉavāḷika tūum ūraṉ eṉṟār kuṟuntokiyilum (8).
`cūḻi yeṉpatu uccikkoṇṭi (piṅkalanti); `paṉicci yeṉpatu piṉṉi vaḷittu muṭittalām; iḵtu aivaki muṭippukaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉka (tivākaram).
tiruvatu, cevvitu, eṉṟāṟpōl īṟupeṟṟu vantatu; tiruvattavar (nālaṭiyār, 57) eṉap piṟavāṟum varutal kāṇka.
ippakuti murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ aḻakiya talimayirc ciṟappiṉik kūṟi makiḻntavāṟām.
(10-12) cemmaṇip palakiyiṉ - civanta māṇikkak kallālākiya oru palakiyiṉkaṇ, ammaṇikuyiṟṟi - aḻakiya nīlamaṇikaḷi aḻutti, varampu uṟa viṭutta vaṇṇampōla - appalakiyiṉ ōrattiṟ porunta amitta vakipōla, puruvakkoṭi paṭarnta - karuniṟamāṉa puruvameṉṉuṅ koṭiyāṉatu ōṭiya, virinutalāṉum - akaṉṟa neṟṟiyiṉālum;
cemmaṇi, māṇikkamaṇi, atu civantirutataliṉ. ammaṇi - aḻakiya nīlamaṇi; `maṇi eṉṉuñ col taṉṉiyalpil nīlamaṇiyiṉiyē yuṇarttal maṇi nīlamaṇiyē eṉṉun tivākarattiṟ kāṇka. am - aḻaku (piṅkalanti).
kuyiṟṟi - aḻutti; iḵtipporuṭṭātal marakata maṇiyoṭu vayiraṅ kuyiṟṟiya eṉpuḻik kāṇka (cilappatikāram, 5, 147, aṭiyārkku nallāruri).
cemmaṇip palakikkuk karuniṟa varampu amiyuṅ kāṭci aḻakitātal niṉivukūrka. vaṇṇam, vaki.
(13-15) annutal kiḷarnta poṉpōl cevviḻi - anneṟṟiyiṉkaṇ eriyum poṉ otta civanta neṟṟik kaṇṇāṉatu, utti eṉṉum tuttip pi aravu muṭimēl maṇi eṉa - piḷḷiyāratu neṟṟimēl umip pirāṭṭiyār toṅkaviṭṭa utti yeṉṉum aṇikalamāṉatu poṟikaḷ vāynta paṭattiṉiyuṭiya oru ciṟu nākampōṟ ṟōṉṟāniṟka annākattiṉ talimēṟ kāṇappaṭuñ civanta māṇikka maṇiyiṉi yoppat taṅki yiruttalāṉum;
piḷḷiyāratu neṟṟikkaṇ, avaratu neṟṟimēṟ ṟoṅkaviṭap paṭṭa uttiyeṉṉum aṇikalattiṉ muṉiyiṟ patitta māṇikka maṇipōṟ civappāy oḷirntateṉṟum, avvuttiyākiya aṇikalam oru ciṟu nākappāmpiṉiyum avvaṇikalattiṉ muṉiyiṟ patitta cemmaṇi annākattiṉ uccikkaṇṇatāṉa māṇikkattiṉiyum ottiruntaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟiyapaṭi.
utti - aṇikala (āparaṇa)t toṅkal (tivākaram). tutti - poṟi (tivākaram). pi - paṭam (piṅkalanti).
(16 - 18) veḷ paṉit tiraḷi - veṇṇiṟamāṉa paṉik kaṭṭiyi, kaṇṇuṟappōḻntu - iṭam iṭippaṭap piḷantu, tuvar niṟam ūṭṭi - ataṉkaṭ civanta niṟattiyum ēṟṟi, civaṇuṟa amittāṅku - poruttamuṟa amittāṟ pōla, irupuṟam katuppu maruvutalāṉum - mukattiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷiluṅ kaṉṉaṅkaḷa aṇintu tōṉṟutalālum;
`pōḻnteṉṟatu veṭṭuvāyiṟ ṟōṉṟum paḷapaḷap piṉi uvamittaṟkeṉṟu koḷka. tuvar niṟam, cenniṟam; itaṉkaṇ eccavummi tokkatu. `tuvar civappu niṟatti yuṇarttutal tuvaritaḻc cevvāyt tuṭiyiṭiyōyē (cilappatikāram, 6, 26) eṉpatiṟ kāṇka.
`katuppeṉṟatu kaṉṉaṅkaḷi (tivākaram).
(19-20) kuḻiyoṭu keḻumum taḻi cevi iraṇṭum - kuṇṭalaṅkaḷoṭu poruntum vaḷarnta kātukaḷ iraṇṭum, ō eṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvutalāṉum - ō vecaṉṉum ōṅkāra vaṭivattuṭaṉ amintu viḷaṅkutalāṉum;
kuḻi - kuṇṭalam; miṉṉukkuḻiyum poṟṟōṭum miḷira eṉṟār cīvakacintā maṇiyilum (16 58). ōṭuvurupu ataṉōṭiyinta oppalerāppuri (tolkāppiyam, col, 75).
(21 - 23) naṭuvil kuḻintu vaṭṭittu aminta - puṟattē naṭuviṟ kuḻiyāki vaṭṭamā yaminta, paṭupoṉ kiṇṇam - cempoṉṉālāṉa kiṇṇamāṉatu, mukam kaviḻttu aṉṉa - muṉpakkam kaviḻttu vittāṟpōla, mōvāy nivanta muṟimiyāṉum - vāyiṉ mōvāy naṭuviṟ ciṟitu kuḻiyākic cuṟṟi uyarntuviḷaṅkum vakimiyālum,
vaṭṭittal, vaṭṭamātal maruntil kaṇicci varunta vaṭṭittu (puṟam, 42). `paṭupoṉ kiṇṇa meṉpatip `poṉ paṭukiṇṇa meṉak koṇṭu, `poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kiṇṇam eṉap poruḷurittuk koḷka; `nivattal uyartal; āvatu iṅku eṭuttuttōṉṟutal.
(24 - 27) kiḷi ari nāṇal kiḻaṅku - kappukaḷ ariyappaṭṭa nāṇaṟpulliṉ kiḻaṅkāṉatu, maṇaṟku īṉṟa muḷiyum - maṇaliṉ kaṇ viṭṭa veḷḷiya muḷiyum, muruntum - mayiliṟakiṉaṭiyum, viḷinīr muttamum - kaṭalnīril viḷiyum veṇmukattukkaḷum, kūrntu nirintu valleraṉa oḷivirintu eḻuntu oḷir eyiṟṟoṭu poruntuta lāṉuma - kūrmiyāyum variciyāyum valimiyāyum amintu veḷēleṉṟu oḷiviṭṭu niṉṟu viḷaṅkumpaṟkaḷōṭu oṉṟiyamitalāṉum,
mēṟ kappukaḷi yarintu naṭṭa nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku virivil muḷiviṭutalālum, ammuḷi kūrmiyāy veṇṇiṟattavā yiruttalāluṅ kiḷiyari nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaṟ kīṉṟa muḷi uvamiyāka eṭukkappaṭṭatu; ivvaṭi akanāṉūṟṟil tāvil  naṉpoṉ (212) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷilirunteṭutta mēṟkōḷ.
`maṇaṟku itiṟ kuvvurupu ēḻaṉ poruḷil vanta vēṟṟumi mayakkam; nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅkiṉ muḷiyum muruntum muttamum  muṟiyē veṇmiyiṟ kūrmikkum, veṇmiyil varicikkum, veṇmiyil vaṉmikkum uvamikaḷeṉka. īṇṭu varum `oṭu vurupu oppumipporuḷatākum; itu cēṉāvariya ruriṟiyaṟ kāṇka (tolkāppiyam, col 13).
(28 - 30) kovvik kaṉiyum koḻuviya pavaḻamum - civanta kovvip paḻamum ceḻumiyāṉa cenniṟam aminta pavaḻamum, cevviya alleṉa - tamakku nikarāṉa ciṟanta cenniṟa muṭiya valla veṉṟu, ilavu itaḻ puriyum cennīr itaḻoṭu maṉṉutalāṉum - ilavamara malariṉitaḻkaḷi ōkkuñ civanta taṉmiyiyuṭiya utaṭukaḷoṭu poruntutalāṉum,
kovvip paḻattik kiḷḷi kōtutalāṉum, pavaḻakkallik kaṭintutuḷiyiṭṭu makaḷiraṇitalāṉum avipiḷḷiyā ritaḻkaḷukku oppākat takā; maṟṟu ilavamalariṉ itaḻō aṅṅaṉam ūṟupaṭutaliṉmiyiṉ atuvē avaritaḻukku oppākat takkateṉṟavāṟu; ilavitaḻc cevvāy iḷamut tarumpa eṉṟār cilappatikārattilum (14, 136). nīrmi iyalpākiya kuṇam, niṉṉuṭi nīrmiyum eṉṉun tirukkōviyāril iccol ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
(31 - 36) muttak kōvi muṟimuṟi vitta nattuvarc ceppu naṉkaṉam tiṟanteṉa - muttu mālikaḷ varici variciyāy vikkappaṭṭa niṟañ ciṟanta pavaḻac cimiḻiṉ poruttu vāyāṉatu aḻakitākat tiṟantāṟpōlavum, arakku uru ūṭṭiya āmpal naṟumuki taḷi nekiḻntu alarnta taṉmittu eṉṉa - cenniṟattit tōyvitta alliyiṉ nalla pūmoṭṭāṉatu kaṭṭu aviḻntu malarnta viyalpiṉatu pōlavum, muḷ eyiṟu ilaṅka - kūriya paṟkaḷ oḷikāṭṭi viḷaṅka, nakutoṟum nakutoṟum cevvāy tōṉṟum avvāṟṟāṉum - nakikkumpōtu nakikkum pōtellām apperumāṉatu civanta vāyāṉatu viḷaṅkit teriyum avvakiyiṉālum;
nakutoṟum nakutoṟum muḷḷeyiṟu ilaṅkac cevvāy tōṉṟumeṉat toṭaricittuk koḷka. uvamikaḷil muṉṉiyatu paṟkaḷukkum piṉṉiya vāykkumeṉat teḷiyaṟpālaṉa. `nattuvar eṉpatil na - ciṟappupporu ḷuṇarttuvatōriṭic col; nacceḷḷi, nappālattaṉ, nakkīraṉ eṉṟāṟpōla eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (cīvaka cintāmaṇi, 482).
naṉkaṉam -naṉṟāka, aḻakitāka; nāṭaka makaḷirkku naṉkaṉam vakutta eṉpuḻi (maṇimēkali, 2, 30) ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
eṉa, uvamap poruḷil vantatu.
`arakku, civappu; uru, niṟam; arakkuṇ pañcikaḷ tiraṭṭi (kaṉakamāli, 8) eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyil iccol ip poruṭṭātal kāṇka.
(37-39) eḻum iṭam kuḻintu -mutaṟkaṇ eḻukiṉṟa iṭattiṟ kuḻiyāki, viḻumitiṉ oḻuki - cīritāka nīṇṭu, taḷavu arumpu eṉṉa vaḷam kaṉintu ilaṅki - mulli arumpi oppa aḻaku mutirntu viḷaṅki, muṉ eḻu mūkkiṉ taṉmiyāṉum - mukam eḻumpiya mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmiyiṉālum;
vaḷam, aḻaku; kaṉital, iṅku `mutirtal eṉṉum poruṭṭu; kaṉṉimi kaṉiyāk kikkiḷik kāmam eṉṉum paripāṭalil (11, 136) iccol ipporuḷil vantatu.
(40-42) ōr aruḷ kaṭal iṅku īr uru vākik kari puṇarntu kiṭanta muṟimipōla - ōr aruṭṭaṉmiyuṭiya kaṭalāṉatu iṅku iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷāyp pirintu kari poruntik kiṭanta vakimipōla puruvakkoṭi kaṭavāp peruviḻiyāṉum - puruvamākiya oḻuṅkiṉik kaṭantu cellātu amiyum periya viḻikaḷālum,
`aruṭkaṭal eṉappaṭṭatu, kaṇkaḷiṉ aruṭ ṭaṉmiyi eṇṇi; uvamiyik karuttūṉṟuka.
koṭi - paṭarkoṭi, oḻuṅku eṉa iruporuḷum urittuk koḷka; puruvatti nōkkiyavaḻi avi paṭar koṭiyi yottiruntaṉa; kaṇṇākiya kaṭali nōkkiyavaḻi avi ak kaṭalukku vakutta kariyoḻuṅki yottiruntaṉa veṉka.
(43-45) viriaviḻ tāmari naṟi ukutteṉṉa - maṇam virikkuñ centāmari malar tēṉ corintāṟpōla, kēṭṭoṟum iṉikkum pāṭṭoṭu taḻīic ceḻuvāy malarnta moḻiyiṉāṉum - kēṭkum pōtellān teviṭṭātu cuvikkiṉṟa icikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu aḻakiya malarvāyiṉ kaṇiruntu tōṉṟuñ coṟkaḷālum;
pāṭṭu, ici; piḷḷiyāratu malarvāyp piṟakkuñ coṟkaḷ iciyiṉimi vāyntaṉa eṉṟapaṭi.
itu kāṟum murukap pirāṉṟaṉ piḷḷimik kōlatti niṉintu aṇinturittatākum; mēl varuvaṉa avaṉṟaṉ kaṭṭiḷamik kōlattiyun tirumaṇak kōlattiyum niṉintu puṉinturittatākum eṉka.
(46-48) valampuri aṉṉa vāl naṟum kaḻuttoṭum - valam puric caṅki otta veṇmiyāṉa nalla kaḻuttōṭum, ēṟṟiṉattu eruttiṉ murippu eṭuttataṉṉa kuvavip puṭitta piṭariṉālum - ciṟanta ēṟukaḷiṉ iṉatticcērnta eruttu māṭṭiṉ imil uyarntāṟ pōṟ ṟiraṇṭu parutta piṭariṉālum;
tirumēṉiyeṅkum nīṟu pūcappaṭṭiruttaliṉ veṇmi yāṉa kaḻutteṉṟār; iṉi, vāṉ naṟuṅ kaḻuttu eṉappirittu aḻakiya nalla kaḻutteṉalumām; aḻakāvatu caṅkupōl vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa vaṭivuṭiyatāyiruttal.
eṭutteṉṟatu, iṅku uyarnteṉṉumporuṭṭu; kāḷiyān taṉmimikunta imilēṟu eṉṟaṟku `ēṟṟiṉat teruttiṉ eṉappaṭṭatu.
`piṭareṉpatu, puṟaṅkaḻuttu, murippu - imil (cilappatikāra uri, 17).
(49-62) muḻavu eṉac carintu -  mattaḷam pōṟ carivuṭiya tāy, val valam eṉat tiraṇṭu - valiyatiraḷ kalli oppat tiraṭciyuṭiya tāy, vari eṉa nimirntu - maliyippōl uyarntu, piṉ eḻu eṉat tiṇintu - piṉṉum irupput tūṇpōl tiṭpam vāyntu, maliyoṭu poruta mālkaḷiṟu eṉṟu pulavōrkūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭa - maliyiṉoṭu pōrceyta periya āṇyāṉi yeṉṟu pulavarkaḷ urikkum meyyurikku ilakkiyamāka, yāṉi eṉṉum pālnaku tirumoḻi koṅki maruppiṉ nuṅkak kutti orupāl perumpōr viḷippa - teyvayāṉiyeṉṟu collappaṭum amiḻtattiyum ikaḻum iṉcolliṉi yuṭiya ammiyār tamatu koṅkiyākiya kūrṅ kompiṉāl murukavēḷ mārpan taṉṉi viḻuṅka aḻutti orupakkattil mikunta kātaṟpōr ceyya, orupāl - maṟṟoru pakkattil, vaḷḷi yeṉṉum kaḷ aviḻ kuḻalāḷ - vaḷḷiyammiyār eṉappaṭun tēṉcintuṅ kūntaliyuṭiya pirāṭṭiyār, taṭamuli vētiṉ - tamatu akaṉṟa koṅkiyākiya oṟṟaṭattāl, paṭupuṇ oṟṟa uḷam koṇṭu eḻīi - teyvayāṉi yammiyār muṉpu kātaṟpōr ceytamiyāl uṇṭāṉa puṇṇi oṟṟutaṟku uḷam niṉintu eḻuntu, uṟum iṭaṉ paṭāatu orupuṟam oṟṟa varum muṟi nōkki - ap puṇṇuṟṟa iṭattiṟ paṭātapaṭi maṟṟōriṭattil oṉṟutaṟku varun taṉmiyik kaṇṭu, koḻunaṉi piṇit tamarā naṟum toṭali - am murukavēḷ mārpiṟ poruntik kiṭakkiṉṟa koḻumiyāṉa moṭṭukaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa maṇaṅkamaḻuṅ kaṭappa māliyāṉatu, muṟuvalittu eṉṉa muṟukku uṭintu aciya - puṉṉaki purintāṟpōlak kaṭṭuviṭṭu malarntu puraḷa, vāḷvali niliyum tōḷiṉālum - vāṭpaṭiyiṉ vallami nilitta tōḷkaḷālum;
ulam - tiraḷkal (tivākaram). eḻu - irupputtūṇ (tivākaram). 
maliyoṭuporuta mālyāṉi iḷampūraṇar uri (tolkāppiyam, col, 73)
pulavōr kūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭat teyvayāṉi koṅkikaḷ murukavēḷ mārpoṭu porutaṉaveṉka; vaḷḷi mēluḷḷa cīṟṟattāl teyvayāṉi murukappirāṉ mārpoṭu porutaṉaḷeṉavun, taṉ kātalaṟku avaḷ ceyta ūṟu pāṭṭiṉit tīrppāḷpōl vaḷḷi taṉ taṭamuli vētu koṇṭu oṟṟiṉāḷeṉavum orunayan tōṉṟa urittār.
nuṅka - viḻuṅka; mārpiṉkaṇ aḻuntiya kuttiṉi ammārpu ēṟṟu viḻuṅkiyati yottateṉa murukappirāṉukku ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa vēṭkiyiṉik kuṟippiṭṭār; nuṅkal viḻuṅkutaṟ poruṭṭātal cīvakacintāmaṇi (4, 244) nacciṉārk kiṉiya ruriyiṟ kāṇka.
iṭappuṟam amarntirukkum teyvayāṉiyammiyārāl nērnta iṭatupakkattup puṇṇi oṟṟi āṟṟātu, valappuṟam amarntirukkum vaḷḷiyammiyiṉ koṅki perumāṉukku valatu puṟattilēyē poruntumākaliṉ, uṟum iṭaṉ paṭāatu orupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟi nōkki eṉṟaruḷic ceytār.
vētu - vemmi oṟṟaṭam; mulivētiṉ oṟṟi eṉṟār kalittokiyilum (106).
`kaḷḷaviḻ kuḻalāḷ eḻīi orupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟi nōkki eṉat toṭar kūṭṭuka. `eḻutal, iṅku uḷḷaveḻuc ciyik kuṟittatu.
kaṭappamāliyiṉ malar malarntirukkun tōṟṟatti nakuvatākak kūṟiyatu taṟkuṟippēṟṟam.
marā- veṇkaṭampu (tivākaram); piḷḷiyār uvantatu kaṭappa māliyātal uruḷiṇarkkaṭampiṉ olitārōyē eṉṉum paripāṭalālum (5, 81), marāat turuḷ pūntaṇṭār puraḷummārpiṉaṉ eṉṉun tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyālum (10-11) aṟiyappaṭum.
ippakutikaḷiṉ uruvaka uvamik kaṟpaṉiyaḻakukaḷ peritum makiḻaṟpālaṉa vākum.
(63-69) kival vēkaṭi - kivaṉmi yuṭiya maṇiyiḻip pōṉ, aitu uṟak kaṭintu - aḻaku keḻumat tiraṭṭi, vaḻuvaḻuppu ākkiya - maḻamaḻappākac ceyta, koḻum tukirp palaki - vaḷamāḷa pavaḷappalakikaḷ, puṇarntu oruṅku iraṇṭu kiṭantatupōla - iraṇṭu cērntu oṉṟāypporuntik kiṭantatupōla, kaṇakaṉṟu ōṅkiya kaviṉ keḻu mārpattu - iṭam akaṉṟu vaḷarnta aḻaku poruntiya mārpiṉkaṇ, yāṉi maruppu uḻuta cuvaṭupōlavum - yāṉiyiṉ kompu uḻutami yāl uṇṭāṉa aṭiyāḷam pōlavum, variyoṭu paṭarnta vallipōlavum - maliyiṉmēṟ paṭarnta vaḷḷik koṭi pōlavum, vari mūṉṟu oḻukiya tiruviṉāṉum - mūṉṟu kōṭukaḷ nīṇṭu kiṭanta mēṉmiyiṉālum;
vēkaṭi - maṇimācu akaṟṟuvōṉ; tirumaṇikuyiṟṟunari vēkaṭikaḷ eṉpar cilappatikāra urikārar (5, 46). `vēkaṭi tamiḻccol, itu vaṭamoḻikkaṇṇuñ ceṉṟatu. aitu - aḻaku, (cilappatikāruri, 3, 152).
piḷḷiyārtan tirumēṉi niṟañ civappākaliṉ avaratu tirumārpattiṟkup pavaḷappalakiyiṉi uvami kūṟiṉār. yāṉiyeṉṉuṅ kuṟippiṉālum `valli yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippi ṉālum aviyiraṇṭum muṟiyē `teyvayāṉi `vaḷḷi yeṉṉuṅ kātaṟ kiḻattiyariṉ koṅkimukaṭu uriñciya kātaṟceyalkaḷik kuṟippavāyiṉa.
mārpiṉkaṇ varimūṉ ṟoḻukutal ciṟanta āṭavariṉ nallilakkaṇamākum; perumiyiyuṭiya mārpiṟ kiṭakkiṉṟa uttama ilakkaṇamākiya civanta mūṉṟu variyiṉiyum (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyuri, 106) eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivvuṇmiyi naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟuvar. variyakaṉ mārpiṭi variyu mūṉṟuḷa (kēmacariyā rilampakam, 51) eṉpatu cintāmaṇi.
(70-73) maḻiyum maraṉum kuḻintu oruṅkuōṭa - nīr curanta puyaluṅ kaṟpakamaramuñ cōrntu uṭaṉkuliya, viḻumiya poruḷpala vēṇṭinarkku utavum - ciṟanta poruḷkaḷpalavum vēṇṭiya aṭiyārkku aḷikkum, tāḷ toṭu taṭakkiyil muḻantāḷit toṭum aḷavu nīṇṭa periyakiyil, tāy uvantu ītta - aṉṉiyākiya umippirāṭṭi makiḻntu koṭutta, vālitiṉ viḷaṅkum vēliṉāṉum veḷḷitāka miḷirum vēṟpaṭiyiṉālum;
`maraṉ īṇṭuk kaṟpakamaram; aḵtu intira ṉulakil uḷḷatu; maḻiyuṅ kaṟpakamum intira ṉulakiṟkuk kīḻ maṇṇulakiṟ payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷi maṭṭumē taravallaṉa vaṉṟi, vāṉulakiṟku mēluḷḷa uyarnta tēvar nilikaḷiyum aviyiṟṟiṟkum mēlāṉa pēriṉpa vīṭṭulakiṉiyun taravallaṉa alla. maṟṟup, piḷḷiyār tantirukkikaḷō maṇṇulaka viṉpaṅkaḷoṭu, viṇṇulaka viṉpaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟkum mēlāka vīṭṭulaka viṉpamum aḷikka vallaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
āṇmakkaḷiṟ ciṟantārtaṅ kikaḷ muḻantāḷaḷavum nīṇṭiruttal, tāṭṭāḻ taṭakkit taṉimati veṇkuṭiyāṉ eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālic ceyyuḷāṉ uṇarka. `ītta koṭutta eṉap poruḷ taral cempoṉ vayiriyarkku ītta (puṟam, 9) eṉpuḻiyuṅ kāṇka.
(74-77) pālnuri mukanta paricoṭu porūum - pāliṉ nuriyi aḷḷi eṭutta taṉmiyi okkum, tūveḷ aṟuvi acii - tūya veḷḷiya āṭiyi uṭuttu, mūvaki maṭiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅki - mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa maṭippukaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅki, vaṭivuṭaṉ carintu - aḻakiya vaṭivuṭaṉ carivuṭiyatāy, naṭattoṟum kuluṅkum vayiṟṟiṉāṉum - naṭakkuntōṟuṅ kuluṅki yacikiṉṟa vayiṟṟiṉālum;
`pālnuri, tūymikkum veṇmikkum, meṉmikkum uvamiyākaliṉ, `tūveḷḷaṟuvi yeṉa aṟuvi vitanturikkappaṭṭatu. `aṟuvi, taṟiyil nīḷaneytatu piṉ tuṇṭutuṇṭāy aṟukkappaṭutaliṉ vanta peyar.
`acii, uṭuttu, iḵtipporuṭṭātal, pulittōli arikku acittu (maḻapāṭi, 1) eṉavaruñ cuntarar tēvārattāl teḷiyappaṭum.
`maṭi, eṉṟatu, iṅku vayiṟṟiṉ catimaṭippukku.
(78-82) eḻum iṭam tacintu - eḻukiṉṟa iṭam taci koṇṭu, piṉ - ataṟkumēl, oḻukutoṟum melintu - nīḷumiṭamellām varavara melivuṭiyatāy, maḻik kaṭak kaḷiṟṟiṉ puḻikki ottum - maḻikku nikarāy oḻukum matattiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ tutikkikku oppākiyum, koḻum kaṉi vāḻiyiṉ kōḻ ari ēyppa ūṟum oḷiyum vīṟuuṟat tikaḻum - koḻuviya paḻaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vāḻi maraṅkaḷiṉ vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa aṭimaraṅkaḷi oppa maḻamaḻa veṉṉum ūṟum paḷapaḷaveṉṉum oḷiyum ciṟappuṟa viḷaṅkum, niṟam keḻu marapiṉ kuṟaṅkiṉāṉum - cenniṟam poruntiya taṉmiyiṉiyuṭiya toṭikaḷālum;
eḻumiṭam, tōṉṟum mutaliṭam; maḻikkaṭam, uvamat toki; puḻikki, toḷiyuṭiya ki. ūṟu, toṭṭuṇarvu. piḷḷiyār tirumēṉi cenniṟattatākaliṉ `niṟaṅkeḻu kuṟaṅku eṉṟār; niṟam īṇṭuc cenniṟam.
(83-89)eppāl poruḷum tuppuṟa vikum nilikkaḷam eṉpatu peyariṉ kāṭṭi - evvakiyāṉa poruḷun taṉpāl aṇukiṉ avi aḻuk kakaṉṟu tūytāki nilipeṟutaṟkuriya nilikkaḷamām uṇmiyit taṉ peyariṉālēyē viḷakki, muḷi iḷa  ñāyiṟṟiṉ  - kīḻpāl eḻukiṉṟa iḷiya katirava ṉetirē, taḷi ñekiḻntu virinta - muṟukkaviḻntu malarnta, eri aki tāmari pōla-  nerupput taḻittateṉa viḷaṅkun tāmarimalari oppa, viripukaḻt toṇṭarkaḷ etiril kaṇṭiṭa - paraviya aruṭpukaḻi yuṭiya aṭiyārkaḷ etirē kaṇṭiṭalum, malarnta nikar il iṉpa naṟi muṟi oḻukki - uṭaṉē malarntu oppillāp pēriṉpameṉṉun tēṉit toṭarpāka oḻukac ceytu, perumiyoṭu vikum tiruvaṭiyāṉum - aruṭ perumiyoṭu vīṟṟirukkun tiruvaṭiyiṉālum;
tuppu - tūymi (piṅkalanti), `ñāyiṟṟiṉ eṉpataṉ piṉ etirē eṉa oru col varuvitturikka.
`muḷi, iṅku viṉi. `eriyaki tāmariyeṉa iṅku vantāṟpōlavē eriyakintaṉṉa vēṭil tāmari yeṉap porunarāṟṟuppaṭi (159) yiṉ kaṇṇum varutal kāṇka. eriyakintateṉa veṉṟu cila varuvittuk koḷka. ñāyiṟṟi ṉetirē tāmari malarntāṟpōla aṉpoḷi tatumpun toṇṭarkaḷetirē piḷḷiyār tan tiruvaṭit tāmari malarāniṟku meṉka. `aṭi ellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāvatu. tāṭṭāmari kāṭṭit taṉkaruṇit tēṉkāṭṭi eṉṟa tiruvācakat tirumoḻiyum iṅṅaṉamē nuvaṉṟami yaṟika (tikuvammāṉi, 6).
(90-102) peru maṇal ekkarc ciṟukuṭi vāḻkkik kuṟumoḻi mākkaḷ - uyaramāṉa maṇaṟkuṉṟiṉ kaṇṇē ciṟiya kuṭivāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya puṉcoṟpēcum makkaḷākiya neytalnilattu āṭavar, uṟumīṉ paṭuttum - kaṭaliluḷḷa periya mīṉkaḷi valiyilē akappaṭuttum, āḻtiri mūḻki vīḻmaṇi eṭuttum - āḻamāṉa kaṭaliṉ kaṇ mūḻki virumpattakka muttuc cippikaḷi eṭuttum, karum kaṭal ūrnta veḷvaḷi vāriyum - kariya kaṭalnīriṉmēṉ mitantu vanta veḷḷiya caṅkukaḷi aḷḷiyum, niriniri kariyil kuviyiṉar cella - varici variciyākak kariyiṉkaṇ avaṟṟik kuvittaṉarāy ēka, (aññāṉ) maṟikayal ukaḷum tirumuka nuḷicciyar - ōṭi mīḷuṅ kayaṉmīṉkaḷākiya kaṇkaḷ taṉ irupuṟamum puraḷukiṉṟa aḻakiya mukattiyuṭiya avartam neytal nilattup peṇṭir, koṭicci tanta kaṭuppuṭi naṟavum - kuṟiñci nilattup peṇkaḷ koṇarntu koṭutta kaṭuttaliyuṭiya kaḷḷum, kuṭatti koṇarnta palappaṭu mutiriyum - mullinilattup peṇṭir koṇṭuvantu tanta avari kārāmaṇi mutaliya palavākiya payaṟuvakikaḷum, uḻatti eṭutta viḻuttaku paḻaṉum - marutanila makaḷir cumantuvanta ciṟappumikka vāḻi mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷum, peṟuviliyāka - tām peṟukiṉṟa vilip poruḷāka, ciṟumīṉ noṭuttum - avaṟṟiṟku īṭākat tam nilattu vaḷaṅ kaḷākiya ciṟiya mīṉkaḷi viṟṟum, iṭiiṭi muttam puṭipuṭi aḷantum - ataṉōṭu ataṉiṭiyiṭiyē muttuk kaḷi pakkaṅkaṭōṟum aḷantu koṭuttum, kōṭukoṇṭu pīṭuuṟa māṟiyum - caṅkukaḷi yeṭuttup perumipeṟa māṟṟikkoṇṭum, orucār pēroli nikaḻtta- iṅṅaṉamāka orupakkattil ip paṇṭamāṟṟup pērōciyi viḷikka;
ekkar - maṇaṟkuṉṟu (puṟanāṉūṟu, 177).
kuṟumoḻi; piṟari yikaḻntupēcuñcol eṉpar aṭiyārkkunallār (cilappatikāram, 6, 63).
`makkaḷeṉṉātu `mākka ḷeṉappaṭṭār avar ciṟucoṟpēcutaliṉ.
`tiri ākupeyar vīḻ - virumpappaṭum; tām vīḻvār meṉṟōḷ eṉpuḻippōla (tirukkuṟaḷ). `maṇi īṇṭu muttu maṇikaḷiyuṭiya cippiyiṉi yuṇarttiyatu ākupeyar.
`kuviyiṉar muṟṟeccam; cella, kaṭalpaṭu poruḷ kaḷik kariyiṟ koṇarntu kuvittappaṭiyāy nīrukkuṅ karikkuñ ceṉṟu koṇṭirukka veṉka.
tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal (puṟanāṉūṟu, 392) eṉpataṉāl īṇṭuk kūṟiya naṟaviṉ kaṭupput tēḷiṉ kaṭuppi yuṭiya tātal aṟika. naṟavu- kaḷ, naṉimutir cāṭi naṟaviṉ eṉṟār puṟattilum, (297). noṭuttal - viṟṟal, mīṉnoṭuttu neṟkuvii (paṟam, 343). puṭi -pakkam, orupuṭi pāmpu koḷiṉum (nālaṭiyār, 148).
(102-108) oru cār - maṟṟoru pakkattil, paṉimali nivanta paṇpupōla - paṉiyiṉāl mūṭappaṭṭa malikaḷ uyarntuḷḷa taṉmiyi oppa, cēṇtoṭa ōṅkiya uppuk kuṉṟam - vāṉam toṭum paṭi uyarnta uppu malikaḷi, taliyaḻittu umaṇar potiyuṟac cērttiya - ucciyiliruntu avaṟṟi yaḻittu uppu vāṇikar uppi mūṭṭikaḷākkic cērtta, oḻuki - vaṇṭiyiṉ, uruḷi - cakkaraṅkaḷ, muḻumaṇal putitaliṉ - cumiyiṉāl avviṭattu niṟinta maṇaliṉuḷ aḻuntutaliṉ, piṭarkoṭuttu eḻuppik kaṭumpakaṭu urappum - avaṟṟit tam puṟaṅkaḻuttuk koṭuttu mēltūkkiyapaṭiyāy avvaṇṭiyiṟ pūṭṭiya valiya erutukaḷi vāyāl ataṭṭukiṉṟa, tāḷāṇ mākkaḷ talip paṭum uñaṟṟum - muyaṟciyiṟ ciṟanta makkaḷiṉ mēmpaṭum uḻappum.
nivanta - uyarnta, mākkaṭal nivantu eḻutaruñ, ceñ ñāyiṟṟuk kaviṉi eṉṟār puṟattilum (4).
`cēṇ ākupeyarāy vāṉatti uṇarttum; `cērttiya, `ceyta veṉṉum peyareccat tiripu. umaṇar - uppu viṟpār; `oḻuki, vaṇṭi; iccoṟkaḷukku ipporuḷuṇmi nōṉ pakaṭṭumaṇa roḻukiyoṭuvanta eṉṉuñ ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭi (55) yiṟ kāṇka.
`kaṭumpakaṭu eṉpuḻik kaṭumi valimiyi yuṇarttā niṉṟatu. `neytal nilamākaliṉ, kaḻiyiṭattu maṇalil uruḷi kaḷ putital kūṟappaṭṭatu.
`tāḷ muyaṟci; `talippaṭum eṉpati iṅkuc `ceyyu meṉṉum eccamākak koḷka. `tāḷāṇmākkaḷ eṉṟatu īṇṭuvaṇṭi yōṭṭuvāri.
`uñaṟṟu muyaṟci; māṇṭa uñaṟṟilavarkku (64) eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ.
(109-112) pulavuk kaḻi muḷitta tuvar itaḻt tāmari - pulālnāṟṟam nāṟukiṉṟa kaḻiyiṉkaṇ vaḷarnta civanta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya centāmari malaril, puṉṉi nuṇtātu poṟippa - puṉṉi malariṉ poṉṉiṟamāṉa nuṇṇiya makarantappoṭikaḷ mēliruntu cinta, tuṉṉiya uruvoṭu viḷaṅkum tōṟṟam - aḵtu appoṭikaḷāl, niṟinta poṉṉuruvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa kāṭciyāṉatu, eriyiṭi - eriyum neruppiṉkaṇ, urukiya pacupoṉ kaviḻppa - nīrāy urukiya pacumpoṟ kuḻampik kīḻmukamākak kaviḻttiṭutalum, ōṭiya tiruvoṭum poliyum cevviyum - aḵtu oḻukiya aḻakōṭu oppa viḷaṅkum aḻakum;
`poṟippa, iṅkuc cintiya veṉṉum poruṭṭu; itaṟkut `teṟippa veṉac cintāmaṇi (46) uriyil urikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutuvar.
`tuṉṉiya veṉpatu `niṟintaveṉap poruṭaral, tuṉṉik kuḻikoṇṭu tāḻnta kuḷirmaram eṉṉum nālaṭiyāriṟ kāṇka. (167).
`eri centāmari malarukkum, `pacumpoṉ puṉṉinuṇ tātukkum uvamiyākum.
cevvi - aḻaku (cūṭāmaṇinikaṇṭu, 11).
pacumpoṉṉip pōlavē puṉṉittātum mañcaḷniṟa muṭiyatātal kāṇka.
tiruvoṭum, ōṭu oppumikkaṇ vantatu.
(113-115) maruviya - poruntiya; neytal am kāṉal aitu uṟakkiṭanta oṟṟimānakaril - kaṭaṟkarikkaṇ uḷḷa kaḻiyaṭutta cōli aḻakumikak kiṭantu tiruvoṟṟimā nakariṉkaṇ.
kāṉal - kaḻikkariccōli (piṅkalanti).
`aitu, aḻaku; piṟaviyaḷ āyiṉaḷ peṟṟiyum aiteṉa eṉpatu maṇimēkali (10,2). orucār pēroli nikaḻtta, orucār (102) tāḷāṇmākkaḷ talippaṭum   uñaṟṟum (108) tiruvoṭum poliyuñ cevviyum (113) maruviya neytalaṅ kāṉal kiṭanta tiruvoṟṟi mānakareṉak koḷka. `nikaḻtta, uñaṟṟum cevviyum maruviya, kāṉal kiṭanta tiruvoṟṟi mānakar eṉa viṉimuṭikkappaṭum.
tiruvoṟṟiyūr neytalum marutamum mayaṅkiyatākaliṉ, iṅkut tiṇi mayakkaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu. itu mutaṟceyyuḷilum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭami niṉivukūrka.
(115-116) kaṟṟit tōki mayiṉmēliruntu payilutalāṉum - kaṟṟiyā yirukkum pīliyiṉi yuṭiya mayiliṉmēl eḻuntaruḷiyiruntu palakālum viḷaṅkutalālum;
`payiluta leṉpatu palakāl nikaḻcciyiṉi yuṇarttal karappiluḷḷamoṭu vēṇṭumoḻi payiṟṟi (puṟam, 34) yeṉṉumiṭattup palakāṟ kūṟi yeṉavarum paḻiya urikoṇṭu iccoṟku ipporuḷuṇmi teḷiyappaṭum.
(117-118) kāṇṭoṟum iṉikkum kāṭciyum uṭiyaṉ - pārkkumpōtellām makiḻvit tarukiṉṟa tōṟṟamum uḷḷavaṉ; urippat tīrāc ciṟappum uṭiyaṉ - colli muṭiyāp perumiyum uḷḷavaṉ;
urippat tīrāc ciṟappeṉpatu, evvaḷavu coṉṉālum muṭitalillāta perumiyeṉka. `ummi, eccam;
(119-123) tam uyirkku iṉiya cemmoḻip putalvari nōkka nōkka - tamatu uyirkkiṉiya iṉcoṟ putalvarkaḷip pārkkap pārkka, mīk kiḷar kātal tantiyarkku eḻutalpōl - mēṉmēṉ mikukiṉṟa aṉpu tantimārkku viḷitalpōla, kaṇṭiṭum viḻi aḷavu amiyāk kaḻiperum kātal - taṉṉik kāṇuṅ kaṇkaḷiṉ aḷavil amiyāta niṟinta pēraṉpiṉi, eytiṉārkku uṟukkum ceytiyum uṭiyaṉ - taṉṉi aṇukikkāṇum pēṟupeṟṟa aṭiyārkaṭku viḷikkuñ ceykiyumuḷḷavaṉ;
`cemmoḻi yeṉpuḻic `cemmi yeṉpatu iṅkut tantiyarkku iṉitākun taṉmiyik kuṟiyāniṉṟatu. `uṟukkum uṇṭākkum.
(124 - 125) urukeḻu tōṟṟam maruvuvaṉ eṉṟu - accam poruntiya tōṟṟatti mēṟkoḷvaṉō eṉa niṉintu, veruvaral oḻiti iṉṟē - niṉ añcutali oḻippāyāka ippoḻutē;
uru - accam, uru uṭkākum (tolkāppiyam, uriyiyal, 4); iṟivaṉ taṉ aṭiyār añcutaṟkētuvāṉa taṉ ṟeyva vaṭivik kāṭṭātu avar kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuttakka aḻakiya iḷavaṭiviṉiyē kāṭṭi aruḷ ceyvaṉeṉpatu,
  aṇaṅkucāl uyarnili taḻīip paṇṭittaṉ
  maṇaṅkamaḻ teyvat tiḷanalaṅkāṭṭi
eṉṉun tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi aṭikaḷāl (289-290) aṟiyap paṭum. veruvaral - añcutal, (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 241). `āl aci.
(125-128) murukaṉ kuḻavik kōlattum viḻumitiṉ amarvaṉ - murukapperumāṉ kuḻanti yuruvattiluñ ciṟantu tikaḻvaṉ; maṭavāṉ makaḷir taṭamuli kuḻikkum iḷamik kōlattum vaḷamiyoṭu polivaṉ - maṭappattiṉi yuṭiya peṇkaḷiṉ periya koṅkikaḷik kuḻiyumpaṭi ceyyuṅ kāḷip paruvattiluñ celvap polivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkuvaṉ; ataṉāṉ - ataṉāl;
`maṭavaral makaḷir - `maṭappattiṉiyuṭiya makaḷir eṉap poruḷurippar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (neṭunalvāṭiyuri, 39).
(129-135) miṉ eṉat tōṉṟik koṉṉuṟa maṟintum - miṉṉalpōṟkāṇappaṭṭup payaṉillāmaṟ kaḻintum, vātuvaṉ naṭavāk kalimā pōlat tītuuṟu neṟiyil tiṟampaṭac ceṉṟum-kutirip pākaṉāl naṭattappaṭāta cerukkumikka kutiriyip pōl tīmi mikukkum vaḻiyil tiṟamiyāka ōṭiyum, pāku aṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉ paṇpupōla uṟutikoḷḷāc ciṟumiyiṉ mikuntum - yāṉip pākaṉik koṉṟu neṟippaṭātu mīṟum yāṉiyiṉ iyalpip pōl oru naṉmiyi mēṟkoṇṭoḻuka māṭṭāk kīḻmiyil mikutippaṭṭum, oruvaḻi niliyātu ōṭip palavaḻik kavarkkum eṉ nilimi il maṉaṉē - oru muṟiyilum nilippatillāmal ōṭip palavakiyilum piḷavupaṭṭoḻuku kiṉṟa eṉatu nilittalillāta neñcamē yeṉpatu.
maṉaṉē eṉpataṉ ēkāram viḷi; `miṉ tiṭumeṉat tōṉṟatalukku uvami; `koṉ payaṉiṉmip poruḷatu; koṉṉē vaḻaṅkāṉ (nālaṭiyār, 1, 9,) eṉpuḻippōla.
vātuvaṉ, kutiri ōṭṭuvōṉ (tivārakam). `kali, iṅkuc cerukkiṉi uṇarttum (puṟam. 15).
pāku, pākaṉ, (cilappatikāram, 15, 46). ciṟumiyiṉ, ciṟumi yiṉkaṇ; kavarttal, piḷavu paṭutal (puṟam, 35). āvatu, orē niṉi vāyillāmaṟ pala niṉivukaḷuṭiyatātalik kūṟiyavāṟām.
maṉaṉē (135) ataṉāṉ (128) veruvara loḻitiyāl (125) eṉa muṭikka.
eṉavē, maṉaṉē (135) eṉat talivi taṉatu neñcatti viḷittu, naṟuṅ katuppoḷirum peruñ ciṟappāṉum (9) virinuta lāṉum (12) muṭimēṉ maṇi eṉak kuṭikoḷalāṉum (15) katuppu maruvutalāṉum (18) ōveṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvutalāṉum (20) mōvāy nivanta muṟimiyāṉum (23) eyiṟṟoṭu poruntu talāṉum (27) itaḻoṭu maṉṉutalāṉum (30) cevvāy tōṉṟu mavvāṟṟāṉum (36) mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmiyāṉum (39) peruviḻiyāṉum (42) moḻiyiṉāṉum (45) piṭariṉāṉum (48) tōḷiṉāṉum (62) varimūṉṟoḻukiya tiruviṉāṉum (69) vēliṉāṉum (73) vayiṟṟiṉāṉum (77) kuṟaṅkiṉāṉum (82) tiruvaṭiyāṉum (89) mayiṉmēliruntu payilutalāṉum (116) kāṭciyumuṭiyaṉ (117) ciṟappu muṭiyaṉ (118) ceytiyum uṭiyaṉ (123) tōṟṟam maruvuvaṉ eṉṟu (124) taṉ talivaṉākiya tiruvoṟṟic cevvēḷiṉ eḻil nalaṅkaḷ kūṟik, kuḻavik kōlattum, amarvaṉ (126) iḷamik kōlattum polivaṉa taṉāṉ (128) eṉṟum avaṉ kuḻavikkōlattilēyē iruppa vaṉallaṉ; avaṉ kaṭṭiḷamik kōlattiṉ kaṇṇum polintu nampāl aṇuki naṅ kātaṟṟuyarattiyuṅ kaṭṭāyan tīrppāṉ kāṇ eṉat tuṇivu viḷakki, veruvaral oḻitiyāl iṉṟē (125) eṉa aṟivuṟuttiyavāṟu teḷiyaṟpālatu. eṉavē icceyyuḷ eḻil nalaṅ kūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttaṟkaṇ vantami kāṇka veṉpatu. (22)
23. aruṭara vēṇṭal
  maṉṉu moruyāṉi yūṭa maṟuyāṉi
  taṉṉutavi vaḷḷi taṭamulika - ṭuṉṉuvippa
  voṟṟi nakarvikum vōlō yoruperitō
  paṟṟi yeṉiyāḷ paricu
(i-ḷ). maṉṉum oru yāṉi ūṭa - taṉakku oru kūṟāyp poruntun teyvayāṉi eṉṉum orutti taṉṉuṭaṉ ūṭutal ceyyumpaṭi, maṟuyāṉi taṉ utavi - taṉakku muṉṉōṉāṉa yāṉi mukak kaṭavuḷiṉatu aruḷutaviyāṉatu, vaḷḷi taṭa mulikaḷ tuṉṉuvippa - vaḷḷiyammiyāriṉ perutta koṅkikaḷip poruntuvippa, oṟṟi nakar vikum vēlōy - ittakiya kātal viḷiyāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ tiruvoṟṟi nakaril eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum vēṟpaṭiyi yuṭiya murukapperumāṉē, oruperitō paṟṟi eṉi āḷ paricu - aṇuki ēḻiyēṉi āṇṭaruḷun taṉmi tēvarīrukku oru peritākumō veṉpatu.
yāṉi mukak kaṭavuḷ iṅku `yāṉi yeṉappaṭṭār; avar, tam iḷavalākiya āṟumukak kaṭavuḷukku vaḷḷiyammiyi maṇaṅ kūṭṭivitta varalāṟu kanta purāṇattu `vaḷḷiyammi tirumaṇap paṭalattil 110-vatu ceyyuḷiliruntu 115-vatu ceyyuḷ variyiṟ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
paricu - paṇpu, taṉmi (tivākaram); pōrum paricu pukaṉ ṟaṉarō eṉṟār tirukkōviyārilum (182).
24. tōḻi talivi kuṟippaṟital
paricu piṟitoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parumuttap pimpaṇicūḻ
  liripuka ḻoṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum viṭikkaḷiṟoṉ
  ṟaritiṟ peyarntiṅku vantatuṇ ṭōveṉpa raṅkucelvar
  teriviṟ periyar ceyalō ciṟiyareñ cēyiḻiyē.
(i-ḷ) paricu piṟitu oṉṟu aṟiyēṉ - uṇmi vaki vēṟoṉṟun terintilēṉ, parumuttup pim paṇicūḻ viripukaḻ oṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum - parumiyāṉa muttaṅkaḷ uḷḷa paciyavayalkaḷ cūḻnta paranta pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya tiruvoṟṟi nakariṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi viḷaṅkivarum vēlavar pōlum iṅkuvantu eṉṉi viṉāviyavar, viṭikkaḷiṟu oṉṟu - āṇ yāṉiyoṉṟu, aritiṉ peyarntu iṅku vantatu uṇṭō eṉpar - eyta eṉ ampiṉukku arumiyākat tappi yōṭi ippakkam vantatuṇṭōveṉa eṉṉi viṉavuvar, aṅku celvar - yāṉ illi yeṉavē appakkam pōvar, terivil periyar ceyalō ciṟiyar - avar aṟiviṟ periyarā yiruntum aṭuttaṭuttup pōntu aṅṅaṉam viṉavuñ ceyaliṟ ciṟiyarāyu mirukkiṉṟār; em cēy iḻiyē cevviya nakikaḷi yaṇinta em taliviyē eṉpatu.
iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum muttam vayalkaḷil viḷiyum muttameṉka. 
iṅṅaṉamellāṅ kūṟuṅkāl talivikku neñcaṅ kaṭuttatu kāṭṭum veḷik kuṟippukaḷ koṇṭu tōḻi avaḷatu kātaṟkaruttit terintukoḷvaḷ. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟik kuṟippaṟiyu miyalpu iruṅkaḷiyāyiṉṟiyā ṉiṟumāppa eṉṉun tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāriṉālum (52) uṇarappaṭum.
25. kaḻaṟṟetirmaṟi
terivu, aṟivu; ipporuṭṭātal terivuṟak kēṭṭa tiruttaku nallīr eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattiṟ kāṇka. (30, 185).
(1-15) iḻi nekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ - tām aṇinta aṇikalaṅkaḷik kaḻalac ceytaṟku ētuvāṉa taṅ kātalarp pirinta tuṉpamuṟum makaḷirippōl, maḻittaṭam tōḷkaḷ iḷittut tōṉṟa - kuḷircci poruntiya periya tōḷkaḷ melintu tōṉṟavum, pāl kaṭalpōlat tūttakap paranta nūlpaṭu kēḷviyum cīrppaṭātu oḻiya - pāṟkaṭalip pōl tūymippaṭavirinta nūlārāycciyiṉ kaṇ aṭippaṭu kiṉṟa niṉatu kēḷviyaṟivum cemmip paṭāmal tavaṟi yoḻiyavum, ciṟuneṟiccellum aṟiyā māntarum nalvaḻic cellat teḷḷitiṉ kāṭṭum iḻukkā naṭiyum vaḻukkip pōka - ciṟumiyāṉa tīyavaḻikaḷilē oḻukum naṉmi yaṟiyā makkaḷum nalla vaḻiyilē tirunti yoḻukumpaṭiyāka attuṇi viḷaṅkak kāṭṭum niṉatu paḻutupaṭāta oḻukkamum vaḻuvip pōkavum, innili nīyum tiriyiṉ - ittakiya iḻiniliyil nīyum nilitirivataṉāl, piṉ ataṟkumēl, oru puvanīr vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunīr uṇṭō - oru kaṭalnīr veppaṅ koḷvataṉāl ataṉi vaḷāvukiṉṟa ataṉiṉuṅ kuḷirnta perunīrum vēṟu uṇṭō? illiyē; kuṉṟu nilitavaṟip pantupōl uruḷil ceṉṟu vaḻi aṭikkum kuṉṟiyum uṇṭō - oru maliyē taṉ iruppiṉiṉṟum piṟaḻntu pantu pōl uruṇṭu ōṭumāṉāl ataṉi ataṉ etiriṟceṉṟu vaḻiyaṭittu nilippikkuñ ciṟiya kuṉṟimaṇitāṉum uṇṭō? illiyē; kaṭum kaḷiṟṟu oruttal toṭu maṭuttu uṇṇiṉ muruṅkātu ōmpum peruṅkalam uṇṭō - valiya āṇyāṉi oṉṟu  vayaliṉ uṭpukuntu uṇavu koḷḷumāṉāl aḵta ḻiyātapaṭi pātukākkum periya mūṭukalam oṉṟu  uṇṭō? illiyē; eṉṟu pala kūṟi niṉṟu - eṉṟu pala aṟivuri kaḷellām eṉakkuk kūṟiniṉṟu, eṉi neruṅki naṉmi nāṭiya eṉ uyir naṇpā - eṉṉik kaḻaṟi eṉ naṉmiyi virumpiya eṉ uyir naṇpaṉē eṉpatu.
pirivāṟṟāt tuṉpam makaḷiri melivittaliṉ itaṟku muṉ avaruṟuppukaḷil iṟukkamāyirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷ nekiḻtal paṟṟi `iḻi nekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ eṉṟār.
paruvaral, tuṉpam; tū, tūymi; tūttaka, tūymippaṭa; nīyum eṉṉum ummi uyarvu.
vaḷāvu nīruṇṭō, kuṉṟiyumuṇṭō, peruṅkala muṇṭō veṉṟatu, pāṅkaṉ talimakaṉip pārttu, `ellā muṇarntoḻukum periyiyākiya nīyē nilitavaṟi niṉṟāl niṉṉiṟ ciṟiyarākiya ēṉiyōruḷ niṉṉit tiruttuvārum uḷarkol eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttuṅ kuṟipput tōṉṟutaṟ poruṭṭu. ivi iṅṅaṉamē,
kuṉṟam uruṇṭāṟ kuṉṟi vaḻiyaṭi yākātavāṟu pōlavum, yāṉitoṭu uṇṇiṉ mūṭuṅ kalam illatu pōlavum, kaṭal vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunīr illatu pōlavum emperumāṉ niṉ uḷḷam aṟivu niṟi ōrppuk kaṭippiṭiyiṉ varittaṉṟik kimik kōṭumē yeṉiṉ niṉṉit teruṭṭaṟ pāla nīrmiyar uḷarō nī piṟaritteruṭṭiṉallatu
eṉa iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷ (3 -m nūṟpā) uriyilum āciriyar nakkīrar mikavum aḻakāka urittiruttal niṉivu kūraṟpālatu.
`oruttal, yāṉi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ āṇukkup peyar. ipporuḷ.
pulvāy puliyuḻi mariyē kavari
  colliya karāmō ṭorutta loṉṟum
  vārkōṭ ṭiyāṉiyum paṉṟiyu maṉṉa
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl (marapiyal, 35, 6) uṇarappaṭum. kaḷiṟukkuk kaṭumiyāvatu valimi; `kaṭumi, valimi;
`toṭu, vayal; maruta nilamum .............. toṭuveṉalākum (10:656) eṉpatu piṅkalanti. yāṉi yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippāl ivvayalik karumpu viḷi vayal eṉal okkum.
`muruṅkal, aḻital; kuṉṟiyum, itaṉ ummi etirmaṟi.
`neruṅki yeṉṟatu, iṅku arukil niṉṟeṉpataṉṟu, iṭitturittu allatu kaḻaṟi eṉṉum poruṭṭu; tol kāppiyaṅ, kaṟpiyalil (150) piḻittu vantirunta kiḻavaṉi neruṅki eṉpuḻi neruṅki eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar `kaḻaṟi eṉap poruḷurittatūuṅ kāṇka.
(16-17) maṟi iḷam kaṉṟu - māṉ kaṉṟukaḷ, muṟi uṇum tāyoṭu - taḷirilikaḷik kaṟittut tiṉṉun tattan tāyuṭaṉ, kuḻip putaltōṟum - iḷantaḷirkaḷ niṟintuḷḷa ciṟu tūṟukaḷilellām, kuḻīi ukaḷa - kūṭit tuḷḷittuḷḷip pāytalālum;
maṟi eṉpatu māṉukkum peyarātal yāṭuṅ kutiriyum navviyum uḻiyum, ōṭumpulvāy uḷappaṭa maṟiyē eṉṉun tolkāppiya marapiyaṟ cūttirattiṟ (12) kāṇka.
putal, ciṟu tūṟukaḷ; pimputal erukki (25) eṉpatu mullippāṭṭu, `kuḻīi aḷapeṭi, iṅkuccollici; ukaḷa, ukaḷalālumeṉak kāraṇapporu ḷurikka; mēl varuvaṉavellāmum iṅṅaṉamē urikkaṟpālaṉa. ukaḷal - tuḷḷal, tirimaruppirali pullarun tukaḷa eṉṟār aka nāṉūṟṟilum (14).
(18-19) variyāṭu varuṭi iṭivāy tāṇṭi-malikaḷil viḷiyāṭukiṉṟa `varuṭi yeṉṉum oruvaki māṉ iṉam aṅkuḷḷa malippiḷavukaḷit tāṇṭi, uḷḷam cerukkit tuḷḷit tiritara - uḷḷam eḻuccikoṇṭu kutittu ulāvu talālum;
`varuṭi itu `varuṭimāṉ eṉa oruvaki māṉ. itu variyāṭu varuṭit tōṟṟam pōla (139) eṉa iṅṅaṉamē varum paṭṭiṉappāliyaṭiyiṉ uriyāl iṉitaṟiyappaṭum.
`cerukkutal, iṅku `eḻuccikoḷḷutal eṉakkoḷka. malikaḷil tiritarum vēṟoruvaki māṉiṉaṅkaḷ makiḻcciyāl maliveṭippukaḷit tāṇṭi viḷiyāṭutal iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
(20-21) nilamtoṭṭu vīḻnta kuluṅku mayirk kavari - nilattiṟ paṭṭuk kīḻttoṅkiya kuluṅkukiṉṟa mayirkkaṟṟiyi yuṭiya `kavarimā veṉṉum oruvaki vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ, mel mela acii mētakac cella - mella mella acintu naṭantu perumippaṭa ēkutalālum;
(22-23) muḷavumā tolicciya vaṉkaṇ kāṉavar - muḷḷam paṉṟikaḷik koṉṟa vaṉkaṇmi mikka vēṭṭuvar, ñelikōl pottiya neruppil kāycca - avaṟṟiṉ iṟicci kaḷit tīkkaṭi kōlāl uṇṭākkiya neruppil iṭṭu vatakkutalālum;
`muḷavu eṉpatu, muḷḷam paṉṟiyiṉ peyar; mūṇṭeḻu ciṉattucceṅkaṇ muḷavu muḷḷarintu kōtta (kaṇṇappa, 20) eṉṉun periyapurāṇat tirumoḻiyil ipporuḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. muḷavumā tolicciya eṉa malipaṭu kaṭāttiṉ kaṇṇum (176) ittoṭar iṅṅaṉamē varutal niṉivu kūraṟpālatu. ñelikōl - tīkkaṭikōl eṉavum, `pottutal - koḷuttutal eṉavum poruṭaral, olikaḻi picinta ñelicori yoṇpoṟi, paṭuñemal putiyap potti eṉṉum akanāṉuṟṟiṟ (39) kāṇka.
(24-26) ōri paranta - nīlaniṟam paraviya, tēṉ iṟāl eṭumār - tēṉkūṭṭiṉi aḻitteṭuttal vēṇṭi, kaḻikkaṇ kuṟittu - mūṅkiṟ kaṇukkaḷit taṟittu, nalattaka iyaṟṟiya -naṉṟākac ceyta, mālpu vittu iṭantoṟum kuṟavar ēṟa - kaṇṇēṇi porutti am malikkāṭṭiṭaṅkaḷellāṅ kuṟavar ēṟutalālum;
`ōri, nīlaniṟam; ipporuḷuṇmi nīṉiṟavōri pāynteṉa (524) eṉṉum malipaṭukaṭāttaṭiyālum, ōriyeṉpatu tēṉ mutirntāṟ parakkum nīlaniṟam eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟ (109) ṟuriyālum aṟiyappaṭum.
`iṟāl, tēṉkūṭu; `eṭumār, viṉiyeccam; `tītumaṟuṅ kaṟumār eṉpuḻippōla (puṟanāṉūṟu, 93). kuṟittu, taṟittu; `mālpu mūṅkilēṇi; kaṇṇēṇiyeṉpatum atu; mālpu kaṇ ṇēṇiyiṉ pēr (7, 54) eṉpatu cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu.
`iṭantoṟum, tēṉkūṭukaḷ toṅkuñ ceṅkuttāṉa malippāṟikaḷiṉ iṭamelāmeṉpatu. kuṟavar, kuṟiñcinila makkaḷ; avar vēṭar.
(27-28) tiṉik kuṟu makaḷir - tiṉinelli ulakkiyāṟ kuttum peṇkaḷ, cuvippaṭa miḻaṟṟum kiḷḷi uṟakkum vaḷḷi olippa - cevikku iṉimi yuṇṭākumpaṭi icikkum  kiḷikaḷiyum uṟaṅkavikkum paṭiyāṉa ulakkip pāṭṭiṉi olittalālum;
kuṟutal - kuttutal kolyāṉik kōṭṭāl vetir neṟkuṟuvām nām eṉṟār kalittokiyilum (42).
miḻaṟṟal - collal (tivākaram), iṅkē pāṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu.
ūḻmuṟi yulakki yōcci vāḻiya, takumaṉik kiḻavar tam vaḷaṅ kūṟi, makaḷir pāṭuvatu vaḷḷippāṭṭē eṉṉun tivākarattāl vaḷḷi yiṉṉateṉṟuṇarka.
`uṟakkum, meṉmi valittup piṟaviṉipporuḷ payantatu.
tiṉikuṟu makaḷir icipaṭu vaḷḷiyum eṉṉum malipaṭukaṭāttaṭiyōṭu (342) itaṉi yoppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(29-33) maruppu iṭic cuṟṟiya poruppu uyar vēḻattup puḻikki ēyppa - tan tantaṅkaḷiṭattuc cuṟṟiya malipōl uyarnta yāṉiyiṉ tutikkiyi oppa, viḻuttaka mutirnta tāḷoṭu vaḷinta kural - tiṉippuṉattu naṉṟāka muṟṟiya tāḷkaḷuṭaṉ nuṉivaḷinta katirkaḷiyuṭiya tiṉippayirkaḷ uḷḷa puṉattiṉkaṇ, paṭukiḷi ōppum viṭukavaṇ cuḻaṟṟi - appuṉattilē viḷinta tiṉikkatirkaḷik koṇṭupōka vantu viḻukiṉṟa kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭukiṉṟa kalviṭu kavaṇik kiyiṟ cuṟṟiyapaṭiyāy, kōlvaḷik koṭicci - tiraṭci yaminta vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta kuṟattiyar, mēl itaṇ amara - uyarntu mēlē uḷḷa paraṇiṟ polivuṭaṉiruttalālum;
`cuṟṟiya puḻikki yeṉak koḷka. maruppiṭic cuṟṟiya puḻik ki, tāḷoṭu vaḷinta kural tiṉippayirkku uvami; kural, katir; `neṟkural kuviya (2) veṉa mutaṟceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇum iḵtipporuḷil vantami kāṇka.
`opputal, ōṭṭutal; viṭukavaṇ, eṟikavaṇ; āvatu kalleṟi kavaṇ. `kōḷvaḷi tiraṭci poruntiya vaḷiyal; āykō lavir toṭi eṉṉum maturikkāñciyil (563) ic col ipporuḷil varutal kāṇka. `itaṇ, paraṇ; neṭṭitaṇ ēṟum ip puṉattiṉaḷē (53) eṉpatu kallāṭam. ip pakutiyik kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṟ pōnta
neṟkoḷ neṭuvetirk kaṇanta yāṉi
  muttār maruppiṉ iṟaṅkuki kaṭuppat
  tuyttali vāṅkiya puṉiṟutīr peruṅkural
  naṟkōṭ ciṟutiṉip paṭupuḷ ōppi
eṉṉum pakutiyōṭu (35-38) oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(34-35) irumkal viyal aṟi varippa - periya akaṉṟa kaṟpāṟimēl taṅkiḷikaḷ paṭṭuk kīṟumpaṭiyāka, tāaya nalamuṟu vēṅki polam vī ukuppa - paranta aḻakumikka vēṅkimaraṅkaḷ poṉṉiṟamāṉa tam pūkkaḷik kīḻē coritalālum;
varittal - kīṟal, kōlañceytal, eḻutal eṉṉum poruḷatu; tāya-  paranta; iccoṟkaḷ kāyāñcemmal tāayp palavuṭaṉ, īyaṉmūtāy varippa eṉa akanāṉūṟṟil (14) ipporuḷil varutal kāṇka. `iruṅkalviyalaṟi eṉṉum ic coṟṟoṭar kuṟuntokiyil nirivaḷi muṉki (335) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil varutal kāṇka.
nalam, iṅku aḻaku, iḷanalam kāṭṭi (tirumurukāṟṟup paṭi, 290) eṉpuḻippōla. polam, poṉṉiṟam.
(36-42) tēkkuili puytta puṉtalic ciṟāar - tēkkilikaḷip paṟitta pulliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷ, mīkkiḷar kōṭṭil paritiyiṉ toṭutta tēttaṭi piṟikkōṭu uḻuteṉak kīṇṭu - mēlē vaḷarntu viḷaṅkiya maliyiṉ koṭumuṭiyil ñāyiṟu pōṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa tēṉaṭiyiṉi nilāviṉ vaḷinta nuṉiyāṉatu uḻutuviṭavē ataṉāṟ kīṟuṇṭu, māyōṉ mārpil ceñcēṟu kaṭuppap pāyiḻiyum paṭunaṟavu mukantu - tirumāliṉ mārpiṉmēṟ pūciya civanta naṟumaṇakkuḻampi oppap pāyntoḻukukiṉṟa iṉiyatēṉi ōr ēṉattil mukantuvantu, pacum tiṉip piṇṭiyōṭu acumpuuṟa aḷāy - putiya tiṉimāvuṭaṉ at tēṉ tuḷikkumpaṭi kalantu, niriniri vittu virivoṭum āra - varicivariciyāka vittu virivāka uṇṇutalālum;
`puyttal, paṟittal; kōṭupuykkallātuḻakkum nāṭakēḷ (38) eṉak kalittokiyil iḵtuipporuḷil varutalkaṇṭu  koḷka; iccol  ikkālattiṟ `piyttal eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vēṭṭuvacciṟār eṇṇey muḻukkuk kāṇāmiyiṉ avar tantalimayir paḻuppuniṟamā yiruttal paṟṟip puṉṟalic ciṟār eṉṟār; puṉmi - paḻuppu niṟam (piṅkalanti).
`paritiyum piṟikkōṭuṅ kūṟiyatu maliyiṉ uyarn tōṅkiya ucciyik kuṟittaṟ poruṭṭeṉka.
tēṉ, aṭi eṉpaṉa oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittē eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl (puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal, 49) nilimoḻiīṟu keṭṭu iṭiyē takaravoṟṟu iraṇṭupeṟṟut `tēttaṭi yeṉap puṇarntaṉa.
`kīṇṭu, kīṟuṇṭu (tivākaram); kīṇṭa pāṟoṟum (acurartō. 15) eṉak kantapurāṇattum pōntatu. tirumakaḷ ñemuṅkap pullutaliṉ, avaḷ mārpattiṉ ceñcēṟu tirumāl mārpiṉkaṇṇum poruntuvatāyiṟṟu.
paṭunaṟavu, cuvipaṭunaṟavu; piṇṭi-mā; `acumpu nīrttuḷi; acumpiṉiṟ ṟuṉṉi eṉṉun tirucciṟṟampalakkōvi yāril (1`49) ipporuṭṭāy varutal kāṇka.
ippakutiyiṉ kaṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa uvamikaḷ mikka pulamit tiṟam vāyntavi.
(43) kaṇkaḷi koḷḷum paṉmaliyaṭukkattu - kaṇkaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻcciyaṭikiṉṟa palamalikaḷi yaṭutta maliccāralil,
aṭukkam - maliccāral, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 168). `aṭukkattu ēḻaṉ urupu virittukkoḷka.
ukaḷa (17) tiritara (19) cella (21) kāycca (23) ēṟa (26) olippa (28) amara (33) ukuppa (35) āra (42) kaṇkaḷikoḷḷum paṉmaliyaṭukkattu (43) eṉat toṭarntukoḷka veṉpatu.
(44-53) maravamum - kuṅkuma maramum; kuravamum - kurāmaramum, porikeḻu puṉkum- neṟporipōṉṟa pūkkaḷiyuṭiya puṉka maramum, erikiḷar ceyaliyum - tī eritali oppa viḷaṅkum acōka maramum, viri iṇar māvum - virinta pūṅ kottu kaḷuṭiya māmaramum, parāric cāntum - parutta arikaḷi yuṭiya cantaṉa maramum, pāvi ñāḻalum - cittirap pāvipōl viḷaṅkum malarkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya kōṅka maramum, viritali vāḻiyum - taliviritaluḷḷa vāḻi maramum, viyaṉ ciṉip palavum - periya periya kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya palāmaramum, koṉṟiyum - koṉṟi maramum, puṉṉiyum - puṉṉi maramum, `tuṉṟiya naṟavamum - aṭarntuḷḷa aṉicca maram, eṉa ivikaḷ, kuḻimukam tōṟṟi - taḷir nuṉikaḷit tōṟṟi, koḻunaṉi arumpi - koḻumiyāṉa mokkukaḷ arumpeṭuttu, puriaviḻ malaril ciṟuvaṭuk kiḷarntu - avvarumpukaḷ kaṭṭaviḻntu malarnta malarkaḷiṟ ciṟiya vaṭukkaḷ viḷaṅki, tīviḷiyāki - pacuṅkāy āki, tīm kaṉi tūṅki - iṉiya paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭṭu, taṇṇiḻal payantu - kuḷirnta niḻalit tantu, kaṇ kavar vaṉappiṉ - kaṇkaḷik kavarkiṉṟa aḻakuṭiyatāy, mukil kaṇṇuṟaṅkat talimiyoṭu polimē orupāl - mēkaṅkaḷ taṉ ucciyil amarntu uṟakkaṅ koḷḷumāṟu ammaliccāraṟ cōli uyarvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum oru pakkalil eṉpatu.
maravam - kuṅkumamaram (tivākaram). kuravam - pāvipōṉṟa pūkkaḷuṭiya oruvakimaram, naṟamalark kuravampāvi, niṟiyap pūttaṇintu eṉṟār cintāmaṇiyilum (1270). puṉkamarattiṉ pūkkaḷ neṟpori pōṉṟiruttaliṉ `porikeḻupuṉku eṉṟār; poriyeṉap puṉku aviḻ akaṉṟuṟi eṉpatu akanāṉūṟu (116).
tīkkoḻuntu eritalpōl attuṇic cevvoḷiyuṭaṉ acōkiṉ taḷirkaḷ viḷaṅkutalāl `erikiḷar ceyali eṉappaṭṭatu. attac ceyalit tuppuṟaḻ oṇtaḷir (273) eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟ ṟaṭiyiṉkaṇṇum ivviyalpu tuppu uṟaḻ oṇtaḷir eṉṟu kiḷantōtappaṭutal kāṇka. ñāḻal - kōṅkamaram (tivākaram).
naṟavam - aṉiccamaram (tivākaram).
parumi ari, parāriyeṉat tirintatu; parāri marāattu (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, ) eṉpuḻippōla.
`vaṭu veṉpatu, iṅkup piñcukaḷ tōṉṟum vaṭu. tīviḷi - pacuṅ kāy (piṅkalanti). `tūṅkal toṅkaleṉṉum poruṭṭu. mukilkaḷ tavaḻumpaṭi attuṇi uyaramāyc cōlikaḷ viḷaṅkiṉaveṉka.
`polimē yeṉṉuñ ceyyumeṉṉu muṟṟil īṟṟayal ukarattoṭuṅ keṭṭatu.
(54-64) cuḷiniri aminta koḻum paḻam tūkki - cuḷikaḷ variciyāy aminta koḻuviya kaṉikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu, kuṟumalar aviḻnta naṟuviri teḷittu - taṉ ciṟiya pūkkaḷ malarntamiyāl iṉiya maṇam parappi, vīcukāl aciyum pācili narantamum - vīcukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉāl aciyum pacumiyāṉa ilikaḷ nirampiya nāratta maraṅkaḷum, nalaṉuṟu makaḷir ilavu itaḻ katuviya muṟuval aṉṉa - aḻaku vāynta makaḷiriṉ ilavampūvi ṉitaḻi okkum itaḻkaḷiṉ cenniṟattip paṟṟiya muḷippaṟkaḷ oppa, ciṟuviti peyta polaṉ kuṭam eṭutta ulavi mātuḷiyum - ciṟiya vittukkaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa poṟkuṭam okkum paḻaṅkaḷ tūkkiya kōṭukaḷiyuṭiya mātuḷa maraṅkaḷum, karumaṇi puriyum oru kaṉi nāvalum - karumiyāṉa maṇiyi okkum oppaṟṟa paḻaṅkaḷiyuṭiya nāvaṉ maraṅkaḷum, uvarkkaḻi maṇakkum tuvarkkāl vakaḷamum - uvartta nīruḷḷa kaḻiyiṭaṅkaḷum maṇakkumāṟu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻuñ civanta kāmpukaḷuḷḷa pūkkaḷ niṟinta makiḻa maramum, poṉtiraḷ kaṭuppat teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum eḻilkaṉi paḻutta elumiccam putalum - poṉṉuruṇṭi oppat teḷivāy viḷaṅkum aḻakiya kaṉikaḷ paḻutta elumiccam putarkaḷum, kāvatam kamaḻa mēvuvaaṉṟē orupāl - kāvatattolivu maṇaṅkamaḻumpaṭi ammaliccōliyi ṉoru pakkaliṟ porunti niṟpaṉavākum; aṉṟu ē acinili.
niri, varici; pacumiili, pācili; `narantam nāranta maram, (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 94).
nalaṉ, `aḻaku; pūnalam eṉpataṟkup `pūviṉākiya aḻaku eṉṟu parimēlaḻakiyār urikūṟiṉār (paripāṭal, 16, 18).
ilavitaḻ mātuḷavitikaḷiṉ orupāṟ cemmikkum muṟuval orupāl veṇmikkum uvamikaḷ.
`polaṉkuṭ meṉpatu īṇṭuc ciṟu poṟkuṭam pōṟṟōṉṟum mātuḷampaḻattiṟku āyatu uvamiyākupeyar. ulavi marakkompu (tivākaram).
vakuḷam - makiḻamaram (tivākaram); tuvarkkāl - pavaḷaniṟam uḷḷa kāmpu; ataṉiyuṭiya malarum ammalariyuṭiya maramum īṇṭu `vakuḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa; ippavaḷakkāl malliki yiṉiyun tamiḻ nāṭṭavar `makiḻ eṉkiṉṟaṉar; pavaḷaniṟakkāmpu illāta vēṟoru vakiyiṉiyum `makiḻ eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
`kāvata meṉpatu iraṇṭēkāṟkal; itu muṉṉuṅ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
(65-78) kulikam ūṭṭiya talimiyiṉ tikaḻntu - cenniṟam ēṟṟiya mēṉmiyil viḷaṅki, navvi nōkkiyar cevvāypōla - māṉ pārviyiṉarāṉa makaḷiratu civanta vāypōṉṟa, koḻum kaṉi uṭiya kovvik koṭiyum - koḻuviya paḻaṅkaḷiyuṭiya kovvik koṭikaḷum, kaṟpu uṭi makaḷir muṟpaṭac cūṭum variciyiṉ mikunta paricu uṭi mulliyum - kaṟpoḻukkamuḷḷa mātar mutaṉmiyākac cūṭikkoḷḷun takutiyiṉ mēmpaṭṭa perumiyiṉiyuṭiya mullik     koṭikaḷum, tiṉpatu kallāp puṉtali mantikaṟittut tuḷḷum kaṟivaḷar koṭiyum - vakiterintu tīṉi tiṉṉutali iṉṉun teriyāta ciṟiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kuraṅkukkuṭṭikaḷ tampaṟkaḷāṟ kaṭittu uṟippiṉit tāṅkāmal tuḷḷukiṉṟa miḷakukāykaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟa miḷakukoṭikaḷum, aṇil puṟam kaṭukkum variyuṭik koṭumkāy koḷḷiyil kāytta veḷḷarik koṭiyum - aṇiṟpiḷḷiyiṉ mutukiṉi okkum mūṉṟuvarikaḷuḷḷa vaḷinta veḷḷarikkāykaḷ mikutiyākak kāytta veḷḷarikkoṭikaḷum, coluyar marapiṉ mel ilik koṭiyum - pukaḻmikka taṉmiyiṉiyuṭiya veṟṟilik koṭikaḷum, ciṟupaḷikku uruṇṭi 
ceṟivuṟuttaṉiya tīm kaṉik kulikaḷ tūṅka vayiṉvayiṉ nantātu vaḷarum muntirik koṭiyum - ciṟiya kaṇṇāṭi uruṇṭikaḷ oru kottāka nerukki vittāṟpōla iṉiya paḻakkulikaḷ toṅkā niṟka iṭantoṟuṅ keṭātu vaḷarkiṉṟa koṭimuntirik koṭikaḷum, iṭiiṭi tāay miṭiyumāl orupāl - iṭiyiṭiyē tāvip piṉṉit tōṉṟum maṟṟorupakkattē eṉpatu.
kulikam, cenniṟam (tivākaram).
`nōkki yeṉpataṉ ikaram peṇpāl vikuti.
kaṇavaṉip pirintu avaṉ pirivuṉṉit taṅ kaṟpiṉip pēṇum makaḷiratu oḻukkamē mulliyoḻukkamākaliṉ, `kaṟpuṭi makaḷir cūṭum mulli yeṉa aḵtiṅkuk kiḷantu kūṟappaṭṭa. kaṟpoṭu poruntik kaṇavaṉcoṟ piḻiyātu illiruntu nallaṟañ ceytal makaḷiratu iyaṟki mulli yātaliṉ atu muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tolkāppiyam, akattiṇiyiyal, 5). varici - takuti (ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, 217).
miḷakukāykaḷ kāramuṭiyavākaliṉ avaṟṟit tiṉṟāl nā eriyum eṉpatit teriyā iḷaṅkuṭṭikaḷ eṉpār tiṉpatu kallāp puṉṟali manti eṉṟār. `tuḷḷuṅkaṟi tuḷḷutaṟkētuvākiya kaṟi eṉak kāriya kāraṇap poruṭṭu.
veḷḷarikkāy aṇil mutukupōl varikaḷ uṭimi, aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉālum (246) aṟiyappaṭum.
mellili - veṟṟili, (cīvakacintāmaṇi, 62); vāyiṉuṭ peytu mellappaṭutaliṉ veṟṟili `mellili eṉap peyar peṟalāyiṟṟu; viṉittoki; ellāc ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷilum veṟṟili vaḻaṅkappaṭum pukaḻuṭiyatākaliṟ `colluyar marapiṉ eṉṟu aṭikoṭuttār.
ceṟivuṟuttal - neruṅkaccērttal, ceṟaviṉip polinta cempūṅ kaṇṇiyaṉ (paripāṭal, 22, 21) eṉpuḻippōla.
`nantātu, keṭāmal; nantāviḷakku (cintāmaṇi, 3144); nāṭaṟkariya nalatti nantāt tēṉi (tiruvācakam, tiruppoṟ cuṇṇam, 15) eṉṟaruḷicceyvar maṇivācakapperumāṉ.
tāay - tāvi, kalitāya uyarcimiyattu eṉṉum maturik kāñciyiṟ (332) pōla.
(79-81) naṭuvil - ittakiya cōliyiṉnaṭuvil, pāyoḷi matiyam - paraviya oḷiyiṉiyuṭiya tiṅkaḷāṉatu, paritivemmi yiṉ - katiravaṉ vemmiyiṉāl, iḷakip puṉalāy iḻintatu kaṭuppa - uruki nīrāyk kīḻē oḻukip paraviyatupōla, teḷinīr vāvi oṉṟuuḷatu - teḷinta nīriṉiyuṭiya kuḷam oṉṟu uḷḷatu;
matiyamē iḷaki nīrāy iḻintateṉṟatu, nīriṉ kuḷircci tūymi cuvi naṉmi miṉumiṉuppu teḷivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ keṉka.
`kaṭuppa uvamurupu; tēṅkamaḻ marutiṇar kaṭuppa (tirumuruku, 34) eṉpuḻippōla. ivvuvami mika aḻakitu.
(81-84) atu ciṟukāltōṟum uṟunīr oḻukki - antak kuḷam ciṟuciṟu vāykkāl kaṭōṟum mikunta nīriṉi oḻukacceytu, vālukam paranta kōliya pāttiyil - veṇmaṇal nirampiya vaḷittamikkappaṭṭa pāttiyiṉkaṇ, pacciḷampulli niccalum vaḷarkkum - pacumiyāṉa iḷampulli eṉṟum vaḷarac ceyyum; atāṉṟu, atuvumallāmal;
maṟṟu, aci; uṟunīr, mikkanīr; vālukam - veṇmaṇal; vēlivālukattu eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattiluṅ kāṇka. (6, 131).
pātti, ciṟuvayal; karumpiṉ pātti eṉpatu patiṟṟup pattu (13, 3).
`niccal eṉṉuñ collum poruḷum muṉṉē viḷakkap paṭṭaṉa.
(85-98) aṉṉac cēval aṇimayirp peṭiyoṭum poṉṉam tāmari polintu vīṟṟiruppavum - aṉṉap paṟaviyiṉ āṇ aḻakiya tūviyiṉiyuṭiya taṉatu peṇ aṉṉattuṭaṉ poṉpōṉṟa aḻakiya centāmari malariṉ mēṟ kātaliṉāl viḷaṅki oṉṟāy makiḻntirukkavum, nittilam payanta nattu-muttukaḷīṉṟa caṅkaṅkaḷ, vāṉ maṭuvil - vāṉamākiya nīr niliyil, mīṉiṉam vaḷiiya nāḷmatipōla - viṇmīṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ cūḻnta muḻunilāppōla, pāl keḻu marapiṉ parantu cellavum - taṉpakkattē poruntiya taṉmiyuṭaṉ paravi ēkavum, koḻum kayal mātar viḻi eṉa miḷiravum - koḻumi yāṉa keṇṭi mīṉkaḷ makaḷiruṭiya kaṇkaḷ pōṟ puraḷavum, karuverinuṭiya paruvarāl ukaḷavum - kariya mutukiṉiyuṭiya parutta varāl mīṉkaḷ aṅkumiṅkum puraḷavum, muṟṟāmañcaḷ ciṟupuṟam kaṭukkum iṟavu kari maruṅkil curintu tuḷḷavum - muṟṟāta pacumañcaṭ kiḻaṅkiṉ ciṟiya mutukiṉi okkum iṟāmīṉ kariyarukiṟ curuṇṭu tuḷḷavum, ciṟuvaḷi eṭuppac ciṟutiri eḻīi muṟimuṟi urāayk kariyiṉic cāravum - ciṟukāṟṟu eḻuppa ataṉāl melliya ciṟṟalikaḷ eḻuntu oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp parantuceṉṟu kariyiṉip poruntavum, ceḻunīrk kuvaḷiyum kaḻunīrppōtum muḻuneṟi āmpalum kaḻiyavum kuḻumik kāṇpōy piṇikkum māṇpiṉatu - ceḻumiyāṉa nīrkkuvaḷiyeṉṉum nīlappūvum ceṅkaḻunīrmalarum alli pāṭṭum mikutiyum niṟintu kāṇpavar uḷḷattit taṉvayap paṭuttum māṭciyiṉi yuṭiyatākum akkuḷam eṉpatu.

(98-103) āṅku - ammaliccāraliṭattē, potiyac cāntam tatiya acii - potiyamaliyiṉ cantaṉamaṇam niṟiya ulāvi, arumpu aṟa virinta naṟumpu aḷii - mukiyāntaṉmi nīṅka malarnta maṇamuḷḷa malarkaḷit tuḻāvi, viriyum tātum niṟiyamukantu - muṟiyē naṟumaṇamum, makarantamum nirampa aḷḷikkoṇṭu, talittali vīci nalattaka ulāam teṉṟalum eṉṟum ōvātu - iṭantoṟum vīci iṉimiyāka aciyun teṉṟaṟkāṟṟum ennēramum nīṅkātu; atāaṉṟu - atuvumaṉṟi;
cāntattiṉiṉṟu viriyum, naṟumpūviṉiṉṟu tātum mukanteṉa niral niriyāk koḷka.
`tatiya, niṟiya, tati ili vāḻi (47, 10) eṉpatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu.
(104-125) kuṉṟik kaṇṇa kuyilka ḷeṅkum tuṉṟiya makiḻviṉ naṉṟu kūvummē - kuṉṟimaṇiyiṉ cenniṟamuṅ karum puḷḷiyumpōṟ cuṟṟac cenniṟamum naṭuviṟ karuniṟamum vāynta viḻikaḷi yuṭiyavāṉa kuyiṟ paṟavikaḷ accōli yeṅkum mikunta kaḷippiṉāl iṉitākak kūvāniṟkum; āṭu vāl ciralkaḷ nāṭoṟum kūṭi ōṭumīṉ arunti vīṭuṟā - āṭukiṉṟa vāliyuṭiya cicciliyeṉṉum oruvakik kuruvikaḷ nāṭōṟum oṉṟucērntu annīr niliyil ōṭukiṉṟa ciṟu mīṉkaḷik kuvi viḻuṅki eṉṟum avviṭattiviṭṭu nīṅkā; tukir kōtta aṉṉa ukiruṭic ciṟukāl puṟa marap potumpiṉ iṟikūrummē - pavaḷaṅkaḷi oṉṟu kōttāṟpōṉṟa nakaṅ kaḷiyuṭiya ciṟukāliṉiyuṭiya puṟākkaḷ marappontiṉ kaṇ taṅkāniṟkum, tūṅkaṇam kurīip pāṅkupaṭat teṟṟiya aruntoḻil kuṭampiyiṉ poruntum - tūkkaṇaṅ kuruvikaḷ tām aḻakupaṭap piṉṉiya ariya vēlippāṭuṭiya kūṭukaḷil aṭiyum, pim ciṟikkiḷḷi orukāl tūkkip puṉciṟu kiḷiyil kaṇtuyil koḷumē - pacumiyāṉa iṟakkikaḷi yuṭiya kiḷikaḷ tamatu orukālit tūkkik koṇṭu melliya ciṟu kiḷikaḷil niṉṟu kaṇkaḷ tūkkaṅkoḷḷum, aṟuvi maṭittaṉṉa paṟikeḻu nāri ciṟukāltōṟum varum mīṉ nōkkit tokutiyāka mikutiyoṭu vatimē - melliya veḷḷit tuṇiyi maṭittāṟpōṉṟa iṟakkikaḷ poruntiya nārikaḷ ciṟiya vāykkālkaṭōṟum ōṭivarukiṉṟa mīṉkaḷi etirpārttuk koṇṭu kūṭṭamāka maṉakkiḷarcciyuṭaṉ taṅkiyirukkum, vaṇṭum tēṉumvarikkaṭip piracamum immeṉa muraṉṟu naṉmalartōṟum aḷinaṟa māntik kalikūrummē - vaṇṭum tēṉvaṇṭum varivaṇṭum tēṉīyum eṉpaṉa immeṉṉum oliyuṇṭāka olittu nalla malarkaḷ tōṟuñ ceṉṟu mutirnta tēṉ paruki makiḻccimikum, pūvākkaṇṇa tōki virittu muḷi iḷa ñāyiṟṟu iḷaveyil eṟippap pīṭuṟu maññi āṭuṟum - iyaṟkiyē tiṟantiruppatallatu iṭiyē tiṟavāta kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyavāṉa tam tōkikaḷi vaṭṭamāy virittuk kāliyiṟ ṟōṉṟum iḷaṅkatirōṉatu meṉmiyāṉa veyiloḷi vīca appōḻtu perumi mikka mayiliṉaṅkaḷ viḷiyāṭā niṟkum, iṉṉum kūṟalvēṇṭiṉ - iṉṉum eṭuttuc colla vēṇṭumāṉāl, piṉṉum - mēlum, eṉ col aḷavu amiyā - eṉatu colvarampilamiyāta, mallal ampotumparp palperum ciṟappum kaṇṭili - attuṇic ceḻumiyāṉa aḻakiya am maraccōliyiṉ pala peruñciṟappukkaḷum nī kaṇṭaṉiyalli yeṉavē kūṟal takum eṉpatu.
ip pakutiyiṟ cōlivaḷam mika aḻakākak kūṟap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.

(125-147) aṉṟi āṅku - allatu appotumpariṉkaṇ, aṉiccam mēl paṭiṉum taṉittuyar uḻakkum taḻimalarc cīṟaṭi kuḻipaṭa naṭantu - aṉiccamalariṉmēṟ paṭṭālum mikka tuṉpa maṭiyun taḻinta centāmari malaraṉiya ciṟiya tiruvaṭikaḷ tacimella  naṭantu, poḻiliṭam pukuntu - cōliyuḷ nuḻintu, pūm taṭamaruṅkil - pūkkaḷ niṟinta akkuḷattarukil, viḻitaka niṉṟa paḻutil pāviyi - viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi niṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta pāvi pōlvāḷi, eṉuḷam eṉṉum taṉ amar kiḻiyil - eṉatu uḷḷameṉṉun taṉakkupporuntiya iraṭṭut tuṇiyil, vaḻuviṉṟu eḻutiya eḻil ōviyatti - piḻiyillāmal eḻutiya aḻaku cittiram pōlvāḷi, amiḻtu poti tuvarvāyk kiḷavi cila miḻaṟṟum - amiḻtam niṟinta taṉatu civanta vāyiliruntu coṟkaḷ cila pēcum, māniṟam koṇṭa tū niṟak kiḷiyi - pacumi niṟam koṇṭa tūya uruviṉiyuṭiya kiḷipōlvāḷi, kāṭu uṟi vāḻkki taṉ iṉam pirintu - kāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟa vāḻkkiyi yuṭiya taṉ kūṭṭatti viṭṭu nīṅki, nāṭu uṟi vāḻkki naṉkaṉam maruviya - nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkiyi nallapaṭiyākap poruntiya, kuṭam māṇ koṅki maṭamāṉ piṇiyi - kuṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa periya koṅkikaḷi yuṭiya iḷamāṉpēṭu pōlvāḷi, kiḷicciṟi eṉṉum paḻippuaṟu pacumpoṉ karittu - kiḷicciṟi eṉṉum mācaṟṟa oruvakip pacumpoṉṉi nīrāy urukki, īṇṭu eḻutiya tarik kiḷar valliyi - ataṉāl innilavulakil eḻuti yamitta nilattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta poṉṉiṟamāṉa vallik koṭi pōlvāḷi, kāmak kaṭum paci nām uṟak kaḷiyum - kāmam eṉṉum koṭiya paciyāṉatu accam aṭiyumpaṭi ataṉi nīkkum, peṟutaṟku ariya uṟutuṇi marunti - aṭivataṟku arumiyāṉa ciṟanta tuṇiyāyuḷḷa maruntupōlvāḷi, kāṇṭal cellā aruvapporuḷeṉa - kāṇṭal iyalāta uruvamil poruḷeṉṟu kūṟum, pūṇṭōr pukaḻuri puripaṭṭu oḻiya - pēr pūṇṭa periyōrkaḷatu pukaḻ takka uriyāṉatu paḻutupaṭṭoḻiyu māṟu, miṉ eṉa miḷirntu eṉ kaṇ etir polinta - miṉṉalpōl piṟaḻntu oḷi vīci eṉ kaṇkaḷmuṉ viḷaṅkiya, karumpiṉum iṉikkum eṉ arumpeṟal uyiri - karumpip pārkkiṉum iṉikkiṉṟa eṉ peṟaṟkariya uyir pōlvāḷi, eṉ uḷam maṉṉiya tuṉ iruḷ nīṅka - eṉatu uḷḷattil nilipeṟṟa ceṟinta malaviruḷāṉatu oḻiyumāṟu, muṟpaṭak koḷuviya poṉcuṭark koḻunti - eṉṉetiriṟ poruttiya poṉṉiṟamāṉa oḷikkoḻuntupōl vāḷi, pulam koḷak kāṇum poṟiyum iliyāl - kāṭciyāka kāṇum nalviṉi yum niṉakkilli yeṉpatu.

kiḻi, eḻutupaṭam; īcaṉa cāntu merukku maṇintōr kiḻipiṭittu ( tirukkōviyār, 74) eṉpar maṇivācakaṉār; aḵtiṅku appaṭameḻutun tuṇiyi uṇarttāniṉṟatu. amartal - poruntutal, (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli, 4, 5).
uṇmiyuṭiyaḷākaliṉ, cilacoṟkaḷē avaḷ vāyi ṉiṉṟum eḻuvavāyiṉa. aṉṟi muḷḷeyiṟupōḻtal añcic cilacolvē pēcuvaḷeṉa urittalumām. miḻaṟṟal, nirampā meṉcoṟ kūṟal; talivi puttiḷamiyuṭiya maṭavaralākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam `miḻaṟṟu meṉappaṭṭatu. uruvakamāy vanta kiḷiyiṉiyaṟkikkum iccoṟporuḷ okkum. `amiḻtu poti vāy eṉṟamiyiṉ, avvāyiṉiṉṟeḻuṅ kiḷavikaḷellām amiḻtamākumeṉpatu kuṟippāṟ peṟappaṭum. niṟam iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu vaṇṇamum piṉṉatu uruvamum uṇarttum.
