(87-91) taṉakku nikarākunaroṭu nakitarukēṇmi ceyalākuvateṉa moḻikuvar ataṉāl - taṉakku oppavārōṭu oruvaṉ makiḻccitarum naṭpucceytalē ākkantaruvatu eṉaccāṉṟōr āṇi taruvārākaliṉ, nuntiyum muṉikuvaṉ allaṉ - num tantiyākiya civaperumāṉum nummi ataṟkāka veṟuppāṉ allaṉ; aṉṉiyum nunti vaḻippaṭūum takiyaḷ - num tāyākiya umiyammiyum num tantiyiṉ vaḻiniṟkuṅ kaṟpuṭiyaḷ;
naṭpu ottārkaṇṇateṉpatu uṇarccitāṉ naṭpāṅkiḻami tarum eṉṉum arumit tirumoḻiyāl (tirukkuṟaḷ, 785) iṉitupeṟappaṭum.
`nunti muṉṉili muṟippeyar, ti muṟippeyar īṟu; `num tanti eṉpatu tirintataṉṟu; kuṟṟiyalukara muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ eṉṉuñ cūttiratti nōkkuka (tolkāppiyam, eḻuttu, 34.)
`nuntiyu muṉikuva ṉallaṉ, aṉṉiyum nunti vaḻippaṭun takiya ḷātalāl, eṉakku nī aruṭaraṟku ētum iṭukkilliyeṉak kūṟiyavāṟām; iḵtu avāy niliyāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
(91-99) mintu keḻu poruppippōḻntu - valimi porunti `kiravuñca maliyippiḷantu, viruppoṭum etirnta arimā mukattaṉip poripaṭanūṟi - pōrviruppattōṭum etirntuvanta `ciṅkamukācuraṉ eṉpavaṉip porintupōkap poṭipaṭutti, kāḻ uṟum arakkari nūḻil āṭṭi maṉaviraṅkoṇṭa arakkarkaḷik koṉṟukuvittu, poriyari māvāy virikaṭal mūṭiya cūrmutal mutalaṟa murukki - porinta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya māmaramāki akaṉṟa kaṭaliṉi mūṭiya cūrartalivaṉi mutalaṟumpaṭi muṟittuvīḻtti, pēr aruḷ aḻivupaṭum uḷḷattup paḻiyoṭu piṟaṅkum uliyā vāḻkkit tēvarkku - tēvarīratu pēraruḷi aḻivupaṭṭa uḷḷattōṭum `cūrarkku āṟṟātār eṉṉum paḻiyiṉāl mikuntum keṭāta uyirvāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya tēvarkaḷukku, talimiyoṭu nalkum - aracuceluttuntalimiyuṭaṉ koṭuttaruḷukiṉṟa, tamiḻkiḻavōyē - tamiḻmoḻikku uriyiyāṉa murukakkaṭavuḷē;
`poripaṭanūṟi, porintupōkap poṭipaṭutti; `nūṟu poṭi, (tivākaram). `nūḻilāṭ ṭeṉpatu, koṉṟukuvittal (tolkāppiyam, puṟattiṇiyiyal, 17).
poriyari, maraṅkaḷiṉ nālvakiyarikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; atu porinta aṭimaram.
mutalaṟa, aṭiyōṭu; murukki, muṟittu; itaṟku ipporuḷ uṇmi kāppuṭiya veḻu murukki (14) eṉṉum puṟānāṉūṟṟu riyiṟkāṇka; āvatu iṅkuk koṉṟeṉṉum poruṭṭu. piṟaṅku tal - mikutal (puṟam, 49).
`uliyā vāḻkkiyit tēvarkaḷukku āḷuki ceyyum urimiyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟaṟkut `talimiyoṭu nalkum eṉap paṭṭatu; uliyā - keṭāta (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli, 10, 10). māṉam aḻintum uyir nīkkamāṭṭāc cāvā vāḻkkiyuṭiya tēvarkku mīṇṭum aracurimi nalki avaratu māṉatti niliniṟuttiṉami kūṟiyapaṭi.
iṉṉōci nalaṅkarutit tamiḻ kiḻavōyē eṉamikātu iyalpāy vantatu. paḻamutircōli malikiḻavōṉē (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 317) eṉpuḻip pōla.
`tamiḻkkiḻavōyē, taṉakku nikarākunaroṭu kēṇmi ceyalākuvateṉa moḻikuvarataṉāl nuntiyu muṉikuvaṉallaṉ aṉṉiyum nuntivaḻippaṭūun takiyaḷ, ākavē niṉṉoṭum eṉṉiṭip paṭṭa ivviyipāl eṉakku nī yaruṭaraliyiyum eṉa viṉimuṭittuk koḷka. (10)
11. pirivāṟṟāta talivi matiyoṭu varuntal
kiḻami niṉiyāta kēḻmatiyē yoṟṟik
kuḻakaṉ pirinta kuṟippāṟ - poḻutaṟintu
cellumuyirp pētikkut tīṅkiḻippāy niṉṉiyaṭum
vallaravik kollā mayil.
(i-ḷ). kiḻami niṉiyāta kēḻmatiyē - urimi karutāta niṟaṅ kiḷarntu viḷaṅkum nilavē, oṟṟikkuḻakaṉ pirinta kuṟippāl - tiruvoṟṟimurukaṉ eṉṉippirinta kuṟippiṉāl, poḻutu aṟintu - nēranterintu, cellum uyirppētikkut tīṅku iḻippāy - pirivāṟṟāmiyāl uṭampiṉiṉṟum pirintu cellukiṉṟa uyiriyuṭiya pētiyākiya eṉakkut tīmi purikiṉṟaṉi; niṉṉi aṭumval aravi mayil kollā - ivvāṟē nī ceyviyāyiṉ niṉṉi viḻuṅkum vallami vāynta `irāku eṉṉum pāmpi eṉ talivaṉ ūrntuvarum mayil kollātu eṉka.
tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟukku maṉiviyāyaminta peṇ pālākalāṉ taṉṉōṭotta peṇpāl ivaḷaṉṟē eṉṉum urimi karutāmal atu varuttutaliṟ `kiḻami niṉiyāta eṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kiḻami niṉiyātu varuttukiṉṟuḻi oḷikiḷarntu niṟṟal peṇmikku iḻukkāmākaliṟ `kēḻmatiyē eṉṟuṅkūṟiṉāḷ.
`kuḻakaṉ, iṅkuk kaṭṭiḷamiyuṭiyavaṉ; avaṉ murukaṉ; `murukaṉ eṉṉuñcoṟkum atuvē poruḷākalāṉ, `kuḻakaṉ eṉpataṟku murukaṉ eṉavē urikūṟappaṭṭatu. `kuḻavu iḷamip poruṭṭātal, maḻavuṅ kuḻavu miḷamipporuḷa (tolkāppiyam, col 312) veṉpataṉā laṟiyappaṭum.
`kuṟippāṟ poḻutaṟintu eṉappaṭṭatu, pirinta kuṟippiṉāl varuttum nēramaṟintu eṉṟaṟku.
`talivaṉ pirivip poṟukkalāṟṟāmiyāl varunti uyir nīṅkikkoṇṭirukkun taṟuvāyil, avvuyir piṉṉum virintu nīṅkiccellumāṟu matiyē, nī tīṅkiḻikkiṉṟaṉi, yeṉṟamiyāl, varuntuvārip piṉṉum varuttuṅ kaṉṉeñcamum, eḷiyāri varuttum pēṭittaṉmiyum ākāeṉak kūṟiṉāḷāyiṟṟu.
talivaṉ pirivip poṟumiyuṭaṉ āṟṟik koṇṭirukkum aṟivāṟṟal illāmiyiṉ iṅkut talivi `pēti yeṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭami aṟintu makiḻaṟpālatākum.
tiṅkaḷ iḻikkun tīṅkāvatu kāmanōymalarum māli nērattil tam talivarip pirintu pirivāṟṟāmiyāl varuntum makaḷirmēl taṉ taṇkatirkaḷiccorintu avvāṟṟāl avarkku avartaṅ kēḷvariṉ iṉiya kātalniṉivi mēlum mēlu eḻuppi varuttutalākum. annērattil ammakaḷir nilaviṉoḷiyāl varuttam eytutaliṉ, annilavu tamakkut tīṅkiḻippatāy avar karutuvārāyiṉareṉka. itu karutiyē nantamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ attaṇṇeṉkatiri veyteṉa aṉalāka uruvakappaṭutturippatum marapāyiṟṟu.
  ceppiḷaṅ koṅkimīr tiṅkaṭ cuṭarpaṭṭuk
  koppuḷaṅ koṇṭa kuḷirvāṉi - ippoḻutu
  mīṉpotintu niṉṟa vicumpeṉpa teṉkolō
  tēṉpotinta vāyāl terintu.
eṉṉum naḷaveṇpāppāṭṭu (cuyamvarakāṇṭam, 104) iṅku niṉivukūraṟpālatu.
`kollātu eṉpatu īṟukeṭṭuk `kollā eṉa niṉṟatu.
nī eṉakkut tīṅkuceytāy eṉpati eṉ talivaṉatu ūrti aṟiyumāyiṉ, atu niṉmēṟ pakikoṇṭu niṉṉi viḻuṅkum vallaravik kollāmal viṭṭu avvaravu niṉṉi viḻuṅkutaliyum ataṉāl appōtu nī varuntutaliyuṅ kaṇkuḷirap pārttuk koṇṭirukkum eṉat talivi karutikkūṟiṉāḷeṉpatu īṟṟaṭiyiṉ karuttu. (11)
12. tōḻi paruvaṅkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttal
mayiṉmī tivarntu tiruvoṟṟi yūrcceṉṟa maṉṉavartā
   mayiṉmē lamarttakaṇ ṇāypirin tāralla raṉpu mikkup
  payiṉōṭu cērttiya kaṟpōṟ ṟuṇiyip payirntakuraṟ
  kuyiṉmā voṭuṅkum poḻutumvan taṉṟiṉik kūṭuvarē
(i-ḷ) mayilmītu ivarntu tiruvoṟṟiyūr ceṉṟa maṉṉavartām - mayilmēl eḻuntaruḷit tiruvoṟṟiyūrukkuc ceṉṟa niṉtalivar, ayilmēl amartta kaṇṇāy - vēloṭu māṟupaṭṭa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyāy, pirintār allar - uṇmiyil niṉṉippirintu ceṉṟaṉar allar; aṉpumikku - aṉpumikuntu, payiṉōṭu cērttiya kalpōl tuṇiyi - arakkoṭu cērkkappaṭṭa kalli okkum uḷḷam piriyāp peṭippaṟaviyi, payirntakural kuyil mā oṭuṅkum poḻutum vantaṉṟu - aḻitta kuraliyuṭiya kuyiṟcēval māmarattiṟ ceṉṟoṭuṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya kārkālattu mālip poḻutum vantatu; iṉikkūṭuvar - ātalāl niṉ talivarum iṉi vantu niṉṉiccērvar eṉpatu.
aḻakiyavāyk      kāṇpārkku makiḻccimikap piṟaḻum mātarkaṇkaḷiṉ iyaṟki, puraḷuntōṟuṅ kāṇpārkku accattiṉi viḷikkum vēṟpaṭikku iṉmiyiṉ vēloṭu māṟupaṭṭa eṉṟār; vaṭivattāl vēlōṭu oppiṉuñ ceyalil ataṉōṭovvā kaṇkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu; amarttal - māṟupaṭal, peṇṭakip pētik kamarttaṉakaṇ eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟpōla (1084).
payiṉoṭu cērttiya kal pirivaṟiyāt tuṇikku uvamiyātal, ciṟukārōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya kaṟpōl eṉa aḵtu aka nāṉūṟṟil 1, 356 āñ ceyyuṭkaḷil irukāṟpōntamiyāṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
`mikku eṉṉum viṉiyeccam `payirnta veṉṉum peyarecca viṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu. payirtal - aḻittal; tuyttali mantiyik kiyiṭūup payirum eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ (158) uriyik kāṇka. vantaṉṟu, vantatu; aṉcāriyi.
`oṭuṅkumpoḻutu eṉṟatu kārkālattu māliyi; kuyilkaḷ vēṉiṟkālattil makiḻntu kūvutalum mārikālattil varunti vāyavitalum iyalpu; ivi māmarattilēyē virumpiyuṟiyum. viṉimēṟ ceṉṟa talivaṉ viṉimuṭittu mīṇṭu taṉ taliviyic cērunēram ituvē yeṉpatu perumpeyal poḻinta ciṟu puṉmāli (6) eṉa mullippāṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭutal iṅku niṉivukūraṟ pālatu. eṉavē, talivaṉ varuṅkār kālattu mālip poḻutu vantateṉṟu tōḻi talivikkup paruvaṅkāṭṭi avaḷi āṟṟāmi taṇiyumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷāyiṟṟeṉka. (12)
13. aruṇili viyattal
ellām valla iṟivaṉ tamakkup piṟavikaṭōṟuñ ciṟituciṟitāka meyyaṟivu  tōṟṟuvittup, piṉṉart taṉ iṉṉaruḷi vaḻaṅkiyaruḷiya aruṇiliyi aṭikaḷ viyanturittal ippāṭṭiṉ poruḷ.
(1-7) kūṭiya iruḷiṉ vāṭuvatu oṉṟō - iyaṟkiyē poruntiya malaviruḷiṉkaṇ aḻunti varuntuvatu oṉṟu maṭṭumō; pīṭu uyar niṉpukaḻ nāṭātu oṉṟō - perumi mikunta tēvarīratu aruṭpukaḻi virumpāmi oṉṟumaṭṭumō, peṟuvatu aṟiyāc ciṟumi oṉṟō - peṟutaṟkuriya niliyi aṟintu koḷḷamāṭṭāta ciṟumiyuṭiyami oṉṟu maṭṭumō, maruvarakkiṭanta terivu ilkālattu - iṅṅaṉam innilikaḷ ellām poruntakkiṭanta ēḻiyēṉatu terital illātakālattil, poruvu il iṉpam taruvatu kuṟittu - oppillāta pēriṉpam aḷippatu tiruvuḷaṅkoṇṭu, pul mutalākiya meyyil pukutti ōr aṟivu uṟuttiṉi cilanāḷ - pul mutalāṉa niliyiyaṟporuḷuṭampu kaḷiṟ celuttip piṟavikaḷit tōṟṟuvittu avvāṟṟāl `ūṟṟuṇarvu eṉṉum ōr aṟiviṉic cilakālam aṟivuṟuttāniṉṟāy;
`iruḷ, āṇavamalavalliruḷ aḵtu uyirkaḷi iyaṟkiyē piṇittiruttalāṟ `kūṭiya iruḷ eṉappaṭṭatu. āṇava iruḷ uyirkaḷi iyaṟkiyē poruntiniṟkum uṇmi,
  aruḷuṭiya parameṉṟō aṉṟu tāṉē
	yāṉuḷaṉeṉṟum eṉakkē yāṇa vāti
  perukuviṉik kaṭṭeṉṟu meṉṉāṟ kaṭṭip
	pēciyataṉṟē yaruṇūl pēciṟ ṟaṉṟē
eṉṟu tāyumāṉac celvaṉār (ākārapuvaṉam citampara rakaciyam, 29) cikkaṟuttut teḷittuk kūṟiyaruḷiya cemmoḻip pāṭṭāl iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
`oṉṟō veṉpatu eṇṇiṭiccol; iḵtu aṟaṉoṉṟō āṉṟa oḻuṅku eṉṉuntirukkuṟaḷ (148) aṭikkup parimēlaḻakaruritta vuriyāṉum, mānakar cuṭutaloṉṟō mataṉaṉi yaḻittaloṉṟō eṉṉuñ cīvakacintāmaṇic ceyyuḷāṉum aṟiyappaṭum.
nāṭātatu eṉaṟpālatu `nāṭā teṉa niṉṟatu tokuttal; itu toḻiṟpeyar; `peṟuvatu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar.
ciṟumi, iṅkuc `ciṟumiyuṭimi yeṉka; uṭimiyān taṉmi uṭimimēl ēṟṟikkūṟappaṭṭatu.
`vāṭuvatoṉṟō, nāṭātoṉṟō, ciṟumiyoṉṟō eṉpaṉa muṟiyē uyirkaḷiṉ malamaṟippiyum, ammaṟip piṉālāṉa aṟiyāmiyiyum, avvaṟiyāmiyālāṉa ciṟumiyiyum uṇarttāniṉṟaṉa.
`maruvara eṉpataṉmuṉ, `iṅṅaṉam allatu `ivvāṟu eṉa orucol varuvittuk koḷka. maruvaral, maruval; `kiṭanta veṉṉuñ col iṅkuc ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭantamiyiṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu.
uyirkaḷ piṟavikku varātataṉmuṉ āṇavamalavalliruḷāl muḻutum viḻuṅkappaṭṭu, aṟivuviḷakkan tiṉittaṉiyu miṉṟit, tammiyun tammiyuṭiya, talivaṉiyun tammiviḻuṅkiya malaviruḷiṉiyum aṟiyamāṭṭāmal, iyaṟkiyēyuṇar villāk kalluṅkaṭṭiyumpōṟ kiṭattaliṉ, akkālam iṅkut `terivil kālam eṉappaṭṭatu. uyirkaḷukku aḵtōr iruḷniliyākum. camayac cāṉṟōr itaṉik `kēvalanili yeṉpa.
`poruvil iṉpam vīṭupēṟṟiṉpam; taruvatu, toḻiṟ peyar; kuṟittu, kuṟikkoṇṭu.
`pul eṉṟatu iṅku uṭampukku; ēṉiya vaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.
`cilanāḷ eṉpatu iṅku ettaṉiyō āṇṭukaḷik kuṟiyā niṉṟatu. ēṉippala piṟavikaḷiṉ aḷavillāk kālavelliyi nōkkap puṉmutalākiya meyyiṟpukutti ōraṟivuṟukkum aḷavil nāṭkaḷ cilanāṭkaḷāyiṉaveṉka. mēlvaruvaṉavum iṅṅaṉamē koḷḷaṟpālaṉa.
viṉiyilē kiṭakkum uyirkaḷi avviṉiyiṉīṅkiya iṟivaṉ ivvāṟellām puṉmutalāṉa meyyiṟpukuttip piṟaviyuṭpaṭuttu māṟṟāl avaṟṟiṉ aṟivik kiḷarnteḻumāṟu ceytu āṭkoḷḷum aruṭṭiṟamellām meyyaruḷ nili kivanta poyyil tiruvātavūrar,
  viṉiyilē kiṭantēṉip pukuntuniṉṟu
	pōtunāṉ viṉikkēṭa ṉeṉpāy pōla
  iṉiyaṉāṉ eṉṟuṉṉi yaṟivit teṉṉi
	yāṭkoṇṭem pirāṉāṉāy	(tiruvācakam, tiruccatakam, 22)
eṉṟu mikatteḷivāka eṭuttōtiyiruttal perituṅ karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
(7-9) cilanāḷ - cilakālam, nantu mutalākiya uṭampil pukuttu- nattimutalāṉa uṭampukaḷiṟ pukuttivittu,  tantaṉi īr uṇarvu - iraṇṭaṟivukaḷ koṭuttaruḷiṉi;
`nantu īraṟivuyirātal, nantum muraḷum īraṟiviṉavē eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattiṟkāṇka (marapiyal, 29).
īruṇar veṉpaṉa, meyyiṉāl toṭṭaṟiyum aṟivum, nāviṉāṟ cuvittaṟiyum aṟivumām.
(9-11) cilanāḷ - cilakālam cital mutalākiya kurampiyil celīi- kaṟiyāṉ mutalāṉa uṭampukaḷiṟcelutti, mū uṇarvu uṇarttiṉi -mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa uṇarvukaḷi uṇartti yaruḷiṉi;
eṟumpu mutalāṉavikaḷuñ citaliṉattiṟ cērkkappaṭum; citalum eṟumpum mūvaṟiviṉavē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār (marapiyal, 30).
celīi- piṟaviṉi, viṉiyeccam; iciniṟiyaḷapeṭi, itu piṟaviṉikkaṇ varutal, mēlkoṇṭavi celīi eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālic ceyyuḷaṭiyiṟ kāṇka. (12, 13); itu ceṉṟu eṉat taṉviṉi viṉiyeccamāyum varum.
mūṉṟuvaki uṇarvukaḷāvaṉa, mēṟkāṭṭiya iruvaki yuṇarvukaḷoṭu mūkkiṉāl mukantuṇarum uṇarvumākum. maṉṉum ōvum aci.
(11-13) cilanāḷ - iṉṉuñ cilakālam, naṇṭu mutalākiya poṉṟu uṭal kaṭavi - naṇṭu mutalākiya aḻiyum uṭampukaḷiṟ celutti, nāṉku aṟivu ōṅkuṟac ceytaṉi - nāṉku aṟivukaḷ kiḷarnteḻumāṟu ceytaruḷiṉi;
naṇṭōṭu iṉappaṭṭa nāṉkaṟivu uyirkaḷ tumpi mutalāyiṉavātal, naṇṭun tumpiyum nāṉkaṟiviṉavē (tolkāppiyam, marapiyal, 31) eṉpataṉāṉ aṟika.
ēṉiya uṭampukaḷum aḻiyum peṟṟiyavēṉum, māntaril ūṉ uṇpār kūṭṭam perumpāṉmiyum innaṇṭu mutalāṉa uṭampukaḷik koṉṟaḻikkiṉṟatākaliṟ, `poṉṟuṭampu eṉak kiḷantōtiṉār.
`kaṭaval celuttal; iḵtipporuṭṭātal kiyoḷir vēlavaṉ kaṭava eṉapuḻik kāṇka (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli 12, 1, koḷu).
itaṉkaṭ kūṟappaṭum nāṉkāvatu aṟivu kaṇkaḷāṟkaṇṭu aṟiyum aṟivākum. ōṅkuṟa, orucol.
(13-16) īṅku - ippāl, māvum mākkaḷum mēviya piṉṟi - vilaṅkuṭampukaḷum mākkaḷuṭampukaḷum poruntiyapiṉ,  aiuṇarvu eṅkum meypeṟak koḷuvi oḻuṅkuṟa uṇarttiṉi oru cila pakal - aintu uṇarvukaḷ muḻumiyum meyyākavē nilavap porutti oḻuṅkupeṟa uṇarttiyaruḷiṉi orucilanāṭkaḷ.
`īṅku, eṉpatu aṅṅaṉamellām mēṟceṉṟa piṟavikaḷiṟ piṟappittu aṟivuvaḷaracceytu piṟaku ippāl eṉapporuḷ payantatu; iḵtu aiyaṟivuṭiya piṟavikku vantataṉ arumiyuṇara niṉṟatu.
`mā veṉpatu vilaṅku; `mākka ḷeṉṟatu, avvilaṅkup piṟappiṉum uyarntu ēṉi makkaṭpiṟappiṟ ṟāḻntu avviraṇṭiṉ naṭuvaṇatāy avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāṉatoru piṟappām. ippiṟaviyuṭpaṭum `mākkaḷ eṉṉum uyirkaḷ vilaṅkiṉ uruvamum makkaḷuruvamuṅ kalantatōruruvatti uṭiyavāyirukkumeṉpa. iṅṅaṉamoru paṭippu vilaṅkup piṟappiṟkum makkaṭpiṟappiṟkum iṭiyē āṇṭavaṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa ikkālattu uyir nūlvallārum uyttuṇarntu, aṅṅaṉantām uṇarntavāṟē  yiruppatoru paṭippiṉi `āttirēliyā (Australia) eṉṉum nāṭṭil nēriṟkaṇṭu, tamatu tuṇipiṉi meyppaṭuttu vārāyiṉar. iṟṟikku ēṟakkuṟiya aiyāyirattu ainnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē nam mututamiḻppērāciriyarāy viḷaṅkiya tolkāppiyaṉār ivvuṇmiyi naṉku teḷintu, avviṉattavām uyirkaṭku aintu aṟivukaḷ uṇṭueṉa aṟivu variyaṟikāṭṭi māvum mākkaḷum aiyaṟiviṉavē. (marapiyal, 32) eṉat tamatu arumpeṟal nūlil nūṟpā yātturittatu peritum niṉivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum.
muṟkūṟappaṭṭa nāṉku uṇarvukaḷuṭaṉ ceviyāṟkēṭṭu uṇarum uṇarvuṅkūṭa iṅku aiyuṇarvukaḷuṇṭāyiṉa. `eṅku meṉṟatu, muḻumiyumeṉṉum poruṭṭu. ceviyuṇarvu peṟṟapiṉṉarē uyirkaḷ cēymikkaṇiruntu tamakkuvarum ētam uṇarntu uyir piḻikkiṉṟaṉavākaliṉ, aiyuṇarvukaḷum meyyāka nilipeṟṟu nilaval māvum mākkaḷumākap piṟavi yeṭutta piṉṉarēyātal tēṟṟutaṟku meypeṟakkoḷuvi eṉṟār; koḷuval, poruttal, kali nalaṅkoḷutti yiṭṭāṉ eṉpuḻippōla (cīvakacintāmaṇi,  673). pakal, nāḷ; iṅkuk kālam eṉṉum poruṭṭu.
(17-21) āṅṅaṉam oḻinta aḷavu il kālattu - avvāṟu kaḻinta aḷavillāta kālattiṉ elliyil, niṟivuṟu kuṟṟam muṟi muṟi tēya - iyaṟkiyē niṟintirunta tukaḷ aṭi vaṭivē kuṟiyē, pakuttu uṇar kāṭci mikuttuttōṉṟa - avvāṟṟāṟ pakuttuuṇarum aṟivu mikuntu viḷaṅka, makkaḷ yākkiyil takkavā niṟuvi - makkaḷuṭampil takutiyāka niṟutti, pala piṟappu uṟīiya nāḷkaḷum pala - pala piṟappukaḷil aṭiyēṉi uṟuvitta kālaṅkaḷum palavām.
kuṟṟam - mala aḻukku. muṟimuṟi - aṭivaṭivē, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 29).
`pakuttuṇar kāṭciyāvatu, evaṟṟiyum nallatu itu, tīyatu itu eṉap pirittu uṇarum āṟāvatu aṟivākum. `kāṭci, aṟiveṉṉum poruṭṭātal maruṭīrnta mācaṟu kāṭciyavar. (tirukkuṟaḷ, 199) eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.
`uṟīiya: piṟaviṉip peyareccam; (patiṟṟuppattu 44).
eṉavē mutalaṭimutal ivvirupattōrām aṭikāṟum iyaṟkiyē viṉiyiṟkiṭanta uyirkaḷ āṇṭavaṉ ṟiruvaruḷāṟ puṉmutalāṉa pala piṟavikaḷiṟ piṟantu piṟantu ōraṟivumutalāka āṟaṟivukaḷ viḷaṅkapeṟumāṟṟi āciriyar tammēl vitturittami kāṇka. viṉiyilē kiṭanta uyirkaḷ ivvāṟellām paṟpala kālamākap palvakip piṟaviyuṭpaṭṭu uḻalumāṟṟāl muṟimuṟiyē aṟivupeṟuvatāṉa uṇmi paṇṭittamiḻp pērāciriyarāṉa tolkāppiyaṉār mēleṭuttukkāṭṭiya marapiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷil ōtiya vāṟṟāṉum, camayap pērāciriyarāṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ,
  pullākip pūṭāyp puḻuvāy maramākip
  palviruka mākip paṟaviyāyp pāmpākik
  kallāy maṉitarāyp pēyāyk kaṇaṅkaḷāy
  vallacura rāki muṉivarāyt tēvarāyc
  cellāa niṉṟavit tāvara caṅkamattuḷ
  ellāp piṟappum piṟantiḷittē ṉemperumāṉ
  meyyēyuṉ poṉṉaṭikaḷ kaṇṭiṉṟu vīṭuṟṟēṉ
(tiruvācakam, civapurāṇam)
eṉa aruḷicceytavāṟṟāṉum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
(22-32) avvāṟu oḻintaṉapōkac cevviya innāḷ eṉakku nal neṟi kāṭṭa - aṅṅaṉam muṭintakālaṅkaḷ kaḻiya iññāṉṟu eḷiyēṟku nalvaḻi kāṭṭumporuṭṭu, cevvantippōnta tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil tōṉṟiyaruḷiya, poyyānāviṉ centamiḻ varampu muntukaṇṭuṇarntu - poyttal aṟiyāta nāviṉāṟ centamiḻelliyi muṟpaṭat tāṉ ōti uṇarntu, meypporuḷ aṟivum coṟporuḷ vaṉmiyum poṟiyum ceṟivum niṟiyakkoṇṭu - meypporuḷ uṇarvum coṟporuḷ ārāycciyum poṟumiyum aṭakkamum nirampappeṟṟu, tamiḻmāṇākkar amiḻtueṉa nukara viritturi kiḷakkum teripparum perumi - tamiḻkaṟkum māṇākkarkaḷ `iḵtu amiḻtākum eṉac cuvittuṇarumpaṭi virittu urikūṟun terital ariya perumiyiyuṭiya, nārāyaṇaṉ eṉum tōlāppulavōṉ nērpaṭum uḷattuc cīrpaṭa amarntu - nārāyaṇa cāmippiḷḷi eṉṉun tōlāta pulavaṉatu cevviya tiruvuḷḷattiṟ ciṟappuṟavīṟṟiruntu, karuvinūl uṇarttiṉi pala -karuvi nūlkaḷākiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala uṇarttiyaruḷiṉi;
kīḻppiṟavikaḷiṉ tuṉpaniliyum iḻivuṅ karuti appiṟavikaḷik koṇṭa kālaṅkaḷ `oḻintaṉa veṉa veṟuttukkūṟappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉamē pakuttaṟivu mēmpāṭṭāl makkaṭpiṟavikku uriya perumi karutic `cevviya innāḷ eṉa appiṟaviyikkoṇṭa kālaṅkaḷ ciṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa
`cevvanti, tiruccirāppaḷḷi; cevvantippurāṇam eṉṉum vaḻakkāl ītuṇarappaṭum. itu `tiricirapuram eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. `kaṇṭu, ōti yeṉṉumporuṭṭu. moḻiyaṟivu koṇṭu makkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkuriya karuttukaḷ iṅku `meypporuḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. coṟporuḷvaṉmi yeṉappaṭuvatu, collum accollukku uriya poruḷumāṉa ilakkaṇa ilakkiya moḻiyārāycci.
`ceṟivu aṭakkam; iḵtu ipporuṭṭātal ceṟivaṟintu cīrmi payakkum eṉṉuntirukkuṟaḷuriyiṟ kāṇka. mey moḻi maṉaṅkaḷ tīneṟikkaṭcellātu aṭaṅkutal aṭakkameṉavum, kāraṇampaṟṟiyātal maṭamiyā ṉātal oruvaṉ tamakku miki ceytavaḻit tāmum ataṉi avaṉkaṭ ceyyātu poṟuttal poṟiyeṉavum tirukkuṟaḷ (aṭakkamuṭimi, poṟiyuṭimi) urikārar parimēlaḻakar kūṟukiṉṟami iṅku niṉivukūrntu poṟiyum ceṟivum vēṟuvēṟātal uṇarntukoḷka.
`viritturi kiḷattal eṉṟatu, īṇṭuppulami tōṉṟa uriyāṭali, āvatu `colvaṉmiyeṉka. `terippu toḻiṟpeyar.
`nārāyaṇaṉ eṉpatu aṭikaḷukkut tamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceviyaṟivuṟuttiya iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyariṉ peyar; `tōlāp pulavōṉ tōlviyaṭiyāta pulavaṉ. `tōlā moḻit tēvar eṉap piṟarum ipporuṭṭāyc ciṟappikkap paṭutal kāṇka. `pulavaṉ, `īṟutirintu `pulavōṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
meypporuṭkaruttukaḷ aṟiyavēṇṭum oruvaṉ avaṟṟi orumoḻiyiṉ vāyilākavē aṟital vēṇṭu mākaliṉ, īṇṭut tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷ `karuvinūl eṉappaṭṭaṉa. aviyāvaṉa, tolkāppiyamum pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭut toki patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku aimperuṅkāppiyam mutalāyiṉavumām.
aṭikaḷ ippakutiyil tamakkut tamiḻceviyaṟivuṟuttiya tam iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyariṉ arumiperumikaḷi iṉiteṭuttuk kūṟi makiḻntami karuttiṟpatikkaṟpālatu.
(32- 46) ataṉpiṉ - ataṉpiṉpu, oruvā yāṇar maruvuṟu ceṉṉit tavampeṟat tōṉṟi - nīṅkāp putuvaruvāy maruvutaluṭiya ceṉṉimānakariṉ kaṇ tavamuṇṭākat tōṉṟiyaruḷi, navi aṟu tamiḻum vaṭanūl parappum nili kaṇṭu uṇarntu - kuṟṟam aṟṟa tamiḻnūlkaḷum vaṭanūṟ paraviyum āḻamvariyiṟceṉṟu terintu, āṅku uḷ uṟi karuvāyt teḷoḷi virikkum civacittānta muṭipporuḷ eṭuttu - avaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṭkiṭakkum mutaṟ poruḷāyt teḷinta aṟivoḷivirikkuñ civacittāntam eṉṉum muṭintaporuḷiyeṭuttu, īṅku aṟam kari nāviṉ tiṟampaṭa viḷakkip puṟampaṭu camayap poypporuḷ nūṟi - ittamiḻakattil tamatu aṟattiyē navilum nāviṉāl tiṟam uṇṭāka viḷakki avvāṟṟāṟ puṟamāy oḻiyum puṟaccamayaṅkaḷiṉ poymip poruḷkaḷi aḻittu, nalam kiḷar taṉmiyum pulamkoḷāk kāṭciyum iyintu oruṅku īṇṭiya amiyāpperumic cōmacuntarat tōm aṟutēcikaṉ - naṉmimikka iyalpum eḷitil aṟitaliyalāta aṟivuñ cērntu oṉṟāyttiraṇṭa aṭaṅkāp perumiyi yuṭiya cōma cuntaraṉ eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamaṟṟa āciriyaṉuṭiya, uṭalporuḷ āvi iṭam eṉak koṇṭu - uṭalum poruḷum āviyun tēvarīr amarun tirukkōyileraṉakkaruti eḻuntaruḷi, poruḷ ār civam teruḷuṟak koḷīi - uṇmi nirampiya civacamayapporuḷi aṟiviṟ poruntumpaṭi aṟuvuṟut taruḷi, ataṟku ami poruḷum nī yeṉakkāṭṭit tiṟampaṭi uṇarttiṉi mikavē - accivacamayattukku uriya muḻu mutaṟporuḷun tēvarīrē eṉatteḷivittut tiṟamuṇṭākap peritum uṇartti yaruḷiṉi; ataṉpiṉ - ataṉ piṉpu;
orukāl vanta varuvāy oḻiyumuṉṉarē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum  putupputu varuvāypporuḷkaḷ kalattāṉuṅ kālāṉum mēlumēlum vantu cērtaluṭimiyiṉ, īṇṭuc ceṉṉa paṭṭiṉam “oruvāyāṇar maruvuṟu ceṉṉi” yeṉappaṭṭatu; `yāṇar’ eṉṉuñcol `putuvaruvāy’ eṉapporuṭaral, “putitupaṭaṟ poruṭṭē yāṇarkkiḷavi’ (tolkāppiyam, uriyiyal., 81) eṉpataṉālaṟiyappaṭum.piracam tūṅkum aṟāa yāṇar
  variyaṇi paṭappi nalnāṭṭu
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟil (375) kaṇṇum iccol ipporuṭṭāy varutalum, aṭikaḷ iṅku `oruvāyāṇar eṉṟāṟpōlavē, `aṟāa yāṇar eṉa aṭiyaṭuttu varutalum niṉivukūraṟpālaṉa.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa nūlkaḷ cilavēyāyiṉum avi poymmi naviluṅ kuṟṟam aṟṟup paṭṭāṅku kiḷappaṉavāyiruttaliṉ `naviyaṟutamiḻ eṉavum, vaṭamoḻi yiṉkaṇ avvāṟiṉṟi nūlkaḷmaṭṭum nirampak kāṇap paṭutaliṉ vāḷā `vaṭanūṟparappu eṉavum avviruperu moḻikaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi tēṟṟi aṭikaḷ iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya tiṭpanuṭpam peritum aṟintu makiḻaṟ pālaṉavām.
`karu mutaṟporuḷ;
`civa cittāntam eṉṉum meypporuḷ, muṭinta aṟivu ārāyccikaḷ vāyntu avaṟṟiṉvaṭivāy amintiruttaliṉ, aḵtu `oḷivirikkuñ civacittāntam eṉavum, `muṭiporuḷ eṉavuṅ kiḷantōtappaṭṭatu. `oḷi yeṉpatu īṇṭu `aṟivu oḷi yeṉka. ēṉiccamayapporuḷkaḷ attaṉiyum aṅṅaṉam muṭinta aṟivārāyccikaḷāṉ iyalātu aṟiyāmiyiruḷum uṭaṉvirāy maṅkutaliṉ icciva cittāntam maṭṭum avaṟṟiṉ uḷḷuṟi karuvāykkiṭantu teḷḷoḷivirikkum muṭiporu ḷāyiṟṟeṉṟuṇarntu koḷka. tamiḻnāṉ maṟikaḷilum vaṭanūṉmaṟikaḷilum civacittāntam iṅṅaṉam muṭinta niliyil vittu aṟivuṟukkappaṭṭirukkum ariya uṇmi.
vēta meṉṉum pātavam vaḷarttaṉi
pātava mataṉiṟ paṭupayaṉ palavē
avaṟṟuḷ,
ilikoṇṭu uvantaṉar palarē iliyorīit
taḷirkoṇ ṭuvantaṉar palarē taḷirorīi
arumpoṭu malarpiñ caruṅkā yeṉṟivi

ōrum vētāntameṉ ṟucciyiṟ paḻutta

eṉak kumarakurupara aṭikaḷ tiruvāy malarntaruḷiya paṇṭāra mummaṇik kōvi (10) yaṭikaḷālum iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟami kāṇka.
`aṟam kari nā, naṉṟāvaṉavē eṭuttup pēcumnā; aṟam nallatu, takkatu; aṟameṉpatu takkatu eṉpar iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuriyāciriyar (29).
`puṟampaṭucamayaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa: ulakāyatam, nālvakip puttam, ārukatam eṉa aṟuvakippaṭum puṟappuṟaccamayaṅ kaḷum; tarukkam, mīmāñci, ēkāṉmavātam, cāṅkiyam yōkam, pāñcarāttiram eṉa āṟuvakippaṭum puṟaccamayaṅkaḷumām; iṉip, pācupatam, māviratam, kāpālam, vāmam, viravam, aikkiyavātacivam eṉa aṟuvakippaṭum akappuṟaccamayaṅkaḷum; pāṭāṇavātacivam, pētavāta civam, civacamavāta civam, civacaṅkirāntavāta civam, īcuvara vavikāravāta civam, civāttuvita civam eṉa āṟuvakippaṭum akac camayaṅkaḷum ākum. ivi kuṟipāṭukaḷ pala uṭiyavākaliṉ, aviyum cittānta civattiṟkup `puṟampaṭucamayap poypporuḷ kaḷāyiṉa veṉpatu.
puṟappuṟam, puṟam, akappuṟam, akam eṉṉum puṟaccamayaṅkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷ ellām civañāṉa pōtamā pāṭiyattil (civañāṉa pōtam, aviyaṭakkam) āciriyar civañāṉa muṉivarāṟ camaya ilakkaṇa aḷavinūṉeṟikaḷ vāḻāmal naṉkeṭuttu  viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku virippiṟ perukum. nūṟi - aḻittu; aruñcamantatiya nūṟi (puṟam. 93).
iṉi ivvellāc camayaṅkaṭkum muṭintaniliyil amarntu teḷḷoḷivirikkuñ cittānta civattiṉ ciṟanta kōṭpāṭukaḷi viḷakkum vāyil piṉṉē pulavarāṟṟup paṭiyil (193 - 205) varutaliṉ, avitammi  aṅkē kaṇṭukoḷka.
ēṉiyō, raṟivukku eṭṭāta akaṉṟaaṟivu iṅkup `pulaṅ koḷākkāṭci yeṉappaṭṭatu. īṇṭiya - tiraṇṭa, (puṟam, 17). `amiyāveṉṟatu, eṇṇutaṟkuñ collutaṟkum aṭaṅkāta veṉakkoḷka. tōm - kuṟṟam; tōmaṟukaṭiki (cilappati kāram, 10, 98).
`cōmacuntaratēcikaṉ eṉpār aṭikaḷukkuc civa cittānta meypporuḷ aṟivuṟuttaruḷiya camayāciriyarāvar.
`poruḷ ār civam, meymi niṟinta civacamayam; meymiyāvaṉa āṇṭavaṉukkup piṟappiṟappukkūṟāmiyum, māyiyum uyirum iṟivaṉēyeṉak kiḷavāmiyum pōlvaṉa. teruḷ - aṟivu, (piṅkalanti).
murukapperumāṉ piṟappiṟappillā muḻumutaṟ cemporuḷākiya civattiṉ iḷamikkōlamēyākaliṉ, avaṉ civacamayattukku uriyaporuḷāṉāṉeṉpatu.
iṉit `toḻumpu pūṇ ṭeṉpatu toṭṭu `oṟṟiyūreṉum peyar muṟṟattōṉṟu meṉpatu variyil aivaki ētukkaḷ kāṭṭit tiruvoṟṟiyūreṉṉum peyaramippu eṭuttōtappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
(47-50) toḻumpu pūṇṭu oḻukum aḻuṅkā uḷḷattu - toṇṭu ceytali mēṟkoṇṭu naṭakkum aḻiyāta tiruvuḷḷattiyuṭiya, toṇṭarum narakarum aṇṭarum piṟarum - aṭiyārum nākarum tēvarum ēṉiyōrum, vaḻivaḻi aṭimi perukuṟa eḻutiya ōlikoṇṭa orumarapāṉum - tām vaḻivaḻi aṭimiyāyiruttali mēlum mēlum perukumpaṭi oṟṟivitteḻutiya oṟṟi ōliyiṉik kikkoṇṭaruḷiya orutaṉmiyāṉum.
toṇṭu pūṇṭoḻukutaliṟ ciṟitun taḷarvaṭiyāmal mikka uṟippoṭu niṟkum uḷḷamē iṅku `aḻuṅkāuḷḷam eṉappaṭṭatu; aḻuṅkal - aḻital, piṇaṉ aḻuṅka puṟam, 98. toḻumpu aṭimi; āḷuntoḻumpum aṭimiyākum tivākaram.
`narakar kīḻulakattavarāvar; avar `nākar, `aṇṭar mēlulakattavar; avar tēvarāvar; cūṭāmaṇinikaṇṭu. `piṟa reṉpatu ivarallāta ēṉi mēṉmēlulakaṅkaḷiliruppavar. avar `viññāṉakalar mutaliyōrāvar. ivarellārum āṇṭavaṉukkut talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka aṭimip paṭṭiruttaliṉ, iṉimēl  varun talimuṟikaḷilum aṅṅaṉamē aṭimippaṇi purivatākat tamatu aṭimiyi avaṉṟiruvaṭikku oṟṟivittu eḻutikkoṭutta ōliyeṉpatu viḷaṅka `vaḻivaḻi yaṭimi perukuṟa eḻutiya ōli eṉappaṭṭatu. `oṟṟi ōli koṇṭamiyiṉ oṟṟiyūrāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
marapu - taṉmi; teṟalarumarapu puṟam, 126. `āṉ eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉurupu ētupporuḷatu.
(51- 53) ulakil ciṟantu tāṉ oṉṟē ākiyum - ulakam eṅkaṇum uḷḷa ēṉittiruppatikaḷiṉum pārkkat teyvat taṉmiyāṟ ciṟantu aṅṅaṉañ ciṟakkun taṉimiyāl tāṉ oṉṟēyāyiruntum, alaku il āṟṟal talivarum eṉpatu - aḷavillāta teyva valimi talimiyāk kūṭumeṉpatu viḷaṅka, valoṟṟu ākkiya kuṟippiṉāṉum - melliṉamey valliṉamey ākkiṉa kuṟippāṉum;
iṇiyillāmiyiṉ taṉimiyāyiṟṟu, atāvatu oṉṟāyiṟṟeṉpatu; aṅṅaṉan taṉimiyāy oṉṟēyāyiṉum, pērāṟṟaluṭiyatākumeṉpatu tōṉṟa iṭiniṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu valittu valloṟṟāy oṟṟeṉappaṭṭup, piṉṉarp peyar vikutiyākiya ikaram maruvi `oṟṟi yāyiṟṟeṉpatu. alaku- aḷavu; āṟṟal - valimi; piṅkalanti. tali - talimi, (irēvaṇācittar cūttiram).
(54-55) oṟṟi iyakkum uyirē pōla - oṟṟeḻutti iyaṅkacceyyum uyireḻuttēpōl, neṟṟi am kaṇṇaṉ nilavutalāṉum - neṟṟikkaṇ uṭiyavaṉāṉa civaperumāṉ ittiruppatiyi iyakkikkoṇṭu āṅku viḷaṅkutalāṉum;
oṟṟeḻuttōṭottaliṉ tāṉum `oṟṟi yeṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟeṉpatu. muṉpōṟ peyarvikuti puṇarttuk koḷka. mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum. ēkāram iciniṟi; amcāriyi.
(56-57) iṟivaṉ aintoḻil - paṭittal kāttal aḻittal, maṟittal, aruḷal eṉṉum iṟivaṉuṭiya aintoḻilkaḷiyum, niṟiuyirmāṭṭup poruntac ceyyum arum tiṟalāṉum - uṭampukaḷil niṟintuniṟkum uyirkaḷiṉ pāṟ poruntumpaṭi ceyyum ariyavallamiyāṉum;
iṟivaṉin toḻilkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷ `nīyē, āruyirt tokutikkup pēṟutaral vēṇṭi yintoḻi liyaṟṟuvi mātō (27-8) eṉṟaviṭattu viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
`niṟiyuyir uṭampukaḷil niṟintu kāṇappaṭuma uyirkaḷ; aṉṟik kaṇakkillāta uyirkaḷ eṉalumām.
uyirkaḷ tiruvoṟṟiyuriṉ kaṇ vāḻtalāṉ āṇṭavaṉ ṟiruvaruḷip peṟutaṟkuriya takutiyi yaṭintu avaṉṟaṉ aintoḻilkaḷ poruttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam avvuyir kaṭku avvintoḻilkaḷiyum  oṉṟacceyyum āṟṟal uṭimiyiṉ ivvūr oṟṟiyūreṉappaṭuva tāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
oṉṟiyūr eṉpatu oṟṟiyūreṉa valittatu.
(58-59) aintoḻiṟpaṭṭa āruyirt tokikaḷ - mēṟ kūṟiya avvivakit toḻilkaḷiṟ poruttappaṭṭa niṟinta uyirkkūṭṭaṅkaḷi, kantaḻip poruttum mintiṉāṉum - `kantaḻi yeṉṉun tīppiḻampākiya civattiṉ tiruvaruḷil talikkūṭṭum valimiyiṉāṉum;
`kantaḻi yeṉpatu tīppiḻampu; ellām valla muḻumutaṟ civapirāṉuṭiya teyvaviyalpukaḷ perumpāṉmiyum maṇmutalāṉa aimmutaṟporuḷkaḷuḷ tīyiṉkaṇ maṭṭumē naṉku viḷaṅkutalpāṟṟik `kantaḻi yeṉpatu iṅkuc civaperumāṉi  uṇarttuvatāyiṟṟu.
  koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi yeṉṉum
  vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟuṅ
  kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē.	
(tolkāppiyam, puṟattiṇiyiyal, 33)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉkaṇṇuṅ `kantaḻi yeṉṉum iccol tīppiḻampi  uṇarttumukattāl muḻumutaṟ civattiṉ vaḻipāṭṭukku ōr oppaṟṟa vāyilāy niṟṟal aṟiyaṟpālatu. aṭikaṭku ikkaruttuṇmi, aṭikaḷē tām peritārāyntu iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum mikapperiya ariyanūlil (pakkam, 487-492) ittolkāppiya nūṟpāviyē yeṭuttu mika nuṇakkamāṉa viḷakkavuri viritturittalkoṇṭu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
`mintu valimi; `mintuṭik kaḻitiṉ yāṉi (puṟam, 73) eṉpataṉuriyiṟ kāṇka..
kantaḻiyil uyirttokikaḷi oṟṟum ūrākaliṉ, oṟṟiyūreṉappaṭṭatu.
(60-61) oṟṟiyūr eṉumpeyar muṟṟattōṉṟum - eṉa ivvāṟṟāl tiruvoṟṟiyūr eṉṉum peyar muṭiyaviḷaṅkum, perunaṉ mānakar tiruviṉpolintu - periya nalla aḻakiya nakarattil ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅki;
`muṟṟa eṉpatu muṟṟattuṟattal, muṟṟakkaṭital eṉuñ coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṟpōla muḻutum eṉṉum poruṭṭu; aḵtāvatu eñcāmi; `oṟṟiyūr eṉum peyarkkuriya poruḷkaḷellām eñcāmip peṟutal.
(62-63) kuvaḷipūtta pavaḷattāmari-  iraṇṭu ciṟiya kuvaḷip pūkkaḷ taṉkaṇ pūkkappeṟṟa pavaḷaniṟam vāynta oru periya centāmari malar, ceviramum paṇilamum puṭipaṭa - pāciyuñ caṅkun taṉ mēlkīḻp pakkaṅkaḷiṟ porunta, iṭiyil tōṉṟiyaṉṉa - avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōla, karuneṟik kūntalum perukiya kaḻuttum mēluṅ kīḻuntōṉṟa - neḷiyiṉiyuṭiya kariyakūntal mēlum perutta kaḻuttuk kīḻumāy viḷaṅka, kāṇṭaku tirumukam - avaṟṟiṉiṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum aḻakiya tirumukattiṉkaṇ, vāliya naṟumuttu aṉṉa muṟuval ceṟitoṟum miṉ eṉa miḷirum - veḷḷiya nalla muttiyotta iḷanaki tōṉṟimaṟiyum pōtellām miṉṉalippōl oḷivīcum, poṉmāmēṉi makaḷiroṭu viḷaṅkum takicāl tōṟṟattu -polivukoṇṭa aḻakiya tirumēṉiyiyuṭiya vaḷḷi teyvayāṉi eṉṉum makaḷirōṭu iṇintu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻaku nirampiya kāṭciyuṭaṉ;
kuvaḷi kaṇkaḷukkum, pavaḷattāmari civanta mukattukkum, ceviram kūntalukkum, paṇilam kaḻuttukkum uvamiyākum.
`tirumuka meṉpataṟkum, `tōṟṟattu eṉpataṟkum muṟiyē ēḻaṉurupum mūṉṟaṉurupum virittukkoḷka.
aliyoḻuṅkupōl neḷineḷiyāyk kāṇap paṭutaliṉ, `neṟikkūntal eṉappaṭṭatu.
`vāl veṇmip poruṭṭātal vāl vaḷimēṉi vāli yōṉ (5: 171) eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattā luṇarappaṭum. ceṟitoṟum - tōṉṟi maṟiyuntōṟum, `tōṉṟi avāy niliyāṉ vantatu; `ceṟital ipporuṭṭātal, naḷimalarc ceṟiyavum (cilappatikāram, 2, 56) eṉpataṉuriyiṟ kāṇka. miḷirum eṉṉum peyareccam `makaḷir eṉṉum peyar koṇṭu muṭintatu.
(70-77) vaṭivēl ēntik kaṭimayil ūrntu - kūriya vēṟpaṭiyit tirukkiyiṟpaṟṟi virivumikka mayiliṉmēl ivarntu vantu, eṉ uḷḷattāmari viḷḷa vīṟṟiruntaṉi - aṭiyēṉatu uḷḷameṉṉun tāmarimoṭṭu malara āṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiṉi, pakaloḷi viḷakkum palkatir ñāyiṟu - pakalākiya oḷiyi eṅkum viḷaṅkacceyyum palavākiya katirkaḷiyuṭiya katiravaṉ, malinivantu aṉṉa nili uyar māṭattum - maliyuyarntāṟpōl eḻuniliyuyarnta periya māḷikiyilum, kuṭikaḷ taṅkāp paḻiyoṭum iṭintu veruppēy cēra erukkumuḷittup pāḻpaṭu pollā illiṉum - kuṭiyiruppup poruntāta paḻippiṉiyuṭimi yōṭuñ cuvarellām iṭintu accan taraṟkuriya pēykkūṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭamāṭa erukkañ ceṭikaḷ muḷittup pāḻpaṭṭa tīya kuṭiliṉkaṇṇum, pōḻkatir virittup polivatāl eṉa - iruṭpiḻampip piḷakkun taṉ oḷik katirkaḷi virittu viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōlaveṉka.
polivatāleṉa viḷḷavīṟṟiruntaṉi yeṉa viṉimuṭikka, aruḷceytaṟporuṭ virintuvarutaliṟ `kaṭimayil eṉṟār.
makkaḷiṉ uḷḷam tāmari mukipōl amintirut talāl `uḷḷattāmari eṉappaṭṭatu. aṉpumutaliya nalliyalpukaḷāl avvuḷḷattāmari malarumāyiṉ appōtu aḵtu āṇṭavaṉ eḻuntaruḷi viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya iṭamākum. malarmici ēkiṉāṉ (tirukkuṟaḷ 3) eṉṟu teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyaruḷiṉamiyuṅ kāṇka. mēlum innūlāciriyar iṅṅaṉamē `viriyaviḻtāmari puri uravōr uḷam puri nekiḻntu alara (19-102-3) eṉṟu avvuḷḷattiṉ malarcciyilēyē karuttuṭiyarāyp piṟāṇṭum aruḷicceytal kāṇka.
`viḷḷal malartal, ipporuṭṭātal piṅkalantiyiṟ kāṇka.
`nivattal īṇṭu uyartal, itu muṉṉum vantatu. `veru veṉpatu accapporuṭṭu,
kuṭiyillāta vīṭu iṭintu kuṭṭiccuvarāy erukku muḷittup pēykaṭkē iruppiṭamāyp pāḻāy oḻital kaṇkūṭu. eṉiṉum, eṅkum oḷiviḷaṅkuṅ katiravaṉ, eḻunili māṭattiṉkaṇ eṅṅaṉantaṉ oḷikāṭṭi viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉō, aṅṅaṉamē appāḻāṉa vīṭṭilum ōramillāmal veyil kāṭṭi viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. aṅṅaṉamē naṭunili vaḻāta āṇṭavaṉ ellā nalliyalpukaḷum oruṅku poruntiya cāṉṟōr tiruvuḷḷattiṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi yiruttalpōlavē, annalliyalpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu tāṉumillāta ēḻiyōṉ uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇum eḻuntaruḷiṉaṉ eṉṟaruḷicceytār. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyatu āṇṭavaṉuṭiya perumiyiyum naṭunilivaḻā aruṭṭaṉmiyi yum viḷakkiyapaṭiyām.
14. talimakaḷ pirivāṟṟātu pulampal
  poliyu matiyum pulipōlat teṉṟal
  naliyū namaṉāṉāṉ nalvē - ḷolitami
  ḻōvā ticikkun tiruvoṟṟi yoḷvēlāṉ
  mēvāṉ ceyalaṟiyēṉ mēl
(i-ḷ) poliyum matiyum - tiṅkaḷum nilavu virittu viḷaṅkum, pulipōlat teṉṟal naliyum - puliyippōl teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟum melleṉa vīci varuttum, namaṉ āṉāṉ nalvēḷ - iṉiya kāmaṉuṅ kūṟṟuvaṉāṉāṉ. (āṉāl) olitamiḻ ōvātu icikkum tiruvoṟṟi oḷvēlāṉ - iṉṉoli mikka tamiḻmoḻiyiṉkaṇ ākkappaṭṭa tamiḻ nāṉmaṟikaḷ iṭiyaṟāmal ōtappaṭun tiruvoṟṟi nakarkkaṇ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum oḷḷiya vēṟpaṭiyiyuṭiya murukaṉō, mēvāṉ - eṉpāl aṇukāṉ; ceyal aṟiyēṉ mēl - iṉicceyvatu aṟiyēṉ eṉpatu.
tiṅkaḷ nilavu virittalum, teṉṟal kuḷirntu vīcutalum, vēḷkāma vēṭki eḻupputalun taṉakkut tuṉpam payattal paṟṟit talivi avaṟṟi iṉṉātaṉavāka urittaṉaḷ. teṉṟal, namaṉ eṉpavaṟṟil ummi virittukkoḷka.
teṉṟal taṉṉinōkki vīcivarutal, puliyē cīṟivarutal pōl appōtu avaṭkut tōṉṟiṉamiyāṟ `pulipōlat teṉṟal naliyu meṉṟāḷ. `nalital iṅkup piṟaviṉip poruḷatu; naṭuṅkañar naliya eṉṟār. puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmaliyilum (12, 15).
kātalaṉ taṉakkarukil irukkuṅkāl iṉpam viḷittalpaṟṟi `nalvēḷ eṉavum, piṉ pirintakkāl uyiri varuttutalpaṟṟi `namaṉāṉāṉ eṉavuṅ kāmavēḷ kūṟappaṭṭāṉ.
iyaṟkiyāṉa mellōci tamiḻkku uḷatātal paṟṟi `oli tamiḻ eṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu. `tamiḻeṉṟatu icikku meṉṉuṅ kuṟippāl īṇṭu nāṉmaṟikaḷi uṇarttiṉa. nāṉmaṟikaḷāvaṉa: mūvar tēvāramum māṇikkavācakar tiruvācakamumām.
talimakaḷ taṉ kātalaṉip pirintu pirivāṟṟāmaṟ pulampum poruḷ itaṉkaṇ veḷippaṭutal kāṇka.         (14)
15. arumicey tayarppat talivi kūṟal
  mēlāṉa muttē tiruvoṟṟi mēviya vēlavaṉē
  kōlākalañ ceytu kūṭāma lēkal kuṟivukaṇṭāy
  nūlā vuṭikaṭṭi yēlāta kuṉṟiṉum nōṉmimikuṅ
  kōlā vuyarkuṇak kuṉṟiṉuñ ceṉṟa kuṟippaṟintē
(i-ḷ). mēlāṉa muttē - ciṟanta muttup pōṉṟavaṉē, tiruvoṟṟi mēviya vēlavaṉē - tiruvoṟṟi nakarip poruntiya vēṉmurukaṉē, kōlākalam ceytu kūṭāmal ēkal kuṟivu kaṇṭāy - kāmavēṭki mikutaṟku uriyaṉavellāñ ceytu piṉ avvēṭki taṇitaṟkāṉa kūṭṭatti nalkāmaṟ cellutal niṉatu aruṭṭaṉmikku ōr iḻukkākuṅkāṇ, nūlā uṭikaṭṭi - makkaḷ kiyāl nūṟkappaṭāta āṭiyiṉi yuṭuttu, ēlāta kuṉṟiṉum - taṉatu vaṉṟaṉmiyāl niṉmeṉ ṟaṉmikku iciyāta kaṉmali mēlum, nōṉmimikum - poṟukkum āṟṟal mikka, kōlā uyar kuṇakkuṉṟiṉum - amikkappaṭāta  uyarnta kuṇamākiya malimēlum, ceṉṟa kuṟippu aṟintu - pōy amarnta aruṭkuṟippi aṟintu vittum eṉka; ē aci.
icceyyuḷ, talimakaṉ taṉṉik kātalitta talivikku eḷiyaṉāy vantāṉpōṟ kalavikkumuṉ ceyvaṉa vellāñ ceytu piṉ kalattalic ceyyātu ataṟku ariyaṉākit taṉakku maṉakkavaṟciyiṉi viḷivitta vaṉkaṇmiyiṉit talimakaḷ taṉ ṟalimakaṟku eṭutturikkum niliyil vittup pāṭappaṭṭatu.
tiruvoṟṟi nakar kaṭaliyaṭuttuḷatātal uṭkoṇṭu kaṭaluḷ maṟintukiṭanta muttu makkaṭkup payaṉpaṭutaṟ poruṭṭuk kari mēl vantāleṉṉa, ulakeṅkum maṟintu niṟintu niṟkum iṟivaṉ, taṉṉi vēṇṭum aṭiyākku aruḷ puritaṟ poruṭṭu innakariṉkaṇṇē tiruvuruk koṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ eṉpatu kuṟippippār `mēlāṉamuttē eṉṟār.
kōlākalam - muṟipiṟaḻntanili; iccol `kōlālam eṉat tiruvācakattil vantatu; kōlālamākik kurikaṭalvāy aṉṟeḻunta, ālālamuṇṭāṉ (tiruccāḻal, 8). iḵtu olikkuṟippu, vaṭacol aṉṟu.
`nūlāuṭi eṉpatu oruvar kiyāṉ nūṟkappaṭāmal aṭiyār poruṭṭut tōṟṟa māttiriyāyt tōṉṟum āṭi; iṟivaṉ koṇṭa uruvum avvuruviṉmēṟ kāṇappaṭum āṭi aṇikalam mutaliyaṉavumellām avaṉaruḷiṟ ṟōṟṟa māttiriyēyāyk kāṇappaṭum iyalpu,
  uruvaruḷ kuṇaṅkaḷōṭum uṇarvaruḷ uruviṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ
  karumamum aruḷ araṉṟaṉ karacaraṇāti cāṅkan
  tarumaruḷ upāṅkamellān tāṉaruḷ taṉakkoṉṟiṉṟi
  aruḷuru vuyiruk keṉṟē ākkiṉaṉ acinta ṉaṉṟē
eṉṉuñ civañāṉa cittiyār cupakkacceyyuḷāl (1, 47) uṇarttappaṭutal kāṇka. nūlā uṭi ipporuṭṭātal patiṟṟuppattil nūlākkaliṅkam vālarikkoḷīi (12, 21) eṉpataṉuriyiluṅ kāṇappaṭum.
kōlal - vaḷittuc ceytal, iḵtipporuṭṭātal, neṭuṅkāḻk kaṇṭaṅ kōli eṉṉum mullippāṭṭiluṅ (44) kāṇka; kiyāl vaḷittu amikkappaṭuñ ceykuṉṟiṉ vēṟu pirittaṟkuk kōlā uyar kuṇakkuṉṟu eṉṟār; kuṇameṉṉuṅ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟār eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ.
māyiyil uruvukoḷḷāmal aruḷil uruvukoṇṭa iṟivaṉ meyyaṉpar poruṭṭu, māyiyil urukkoṇṭa kaṉmali mēlum eḻuntaruḷiṉāṉeṉa avaṉatu aruḷirakkaṅ kūṟiṉār. attuṇi aruḷirakkamuṭiya iṟivaṉ eḷiyēṉ mēl irakkamilaṉāy teṉṉiyeṉpatām. (15)
16. aruṇili yurittal
niliyillā iṉpaṅkaḷi niliyiṉavāka eṇṇi avaṟṟiṉkaṭ paṭintu māntar vīṇē kaḻikiṉṟārākavun, tamakkumaṭṭum niliyāṉa iṉpam īteṉatterivittut taṉatu tirukkōlak kāṭciyiṉiyum naṉku pulaṉākkiyaruḷiya ellām valla āṇṭavaṉatu pēraruṇiliyiṉi aṭikaḷ viyantu kaṉintēttum poruḷiyal itaṉkaṇ nuvalappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(1-2) aṟivum avāvum oru vaḻicceṉṟāṅku - aṟivum ataṉ vaḻi avāvum oru neṟiyiṟ ceṉṟāṟpōṟ ṟōṉṟi, orutiṟam iṉṟip palatiṟam paṭum - oru vakiyiṉṟip palavakippaṭumeṉpatu.
`tōṉṟiyeṉa orucol varuvitturikka.
(3-15) kuy kamaḻ kaṟiyum -tāḷippu maṇakkuṅ kaṟikaḷum, ney kamaḻ tuviyum - ney maṇakkun tuviyalum, poṉniṟap puḻukkalum - poṉṉiṟamāṉa paruppum, kaṉṉal pey pālum - carkkaripeyta āviṉpālum, taṉnikar kuḻampum - taṉakkut tāṉē oppāṉa kuḻampum, paṇṇiya vakiyum - vaṭi iṉṉuṇṭi iṉṉaṭi mutalāṉa tiṉpaṇṭa vakikaḷum, mikkeḻu cuviyiṉ mukkaṉivakiyum - mikkut tōṉṟukiṉṟa cuviyiṉiyuṭiya mā, vāḻi, palā eṉṉum mūṉṟu paḻavakikaḷum, kōḻ ari vāḻik kuruttu akam virittu - vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa ariyiṉiyuṭiya vāḻiyiṉ kurutti uḷvirittu, pālkeḻu taṉmiyiṉ palavēṟu amittu - parimāṟavēṇṭum muṟiyiṟ palavēṟu vakiyāka vittu, muravi pōkiya muḻu veḷarici kival maṭiyaṉ aṭutaliṉ - kīṟṟukaḷ aṟṟa muriyāta veḷḷiya ariciyik kitērnta camiyaṟkāraṉ camittalāl, ney kaṉintu viral eṉa nimirnta aviyal meṉpatam - neykacintu viralpōl neṭukiya kuṟṟalariciyiṉ melliya cōṟṟi, mulli muki eṉa mellitiṉ kuvii-mulli yarumpukaḷ eṉṉumpaṭi meṉmiyāyk kuvittu, puḻukkalam piṟavum aḷāay - muṉṉar iliyiliṭṭa paruppuṅ kaṟikaḷ kuḻampukaḷ mutalāṉavuṅ kalantu, viḻuttaka neyuṭaṉ peytu - maṇamuñ cuviyuñ ciṟakka āviṉ neyyum uṭaṉkūṭṭi, mey maṟantu uṇṭāṅku - taṉṉi maṟantu uṇṭapaṭiyē, āṉātu uṟivōr aḷavilar - piṉṉum maṉamamiyāmal vāḻvōr aḷavillātavarāvar;
`poṉṉiṟam eṉpatu īṇṭu mañcaḷ niṟam; ātalāṟ `poṉṉiṟap puḻukka leṉpatu poṉṉiṟamāṉa tuvaram paruppiṉ puḻukkal; puḻuṅka ventaparuppup puḻuṅkal eṉap paṭṭatu; avarivāṉ puḻukku eṉṟār perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭiyilum (195). pāli iliyil taṉiyē peytal ākāmiyiṟ kiṇṇattiṟ peytu vittu kāṇka.

(16-22) mukil toṭukuṭumiyum - tīṇṭukiṉṟa mukilākiya kuṭumiyum, muḻi keḻu vāyum poruntukiṉṟa kukiyākiya vāyum uṭittāy, aruvi eṉṉum aṟuviyum uṭīi - aruvikaḷ eṉṉum uṭiyumuṭuttu, antikkālattu ñāyiṟoṭu porutu - cāyṅkālattiṟ katiravaṉoṭu pōrceytu avvāṟṟāṟ kāṇappaṭṭa, cevvāṉ eṉṉum meykāl kurutiyum - cevvāṉam eṉṉum meypoḻiyuñ cennīrum, kaṇitoṭu puṇṇiṉ mukuḷittaṉṉa pavaḷamum - ampukaḷ paṭṭa puṇṇiṉiṉṟuṅ koppuḷittāṟpōṉṟa pavaḷaṅkaḷum, citarnta - cintiya, tivaḷ oḷi ceḻūum arakkaṉ aṉṉa poruppiṉ kāṭciyum - viḷaṅkiya oḷikaḷ poruntiya arakkaṉi okkum maliyiṉ tōṟṟamum;
itu kuṟiñci nilattiṉ kāṭciyi eṭuttu virittōtu kiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṭ kuṟiñcinilattu mali ōr arakkaṉukku oppāka vittu aḻakākap puṉinturikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. maliyucciyil vantu tavaḻuṅ karumukil ammaliyeṉṉum arakkaṉukkuk kuñciyākavum, kukikaḷiṉ cantu vāyākavum, aruvi uṭiyākavum, cevvāṉ cennīrākavum, cennīrk kumiḻi pavaḷamākavum eṭuttukkāṭṭiya aḻaku peritum uṇarntu makiḻaṟpālatākum. periya tōṟṟamuṅ kariya niṟamuṭiyatāy muṭceṭikaḷuṅ kaṟpāṟikaḷum malintu tōṉṟutaliṉ, mali arakkaṉukku nikarāyiṟṟu. mali arakkaṉōṭu oppavit turikkap paṭṭamiyiṉ ataṟkēṟpap pōrnikaḻcciyum poruttamāy eṭuttukkāṭṭiya nuṭpaṅ kuṟittuṇarka.
iṭiyē   cilacoṟkaḷ varuvittukkoḷka. cevvāṉ eṉṉuṅ kuruti yeṉpatu. kurutiyum pavaḷamuñ `citarnta veṉpataṉ viṉimutalvaṉ. oḷikeḻūum poruppiṉ kāṭciyeṉak kūṭṭuka. `aṟuvi, uṭi; kāmpu colittaṉṉa aṟuvi uṭīi eṉpatu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi (236) kālal, poḻital.
(23-31) maṭamāṉ piṇikaḷ kaṉṟoṭukuḻīik kāṉyāṟṟu oṇpuṉal niriniri arunta - meṉṟaṉmi vāynta peṇ māṉkaḷ tattaṅ kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭikkoṇṭu kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa viḷakkam mikka taṇṇīri varici variciyāy niṉṟu parukavum, kāṉamkōḻituṇi kūppeyarntu ñāyiṟu tōṉṟum kāli aṟivippa - kāṭṭukkōḻikaḷ vikaṟiyil eḻuntu tattam peṭiyiṉaṅkaḷik kūvi aḻikkumukattāl ñāyiṟu tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟ kālattiṉi makkaṭkut teriyappaṭuttavum, niṟam keḻu tārā kuṟumkayam maruṅkil kuṟukuṟu naṭantatu nīnta - niṟam poruntiya tārā eṉṉum nīrppaṟavi ciṟiya kuṭṭiyiṉ arukiṟ kuṟukuṟuveṉa naṭantu nīriṟ pāyntu nīntavum, veṟipaṭutaḷavam arumpi eyiṟu eṉa viḷaṅka - maṇam vīcum mulli moṭṭukaḷ arumpi veṇpal eṉa viḷaṅkutalāṉum, koṉṟi poṉvīukuppa - koṉṟi maraṅkaḷ poṉṉiṟamāṉa malarkaḷi utirttalāṉum, maṉṟal kāvatam kamaḻum kāṭukeḻu kāṭciyum -kāvatat tolivu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻuṅ kāṭṭiṉkaṭporuntiya tōṟṟamum;
ip pakutiyiṉkaṇ mulli nilattiṉ kāṭci viritturikkappaṭutaliṉ an nilattiṟku uriya māṉpēṭuṅ kāṉyāṟuṅ kāṉaṅ kōḻiyun tārāvun taḷavamuṅ koṉṟiyu mutalāṉa karupporuḷkaḷ iṉiteṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.

(32-38) koḻuvaḷi īṉṟa ceḻunīr muttam cen tāmariyiṉ miḷirvaṉa - koḻuviya valampuric caṅkukaḷ īṉṟa ceḻunīrmi vāynta muttukaḷ centāmari malarkaḷiṉmēl viḷaṅkuvaṉavāṉa tōṟṟam, maṅkiyar tirumukam kuṟuviyar poṭittal - māṉa -maṅkip paruvattup peṇkaḷiṉ aḻakiya mukaṅkaḷ ciṟuciṟu viyarvit tuḷikaḷ arumputali oppavum, kuṭampuri ceruttal taṭaṅkaṇ mēti - nīriṉkaṭ paṭintuḷḷa kuṭam okkum maṭiyiyum akaṉṟa kaṇkaḷiyum uṭiya erumikaḷ, iluṟi kuḻakkaṉṟu uḷḷu toṟum oḻukum tīm pāl - vīṭṭil uṟiyun tam iḷaṅkaṉṟukaḷi niṉikkuntōṟum avaṟṟiṉ maṭikaḷiliruntu curantoḻukun tittippāṉa pāli, mānti  - paruki, paruvarāl ukaḷa - pariya varālmīṉkaḷ puraḷavum, malipūm poyki marutak kāṭciyum - ivvakiyāṉa aḻakukaḷ malinta malarp poykikaḷ āṅkāṅku viḷaṅkum marutanilat tōṟṟamum;

(39-52) tēṟṟāp pulavarnūl tiṟamkaṭuppa - teḷivaṭiyāta pulavaratu nūli ṉiyalpi oppa, eḻuvāy ōṅki muṟimuṟi tēyntu karicārntu aviyum tiriyē - eḻumiṭattiṟ peritāyp piṉ varavarak kuṟintu ciṟitākik karicērntu aḻiyum aliyum, kari toṭuttu maṟukari kāṇātu akaṉṟu naṉi āḻntu - orukari toṭaṅkimaṟukari kāṇavoṭṭāmal attuṇip parantu mikavum āḻamuṭit tāy, nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷ        pōla-  maṟuvaṟṟa nalla pukaḻiyuṭiya pulavaratu ceyyuḷip pōl, palporuḷ ceṟitta kaṭalē - muttu caṅku mutalāṉa pala ariya paṇṭaṅkaḷ nirampiya kaṭalum, pollā oḻukkam talivanta paḻippuṭi yāḷarum - tīyavoḻukkattil muṉvanta paḻippiṉiyuṭiya tīyōrum, viḻukkuṇam mēvuvareṉpatu nalattaka - ciṟanta nalliyalpiṉi uṭiyarāvar eṉṉum uṇmi naṉku poruntumpaṭi, muḷ uṭik kuḻiyiṭik koḷḷimaṇam aviḻttu vālitiṉ virinta kiti amkaḻiyē - nuṉiyilum ōraṅkaḷilum muṭkaḷ uṭiya paciya maṭalkaḷiṉiṭiyil mikka maṇam parappi veḷḷitāy alarnta tāḻampūviṉiyuṭiya kaḻiyum, miṉ citarntu aṉṉa poṉ citar parappi - miṉṉoḷi citaṟiṉāṟpōlap poṉṉiṟa māṉa pūntātukaḷi eṅkuñ cinti, eri kiḷarntaṉṉa viripūñāḻal poḻilē - tīp piḻampu oḷirntāṟpōla itaḻvirinta pūkkaḷiyuṭiya ñāḻaṉmaraṅkaḷ nirampiya cōlikaḷum, eṉum ivi eḻilpeṟat tōṉṟum neytal kāṭciyum;
neytal nilameṉpatu kaṭaluṅ kaṭalcārnta iṭamumākum; annilattuk kāṭci ip pakutiyiṉkaṇ iṉiteṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu.
arikuṟiyākak kaṟṟut teḷiveytāta pulavar īṇṭut `tēṟṟāp pulavar eṉappaṭṭār. avariyaṟṟum nūlkaḷellān toṭakkattil maṭṭum āṅku vēṇṭum poruṭ parappellān tēṭikkoṇarntu uriyacoṟkaḷuṭaṉ iṉitaṭakki amittu eḻucciyāy iyaṟṟappaṭṭup, piṉ varavara avveḻucciyum avveḻucciyiṉ vaḻik kiḷarntuvaruñ coṟporuṭ parappumellām muṟimuṟiyē curuṅki avi muṭiya vēṇṭum varampukku varu muṉṉarē iṟṟuppōm. pulavaṉ nūliṉkaṭ ceyyuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pattaṉuḷ ituvum oṉṟu; itu `ceṉṟutēyn tiṟutal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām; iṅṅaṉamē kaṭaliṉkaṇ uṇṭākum alikaḷ mutalil mika uyaramāyk kiḷampi varavarat tēyntu avintupōtal kāṇka.

māḻāttal, mayaṅkutal;
