muḷi, viṉiccol; appōḻtu eṉa orucol varuvikka āṭuṟum, - āṭum;
īṟṟiṟ kaṇṭili eṉavē kūṟal takum eṉac cilacoṟkaḷ eccamāka varuvittukkoḷka. mallal - ceḻumi.
(125-147) aṉṟi āṅku - allatu appotumpariṉkaṇ, aṉiccam mēl paṭiṉum taṉittuyar uḻakkum taḻimalarc cīṟaṭi kuḻipaṭa naṭantu - aṉiccamalariṉmēṟ paṭṭālum mikka tuṉpa maṭiyun taḻinta centāmari malaraṉiya ciṟiya tiruvaṭikaḷ tacimella  naṭantu, poḻiliṭam pukuntu - cōliyuḷ nuḻintu, pūm taṭamaruṅkil - pūkkaḷ niṟinta akkuḷattarukil, viḻitaka niṉṟa paḻutil pāviyi - viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi niṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta pāvi pōlvāḷi, eṉuḷam eṉṉum taṉ amar kiḻiyil - eṉatu uḷḷameṉṉun taṉakkupporuntiya iraṭṭut tuṇiyil, vaḻuviṉṟu eḻutiya eḻil ōviyatti - piḻiyillāmal eḻutiya aḻaku cittiram pōlvāḷi, amiḻtu poti tuvarvāyk kiḷavi cila miḻaṟṟum - amiḻtam niṟinta taṉatu civanta vāyiliruntu coṟkaḷ cila pēcum, māniṟam koṇṭa tū niṟak kiḷiyi - pacumi niṟam koṇṭa tūya uruviṉiyuṭiya kiḷipōlvāḷi, kāṭu uṟi vāḻkki taṉ iṉam pirintu - kāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟa vāḻkkiyi yuṭiya taṉ kūṭṭatti viṭṭu nīṅki, nāṭu uṟi vāḻkki naṉkaṉam maruviya - nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkiyi nallapaṭiyākap poruntiya, kuṭam māṇ koṅki maṭamāṉ piṇiyi - kuṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa periya koṅkikaḷi yuṭiya iḷamāṉpēṭu pōlvāḷi, kiḷicciṟi eṉṉum paḻippuaṟu pacumpoṉ karittu - kiḷicciṟi eṉṉum mācaṟṟa oruvakip pacumpoṉṉi nīrāy urukki, īṇṭu eḻutiya tarik kiḷar valliyi - ataṉāl innilavulakil eḻuti yamitta nilattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta poṉṉiṟamāṉa vallik koṭi pōlvāḷi, kāmak kaṭum paci nām uṟak kaḷiyum - kāmam eṉṉum koṭiya paciyāṉatu accam aṭiyumpaṭi ataṉi nīkkum, peṟutaṟku ariya uṟutuṇi marunti - aṭivataṟku arumiyāṉa ciṟanta tuṇiyāyuḷḷa maruntupōlvāḷi, kāṇṭal cellā aruvapporuḷeṉa - kāṇṭal iyalāta uruvamil poruḷeṉṟu kūṟum, pūṇṭōr pukaḻuri puripaṭṭu oḻiya - pēr pūṇṭa periyōrkaḷatu pukaḻ takka uriyāṉatu paḻutupaṭṭoḻiyu māṟu, miṉ eṉa miḷirntu eṉ kaṇ etir polinta - miṉṉalpōl piṟaḻntu oḷi vīci eṉ kaṇkaḷmuṉ viḷaṅkiya, karumpiṉum iṉikkum eṉ arumpeṟal uyiri - karumpip pārkkiṉum iṉikkiṉṟa eṉ peṟaṟkariya uyir pōlvāḷi, eṉ uḷam maṉṉiya tuṉ iruḷ nīṅka - eṉatu uḷḷattil nilipeṟṟa ceṟinta malaviruḷāṉatu oḻiyumāṟu, muṟpaṭak koḷuviya poṉcuṭark koḻunti - eṉṉetiriṟ poruttiya poṉṉiṟamāṉa oḷikkoḻuntupōl vāḷi, pulam koḷak kāṇum poṟiyum iliyāl - kāṭciyāka kāṇum nalviṉi yum niṉakkilli yeṉpatu.
taḻimalar, viṉittoki; ciṟumi aṭi, cīṟaṭi.
kiḻi, eḻutupaṭam; īcaṉa cāntu merukku maṇintōr kiḻipiṭittu ( tirukkōviyār, 74) eṉpar maṇivācakaṉār; aḵtiṅku appaṭameḻutun tuṇiyi uṇarttāniṉṟatu. amartal - poruntutal, (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli, 4, 5).
uṇmiyuṭiyaḷākaliṉ, cilacoṟkaḷē avaḷ vāyi ṉiṉṟum eḻuvavāyiṉa. aṉṟi muḷḷeyiṟupōḻtal añcic cilacolvē pēcuvaḷeṉa urittalumām. miḻaṟṟal, nirampā meṉcoṟ kūṟal; talivi puttiḷamiyuṭiya maṭavaralākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam `miḻaṟṟu meṉappaṭṭatu. uruvakamāy vanta kiḷiyiṉiyaṟkikkum iccoṟporuḷ okkum. `amiḻtu poti vāy eṉṟamiyiṉ, avvāyiṉiṉṟeḻuṅ kiḷavikaḷellām amiḻtamākumeṉpatu kuṟippāṟ peṟappaṭum. niṟam iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu vaṇṇamum piṉṉatu uruvamum uṇarttum.
`naṉkaṉam eṉpataṟku `naṉṟāka eṉṟu poruḷurippar paripāṭaluriyil (15:25).
talivi poṟkoṭipōl uḷḷāḷ eṉpatu karuttu. kiḷartal - eḻutal (tirumuruku. 82).
nām - accam (tolkāppiyam, uri, 67); paciyē accamuṟumpaṭi ataṉik kaḷiyum marunteṉka. kaṭumi, iṅkuk koṭumi mēṟṟu; kaṇṇīr naṉikkuṅ kaṭumiya kāṭeṉṟāl (6) eṉṉuṅ kalittokiyiṟpōla. kāmam viḷippavaḷē kāmatti nīkkum maruntātalum vēṇṭumākaliṉ, `uṟutuṇi maruntu eṉappaṭṭāḷ. ikkaruttuṭ koṇṭē.
  irunōk kivaḷuṇka ṇuḷḷa torunōkku
  nōynōkkoṉ ṟannōy maruntu
eṉṟu teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉārum (tirukkuṟaḷ, kuṟippaṟital, 1) kūṟutal iṅku niṉivu kūraṟpālatu.
uyireṉa uruvakap paṭuttiṉamiyiṉ, `kāṇṭal cellā aruvapporuḷeṉa ataṉ iyalpurittup piṉ viyantu moḻiyappaṭṭa teṉka.
uṟṟunōkkuṅkāṟ kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum oruvaruk koruvar avaravaruyiric ceṟinta pollāp paḻamalaviruḷi nīkkikkoḷḷum iyaṟkiyuṟaviṉarāvar; itaṉāṉaṉṟē tirukkuṟaḷ vētattiṉum maṉiyāḷ vāḻkkittuṇi (ati. 6) eṉappaṭṭatūu meṉpatu. itaṉi viḷakkutaṟ poruṭṭē ilakkiyaṅkaḷilellām `maṉikku viḷakku maṭavār eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkatturikaḷāl vāḻkkit tuṇiyāvār malaviruḷ nīkkum oḷiviḷakkāka vittup pārāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. itu muṉṉuṅ kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu (innūl, 4: 18 uri).
pāvi, ōviyam mutaliyaṉa uruvakaṅkaḷ.
(148-162) āvaṇa oliyum arum kaṭal oliyum oṉṟu talimayaṅki eṉṟum ōvā oṟṟimānakaril - kaṭitteruviṉ viṟpaṉi oliyum aḷattaṟkariya kaṭaloliyum oṉṟu kalantu eṉṟum nīṅkāmalirā niṉṟa tiruvoṟṟimā nakariṉkaṇ, taṉ takappolinta paciyatōr maññimici amarcevvēḷ - taṉatu aḻakiya iḷamic cevvikkut takumpaṭi viḷaṅkiya pacuminiṟa muḷḷatoru mayiliṉ mēl eḻuntaruḷāniṉṟa cemmi niṟam poruntiya murukaṉ, tēvarum mayaṅkum mūvā māyam palamuṟi iyaṟṟiya talimi tīrcūriṉ pēr uram vacinta kūrvaṭivēlaṉ - tēvarkaḷum mayaṅkumpaṭi yāṉa aḻiyāta māyac ceyalkaḷip palataṭavi ceyta talimi nīṅkun takiyaṉāṉa cūraṉatu akaṉṟa mārpip piḷanta kūrmiyuḷḷa cevviya vēṟpaṭiyi yuṭiyavaṉ, nōṉā uḷḷattu nāṉmukaṉ teḷiyat taliyil kuṭṭiya nalaṉuṟu kumaraṉ - `ōm eṉṉum orumoḻiyiṉ uṇmipporuḷ nāṇun tavam vāyāta uḷḷattiyuṭiya nāṉmukaṉ ataṉ poruḷ teḷiyumpaṭi avaṉ taliyiṟ kuṭṭiya aḻaku  poruntiya iḷamparuvamuḷḷa murukapperumāṉ, viṇṇavarkku ariyaṉ - tēvarkaṭkuṅ kiṭittaṟ karumi yāṉavaṉ, emmaṉōrkku eḷiyaṉ - āṉāl aṭiyēṉpōṉṟa eḷiyōrkkuk kiṭittaṟ keḷimiyāṉavaṉ, piṟimuṭi putalvaṉ - piṟi taṅkiya caṭimuṭiyi yuṭiya civaperumāṉukkup putalvaṉ, maṟi muṭi mutalvaṉ - nāṉ maṟikaḷiṉ muṭiviṭattukkut talivaṉ, umitaru ciṟuvaṉ - umip pirāṭṭiyār īṉṟeṭutta putalvaṉ, emi aḷi uṟuvaṉ - eḷiyēmi aruḷkūrntu kākkum mikkōṉ, paṉṉiru kiyaṉ - paṉṉiraṇṭu tirukkikaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ, eṉ uyirkku aiyaṉ - eṉatu āruyirkku uyirāyuḷḷa talaviṉ eṉṉum murukap perumāṉuṭiya, tiruvaṭi niṉiyā aṟivilarpōla - tiruvaṭi kaḷi eṇṇum pēṟupeṟāta aṟivillā makkaḷippōl, eṉ etirtōṉṟip paṉmuṟi kaḻaṟiṉi - eṉmuṉ vantu palataram iṭitturittaṉi;
talimitīr, talimitīrnta; murukaṉip pakiyāmuṉ ṉirunta talimi avaṉippakittapiṉ tīrntamiyiṉ talimitīr cūr eṉṟār. vacinta, piḷanta; vacittatik kaṇṭamāka (5: 38) eṉpatu paripāṭal.
`nōṉā uḷḷa meṉappaṭṭatu, ōṅkārattiṉ meyp poruḷi uṇarun tavamiyaṟṟap peṟāta uḷḷatti yuṭiya nāṉmukaṉeṉka; nōṉāmi - tavampuriyāmi; nōṉṟal eṉṉuñ colliṉ etirmaṟi. nāṉmukaṉ ōṅkārattiṉ karuttuṇarāmikkāka murukapperumāṉ avaṉ taliyiṟ kuṭṭiya varalāṟu,
eṭṭoṇāta akkuṭiliyiṟ payaṉilit teṉṟē
  kaṭṭurittilaṉ mayaṅkalum itaṉporuḷ karutāy
  ciṭṭi ceyvatu ittaṉmiyatō eṉāc cevvēḷ
  kuṭṭiṉāṉ ayaṉnāṉkumā muṭikaḷuṅkuluṅka
eṉṉuṅ kantapurāṇac ceyyuḷāl (ayaṉic ciṟiparipaṭalam, 11) aṟiyappaṭum.
piṟi muṭi, ākupeyar; maṟimuṭi, maṟikaḷiṉ muṭinta karuttu.
uṟuvaṉ - mikkōṉ (cilappatikāram, 9, 53).
iṟivaṉ ṟiruvaṭi niṉiyā aṟivilār uṇmikāṇum nīrarallarākaliṉ, avarpōl nīyum eṅkaḷ iyaṟkik kāta luṇmiyiyum ak kātaṟpēṟṟi nalkiya eṉ kātaṟ celviyiṉ arumi perumiyiyum uṇarātu kaḻaṟukiṉṟaṉi yeṉat talivaṉ taṉpāṅkaṉi nōkkik kūṟuṅkaruttu viḷaṅka iṅkut `tiruvaṭi niṉiyā vaṟivilarpōla veṉṟaruḷic ceytār.
kaḻaṟutal, iṭittu uṟutiyurittal; aṉpila kaṭiya kaḻaṟi (14: 8) eṉpatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu.
(163 - 167) īṅku itu viṭuttu - iṅku nī iṅṅaṉaṅ kaḻaṟali oḻittu, āṅkucceṉṟu - ammarap potumpariṉ kuḷattarukiṟ ceṉṟu, tuṭiiṭi oruki ūṉṟi vaṭiviḻi ticimukam parappi nacipaṭap pārkkum irumkaṇ māṉpiṇi kāṇil - uṭukki pōṉṟa taṉ iṭuppiṉkaṇ taṉatu orukiyi vittuk koṇṭu vaṭuvakirpōlun taṉ kaṇkaḷi nāṟpuṟaṅkaḷilum pōkkik kaṇṭārkku viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi pārkkum periya pārviyi yuṭiya māṉpēṭupōlum eṉ taliviyip pārttāl, neruṅki niṉṟu iṅku uriyali iṉi - ittuṇi aṇukiniṉṟu iṅṅaṉam iṉimēl urip pāyalliyeṉpatu.
vaṭi māvaṭuviṉpiḷavu, vaṭikkivi eṉṉun tirukkōviyāruriyi nōkkuka.
naci, viruppam, nacitara vantōr nacipiṟakkoḻiya (puṟanāṉūṟu, 15) eṉpuḻippōla.
`iruṅkaṇ eṉpuḻi `irumi perumiyeṉavum, `kaṇ pārviyeṉavum poruḷpaṭum. nerukkam, maṉanerukkam.
naṇpā (15) paṉmuṟi kaḻaṟiṉi (162) māṉpiṇikāṇil (166) neruṅki niṉṟiṅku uriyali iṉi (167) eṉa muṭikka, (25)
26. pirivāṟṟāta talivi tōḻiyoṭu kūṟal
 iṉiyā reṉakkiṅ kiṉiyā riyintār
  muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - paṉimoḻiyāy
  puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu pukaḻoṟṟit tannakarkku
  naḷḷiruṭkaṭ ceṉṟār namar.
(i-ḷ) iṉi yār eṉakku iṅku iṉiyār iyintār - iṉi mēl yār eṉakku iṅku iṉiyarāy iṇaṅkiyuḷḷavar, muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - nammavar ciṉavār, ciṉan toṉṟu pēcār; paṉimoḻiyāy - kuḷirnta iṉcol uṭiya uyirttōḻī, puḷ oṉṟu ivarntu pukaḻ oṟṟit tam nakarkku - mayileṉṉun paṟavi oṉṟaṉmītu mēṟkoṇṭu pukaḻmikka tiruvoṟṟiyūreṉum tam tirunakarattukku, naḷ iruḷkaṇ ceṉṟār namar - ceṟinta iruḷil naṅ kātalar ceṉṟār eṉpatu.
orukālattilum muṉitalum muṉintu pēcutalu millā attuṇi aṉpiṉarāṉa namar, puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu tam nakarkkuc ceṉṟār; iṉi yāreṉakku iṅku iṉiyarāy iyintār irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu karuttu.
iyintār viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar; kātalarāy iyintavar eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ivartal - mēṟkoḷḷal, ivartan teṉmēṉimē lūrumpacappu eṉpataṟkup parimēlaḻakiyā ruritta vurikāṇka (tirukkuṟaḷ, 1182). naḷ iruḷ, naṭu irā. (26)
27. neñcaṟi vuṟuttal
  namarā yiruntupiṉ ētila rāvar nayamilarun
  tamarāva reṉṉiṟ ṟamarpiṟa reṉṟu takavucolli
  amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitukaṇ ṭāyali yāḻiyoṟṟik
  kumarāveṉ ṟōtik kuṭantaṅkoṇ ṭēttak kuṟikkoṇeñcē
(i-ḷ) namarāy iruntu piṉ ētilarāvar makkaḷiṟ cilar cila kālam namakkuc cuṟṟattārāyiruntu piṉ ayalavarāy viṭuvar, nayamilarum tamarāvar - aṉpillāta ayalavarum orukāl namakku vēṇṭiyōrāvar, eṉṉil - eṉṟāl, tamar piṟar eṉṟu takavu colli amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitu kaṇṭāy - ulakattil nammavareṉṟum ayalavareṉṟum perumi pēcip pōrāṭi niṟṟal vīṇeṉṟaṟivāy, ali āḻi oṟṟik kumarā eṉṟu ōti kuṭantam koṇṭu ēttak kuṟikkoḷ neñcē - ātalāl alimikka kaṭali aṇittākavuṭiya tiruvoṟṟimurukā eṉṟu collik kikūppi meyvaḷittu vaḻuttat tuṇivukoḷvāy neñcamē eṉpatu.
`nayam, īram, naṇpāṟṟārāki nayamilaceyvārkkum (tirukkuṟaḷ, 998). takavu - perumi, takavēyuṭiyāṉ ṟaṉic cāra eṉṉun tiruvācakamāmaṟiyil (yāttirippattu, 2) ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
`kuṭantameṉpatu oruvaki añcali; atāvatu `kikūppi meyvaḷittu vaṇaṅkal; kuṭantampaṭṭuk koḻumalar citaṟi eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyilum (229) ituvarum; kuṭantaṅ kikūppi meykōṭṭiniṟṟal eṉṟār tivākarattilum.
kuṟikkoḷal, maṉattuḷ vittuc ceyal muṭikkun tuṇivu koḷḷutal; kuṟikkōṭṭakiyatu koḷkeṉat tantēṉ eṉpatu cilappatikāram, 30, 63. (27)
28. pulavarāṟṟup paṭi
aḵtāvatu, paricupeṟṟut tirumpum pulavaṉ oruvaṉ atu peṟuṅ karuttuṭaṉ etirvarum pulavaṉoruvaṉi vaḻiyiṟ kaṇṭu taṉakkup paricaḷitta aracaṉatu perumiyum avaṉpāl atu peṟavēṇṭum vakimiyum piṟavun teḷiyuṇartti, uṇarttiya anneṟiyē avaṉi uyppatākum. eṉavē `āṟṟuppaṭi yeṉpatu etirppaṭṭārit tām paricupeṟṟa vāṟṟiṟ paṭuppippatu eṉap poruḷtarumeṉka. ivviyalpu.
 āṟṟiṭik kāṭci yuṟaḻat tōṉṟip
  peṟṟa peruvaḷam peṟāark kaṟivuṟīic
  ceṉṟu payaṉetirac coṉṉa pakkamum
eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāṟ (puṟat. 36) peṟappaṭum.
(1-11) neñcu nekku uṭintu - neñcam nekiḻntutaḷarntu, pañcu kaṇ aṭiyā -kaṇ pañcaṭintu, uṭumpu urittaṉṉa kaṭumpaci maruṅkul paḻuppuṭitōṉṟic ceḻippiṉṟi vika - uṭumpit tōlurittāl oppak koṭiya paciyiṉiyuṭiya vayiṟṟiṉ pakkattuḷḷa vilā elumpukaḷ taciyillāmiyāṟ pakkaṅkaḷiṟṟōṉṟic ceḻumiyillāmalirukka, vaṟumiuḻanta uṟumaṉik kiḻattiyoṭu - avvāṟellām vaṟumiyil varuntikkiṭakkum aṉpiṉ mikka maṉikkiḻattiyuṭaṉ, nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu - nāṭōṟuṅ kāraṇamiṉṟiyē caṇṭiyiṭṭu, nōṉāp paciyiṉ āṉātu aḻūum puṉ talik kuḻavitaṉ tiṟam nōkki - poṟuttaṟkariya paciyiṉāl iṭi viṭātu aḻukiṉṟa aḻukkuṟṟa taliyiṉiyuṭiya tam kuḻanti kaḷiṉ niliyikkaṇṭu, kaṉavu kāṇpoḻutum naṉavu eṉat tōṉṟum aruntuyar eṉṟum perum tuyar uṟuppa - avaṟṟi yellām iṭiyaṟātu eṇṇutalāṟ kaṉavukāṇuṅ kālaṅkaḷilum naṉavupōlavē tōṉṟum uṉṉutaṟkum ariya tuyaraṅkaḷ ennāḷum mikka varuttatti mikuttiṭāniṟpa, miṭikeḻuvāḻkkiyil kuṭiyāyiruntu - aṅṅaṉam vaṟumi poruntiya ēḻimi vāḻkkiyilēyē kuṭiyiruppāyiruntu, maḻuṅkiya uḷḷattu oṭuṅkiya pulavōy - kiḷarcciyavinta uḷḷattiṉiyuṭimiyāṟ ceyaloṭuṅkiya pulavōy! eṉpatu.
`aṭiyā ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam; nilaṅkiḷiyā nāṇiniṉṟōḷ (akanāṉūṟu, 16) eṉpuḻip pōla.
`uṭumpurit taṉṉa maruṅkul eṉappaṭṭatu, paciyāl vayiṟu mutukoṭṭi vilā elumpeḻuntu niṟṟaliṉ. paḻu - vilā elumpu, poruvilyāṉiyiṉ paḻuppōṟ poṅkukāyk kuliyavari eṉṟār cintāmaṇiyilum (1561). uṭumpurittaṉṉa eṉpeḻumaruṅkiṟ, kaṭumpiṉ kaṭumpaci eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉum (68) pōntami nōkkuka.
uṟumaṉikkiḻatti, aṉpiṉmikka naṉmaṉikkiḻatti; vaṟumiyiṉ koṭumiyāl illattil aṭikkaṭi kāraṇa miṉṟiyē caṇṭikaḷ uṇṭātal iyalpātalāl, `nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu eṉappaṭṭatu; kalāyttal - caṇṭiyiṭutal, cintāmaṇi 1950.
vaṟumiyil uḻaluñ ceḻumiyillāk kuḻantikaṭkut talicaṭittu aḻukkēṟik, kāṇa aruvaruppāyirukku mātaliṉ avi `puṉṟalik kuḻavikaḷ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
tiṟam - nilimi; miṭimi - tīrāvaṟumi.
(12-13) iyaṟṟamiḻ varampu tiṟappaṭa āyntu - iyaṟṟamiḻ nūṉ muṭivit tiṟampaṭa ārāyntu, pulamkoḷa nirampiya nalam kiḷar aṟivōy - uḷḷattiṉ iṭam muḻutuṅ koḷḷumāṟu niṟinta naṉmimikka aṟivuṭiyōy;
`iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉpatu, muttamiḻkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; ēṉiya icittamiḻ `nāṭakattamiḻ eṉka. iyaṟṟamiḻāvatu tiruntiya vaḻakkāy nām pēcuvatum eḻutuvatumāṉa centamiḻākum. varam peṉṟatu, iṅku nūl varampu.
(14-15) vaḻuoṉṟu illā viḻumiya oḻukkam - piḻipāṭu ciṟitu millāta ciṟanta oḻukkatti, uyiriṉum ōmpum ceyir aṟuperiyōy - taṉ āruyiriṉum peritāka matittuk kākkuṅ kuṟṟamaṟṟa perumi yuṭiyōy;
oḻukkam uyiriṉum ōmpappaṭum eṉṟu poyyā moḻiyuṅ kaṭṭuriyāniṟkum. `oṉṟu iṅkuc ciṟiteṉṉum poruṭṭu; māyikku uṇarvoṉṟu milliyeṉṟē vittiṭum (civañāṉa cittiyār, 1: 17) eṉpuḻippōla. ceyir - kuṟṟam.
(16) koḻuntamiḻ pōlak kuḷirmāṇ kuṇattōy - ceḻuntamiḻ moḻiyippōṟ kuḷircciyum māṭciyum mikka iyalpiṉōy;
cuvinirampiya meṉcoṟkaḷālum āḻntakaṉṟa ciṟanta karuttukkaḷālum namatariya centamiḻmoḻi taṉṉi mēvuvārkkuk kuḷircciyum māṭciyumā yiruttalpōṟ, pulavarun tam iṉcoṟkaḷālum nalloḻukkaṅkaḷālun tammi naccuvārkkut taṇṇeṉṉu māṇpuṭiyarāyk kāṇappaṭuvareṉka. tamiḻukku ittaṇmiyum māṭciyu miruttal, uyarmatiṟkūṭaliṉ āynta oṇtīntamiḻiṉ tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō eṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyta tirukkōviyār tirumoḻi yāṉun teḷiyappaṭum.
(17-18) uḻunturuḷ aḷaviyiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pākkaḷ nūṟunūṟu iyaṟṟuṅ kūṟupaṭu matiyōy - uḻuntu uruḷukiṉṟa avvaḷavu virinta nērattiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ nūṟunūṟākac ceyyum pakuttaṟiyum matinuṭpa muṭiyōy;
aḷavi, iṅkuk kālavelli. kūṟupaṭumatiyāvatu nallataṉ nalaṉun tīyataṉ tītum pakuttuṇarum aṟivu.
(19-20) oḷiccelavu ataṉiṉum vaḷiccelavu ataṉiṉum - oḷiyōṭṭattiṉ virivip pārkkiṉum kāṟṟōṭṭattiṉ virivip pārkkiṉum mikuvirivāka, neṟippaṭak kiḷakkum miṟikkavi vallōy - oḻuṅkupaṭa icikkuñ cittirakkavi vallapulavōy;
oḷiccelaveṉpatu ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṉiṉṟu puṟampōtarum oḷiyōṭṭaṅkaḷ. `celavu, celki; iṅku virivunōkki ōṭṭameṉakkoḷka. veruvaruñ celaviṉ vekuḷivēḻam (porunarāṟṟuppaṭi, 172).
(21-22) aḷavinūl marapil piṟaḻātu - tarukkanūl muṟiyil tavaṟātu, yāṇṭum koḷa uri nikaḻttum vaḷam uṟum uṇarvōy - eṅkuṅ kēṭpōr uḷaṅkoḷa uri virikkum vaḷamuṟṟa uṇarvuṭiyōy;
aḷavinūl, tarukkanūl; vaḷam, ārāyntukaṇṭa nūṟporuḷvaḷam.
(23 - 24) poruḷiyal aṟintu - eṭuttukkoṇṭa virivuripporuḷiṉ vakimi terintu, maruḷ aṟap puṉintu - mayakkamaṟa aḻakuppaṭuttuk kūṟi, kēṭpōr piṇikkum kāṭcicāl uravōy - kēṭpavarkaḷ uḷḷattit taṉ vayappaṭuttum meyyuṇarvu nirampiya aṟivuvaliyuṭiyōy;
`puṉital coṟkaḷiyuṅ karuttukkaḷiyum iṭam nōkkic curukkiyum perukkiyum uvamittum uruvakittu mellām aḻakupaṭuttik kēṭpārkku iṉpam viḻivu vēṭki mutalāṉa meyppāṭu uṇṭākac collutal; iṅṅaṉam aḻakupaṭuttuc collappaṭṭaṉavē paṇṭik kālattil `ilakkiyam eṉavum, `nūl nayam eṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭṭu vantaṉa. `naviṟoṟum nūlnayam pōlum eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvarum itaṉic ciṟanteṭuttup pārāṭṭuvar. āṉāl, iṭik kālattō avvaḻakupaṭuttum uri `kaṟpaṉi yeṉak kūṟappaṭṭup, piṟkālattē atuvum nilimāṟi `iyaṟkikku māṟākap poyyuri puṉital eṉṉum iḻivuporuḷil vantu vaḻaṅkip perumi kuṉṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
(25-28) paṇṭip piṟaviyil taṇṭātu āṟṟum perum tavam uṭiyi maṉṉē - paḻam piṟavikaḷil tavaṟāma liyaṟṟum perumiyāṉa tavattiṉi nirampavum uṭiyi; porunti iṉṟu īṅku eṉit talippaṭṭaṉiyē - poruntutalvara iṉṟu iṅku vantu eṉṉi etirppaṭṭaṉiyē, ōṅki ūḻicelliṉum nīṭu vāḻmati - mēmpaṭac ciṟantu ūḻikaḻiyiṉu neṭuṅkālam vāḻvāyāka;
taṇṭal - tavaṟutal (piṅkalanti); ataṉ etirmaṟi `taṇṭāmi; ivi `taḷ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṟ piṟantaṉa; kaḷvārkkut taḷḷum uyirnili eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
maṉ, mikutikkaṇ vantatu. talippaṭal - etirppaṭal. poruntutal varat talippaṭṭamiyāvatu vaṟumiyuḻantu atu tīrnta oru pulavaṉi, vaṟumiyuḻakkum maṟṟoru pulavaṉ atutīrtaṟ pāṉmiyil vantu etirppaṭṭami eṉka.
(29-34) kīḻttici eḻīi mēṟṟicip paṭarum tītteṟu ñāyiṟu vaṭaticip puṇarntu teṉtici nōkkit ticitaṭu māṟiṉum - kiḻakkup pakkattil tōṉṟi mēṟkup pakkattiṟ cellum neruppeṉac cuṭum ñāyiṟu vaṭakkup pakkañ cērntu teṟkup pakkam nōkkic ceṉṟapaṭiyāy iṅṅaṉan tici taṭumāṟiṉālum, vāṉak kaṭaliṉ mīṉeṉa vayaṅkum uṭuniri utirntu koṭumiyāyiṉum - vāṉameṉṉuṅ kaṭaliṉkaṇ mīṉkaḷeṉa viḷaṅkum uṭukkulaṅkaḷ utirnta viḻutalāl ulakattiṟ koṭiya nikaḻccika kaḷuṇṭāyiṉum, ulavā vāḻkkiyiṉ nilimati ciṟantē - keṭāta nalvāḻkkiyiṟ ciṟantu nilippāyāka;
paṭaral - cellal; uṭu - viṇmīṉ; niri, kūṭṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu; ulakiṉkaṭ kēṭukaḷ viḷiyiṉum niṉ nalvāḻvu keṭātu iṉitūḻi vāḻntu iṉpuṟukaveṉpatu ippakutiyiṉ karuttu.
(35-36) vaṟumi kaḷintu ciṟumi nīṅki - vaṟumiyinīkki eḷimi oḻintu, naṉṉili palavum iṉṉē peṟuti - nalla nilimikaḷellām ippoḻutē peṟuvāyāka;
nili, iṉpaṅkaḷ palavun tuyttaṟkēṟṟa nilimi. `iṉṉē ippoḻutē eṉap poruḷ paṭutal, uṟṟatiṉṉē iṭiyūṟu eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyuḷḷuṅ (226) kāṇka.
(37) varampaṟum iṉpam nirampak kōṭi - elliyaṟṟa iṉpam nirampak koḷvāyāka;
elliyaṟṟa iṉpam, aḷavaṟṟa iṉpam; `kōṭi eṉpataṟkuk `koḷ pakuti; atu mutal nīṇṭu takaram virittalum viyaṅkōḷ īṟum peṟṟuk `kōṭi yeṉṟāyiṟṟu. kōṭi, koḷḷuti; `vāḻmati, nilimati, peṟuti, kōṭi yeṉpaṉa vāḻttutaṟ poruṇmikkaṇvanta viyaṅkōḷ viṉi muṟṟukaḷ.
(38-39) niṉ uyirkku uṟutināṭi - niṉatu uyirkku naṉmi payattaṟkuriyavikaḷi ārāyntaṟintu, tuṉṉi niṉṟu - niṉṉi aṇuki niṉṟu, niriniri kūṟum eṉ uri piḻiyali - muṟi muṟiyākak kaṭṭurittuc collum eṉ coṟkaḷit tavaṟaviṭātiruppāyāka!
nāṭi - ārāyntu; nāṭātu naṭṭaliṟ kēṭilli tirukkuṟaḷ.
(40-42) iṉṉum kēṭṭal vēṇṭiṉ - iṉṉum nāṉ eṭutturittali nī kēṭṭal virumpiṉāyāṉāl, muṉṉum eṉ col aḷavu amintu nalam pala eytiṉar pallōr pallōr paṇṭum uṇṭu - nāṉ niṉittuk kūṟum eṉ moḻikaḷiṉ elliyil niṉṟu naṉmikaḷ palavum aṭintavar palar palar muṉṉum uṇṭu;
(43) iṉiyoṉṟu kūṟuveṉ kēṇmati - ātalāl iṉi oṉṟu colluvēṉ ataṉik kēṭpāyāka! veṉpatu
`kūṟuveṉ eṉpatil `eṉ īṟu taṉmiyorumi viṉi muṟṟu (tolkāppiyam, viṉiyiyal, 6); koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuveṉ kēṇmatiperuma eṉa muṉṉum vantami niṉivu kūrka.
(43-45) paṉikeḻu mulliam koṭikku - kuḷircci poruntiya mulliyiṉkoṭikku, mallal am ceḻumtēr - celvattiṟ ciṟanta aḻakiya tēri, aruḷvara - taṉakku irakka muṇṭāka, ītta- ataṟkuk koḻukompākumpaṭi niṟutti utaviya, periyaṉō ilaṉē - īkiyiṟ periyaṉākiya pāriyō iññāṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
vaḷḷaliṟ ciṟanta pāriyiṉ ivvariya periya īkicceyal,
		          curumpuṇ
naṟuvī yuṟikku nāka neṭuvaḻic
ciṟuvī mullikkup peruntēr nalkiya
piṟaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi vīḻuñ cāraṟ
paṟampiṟ kōmāṉ pāriyum
eṉac ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyiṉkaṭ (87 - 91) peritu pārāṭṭap paṭutalum iṅku niṉivukūraṟpālatu.
`am īriṭaṅkaḷiṉuñ cāriyi; īṟṟēkāram irakkap poruḷatu; mēlvaruvaṉavum aṉṉa.
(46 - 47) viḻiyāk kaṇṇa ceḻumayirt tōkikku - imiyāk kaṇkaḷuḷḷa niṟattāṟ ciṟanta mayirkaḷ vāynta mayilukku, naṟumpaṭām vīciya - kuḷiraṉukāmip poruṭṭut taṉatu iṉiya pōrviyi ataṉmēl eṟinta, uṟuvaṉum ilaṉē - takkōṉākiya pēkaṉum iññāṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
ivvaḷḷaliṉ aruñceyalum,
vāṉam vāytta vaḷamalik kavāaṟ
  kāṉa maññikkuk kaliṅka nalkiya
  varuntiṟa laṇaṅki ṉāviyar perumakaṉ
  peruṅka ṉāṭaṉ pēkaṉum
eṉac ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyiṉkaṇ (84-87) varum. tōki, ākupeyar.
(48-50) pavaṉamā ulakil navi aṟap peṟṟa amiḻtu poti nelli - pātāḷa ulakattiṉiṉṟu paḻutaṟappeṟṟa amiḻtam niṟinta karunellik kaṉiyi, auvikku ītta - auviyeṉṉum nam centamiḻp pirāṭṭikku avar nīṭu vāḻumāṟu utaviya, citiyāp pēṟṟiṉ atikaṉum ilaṉē - aḻiyāp pukaḻākiya pēṟṟiṉi eytiya atikamāṉum iṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
atiyar kōmāṉiṉ ivvaruñceyalum,
		         mālvarik
kamaḻpūñ cāraṟ kaviṉiya nelli
yamiḻtuviḷi tīṅkaṉi yeḷavik kītta
vuravucciṉaṅ kaṉalu moḷitikaḻ neṭuvē
laravakkaṭaṟ ṟāṉi yatikaṉum
eṉṟu (ciṟupāṇ, 99-103) kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
`pavaṉamāvulaku nākarulaku; cākkāṭu keṭuttamiyiṉ, aḵtu `amiḻtupoti nelli yeṉak kiḷantōtappaṭṭatu. ik karunellimaram oru periya malimuḻiñciṉ aṭiyilē niṉṟami, toṉṉilip perumali viṭarakattu arumicik koṇṭa, ciṟiyili nellit tīṅkaṉi eṉṉum auviyār pāṭalāl (puṟanāṉūṟu, 91) aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, ataṉkaṉi pātāḷavulakiṉiṉṟu peṟappaṭṭateṉṟār. pavaṉam - pātāḷam.
(51-53) kuṟunaṭip puṟaviṉ taputi kaṇṭu añci - taṉṉi aṭikkalamāyppukka ciṟiya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya oru puṟāviṉ aḻiviṉikkaṇṭu ataṟku añci, kuruti keḻu pimtaṭi muṟimuṟi taṭintu - ceṅkuruti tōynta taṉatu uṭampiṉ paccit taciyi ataṟku īṭākumpaṭi ciṟitu ciṟitāka aṟuttuvittu, kaṉṉam pukku ēṟiya maṉṉaṉum ilaṉē - aṅṅaṉam vittum aḵtu īṭākāmiyiṉ muṭivil tāṉē niṟittaṭṭil tāviyēṟi ammukattāl īṭuceytu ataṉip piḻippitta oru cōḻavēntaṉum iṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
iṅṅaṉam uyirkoṭuttup puṟavukātta peruṅkoṭit tiṟam,
taṉṉakam pukka kuṟunaṭip puṟaviṉ
  ṟaputi yañcik cīri pukka
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉkaṇṇum (43) ciṟappikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. taputi - aḻivu. taṭi- taci; kaṉṉam - niṟittaṭṭu.
(54-56) arum tamiḻp pulavōr perum tiṟam uṇarntu - arumivāynta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ periyamēṉmiyi uṇarntu pārttu, poṉṉum maṇiyum taṇṇaṭi nilaṉum variyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarum ilarē - avarkaṭkup paricākap poṉṉum maṇiyum marutanilattūrkaḷum vayalkaḷum ērāḷamāy utaviya puravalarkaḷum iññāṉṟu vāḻntilarē!
tiṟam - mēṉmi, cōti tiṟampāṭi, tiruvācakam, 7, 57. taṇṇaṭi - marutanilattūr (tivākaram). `variyātu vaḻaṅkiya veṉṟatu, aḷavupārāmal īntaveṉṉumporuṭṭu. iṅṅaṉam variyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarkaḷ paṇṭiruntaṉa reṉṉum uṇmikkuc cāṉṟu varumāṟu;
`patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum nūlil ēḻām pattup pāṭiya kapilar eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉukkuc cēramāṉ celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal nūṟāyiraṅ kāṇamum oru kuṉṟēṟik kaṇṭa nāṭum paricil koṭuttāṉ.
āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum māpperum pulavarkku avar icitta `paṭṭiṉappāli yeṉṉum nūliṉ ceḻuntamiḻt tiṟam uṇarntu cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ patiṉāṟu nūṟāyirampoṉ paricu aḷittāṉ.
`paraṇar eṉṉum pulavarpirāṉ `patiṟṟuppattil aintām pattup pāṭik, kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṭam umpaṟkāṭṭu vāriyiyum avaṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliyum paricil peṟṟāṉ.
iṉṉōraṉṉa paḻiya paricil varalāṟukaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟaṉa uḷa; aviyellām iṅku virippiṟ perukum.
munnū ṟūrttē taṇpaṟampu naṉnāṭu
  munnū ṟūrum paricilar peṟṟaṉar
eṉavarum (puṟanāṉūṟu, 110) paḻantamiḻilakkiyat tirumoḻikaḷum iṅkuniṉivukūraṟpālaṉa.
(57-59) meyyoṭu miṭiyāp poyporuḷīṭṭi mey vaḻikaḷoṭu kalavāta aḻiyumporuḷkaḷit tokuttu, tām aṟi aḷaviyil piṟarkku oṉṟu īyātu - tāmaṟintavariyil takkārkku oṉṟu koṭāmal, tāmē nukarum tīyarō uḷar- tāmē nukarkiṉṟa tīya iyalpiṉarō mikappalarāy irukkiṉṟaṉar. `tīyarō eṉpatil ōkāram mikutipporuḷ.
miṭiyā - kalavāta; miṭital - kalattal, poyyō ṭiṭimiṭintacol (nālaṭiyār, 80) eṉpuḻippōla.
`tam maṉaccāṉ ṟaṟiya oṉṟuṅ koṭār eṉṟaṟkut `tāmaṟi yaḷaviyiṟ piṟarkkoṉ ṟīyātu eṉṟār. aṉṟi ataṟkut `tamakkut tōṉṟiya aḷavilāyiṉum ētēṉuṅ koṭāmal eṉṟu poruḷurittalumām. `poyporuḷ eṉpuḻip poymi niliyāmip poruḷil vantatu.
(60-61) tamiḻvaḻakku aṟiyātu - centamiḻ vaḻakkukaḷiṉ nuṭpamum āḻamum parappum viḻuppamum aṟiyamāṭṭāmal, amivu ila kūṟum - poruttamillātavaṟṟip pēcum, kaṟṟaṟivu illār iṉip palar - kaṟṟaṟivillātavarē ippoḻutu mikap palarāvar; ēkāran tokkatu. `maṟṟu aci.
centamiḻik kallātavar ataṉ ṟiṟamaṟiyārākaliṉ, poruntātaṉavellāmpēci ikaḻtaluñ ceyvar. centamiḻukku naṉmi ceyyāmiyoṭu tīmiyuñ ceyvārē ulakil mika uḷareṉṟu itaṉkaṭ kuṟippittavāṟām.
(61-64) ataṉāl ātaliṉāl, avarkaṭi toṟum nittalum paṭarntu - av viyalpiṉāruṭiya illiṉ kaṭivāyil tōṟum nāḷuñceṉṟu, vāyvāḷātu vallāṅkuppāṭi - vāy cummā irāmal vallavāṟellām pāṭṭukaḷ pāṭi, koṉṉētirital iṉṉātu maṉ - vīṇē alital tīyatām mikavum; maṟṟu,  ō aci.
vallāṅku - vallapaṭi; vaṭiyanāviṉ vallāṅkuppāṭi (47) eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu. payaṉum iṉṟi iḻivum nērtaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam paṭarntu pāṭittirital iṉṉātāyiṟṟu.
eṉṟitukāṟun, tammi etirppaṭṭa pulavaṉinōkki, aṟivum īkiyaṟaṅkaḷum uḷḷa celvarkaḷātal aṟiñar kaḷātal illiyāyk, kalviyaṟivillātavarum picuṉarumē palarāy nirampiya iññāṉṟu niliyutal illāp poruḷpeṟa vēṇṭi attakiyōri naccinacci uyarnta centamiḻppākkaḷ pāṭi avarpāṟ cellutal peritun tītātal kūṟiṉār. ataṉpiṉ ellāmvalla murukapperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷi viḻintu cēṟalē naṉṟāmeṉak kūṟuvāṉ ṟoṭaṅki mutaṟkaṇ appemmāṉṟirukkōyil koṇ ṭeḻuntaruḷiyirukkun tiruvoṟṟimānakarc ciṟappiṉi nuvalkiṉṟār.
(65-67) koḻunaṉi arumpiya kāñci am kōṭṭil - koḻumiyāṉa moṭṭukaḷ arumpiya kāñcimarak kiḷiyil, puṉtalic ciṟuvar ēṟit tuṉṟukaṭal curitirippāyntu viḷiyāṭṭayaravum - eḷiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya neytaṟ ciṟuvarkaḷ ēṟi akkiḷiyiṉ arukiluḷḷa kaṭaliṉkaṭ curuḷum aliyiṟ kutittu nīr viḷiyāṭutalic ceyyavum;
`kāñci itu marutanilattiṟkuriyamaram eṉpatu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyāṟ (179) pulaṉākiṉṟatu; aḵtu iṅku neytal nilattukkaṇ vantatu karupporuḷ mayakkam; itu tiṇimayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭiniliyilavē eṉṉun tol kāppiya akattiṇiyiyaṟ cūttirattāl amikkappaṭum.
puṉṟalikku viḷakkam muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ayartal - ceytal, (tivākaram)
(68-77) veṇtōṭu virinta kiti am tātu meypaṭat timirnta noyciṟi vaṇṭiṉam - veṇṇiṟamāṉa maṭalkaḷ virinta tāḻampūviṉ makarantappoṭikaḷ uṭampumuḻutum paṭumpaṭi pūcik koṇṭa melliya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya vaṇṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, vili aṟu muttamoṭu talimayakkuṟṟuk kaṇmayakkēṟi eṇṇilakiṭappa - annilattil uṇṭāṉa viliyillā muttukkaḷuṭaṉ oruṅkukalantu tēṉ uṇṭa mayakkan talikkēṟi eṇṇaṟṟaṉa orē niṟamāyk kiṭappa, nuḷic ciṟu makaḷir vaḷikkiyiṉ vāri - neytal nilattuc ciṟu peṇkaḷ tammuṭiya vaḷiyalaṇinta kikaḷāl avaṟṟi aḷḷic cērttu, kuṭampuri paṇilam nirampap peytu - kuṭatti okkuñ caṅkukaḷiṉuḷḷē niṟiyac  corintu, āmi aṭuppil āmpal neruppiṉ tām aṟi tiṟattāl aṭuvuḻi āmi ōṭṭiṉālāṉa aṭuppiṉkaṇ cevvāmpaṟ pūvālāṉa neruppil tāmaṟinta vakiyāṟ camiyal ceyyumpoḻutu, mayakku aṟṟut tuṇṇeṉap paṟantu viṇṇiṭic cella - avvaṇṭukaḷellām kaḷḷuṇṭa mayakkam oḻintu tiṭumeṉap paṟantu vāṉveḷiyiṟ cella viyappiṭi eḻunta mayakkoṭu niṟkavum - ammakaḷirellām viyappūṭeḻunta matimayakkat toṭu niṟkavum;
`timirtal, pūcikkoḷḷutal, (maṇimēkali, 19, 86). noymi - meṉmi; vaṇṭukaḷ tātutimirntu veṇṇiṟa māyt tēṉum uṇṭu aṟivumayaṅki muttukkaḷōṭu muttuk kaḷāy vēṟṟumi aṟiyappaṭāmaṟ kiṭantaṉa. talimayaṅkal - oruṅkukalattal, pukiyavuñ cāntavum pūviṟ puṉinavum, vakiteri vaṟiyā vaḷantali mayaṅkiya (cilappatikāram, 14, 177, 178.) iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa neytalnilac ciṟāriṉ viḷi yāṭṭiyalpukaḷ peritum uṟṟuṇarntu makiḻaṟpālaṉa.
(78-81) vēṟucilar ciṟumiyar - vēṟucilarāṉa neytalnilac ciṟupeṇkaḷ, cāṟukeḻu karumpiṉ - cāṟu nirampiya karumpiyē ulakkiyākappaṟṟi, mīṉciṉi aṉṉa veṇmaṇal kuvii- mīṉ muṭṭikaḷippōṉṟa veṇṇiṟamāṉa noyya maṇalkaḷi nellākak kuvittu, tēmpoti maḻaliyoṭu ciṟupāṭṭu icittu - iṉimi niṟinta maḻali moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟuciṟu vēṭikkippāṭṭukaḷ pāṭi, vayiṉvayiṉ niṉṟu - iṭaṅkaṭōṟum niṉṟapaṭiyāy, makiḻvoṭu kuṟuvavum - makiḻcciyoṭu maṇalnel kuṟṟavum
karumpiyē ulakkiyākak kippaṟṟi maṇaliyē nellākak kuvittu ataṉāṟ kuṟṟic ciṟumiyar viḷiyāṭṭayarvar eṉṟavāṟu
veṇmaṇaliyoppa mīṉciṉikaḷum veṇmiyum meṉmiyum nuṇmiyum uṭiyavātal iṅku niṉivukūraṟ pālatu. kuṟutul kuṟṟutal, iṭittal; melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ kuṟiṉē eṉṟār kuṟuntokiyilum, 89: kol yāṉik kōṭṭāl vetirneṟ kuṟuvāmnām eṉṟār kalittokiyilum, 42.
(82-83) aḻakucāl neytal nalam uṟa maruviya oṟṟi mānakaril - iṅṅaṉamellām aḻaku nirampiya neytalnila iyalpukaḷ cemmiyākap poruntiya tiruvoṟṟi mānakariṉ kaṇ;
(83-87) poṉtoḻil polintu viṇtoṭa nivanta iñci vaḷiiya urukeḻu tirunakar kāṇṭoṟum paraci - poṟ pūccukkaḷālāṉa toḻiṟpāṭukaḷ aḻakupeṟa viḷaṅki vāṉantoṭṭu uyarnta matilkaḷ vaḷinta tōṟṟañ cāṉṟa tirukkōyilip pārkkum pōtellān toḻutu, mali kuyiṉṟu aṉṉa nili uyar talik kaṭi - malikaḷit tuḷittu amittāṟpōṉṟa vāyilnilikaḷ uyarnta tirukkōpuravāyiṟpaṭiyiṉkaṇ, añcuvaru nōkkamoṭu niṉṟeṉaṉāka - añcutaluḷḷa pārviyuṭaṉ otuṅki niṉṟēṉāka
 iñci-  matil, tivākaram. kuyiṉṟu eṉpatil mutaṉili kuyil atu tuḷittal eṉap poruḷpaṭum, kuṉṟu kuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa ōṅku nili vāyil eṉpatu neṭunalvāṭi, 88.
`nakar iṅkuk kōyil; mukkaṭcelvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉkaṇṇum (6) vantatu.

vīṟu - polivu; cāṟayarkaḷattu vīṟupeṟat tōṉṟi tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, 283.
yāriyō yeṉpatil ai ō aci; nampi nī yāri eṉṟāḷ
cīvakacintāmaṇi, 835. pukuttutal - uṭceluttutal.
pōti - varuti muṉṉiliyēval; pōtutal - cellutal varutal iṉi aṅku murukapperumāṉ vīṟṟirunta tirukkōlac ciṟappu 133-ām aṭikāṟum viritturikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(96-97) poṉ puṉi viḷakkam tam kiyiṉ ēnti - poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷit taṅkaḷ kikaḷil ēnti, miṉ puṉi makaḷir niralpaṭa niṟpa - miṉṉoḷiyiṉāl amikkappaṭṭā leṉat tikaḻum iḷamakaḷirkaḷ variciyāy niṟpa;
`viḷakkam eṉpatu irumaṭiyākupeyarāyk karuviyi yuṇarttum. `miṉpuṉi yeṉpuḻi `puṉi uvamapporuḷil vantatu.
puṉital- ceytal, varimaṇaṟ puṉipāvikku, puṟanāṉūṟu, 11.
(98-101) kival pāṭiṉi - yāḻ icittaliṟ kitērnta pāṇmakaḷ, kaṟi aṟap peṟṟa payaṉuṭi eḻāl nalaṉuṟa marīi - kuṟṟam nīṅkappeṟṟa payaṉuḷḷa yāḻi aḻakuṇṭākat taṉmaruṅkē irutti, viral nutiteṟitta - taṉ viral nuṉikaḷāṟ cuṇṭiya, narappu icinōkki - narampukaḷiṉ iciterintu, miṭaṟṟoli porutti neṟippaṭa miḻaṟṟa - taṉ kuralōciyi iṇittu ōcikaḷ muṟippaṭa icippavum;
pāṭiṉi - pāṭumpeṇ; icipāṭutaṟkeṉavē orukulam `pāṇkulam eṉṟu paṇṭināḷil iruntamiyiṉ, `pāṭiṉi yeṉpāḷ pāṇkulappeṇṇāvā ḷeṉka. kaṟi - kuṟṟam; eḻāl - yāḻ; `payaṉuṭiya eḻāl eṉa iṅkuvantāṟpōlavē pāṭalpaṟṟiya payaṉuṭiya eḻāal eṉap porunarāṟṟup paṭi (56) yiṉuḷḷum varutal niṉivukūrka. maruvutal - aṇukutal (tivākaram), maḻīi colliciyaḷapeṭi. `teṟittal, iṅku mīṭṭal.
(102-105) urum atirntaṉṉa maṇkaṉi muḻavamum - iṭi iṭittāṟpōl olikkum mārccaṉiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa mattaḷamum, varivēy turuviya urikeḻu kuḻalum - mali mūṅkilik kaṭintu tuḷitta pukaḻporuntiya pullāṅ kuḻalum, pēṇuṟumarapiṉ pāṇiyum - iṉṉum pōṟṟaṟkuriya taṉmikaḷ vāynta tāḷam mutaliya icikkaruvikaḷum, vallōr - icikkavalla icit tuṇivarkaḷ, pālkeḻu marapiṉ cīritiṉ iyakka - avvavaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ viḷaṅkun taṉmiyiṟ cevvaṉē yiyakkavum;
`maṇkaṉi muḻavam eṉpataṟku `mārccaṉiyiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa muḻavu eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār poruḷurittār; muḻaviṉ oru pakkattē pūcappaṭum paciyoṭukūṭiya maṇṇi mārccaṉi yeṉpar (cilappatikāram, 10,139).
maṇṇami muḻaviṉ paṇṇami cīṟiyāḻ
  oṇṇutal viṟaliyar pāṇi tūṅka
eṉṉum porunarāṟṟuppaṭi (110) yaṭiyuriyilum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar maṇ eṉpataṟku mārccaṉi eṉṟum, pāṇi eṉpataṟkut tāḷam eṉṟum poruḷurippar.
turuval - kaṭintu tuḷittal, ivvāṟu ceytalit turuvit tuḷittal eṉpar aṭiyārkkunallār (cilappati kāram, āycciyar kuravi, kolliyañcāral uri)
(106-107) cenneruppu ārnta poṉ urit taṭavil - civanta neruppu niṟinta poṉpūccup pūciya tūpakkālil, akil ney peytu palarniṉṟu pukippa - akilney ūṟṟip palarniṉṟu pukippavum;
`cenneruppu eṉṟatu, kaṉal mikka neruppi; uri - pūccu; taṭavu - tūpakkāl, iccol ipporuḷ payattal, maṭavaraṉ makaḷir taṭavunerup pamarntu (ūrkāṇ, kāti 99) eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattāl uṇarappaṭum.
(108-113) vaṭamalip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattu - vaṭakku mali yeṉappaṭum imayamaliyiṉkaṇ uṇṭāṉa periya oḷiyuḷḷa vaṭṭakkalliṉkaṇ, teṉmalip piṟanta naṟum kuṟaṭu urittu - teṟkumali yeṉappaṭum potiyamaliyil viḷinta maṇamuḷḷa cantaṉak kaṭṭiyit tēyttu, viḻumiya tiraṭṭiya ceḻunaṟum kuḻampil - cīritākat tiraṭṭiyeṭuttuk karitta ceḻumiyāṉa naṟumaṇak kuḻampil, aivaki viriyum virāay - aintuvakiyāṉa maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷuṅ kalantu, oy eṉat turuttip peytuceṟippa - curukkākat `turutti yeṉṉum oruvakit teḷikkuṅ karuviyilviṭṭu nirappa, palar niṉṟu talippeyal māriyiṉ maliccāntu uṟippa - palapēr variciyāy niṉṟu kārkālamutaliṟ poḻiyum maḻiyip pōl ap potiyamalic cantaṉakkuḻampic coriya;
`vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭam eṉpataṟku `aḻakukeḻumiya vaṭṭakkal eṉap poruḷurip piṉumām.
 vaṭamalip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut
  teṉmalip piṟanta cantaṉa maṟuka
eṉavaruñ cilappatikārat (antimāli. 37-8) tirumoḻikku ataṉ arumpata uriyāciriyar iṅṅaṉamē poruḷurippar.
`aivakiviri āvaṉa; `kōṭṭam, turukkam, takaram, akil, cantaṉam eṉpavi (piṅkalanti); takkōlam, ēlam, ilavaṅkam, cātikkāy, karppūram eṉṉun tivākaramum, aṭiyārkku nallāruriyum (cilappatikāram, 5, 26).
`oyyeṉa virivupporuṭṭu, nuṅkiyatu kēḷā vaḷaviyoyyeṉa (152) eṉpatu porunarāṟṟuppaṭi.
`turutti, paṉṉīrppuṭṭil pōlvatākat tōliṟ ceyyap paṭṭu maṇakkuḻampu pīccuṅ karuvi; ittakiyatoru karuvi paṇṭik kālattilēyē uṇṭeṉpatu, neymāṇ civiṟiyar (paripāṭal, 6, 34) eṉpataṟku uḷ viṭṭa puḻuku neyyāṉ māṇṭa turuttiyarāy eṉap parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟiya vuriyāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
uṟippa - mikaviḻa, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi (379) uri.
(114-122) vāḻiyum kamukum vayiṉvayiṉ nāṭṭi - vāḻi maraṅkaḷum pākkumaraṅkaḷum iṭaṅkaṭōṟum niṟutti, ōviya nuṭpamum oṇpaṇinuṭpamum mēviya paṭāam mēluṟakkaṭṭi - ōviya nuṭpaṅkaḷum ēṉip piṉṉal mutaliya aḻakiya toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷum poruntiya tiriyi mēṟparappil aṇiyak kaṭṭi, kaḻaṅku puri nittilam ilaṅkattūkki - kaḻaṅku eṉṉum oruvaki viḷiyāṭṭuk karuviyippōṉṟa parumuttaṅkaḷik kōviyāka viḷaṅkat toṅkaviṭṭu, kēḻkiḷar paṉmaṇi kāḻpaṭa nāṟṟi - aṅṅaṉamē niṟam mikunta palamaṇikaḷik kōviyākat toṅkavittu, perum taṇ kaṇavīra naṟum taṇ māli pālvēṟu marapil palapaṭa iyaṟṟi - periya kuḷirnta cevvalari māliyiyum oḻinta naṟiya kuḷirnta mālikaḷiyum palavakiyāṉa viyalpiṟ palavēṟu vakiyāka aṅkaṅkum aḻakupeṟa amittu, elimayirk kampalam nilaṉuṟa virittu - elimayiriṉālāṉa virippi nilam paṭiya virittu, viriyiṭi eḻuppiya ariyaṇi mītu - avvirippiṉ naṭuvil niṟuttappaṭṭa ciṅkak kaṭṭiliṉmēl;
ōviyam - cittiram. kaḻaṅku - kaḻaṟcikkāy (tivākaram), aṇaṅkaṟikaḻaṅkil naṟṟiṇi, 8. kāḻ - kōvi, vaṭam, orukāḻmuttam iṭimuliviḷaṅka akanāṉūṟu, 73.
peruntaṇ kaṇa vīra māli, naṟuntaṇmāli eṉap pirittuk kūṭṭip poruḷurikkum nacciṉārkkiṉiya ruriyē īṇṭikkum uriyāka vikkappaṭṭatu.
kaṇavīram - cevvalari; peruntaṇ kaṇavīra naṟun taṇmāli (236) eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyiṉkaṇ ittoṭar iṅṅaṉamē varutal niṉivukūraṟpāṟṟu. `elimayir eṉpatu, veḷḷeliyiṉ aṭivayiṟṟiluḷḷa mika melliya veṇ mayir; elimayir koṇṭu āṭi mutaliyaṉa neytal cilappatikāra vuriyiṉkaṇṇum (14: 205), cenneruppu uṇuñ cevvelim mayir, anneruppaḷavu āy poṟ kampalam eṉṉuñ cīvaka cintāmaṇiyiṉ kaṇṇuṅ (2686) kāṇappaṭum.
(123-129) vem katir ñāyiṟu maṇineṭum kōṭṭil taṅkiyirunta takimi pōlavum - veppam mikunta katirkaḷi yuṭiya pakalavaṉ nīla niṟam poruntiya - uyarnta kīḻmali yucciyil vikittōṉṟiya taṉmipōlavum, vāṉ katir matiyam mīṉ kuḻām cūḻat tāṉpolintu eḻunta talimipōlavum - vāṉattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa taṇkatir nilavu viṇmīṉ kūṭṭañ cūḻat tāṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya taliminili oppavum, aṟumukat toruvaṉ kuṟunaki ilaṅka - āṟu tirumukaṅkaḷiyuṭiya oppaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ puṉṉaki viḷaṅka, nacikeḻu viḻikaḷ ticimukam parappi - viruppam mēviya taṉ paṉṉiru viḻikaḷit ticikaṭōṟuñ cūḻappārttu, kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅka - kuṟiyāta aḻakōṭum aṅkeḻuntaruḷi viḷaṅkā niṟpaveṉka.

takimi pōlavun, talimi pōlavuṅ kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅkaveṉak kūṭṭuka. `eḻunta iṅkut tōṉṟiya veṉṉum poruṭṭu.

(137-145) maṭam tapu cil moḻi makiḻak kūṟi aṟiyāmi keṭukkuñ cila iṉcoṟkaḷi aṭiyēṉ makiḻumpaṭi tiruvāy malarntaruḷi, aṉpuṟu pulavōy - aṉpumikka pulavōy, añcal ōmpumati - añcutali nī oḻippāyāka, kālam kaṭanta kōla vippil nikarā iṉpam mikipaṭa nukarntu kaṭivaḻiyillā niliyoṭu polikuvi - kāla varampillāta aḻakiya mēl ulakattil iṇiyillāta pēriṉpatti nirampanukarntu iṟuvāyillā niliyuṭaṉ nī niliyāy viḷaṅkuvāy, eṉṟu pala nalluri piṉṟiyum kūṟit taṉ maruṅku iruntōṉ kuṟippa - eṉṟu pala naṉmoḻikaḷ piṉṉum moḻinta taṉ pakkal iruntōṉāṉa ēvaḷāḷaṉoruvaṟkuk kuṟippiṭa, avaṉum eṉ am ki paṟṟicceṉṟu koṅkukamaḻ nīrāṭu tuṟiyil niṟuviya aḷavi - avvēvalāḷaṉum eṉ uḷḷaṅ kiyip piṭittuk koṇṭupōy maṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟa nīrāṭumiṭattil niṟuttiya nērattil;
taputal - keṭutal; paṟitapu mutukuruku (180) eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟiṉ uriyiṟ kāṇka.
`cilmoḻi, cilamoḻi; añcil ōti (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 180) eṉpuḻi aḻakiya cilavāṉa mayiriṉiyuṭiyāy eṉpar urikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. añcal ōmpumati eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyiṟ kaṇṭatu (291). `kālaṅ kaṭanta kōla vippu eṉṟatu. murukapperumāṉ vīṟṟirukkum aruḷulakatti yeṉka; kōlam - aḻaku (piṅkalanti). kaṭivaḻi - muṭivunili; `akam ki yeṉpatu nilimoḻiyiṉ mutaloḻiya ēṉiyavellāṅ keṭṭu melliṉam mikuntu `aṅki yeṉa niṉṟatu; itu,

(146-154) kārmiṉ aṉiya ērkeḻu makaḷir kuṟunaki miḷira eṉ arukil pōntu - kārkālattu miṉṉaliotta aḻaku poruntiya iḷamakaḷir puṉṉaki viḷaṅka eṉpakkattil vantu, mācupotintu pāci āki īrumpēṉum tīrātuuṟiyum eṉ puṉtalik kuñci puṇarppuaṟa uḷari - aḻukku nirampip pāciyāy īrum pēṉum nīṅkātu uyirvāḻum eṉatu civanta taliyiluḷḷa mayiric cikkaṟakkōti, puḻukuney urittup paṉinīr āṭṭi īram pulara iṉpuki ūṭṭit tām eṉi viṭuttuppōka - puḻukuney tēyttup paṉinīrāl nīrāṭṭip piṉ ataṉ īram ularumpaṭi maṇamiṉiya akiṟpukiyip pukaṭṭi avarkaḷ eṉṉi viṭṭuc cella, ēṉōṉ piṟitōr nalliṭam pukutta - maṟṟōr ēvalāḷaṉ maṟṟoru ciṟanta iṭattil eṉṉi uṭcelutta;

(154-163) āṇṭi - aṅku, kaviṉkeḻu cilatar viriyiṉar pukuntu - aḻakumikka ēvalāḷar virivuṭiyarāy nuḻintu, viṇṇōr vēntaṉ kaṇ eṉap polinta eṉ tuṉṉal citāar nīkki - viṇṇavar kōṉukku uṭampeṅkum uḷḷa kaṇkaḷpōl viḷakkamāyt terinta eṉatu tikkappaṭṭa kiḻinta kantiyi akaṟṟi, muṉṉiya aravu uriaṉṉa āṭi uṭīi - matikkat takunta pāmpiṉ tōlpōṉṟa melliya āṭiyi uṭuttu, kalavic cāntam pulara aṭṭi- naṟumaṇap paṇṭaṅkaḷ kalanta cantaṉak kuḻampi mārpil ularumpaṭi appi, mukinekiḻntu virinta takicāl nalmalar piṇiyalākki maṇamuṟa milicci - mokku muṟukkaviḻntu malarnta aḻaku nirampiya ciṟanta malarkaḷi māliyākat toṭuttu maṇaṅ kamaḻumpaṭi cūṭṭi, tuḷaṅku oḷik kalaṉkaḷ viḷaṅka aṇintu - niḻalāṭukiṉṟa oḷiyiyuṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi aḻaku viḷaṅkumpaṭi pūṭṭi, maṇittavicu iṭṭu ataṉmici eṉi irīi - aḻakiya irukkiyi iṭṭu ataṉmēl eṉṉi irutti;

tuṉṉal - tittal; citār - kanti; vēṟṟiḻinuḻinta vēr naṉi citāar (69) eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu; `tuṉṉal citāar eṉa iṅku vantāṟpōlavē,

(164 - 174) kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttakam virīi - iḷamiyāṉa vāḻiyiṉ kurutti uḷvirittu, aṟucuvikkaṟivum - āṟu vēṟu cuviyuḷḷa kaṟivakikaḷum, aṭicilum - cōṟum, tuviyum - tuviyalum, veṟikamaḻ kaṉiyum - maṇam vīcukiṉṟa muppaḻamum, naṟuneyum - nallaneyyum, pālum - āviṉpālum, tīm cumit tayirum - iṉiya ēṭṭuttayirum, tēm kaṉi paḻaṉum - tittippuk kaṉinta ēṉip paḻavakikaḷum, kaṉṉalum - carukkariyum, piṟavum - piṟa tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum, tuṉṉa iyaṟṟi - neruṅkappaṭittu, kaṭum pacitīra yāṉ micitoṟum micitoṟum - mikka pacinīṅka yāṉ uṇṇuntoṟum uṇṇuntoṟum, iṭumpiiṉṟi iṭiiṭi iṭupu - tuṉpa meṉpa tillāmal iṭiyiṭiyē avarkaḷ piṉṉum iṭutalāl pāḻ vayiṟu eṉṟum nirampap peytaliṉ - muṉ pāḻāykkiṭanta eṉ vayiṟu niṟiyumpaṭi yāṉ ataṉkaṇ avaṟṟi iṭutalālē, perum poruḷ eytiya vaṟiñaṉiṉ allāntu iruntapiṉ - mikka celvatti aṭinta vaṟiyavaṉippōl makiḻntiruntapiṉ, eṉṉip poruntak kūuy ēṉiyōr iṭattil tāṉ eṉic celuva - eṉṉi maṉam porunta aḻittu maṟṟōriṭattil av vēvalāḷaṉ eṉṉic celutta;
`kumari yeṉpatu iḷamipporuṭṭu, itaṉi `īṉātavāḻi eṉpar cilappatikāra (16, 42) arumpatavuriyāciriyar; kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttakam virittu eṉpatu cilappatikārac coṟṟoṭar.
`cumittayir ēṭṭut tayireṉavum, ēṭu āṭipōl mēl mūṭiyiruttaliṉ, āṭittayir eṉavum vaḻaṅkum; ivvuṇmi tīm pāṟ cumit tayirum (kōvintiyār, 73) eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyāṉum ataṉ uriyāṉun teḷiyappaṭum.
`tēṅkaṉipaḻam eṉpuḻik `kaṉipaḻam viṉittoki. `veṟikamaḻkaṉi yeṉa muṉ vantiruttaliṉ ataṉi muppaḻam eṉavum, `tēṅkaṉipaḻam eṉṉum itaṉi ēṉip paḻavakikaḷeṉavum urittukkoḷka. `piṟavum eṉṟatu, vaṭi pāyacam mutaliya tiṉpaṇṭa vakikaḷ.

(174-185) āṅku - avviṭattil, poḻinīṟu iṭṭu - aruḷ poḻikiṉṟa tirunīṟṟi iṭṭu, viḻimaṇi tāṅki - civapirāṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭuñ civamaṇikaḷ aṇintu, kāntaḷpōlac cēntoḷir aṅkiyil civañāṉapōtam naviyaṟac civaṇi - ceṅkāntaḷ malarkaḷpōlac civantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻakiya kiyiṟ `civañāṉapōtam eṉṉum meykaṇṭanūlik kaṟpāratu kuṟṟam nīṅkumpaṭi ēnti, paḻutuaṟu māṇavar kuḻuvoṭu cūḻa - kuṟṟamaṟṟa māṇākkarkaḷ ēṉit takutiyuḷḷa aṉpar kūṭṭattuṭaṉ cūḻntirukka, vāṉkatir maṇṭilam īṅku vantatupōl tiruvoṭu viḷaṅkum uruku poṉ tavicil - vāṉattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa oḷimaṇṭilamē iṅku iṟaṅkivantāṟ pōla mēṉmiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa urukāniṉṟa poṉṉiṉ oḷivāynta irukkiyil, kalmaṉam urukkum kāṇtaku kōlamoṭu - kallotta vaṉṉeñciyum urukkukiṉṟa kāṇṭaṟkuttakka tirukkōlattuṭaṉ, eṉmaṉam urukki eḻuntiṉitaruḷiya - ēḻiyēṉatu uḷḷattiyum urukki iṉiteḻun taruḷiya, cōmacuntara kuravaṉik kāṇūu - cōmacuntara tēcikaṉik kaṇṭu, ceykuvatu aṟiyēṉ meytaṭu māṟi nilampaṭavīḻntu kalaṅkiniṟpa - ceyvatu teriyēṉāy uṭampu vitirttu nilattiṟ porunta viḻuntu vaṇaṅki añci niṟpa;
`poḻinīṟu aruḷpoḻiyun tirunīṟu; viḻimaṇi, viḻiyākiya maṇi, āvatu `akkamaṇi yeṉpatu. `cērntu, civantu; itaṟkuc cemmi, mutaṉili.

`civaṇal kiyiṟ porunta vittal; āvatu `ēntal eṉṉum poruṭṭām.
vāṉkatir maṇṭilam - cūriyamaṇṭilam, aḵtinnilattu iḻintāṟ pōl eḻuntaruḷiya cōmacuntara kuravaṉeṉakkūṭṭuka.
urukāniṉṟa poṉ oḷi mikutiyumuṭimiyiṉ poṉ miṉukkup pūciya tavicukku aḵtu aṭimoḻiyāyiṟṟu.
`kaṉmaṉam `eṉmaṉam eṉpavaṟṟil iḻivu ciṟappummi tokkatu. kāṇūu, ceyyūveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi yeccam; aṟiyēṉ, muṟṟeccam.
(186-192) yāriyō eṅkaḷ aṟumukattu aiyaṉ tanta tōr aruḷiṉ vantaṉipōlum - nī yār eṅkaḷ āṟumukak kaṭavuḷ aḷittatō raruḷiṉāl iṅku vantaṉipōlum, nalli nalli - nī naṉṉiliyuṭiyi naṉṉiliyuṭiyi, elliyil kālam aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ niliyiṉi - aḷavupaṭāta kālam aḻiyāppēṟṟuṭaṉ niliyāka vāḻvi; viḻumiyi - ciṟap puṭiyi; cevviyi periyi auviyam ili - cemmiyuṭiyi perumiyuṭiyi aḻukkāṟu mutaliya tīya iyalpukaḷili, eṉṟu iṉiyaṉa palavum paṉivarak kiḷantu - eṉa iṉiyavāṉa coṟkaḷpalavum uḷḷaṅ kuḷirumpaṭi iṉimi yāyc colli, taṉ malarkki eṉ puṉtalic cērtti - tamatu malaraṉiya tirukkiyi eṉatu eḷiya talimēl vittu;
malarkkiyit taliyiṟ cērttal, taṉkaṇ niṟinta aruḷāṟṟali māṇākkaṉukku uyttu avaṉṟaṉ uṭal uyirkaḷit tūymiceytaṟ poruṭṭu āciriyaṉceyyum aruḷvayamāṉa aṟituyilmuṟi; itaṉic camayanūlkaḷuḷ `tīkki yeṉpa.
(192-207) pulappaṭa - viḷaṅkumpaṭiyāka, nīyē uṭampum poṟiyu kaṭampaṭukaruviyum aṟivum vaḷiyum alli - uyirākiya nī uṭampum aimpoṟiyum av vimpoṟikaḷākiya kāṭṭu vaḻic cellum akakkaruvikaḷ nāṉkum aṟivum uyirppum alli; aṟivāyc ceṟiyum peṟṟiyi-  aṟiviyaṟki yuṭiyiyāy yātoṉṟu paṟṟiṉ ataṉ vaṇṇamāy niṟkun taṉmiyi; maṟṟuavi aṟiyāp paṇpiṉa - coṉṉa aviyellām aṟiyāta taṉmiyaṉa; aṉiyiyiṉum muṉpē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇṇiṉ miṭipaṭu mācupotinta nīrmiyi - nī aṅṅaṉam aṟiviyaṟki uṭiyiyāyiṉun toṉṟutoṭṭē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇkaḷippōṟ ceṟikiṉṟa aṟiyāmiyākiya kaṟi mūṭiya iyalpiṉi, vīciya oḷip piḻampāki veḷippaṭu murukaṉ āṉātu curanta aruḷiṉaṉākaliṉ - āṉāl iyaṟkiyē viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟivoḷip piḻampāy veḷippaṭu murukapperumāṉ oḻiyātu ūṟṟeṭukkum aruḷuṭiyaṉātalāl, niṉṉimaṟitta tuṉṉiruḷ turantu taṉ aruṭpolivāl niṉ aṟivu koḷīi - niṉṉi iyaṟkiyē maṟitta ceṟinta malaviruḷi ōṭṭit taṉ aruḷviḷakkattāl niṉ aṟivi viḷakki, arukā iṉpam tarutaṟporuṭṭu - ammukattāl niṉakkuk kuṟiyāta pēriṉpattit tarum poruṭṭu, meynili irutti mācupōkkit taṉ nili niṉakkut tantaṉaṉ - cemporuḷ kāṇum meyyuṇarviṉ niliyil niṉṉi irutti niṉatu malavaḻukkik kaḻittut taṉatu tiruvaṭip pēriṉpaniliyi niṉakkuk koṭuttaruḷiṉaṉ; kōṭi  - nī ataṉip peṟṟukkoḷvāyāka; eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟi viṭuppat tēṟip paṉṉirukiyaṉ muṉṉar eyti - eṉṟu uyirkkuṟuti payappaṉa aṟivuṟuttu viṭuppa avvāṟṟāl uḷḷanteḷivaṭintu paṉṉiru tirukkikaḷiyuṭiya murukappemmāṉṟirumuṉ aṭintu;
pulappaṭak (192) kūṟi (206) eṉa iyittuk koḷka.
kaṭampaṭal - neṟiccellal, kaṭam - kāṭṭuneṟi; kaṭampala kiṭanta kāṭuṭaṉkaḻintu (kāṭukāṇ, 90) eṉpatu cilappatikāram; aimpoṟikaḷiṉūṭu taṉvayamiṉṟic cellum maṉavaṟivu palavāṟṟālum alikkappaṭutaliṉ aimpoṟikaḷ kāṭākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷi - uyirppu, mūccu.
`maṟṟavi yeṉṟaṉa; uṭampu aimpoṟi akakkaruvi vaḷi mutaliyaṉa. `mācu āṇavamalameṉṉum aḻukku; `vīciya oḷi vīciya; aḵtāvatu oḷiviḷaṅkiya veṉṟaṟku. `viṉiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya veṉpatu karuttu.
`tuṉiruḷ ceṟinta āṇava valliruḷ; āṇavamalam aṟiyāmi payattaliṉ, iruḷeṉappaṭuvata marapu; tuṉṉiruḷē eṉṉun tiruvācakattil (civapurāṇam) pōla. `turantu ōṭṭi; eṉṉuṭiyiruḷi ēṟat turantum (kīrttit tiruvakaval) eṉṟu maṇivācakapperumāṉ ipporuḷilēyē aruḷic ceytal kāṇka; punāṉūṟṟilum (14) muṉpu turantu caman tāṅkavum eṉappōntatu.
`emnili yeṉṟatu, ematu cērkkiyil eṉṟaṟku. kōṭi, koḷ pakuti; tēṟi, kalaṅkiya uḷḷam teḷinteṉka.
ippakutiyiṟ civacittānta nuṭpaṅkaḷ mika aḻakākat teḷittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(208-231) imiyampūtta paṉikeḻu cuṉiyil amiyātu āṭiya umitaru putalvōy - imiya maliyiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkiya kuḷircciporuntiya nīrūṟṟil tōṉṟi avāaṭaṅkāmal viḷiyāṭiya umippirāṭṭiyār tanta kuḻantāy, eriviḻittu imikkum tirunutal tantikku orumoḻiviritta teḷuṟum uṇarvōy - tīkkaṇ tiṟantu oḷiviṭum aḻakiya nutaliyuṭiya tantiyākiya civaperumāṉukku ōmeṉum orumoḻip poruḷi virittaṟivuṟutta teḷivu mikka pēraṟivuṭiyōy, vāṉōr uṟukaṇ tīrppa mēl ōr aivaṭivēlik kiyiṉil eṭuttōy - tēvarkaḷuṭiya tuṉpaṅkaḷi nīkkumporuṭṭu muṉṉorukāl oppaṟṟa takaṭṭuvaṭiviṉi yuṭiya kūriya vēlik kiyiṉil ēntiyōy, vēntaṉ tanta ēntu - eḻil celvikku varipuri mārpam variyātu aḷittōy - tēvarkōṉ īnta mikka aḻakiṉiyuṭiya putalviyākiya teyvayāṉikku niṉatu malipōṉṟa mārpāṟ ṟaḻuvum iṉpatti aḷavu paṭāmal utaviyōy, kuṉṟavar vaḷartta poṉtikaḻ vaḷḷikkuk koḻukompu ākiya viḻumiya tōḷōy - kuṉṟattuṟivārāṉa kuṟavarkaḷ eṭuttu vaḷartta aḻaku viḷaṅkiya vaḷḷiyammiyākiya pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷukkuk koḻukompāyniṉṟa ciṟanta tōḷkaḷi yuṭiyōy, arumtamiḻk kīraṉip poruntik kāttōy - ariya tamiḻnalamvāynta nakkīratēvari aṇukik kātta pemmāṉē, emmaṉōrkku eḷivanta cemmiyāḷa - ēḻiyēṉ pōṉṟārkku eḷitil eḻuntaruḷivanta cemmiyuṭiyōṉē, oruva - oru muḻu mutalōṉē, ciṟuva - iḷiyōṉē, karumayil muruka - nīlamayili ūrtiyāka uṭiya murukapperumāṉē, maṟimuṭitaṉṉil niṟi tiru uruviṉi- tirunāṉ maṟikaḷiṉ muṭipil niṟikiṉṟa ciṟanta uruvuṭiyi, kaṇṇum uṇarvum katuvāp peṟṟiyi - kaṇṇuṅ karuttum aṇukip paṟṟaviyalāta peruntaṉmiyuṭiyi, aimperum pūtamum ākiniṉṟaṉi - maṇ mutalāṉa aimperum poruḷkaḷum puṇarntu niṉṟaṉi, ulakuniṉ uruvē - avvim mutaṟporuḷkaḷiṉ kalaviyāṉa ivvulakam niṉakku uruvāyuḷḷatu, oḷikaḷ niṉ viḻiyē - ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷun tīyumāṉa oḷikaṉ niṉakkuk kaṇkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa; alakilāc camayattu avvavar tamakku palavēṟu uruvil nilavuki nīyē - eṇṇilāc camayaṅkaḷiyuṭiya avvaccamayat tārkkum palavēṟu vakiyāṉa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷil viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉi nīyē, āṟu niṉ mukam - āṟu niṉ tirumukamām, aintu niṉ vaṭivu - maṇmutalāka aimmutaṟ poruḷkaḷum niṉ uruvākum, nāṉku niṉmoḻi - tirunāṉmaṟikaḷākiya nāṉkum niṉ tirumoḻikaḷākum; mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇ - mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇkaḷākum, iraṇṭu niṉ tuṇi - vaḷḷi teyvayāṉiyeṉa iruvar niṉ vāḻkkittuṇivarāvar, oṉṟu niṉ vēl - oṉṟu niṉ vēlākum, maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimi eytā muruṭaṉēṟkum tiruvaruḷ taruki - mayaṅkiya aṟivoṭu aruṭṭaṉmi aṭiyamāṭṭāta muraṭṭuttaṉa muṭiyēṟkun tiruvaruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉi;
`pūtta, iṅku `viḷaṅkiya veṉṉumporuṭṭu; mīṉāram pūtta viyaṉkaṅki nantiya eṉṉum paripāṭaliṟ (16, 36) pōla. ciṟumakār nīril avāvaṭaṅkāmal viḷiyāṭutal iyaṟki yākaliṉ `amiyātu āṭiya veṉṟār. `eri ākupeyarāl neṟṟikkaṇṇi uṇarttum; imittal - oḷiviṭal, tivākaram. `orumoḻi, `ōm eṉpatu, aḵtu ākupeyarāy ataṉ poruḷi uṇarttiṟṟu; iḵtu eṇṇi yurukum kurunātaṉ eṉpāl uritta ōrmoḻiyē eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum aruḷicceytaṉar. uṟukaṇ - tuṉpam; āvatu tēvarkaṭkuc cūraṉāl viḷinta tuṉpam eṉka.
aimmi - takaṭṭuvaṭivu, tivākaram.
vaṭivēl - vaṭittuk kūrmiyākkiyavēl; murukappirāṉ kiyatu vaṭikkappaṭṭa taṉṟāyiṉum, vēl eṉṉum peyarop pumi paṟṟi avvāṟaṭi koṭuttār; piḷḷiyāratu kivēl aruḷ vaṭiviṉatu; upāṅkamellān tāṉaruḷ eṉṟār civañāṉa cittiyārilum (1, 47). tēvarkaṭku vēntaṉ intiraṉ.
`vaḷḷi yeṉpatu `orukoṭi yeṉavum poruḷpaṭu mākaliṉ, atukaṇṭu aṭikaḷ `koḻukompu eṉat tanturitta aḻaku iṅku niṉivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum. eṉavē `vaḷḷi yeṉṉuñ collukku `vaḷḷiyammi yeṉṉum pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷuk keṉa urikūṟi makiḻkaveṉpatu. teyvayāṉi ammikku `mārpam aḷittōy eṉavum, vaḷḷiyammikkuk koḻukompākiya tōḷōy eṉavum aruḷicceyta kuṟippu, teyvayāṉiyip pōlallāmal vaḷḷiyammiyik kaḷaviṉāl vēṭṭa vēṟupāṭṭuṇmi viḷaṅkavittaṟ poruṭṭeṉka.
karumi, iṅku nīlaniṟam uṇarttum. maṟimuṭi, vētattiṉ muṭinta karuttu. alaku - aḷavu. `uruviṉi eṉpatil `ai muṉṉili vikuti; `nilavuni, nakaram peyariṭinili; `taruki eṉpuḻiyum atu. `mukamē mutalāṉavaṟṟil varum ēkāraṅkaḷ aci. maruṇṭa uṇarvuṭiyār eytā māṭcimiyāvatu iṟivaṉatu aruṭ ṭaṉmiyēyāku mākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam uri kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(231 - 235) eṉṟu yāṉ aṟi aḷaviyiṉ ētta - eṉṟiṅṅaṉamellām yāṉ terintavariyil vaḻutta, tāṉ uvantu kuṟippu arum iṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉi - apperumāṉ maṉamakiḻntu cuṭṭiyuṇartaṟkariya pēriṉpattil uyarttu vittaṉaṉ eṉṉi, atumutal iṉpam ārntu aṅku iruttal allatu tuṉpam eṉpatu aṟiyēṉ - atumutalāka iṉpam nukarntu avaṉ ṟiruvaṭikkaṇ vāḻntirutta lallāmal tuṉpameṉpatu ciṟitum aṟiyēṉ;
tāṉ, perumāṉ; iṟivaṉṟarum pēriṉpanili iṉṉa teṉṟu kuṟittukkūṟal iyalātatākaliṉ, `kuṟipparum iṉpam eṉappaṭṭatu. eṉṟatu, avaṉ cuṭṭiyuṇarappaṭā ṉeṉpatu karutteṉka.
`piṟakkiṉaṉ, piṟaviṉi; `uyarttu vittaṉaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ; `piṟaṅkal, uyartal; perumi piṟaṅkiṟ ṟulaku eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (23) pōla; ārtal - nukartal.
(235-269) aṉpoṭu nīyum avaṉvayiṉ celiṉē pāy aruḷ oruvantam tarukuvaṉmaṉṟa - uḷḷaṉpoṭu nīyum ap perumāṉpāṟ ceṉṟāl avaṉṟaṉ perukiya tiruvaruḷi uṟutiyāy niṉakkut tantaruḷuvāṉ mikavum niṉṉi aṇuki, talippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malippaṭu nīttam - kārkālat tuvakkattiṟ peyyum peru maḻiyiṉāṟ porunti mali mukaṭṭi luṇṭākum aruviveḷḷam, pim niṟap pīliyum cemniṟat tukirum vēḻa maruppum kāḻ akil tuṇiyum oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viriik kuṇṭu kaṇ paṭukaril maṇṭi niṟintāṅku - pacumiyāṉa niṟam vāynta mayiṟṟōkiyum civanta niṟam vāynta pavaḷamum yāṉikaḷiṉ veṇṇiṟamāṉa kompum akakkāḻ uḷḷa atiṟkaṭṭikaḷiṉ tuṇṭum oṉṟāka vārikkoṇṭu ammaliyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil virintōṭi vīḻntu āḻamuṉa iṭattiyuṭiya perum paḷḷaṅkaḷil mikkuc ceṉṟu niṟintāṟpōl, avalak kavaliyum uvalic cuviyum muraṇuṟu aṟivum tirukiya cerukkum mutalaṟap peyarttuc citiyātu ōṭi aṉpiṉ niṟinta iṉpa veḷḷattu - vaṟumiyāluṇṭāṉa kavaliyum ulakap poy nukarcciyum māṟupaṭukiṉṟa tīya aṟivum muṟukiyacerukkum aṭiyōṭu illiyāmpaṭi nīkki iṭiyil aḻiyāmaṟ ceṉṟu aṉpiṉāl niṟinta iṉpameṉṉum veḷḷattiṉkaṇ, paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṉ tuvaṉṟi ēḻulaku aḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu ūḻi ūḻiyum vāḻmaticiṟantē - paḻikkappaṭāta cuṟṟattāruṭaṉ ciṟappāy niṟintiruntu ēḻulakam aḻintālum aḻiyāmal ūḻiūḻikkālamum ellā nalaṅkaḷum mikuntu vāḻvāyāka veṉpatu.
`nīyum eṉṟatu, etirppaṭṭa pulavari; pāyaruḷ - perukiya tiruvaruḷ; oruvantam - uṟuti; iccol ipporuṭṭātal,
veruvanta ceytoḻukum veṅkōla ṉāyiṉ
  oruvantam ollik keṭum
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷāl (563) teḷivāyp peṟappaṭum. `maṉṟa teḷivupporuḷatu; maṉṟaveṉkiḷavi tēṟṟañ ceyyum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (col. 267). maruvaral - maruval; āvatu aṇukaleṉka. aṇittal, kiṭṭal, poruntutal mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkellām itaṉip potuc collāka urippa; talippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malippaṭunīttam, eṉak kūṭṭuka.
pīli - mayiṟṟōki; `pīlipey cākāṭum acciṟum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ, 475. kuṇṭu - āḻam; paṭukar - paḷḷam, tivākaram.
tuṇi - tuṇṭu. varaṉṟal - vārutal. maṇṭi - mikkucceṉṟu. kaṭaṟpaṭi kuḷippamaṇṭi (puṟanāṉūṟu, 6) eṉpataṉuriyik kāṇka.
avalam - miṭi, vaṟumi; keṭuniṉṉavalam perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, 38.
`uvaliccuvi yeṉṟatu, iṅku ulakap poy nukarcciyi; uvali - poy; uvaliccamayaṅkaḷ ovvāta cāttiramām (tiruvācakam, tirutteḷḷēṇam, 17) eṉpuḻip pōla. poynukarcci yāvatu `poruḷallavaṟṟip poru ḷeṉṟuṇarum (tirukkuṟaḷ, meyyuṇartal, 1). uṇarcci yāṉākum nukarcciyeṉka. iṅṅaṉam oṉṟu kiṭakka oṉṟāṉa niliyaṟṟa meyyallāc ciṟu nukarcci iṅku uvalic cuviyeṉṉun toṭarāṟ cuṭṭiyurikkappaṭṭami peritum iṉpam payappatākum.
tiruvaruḷ nōkkattiṟku māṟupaṭṭa aṟivu  eṉṟaṟku `muraṇuṟu aṟivu eṉṟu aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭatu. tirukiya cerukku - mikutiyāṉa allatu muṟukiya cerukku, kaṭuṅkatir tirukaliṉ cilappatikāram, 12, 1.
kaṭumpu - cuṟṟattār, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 153) tuvaṉṟi - niṟin tiruntu. iḷiyarum mutiyaruṅ kiḷiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi (perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, 268) eṉpataṉ uriyik kāṇka. (28)
29. kāppuk kimmikkuk kāmam peruki yurittal
 ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cevvē ḷivari
  maṟaṅtiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
  tiṇṭō ṭaḻuvuva miṉṟēṟ ceḻuvariyik
  kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.
(i-ḷ) ciṟantaporuḷ - ēḻiyēṉ uyirkkuc ciṟanta poruḷ, oṟṟic cevvēḷ - tiruvoṟṟinakaril eḻuntaruḷiyirā niṉṟa murukap perumāṉē yāvar, ivari - ipperumāṉi, maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - maṟantu ivvulakil uyir vāḻamāṭṭōm, paṟantu ōṭittiṇtōḷ taḻuvuvam - virintu ceṉṟu avaratu valiya tōḷit taḻuvuvōm, iṉṟēl - aṅṅaṉam atu kūṭātāyiṉ, ceḻuvariyik kaṇṭu ēṟik kīḻviḻuvam - oru periya maliyik kaṇṭu ataṉ ucciyil ēṟik kīḻē uruṇṭu viḻuntu uyir tuṟappōm;
`ciṟantaporuḷ oṟṟic cevvēḷ ivari maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm eṉpataṉ karuttu,
  eṉṉi lāru meṉakkiṉi yārilli
  eṉṉi lummiṉi yāṉoru vaṉṉuḷaṉ
  eṉṉu ḷēyuyirp pāyppuṟam pōntupuk
  keṉṉu ḷēniṟku miṉṉampa rīcaṉē
eṉṉum apparaṭikaḷ tēvāra (tiruviṉṉampar, tirukkuṟun toki, 1) aruṇmoḻik karuttōṭu ottuniṟṟal niṉivu kūraṟ pāṟṟu.
`cevvēḷ civanta niṟattiṉiyuṭiya vēḷ eṉa murukap pirāṉ mēl niṉṟatu; vēḷ - kāmaṉ; māṭṭōm, taḻuvuvam, viḻuvam eṉap paṉmiyāka vantatu, talivi taṉṉiyotta ēṉik kaṟpuṭi makaḷiriyañ cērttuk kūṟiyavāṟām; allatu avaḷ taṉṉit taṉmip paṉmiyil vittuk kūṟiṉa ḷeṉṟaluḷ oṉṟu. `paṟantu eṉṟatu, virivuk kuṟippu; ceḻuvari, vaḷamāṉa mali āvatu uyarntamali yeṉṟaṟku; kāṇ, muṉṉiliyaci.
itu, cevilittāy mutaliyōratu kāvaliṟ ciṟip paṭṭuḷḷa talivi kāmamperukit taṉatu kaṟpuṭimi uritta vāṟām. taṉṉilimiyip piṟaraṟiyavoṭṭātapaṭi evvaḷavu aṭakkiṉālum taṉṉuḷḷa nikaḻcciyāl aḵtu aṭaṅkātu veḷippaṭutalē iṉiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ `kimmikal eṉappaṭum. kimmikal nalital cūḻcci vāḻttal (poruḷ, 260) eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāṉum ataṉ uriyāṉum itu teḷiyappaṭum. (29)
30. aṭiyuṟiyiṭutal
`aṭiyuṟi yiṭutalāvatu, tiruvaṭikkāṇikki celuttu taleṉka.
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyi yēṉuṅ kacittavaṉpāṟ
  pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅkumoṟṟi
  vāṇap peruntaki yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
  pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōviyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.
(i-ḷ) kāṇap peṟātatōr kāṭciyiyēṉum - ēṉi evvāṟṟāṉuṅ kāṇṭaṟkiyalātatōr ariya tōṟṟa muṭiyiyē yāyiṉum, kacinta aṉpāl - nekiḻnta uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pēṇappeṟum mukkaruviyiṉ kāṇpar - pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa niṉivu coṟ ceyal eṉṉum mūṉṟu karuvikaḷālum niṉṉik kāṇum peruntavamuṭiyōr niṉṉikkāṇpar, piṟaṅkum oṟṟi vāṇap peruntaki eṉpatu koṇṭu - viḷaṅkum tiruvoṟṟimānakaril tirukkōyil koṇṭuvāḻum pēraruṭṭaṉmiyuṭiya perumāṉē! eṉṉum ak karutti uṭkoṇṭu, vaḻaṅkum aṉpāl - ēḻiyēṉ tēvarīrukku aṭiyuṟiyāka vaḻaṅkum uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pūṇappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvi uṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē - tēvarīr aṇiyappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvi yeṉṉum nūl num aḻakiya tiruvaṭikaṭku eṉpatu.
pēṇal iṅkuppātukāttup pōṟṟutal eṉka; eṉṟatu, aṉpu neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piṟaḻātapaṭi kāttup pōṟṟutaleṉpatu, vāṇar, vāḻnar eṉpataṉ tirivu. `peruntaki yeṉṟatu, vaḻaṅkuvār evarāyiṉum avaratu aṉpiṉiyuṉṉi ataṉi ēṟkum peruntaṉmiyuṭiyā reṉṟaṟku. `mummaṇikkōvi innūliṉ peyar.
cālavum ariyiyāyiṉum aṉpiṉāl eḷiyiyātal pōl im `mummaṇikkōvi yeṉṉum nūli nī pūṇṭaruḷutal iciyātēṉum, aṉpiṉāl vaḻaṅkutaliṉ nī ataṉi ēṟṟu aṇintaruḷutal kūṭum eṉṟu urittaruḷiya vāṟām. aṉpiṟkoṉṟumē ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ eḷiyaṉām uṇmi, yāvarāyiṉum aṉpa raṉṟi aṟiyoṇā malarccōtiyāṉ (tiruvācakam, ceṉṉip pattu, 1) eṉṉuñ centamiḻ aruḷuriyāl iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. (30)
